Its efficient variants reduce the cost mainly by stopping full-trajectory sampling early or distributing the number of samples across questions. In this paper, we propose StepBack, a step-level error-localized resampling method for efficient test-time reasoning. Given a small set of draft trajectories, StepBack localizes likely first error positions using lightweight signals and then resamples the detected error step and its suffix, followed by majority answer voting. Empirically, StepBack matches the accuracy of SC on average across seven mathematical reasoning and multi-hop QA benchmarks, while reducing token usage by up to 41.26%. In addition, by nature, StepBack can be combined with adaptive inference methods such as Adaptive Consistency, further improving their inference-time efficiency.

</details>


### [70] [The Gold Rush in AI4Math, II: What are signatures of AI-assisted Proofs?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08412)
*Yuanchuan Guo,Jiashun Jin,Zheng Tracy Ke,Bingcheng Sui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 构建五个共8,281篇论文的队列并提出ProofTrace框架（将论文表示为"即时证明支持图"GIPS并提取可解释的数学组织特征），发现AI参与度呈现定性梯度、AI生成证明存在可复现的组织特征，且在检测"实质性AI参与"时性能优于文本检测器且对AI改写稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI在数学中的使用日益增多，需要把"AI参与证明"与"AI辅助写作"区分开来加以检测。已有研究面临三大挑战：缺乏匹配的病例-对照论文对、AI使用披露可能不完整、缺乏现成可用于刻画证明中AI参与信号的特征。

Method: 将历史arXiv论文、近期披露实质性AI使用的论文与OpenAI发布的手稿，连同研究者用生成式AI生成的稿件合并，构建五个队列（共8,281篇），覆盖不同AI参与水平并包含针对同一研究问题的匹配论文对。提出ProofTrace框架：把每篇论文表示为"即时证明支持图"（GIPS），并从中提取可解释的数学组织特征。

Result: (1) 特征的PCA投影呈现AI参与度的定性梯度：AI改写论文接近人类论文，两个AI生成队列沿大致共同方向偏移，披露实质性AI使用的论文处于中间位置并随时间向AI生成队列靠拢。(2) 识别出AI生成证明的反复出现的特征——外部引用结果更少、更依赖中间结论、图连通性改变，且多数特征在控制研究领域、稿件长度或图规模后依然成立。(3) 独立检测实验中，ProofTrace能区分人类撰写论文与披露实质性AI使用的论文，且在AI改写下性能稳定，而最先进的文本检测器性能显著下降。

Conclusion: 证明的结构与数学组织层面的可解释特征可用于检测数学证明中的AI参与，且比基于文本的检测更稳健；AI参与在证明层面呈现可刻画的梯度与稳定签名。

Abstract: The growing use of AI in mathematics raises a question: can we detect AI involvement in proofs, separately from AI assistance with writing? Historical arXiv papers, recent papers disclosing substantive AI use, and manuscripts released by OpenAI provide a rich empirical basis for studying this question. However, several challenges remain: lack of matched case-control pairs; potential incompleteness of disclosure; and lack of ready-to-use features designed to capture signals of AI involvement in proofs.
  To address these challenges, we construct five cohorts comprising 8,281 papers by combining existing manuscripts with those we produced using generative AI. These cohorts span different levels of AI involvement and include matched pairs of papers addressing the same research problems. We then introduce ProofTrace, a framework that represents each paper as a graph of immediate proof support (GIPS) and extracts interpretable features of mathematical organization.
  Our study yields three discoveries. First, principal-component projections of our features reveal a qualitative gradient of AI involvement, where AI-reworded papers remain close to human papers, the two AI-generated cohorts depart in broadly shared directions, and papers disclosing substantive AI use occupy intermediate positions which shifts toward AI-generated cohorts over time. Second, we identify recurring signatures of AI-generated proofs, such as fewer externally cited results, greater reliance on intermediate conclusions, and altered graph connectivity; many of these signatures persist under controls for research field, manuscript length, or graph size. Finally, in a separate detection experiment, ProofTrace distinguishes human-written papers from those disclosing substantive AI use. Its performance remains stable under AI rewording, whereas the performance of state-of-the-art text-based detectors declines sharply.

</details>


### [71] [Benchmarks as Software: Terminal-Bench and the Case for Continuous Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08417)
*E. Kelly Buchanan,Crystal Zhou,Scott W. Linderman,Christopher Ré*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"基准维护债务"(benchmark maintenance debt)概念，并设计持续校验工作流，对 Terminal-Bench 2.0 审计后约30%任务存在问题，修复后发布 Terminal-Bench 2.1；修复使准确率提升超10个百分点、榜单名次最多变动3位（Spearman rho=0.958），同时证明资源预算（时间限制）本身即测量规范的一部分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体是LLM最具经济价值的落地场景之一，可靠评测至关重要；但即便发布时经过严格校验的基准也会随模型能力提升而"过时"：模型可能给出被旧验证器误判的有效解，评测基础设施变更也会破坏任务与测试中的隐含假设，形成基准分数与真实能力之间的持续扩大的偏差。作者将其命名为"基准维护债务"，并指出资源分配设置同样会影响基准结论，需与任务一并校验。

Method: 提出面向智能体基准的持续校验工作流，整合五类信号：社区反馈报告、任务构件（task artifacts）、智能体执行轨迹（agent traces）、自动化 oracle 校验、以及人工把关的修复（human-gated repairs）。将该流程应用于经过大量人工与LM辅助审查及红队测试的 Terminal-Bench 2.0，完成全量审计并将修复成果发布为 Terminal-Bench 2.1。此外设计资源分配（固定墙钟时间预算）对结论影响的对照实验。

Result: 审计发现约30%的基准任务存在缺陷。修复后评测结果发生实质变化：准确率提升超过10个百分点，榜单名次最多移动3位，但整体排序仍高度相关（Spearman rho=0.958）。资源实验表明固定墙钟时间显著影响智能体表现：延长时间限制可消除 GPT 5.4 上20个百分点的 harness 差距，并将 GPT 5.4 mini 上30个百分点的差距缩小至5个百分点。

Conclusion: 基准维护债务不只是实现层面的 bug 累积；随着模型能力提升，评测假设与资源设置都需被重新校验。基准维护不仅在于修复任务构件，资源预算属于测量规范（measurement specification）的组成部分，必须与任务一同验证，否则将系统性扭曲对智能体真实能力的测量。

Abstract: Coding agents are among the most economically consequential deployments of LLMs to date, making reliable evaluation increasingly important. Yet even benchmarks that are carefully validated at release can become stale. As models improve, they can discover valid solutions that existing verifiers reject, while changes to evaluation infrastructure can invalidate assumptions embedded in tasks and tests. We call the resulting accumulation of defects benchmark maintenance debt: a growing gap between benchmark scores and the capabilities they are intended to measure. We introduce a continuous-validation workflow for agentic benchmarks that combines community reports, task artifacts, agent traces, automated oracle checks, and human-gated repairs. We apply this workflow to Terminal-Bench 2.0, a benchmark that underwent extensive human and LM-assisted review and red-teaming before release. Our audit identifies issues in roughly 30% of the benchmark, and we release the resulting repairs as Terminal-Bench 2.1. These repairs change evaluation results: accuracy increased by over 10 percentage points and leaderboard positions move by up to three ranks, even as the overall ranking remains highly correlated (Spearman rho = 0.958).
  We evaluate how resource allocation shapes benchmark conclusions. We show that fixed wall-clock can materially change agent performance: For example, extending the time limit closes a 20pp harness gap on GPT 5.4 and shrinks a 30 pp gap on GPT 5.4 mini to 5 pp. Benchmark maintenance is therefore not only about repairing task artifacts: resource budgets are part of the measurement specification and must be validated alongside the tasks themselves.
  More broadly, benchmark maintenance debt is not just accumulated implementation bugs: our results show that as models improve, both evaluation assumptions and resource settings may need to be revalidated.

</details>


### [72] [The Power of Meta Access in the Software of the Future --- Demonstrated on LLM Chat UIs and Chat AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08428)
*Julian Kunkel,Ali Doosthosseini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出"元访问（meta access）"概念：让对话式助手获得对界面自身结构化、受权限控制的状态视图，并给出五级能力阶梯，以Meta Chat原型（Chat AI客户端）落地验证，将聊天从单会话窗口扩展为整个工作区的控制层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聊天仍是LLM任务默认入口，但代理型工具（如OpenCode）虽擅长编码与自动化，模型却只能看到当前对话、看不到周围的工作空间；需弥补模型对界面上下文与可操作能力的缺失，同时不牺牲用户控制权。

Method: 提出分级能力阶梯：①功能教学（feature tutoring）；②元数据、设置与偏好访问；③对摘要与消息的检索；④经确认的变更（confirmed mutation）；⑤完整编排（full orchestration）。在Chat AI客户端的Meta Chat原型中实现：从摘要、设置、相关消息构建有界上下文，将引用链接到会话/主题/角色，支持可确认、可回滚的修改，提供可浏览的教程库，并以用户发起的会话形式推荐角色。同时指出代理型harness作为运行时具有反身性，需独立控制平面边界。

Result: 原型展示了结构化暴露应用状态可使助手完成查找、导航、组织与操作；元访问亦可把屏幕阅读器从控件描述扩展到语义级工作区操作。同时揭示落地风险：接地（grounding）、隐私、提示注入与信任。

Conclusion: 元访问把聊天从"一扇看单个会话的窗口"变为"整个工作区的控制层"，在不放弃用户控制的前提下向代理能力延伸；未来嵌入IDE、仪表盘、办公套件与操作系统的AI界面应通过类似调试器的接口开放其既有元模型，暴露结构化、受权限控制的状态与能力。

Abstract: Chat remains the default surface for many large language model (LLM) tasks, even as agentic tools such as OpenCode excel at coding and automation. Yet the model sees the current dialogue but not the workspace around it. We explore meta access---giving the assistant a structured, permissioned view of the interface itself and propose a ladder of levels: feature tutoring; metadata, settings, and preference access; retrieval over summaries and messages, confirmed mutation, and full orchestration. Meta access can turn a chat client from many screens into one entry point for finding, navigating, organising, and operating it, while raising risks around grounding, privacy, prompt injection, and trust. General-purpose agentic harnesses are runtimes; because they can change their own settings, meta access becomes reflexive and needs a separate control-plane boundary. We ground this in the Meta Chat prototype of the Chat AI client. It builds bounded context from, e.g., summaries, settings, and relevant messages; links references to conversations, topics, and personas; supports confirmed, reversible changes; offers a browsable tutorial library; and recommends personas as user-started conversations. By exposing application state in structured form, meta access can extend screen readers from widget descriptions to semantic workspace operations. Meta access thus turns chat from a window onto one conversation into a control layer for the whole workspace, extending chat toward agentic capability without surrendering user control. As assistants become embedded in IDEs, dashboards, office suites, and operating systems, AI-era interfaces should expose a structured, permissioned model of their state and capabilities, ideally by opening their existing metamodels through a debugger-like interface.

</details>


### [73] [ReproBridge: Building Reproducibility into ML Research Artifacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08452)
*Yubo Wang,Rui Meng,Jaehyun Nam,Tomas Pfister,Jinsung Yoon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出标准化"精炼可复现工件"设计与ReproBridge智能体工作流，并构建ReproBridge-Bench（36篇论文/387个实验/1,029任务，每任务5个人工验证参考分），验证保留执行知识可显著提升后续独立复现成功率并降低成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ML实验复现需从论文、代码与附属工件中零散信息重建流程；对编码智能体而言，复现成败取决于其补全缺失细节的能力，而非仅依赖已发布工件。作者因此追问：保留被恢复的流程与决策信息能否改善后续的独立复现。

Method: 定义标准化工件设计（精炼代码库、环境配置、实验启动器、复现指南），提出ReproBridge智能体工作流自动构建精炼可复现工件；构建ReproBridge-Bench基准，智能体在全新会话中使用原始代码或精炼工件执行任务，由独立Auditor核查执行证据；并进行组件消融实验，在四个模型上评估。

Result: 四个模型中，精炼工件将平均复现成功率从76.4%提升至92.6%，下游API开销平均降低19.7%；跨模型成功率差距由21.6个百分点收窄至3.9个百分点，四模型均超过90%。消融显示移除任一组件均降低成功率，移除代码与环境损失最大。

Conclusion: 保留执行知识（流程与决策）可使原始代码表现较弱的模型也能更可靠、更低成本地复现实验，表明工件设计的标准化与信息保真对智能体驱动的实验复现具有系统性价值。

Abstract: Reproducing machine learning experiments requires reconstructing procedures from information scattered across papers, released code, and supporting artifacts. For coding agents, reproduction success can therefore depend on their ability to fill in missing details, rather than on the released artifacts alone. We ask whether preserving recovered procedures and decisions improves subsequent independent reproduction. We define a standardized artifact design comprising a refined codebase, environment setup, experiment launchers, and a reproduction guide, and introduce ReproBridge, an agent workflow for constructing these refined reproducible artifacts. ReproBridge-Bench provides 1,029 tasks from 387 experiments across 36 papers, anchored by five human-verified reference scores per task. Agents attempt tasks in fresh sessions with raw code or refined artifacts; a separate Auditor checks execution evidence. Across four evaluated models, refined artifacts raise mean reproduction success from 76.4% to 92.6% and reduce downstream API spending by 19.7% on average. The cross-model success-rate spread narrows from 21.6 to 3.9 percentage points, with all four models achieving mean success rates above 90%. Removing any component reduces success in the ablation study, with the largest losses from code and environment removal. These results suggest that preserving execution knowledge helps even models with lower raw-code success reproduce experiments more reliably and at lower API cost.

</details>


### [74] [Scientist World: Grow a Research World for AI-Scientists](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08478)
*Taiming Lu,Sachin Konan,Zhuang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出"科学家世界"(Scientist World)，将每篇论文转化为可执行环境，用于评估与训练AI科学家。首发1000个已验证环境；前沿AI科学家在74%情况下超越人类研究者；含6400小时研究轨迹；可将小模型研究构思能力提升158%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类科学家拥有数百年累积的开放问题与共享思想构成的可运行"世界"，而现有AI科学家只能"阅读"文献却无法在其中"运行"与实验，缺少可交互、可执行、可比对的科研环境。

Method: 构建自动化流水线：从每篇论文中提取开放式研究问题，并将其提出的方法复现为基线；AI科学家进入该环境后，与人类作者已取得的成果进行对比评估。以此形成执行环境、评估基准与训练场三位一体的体系。

Result: 发布首批1000个经验证环境；前沿AI科学家在74%的情况下超越人类研究者；积累6400小时研究轨迹（记录想法、实验、失败与成功）；作为训练场使小模型在研究构思上提升+158%，达到与前沿模型相当水平。

Conclusion: AI科学家拥有了从科学自身生长而来、并随科学共同演化的专属"世界"，同时兼具评估基准、过程记录与训练环境三重价值。

Abstract: AI-Scientists arrived, but a world for them to grow in did not. Human scientists take theirs for granted $-$ centuries of open problems and shared ideas, waiting in the scientific literature. To AI-Scientists, that world is readable but not runnable, so we build Scientist World for them: every research paper becomes an executable environment. Our autonomous pipeline extracts each paper's open-ended research question and reproduces its proposed method as the baseline; an AI-Scientist entering the environment is evaluated against what the human authors achieved. We release the first 1,000 verified environments. With the world as an evaluation, we found that frontier AI-Scientists can already surpass the human researchers 74% of the time. As a record of how AI does science, it contains 6,400 hours of research traces, with every idea, experiment, failure, and success. As a training ground, we are able to raise (+158%) a small model to match frontier models in research ideation. At last, AI-Scientists' own world has arrived, grown from and growing with science itself.

</details>


### [75] [SwarmEvolve: Latent Agent Swarms for Evolutionary Discovery and Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08483)
*Qile Jiang,George Em Karniadakis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SwarmEvolve 提出一种潜空间通信的智能体群体进化框架：每轮数百个低成本智能体在隐空间推理、通过传递 KV 缓存共享信息，并以隐状态距离做新颖性筛选，在 21 个数学与科学计算问题上以 3–8 分钟达到其他 SOTA 框架 60 分钟的最佳分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 引导的进化式程序搜索虽能迭代改进并组合高分方案，但每轮都需生成冗长文本思维链，且为维持种群多样性需反复生成，导致过程缓慢、成本高。

Method: 构建 SwarmEvolve 框架：每轮由数百个低成本智能体组成群体并行工作，从不同角度研究问题；智能体在潜空间推理，不解码为文本；通过传递 KV 缓存实现信息共享；新颖性过滤器直接以智能体隐状态向量间距离保留最具差异性的想法；交叉在潜空间中自然发生——汇总智能体接收全体成员合并后的 KV 缓存。

Result: 在 21 个数学发现与科学计算问题上取得最高平均分；达到其他 SOTA 框架需 60 分钟才能取得的最佳分数，中位耗时仅 3–8 分钟；在若干问题上，使用 Qwen3 8B 的 SwarmEvolve 优于 AlphaEvolve 借助前沿 LLM 首次找到的解。

Conclusion: 在潜空间中通信的智能体群体为科学问题的程序搜索提供了快速、低成本的方法，验证了以 KV 缓存共享与隐状态距离度量替代文本思维链与显式交叉的有效性。

Abstract: LLM-guided evolutionary search uses AI agents to improve programs for mathematical and computational problems by iteratively refining solutions and combining high-scoring methods. However, the process is often slow because the LLM generates a long textual chain of thought for each round and must repeat this generation many times to maintain population diversity. We present SwarmEvolve, a framework in which hundreds of low-cost agents per round work in swarms, reason in latent space without decoding to text, and share information by passing KV caches. Each swarm studies the problem from a different angle. A novelty filter keeps the most distinct agent ideas by directly measuring the distances between the agents' hidden state vectors. Crossover of ideas happens naturally in latent space, as a summarizer agent receives the combined KV caches of all swarm members. On 21 problems in mathematical discovery and scientific computing, SwarmEvolve gets the highest average score and needs a median of only 3 to 8 minutes to reach the best scores that other state-of-the-art frameworks reach in 60 minutes. On several problems, SwarmEvolve with Qwen3 8B improves on the solutions that AlphaEvolve first found with frontier LLMs. Our work shows that agent swarms that communicate in latent space offer a fast, low-cost approach to program search for scientific problems.

</details>


### [76] [When Does Test-Time Search Help World Action Models? An Empirical Audit of Candidate Selection and Simulator Integrity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41970)
*Jun Liu,Dajie Sun,Zhenglun Kong,Zhenjiang Fan,Peiyan Dong,Fan Yang,Wenbin Zhang,Geng Yuan,Xue Lin,Yanzhi Wang,Dong Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文以"前提优先、预注册"的方法系统审计世界-动作模型测试时缩放的可信度：在Cosmos Policy与RoboCasa上检验决策信号、可实现效率与评测完整性三个前提，发现选择信号近乎不存在、token剪枝的端到端加速远低于目标、自适应分配实验功效不足，并发现并修复了一处静默渲染损坏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界-动作模型日益采用测试时缩放（候选选择、自适应算力分配）以提升控制性能，但此类收益只有在三个前提同时成立时才可信：候选间存在可利用信号、名义算力节省能转化为真实加速、评测本身可靠。现有工作报告成功率而缺乏对这些前提的逐项审计。

Method: 采用前提优先（premise-first）、预注册（pre-registered）的审计协议，在Cosmos Policy与RoboCasa上进行三类实验：(1) 决策信号审计——比较学习型价值选择器与随机选择、事后oracle（hindsight oracle）的上界增益、状态级候选结果一致性；(2) 效率审计——测量token剪枝的每前向FLOPs削减与端到端/排除解码路径的实测加速比；(3) 自适应分配实验，并设置统计功效门槛（power gate）；同时排查并修复沿高算力规划路径出现的静默渲染损坏。

Result: 干净数据上，学习型价值选择器比随机选择差1.66 pp；事后oracle仅增益1.40 pp，且episode级区间跨越零；82%的状态候选结果完全相同，选择余地极小。token剪枝将单次前向FLOPs最多削减47%，但部署路径端到端加速至多实测1.15×（排除解码路径为1.25×），低于1.5×目标；自适应分配实验在功效门槛下无定论。另发现渲染静默损坏，其发生率随计算密集规划路径显著上升，但不影响官方直接策略结果，并发布检测器、修复与脚本。

Conclusion: 测试时缩放应以"决策信号—实现效率—评测完整性"的前提链条进行审计，而非仅看成功率；表面增益可能源于无信号的选择、名义而非实际的算力节省，或被静默评测缺陷掩盖。结论仅限所测配置。

Abstract: World-action models increasingly apply test-time scaling, spending extra inference computation on candidate selection and adaptive allocation to improve control. Such gains are credible only when candidates carry exploitable signal, nominal compute savings become real speedup, and evaluation stays reliable. We audit all three on Cosmos Policy and RoboCasa with a premise-first, pre-registered methodology. On clean data, a learned value selector is 1.66 pp worse than random; a hindsight oracle gains only 1.40 pp, with an episode-level interval spanning zero; and 82% of states show identical candidate outcomes, leaving little selection headroom. Token pruning cuts per-forward FLOPs by up to 47% but yields at most a measured 1.15$\times$ end-to-end speedup on the deployed path (1.25$\times$ on a decode-excluded path), short of a 1.5$\times$ target, and our adaptive-allocation experiment is inconclusive under a power gate. We further identify and fix a silent rendering corruption whose rate rises sharply along compute-heavy planning paths while leaving the official direct-policy result unaffected, and release a detector, fix, and scripts. These findings show why test-time scaling should be audited as a chain of premises (decision signal, realized efficiency, evaluationintegrity) rather than by success rate alone. Conclusions are scoped to the tested configuration.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [77] [Reducing Representational Interference in Retrieval Embeddings with Trainable Prefix Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07298)
*Seongtae Hong,Youngjoon Jang,Jungseob Lee,Chanjun Park,Heuiseok Lim*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出在嵌入模型训练时于输入前添加可训练的[DUMP] token，通过标准对比检索目标优化，在不改变架构、池化策略与损失的前提下抑制残余token级信息（如attention sink伪影），提升检索表示质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 嵌入模型将变长文本压缩为定长向量，压缩过程会使语义内容与残余token级信息纠缠，例如attention sink行为产生的伪影，这些无信息信号会干扰最终检索表示。

Method: 在训练阶段于输入前拼接可训练的[DUMP] token，保持模型架构、池化策略和训练损失不变，仅通过标准对比检索目标对这些新增前缀位置进行优化；前缀位置在编码时与常规token交互，从而吸收/削弱残余或无信息token级信号对最终表示的影响。

Result: 在标准检索与长上下文基准上，该方法在encoder与decoder类嵌入模型上均稳定优于微调基线；进一步分析显示[DUMP] token改变了与低信息token相关的token级激活和注意力模式。

Conclusion: 可训练前缀token是一种轻量、通用的手段，可在不改动现有训练流程的情况下改善检索表示质量。

Abstract: Embedding models compress variable-length text into fixed-size vector representations, but this compression can entangle semantic content with residual token-level information, such as artifacts arising from attention sink behavior. We propose a simple method that prepends trainable [DUMP] tokens to the input during embedding model training. The method keeps the model architecture, pooling strategy, and training loss unchanged, while optimizing the added tokens through the standard contrastive retrieval objective. By introducing trainable prefix positions that interact with regular tokens during encoding, [DUMP] tokens help reduce the influence of residual or uninformative token-level signals on the final retrieval representation. Experiments on standard retrieval and long-context benchmarks show that our method consistently improves over finetuned baselines across encoder and decoder embedding models. Further analysis shows that [DUMP] tokens alter token-level activation and attention patterns associated with low-information tokens. These results suggest that trainable prefix tokens provide a lightweight way to improve retrieval representations.

</details>


### [78] [CacheGraft: Enhancing Retrieval-Augmented Generation via Latent-Space Document Enrichment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07393)
*Vihan Lakshman,Peter Baile Chen,Zhuohan Gu,Michael Cafarella*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出CacheGraft系统，将语料离线编码为"大模型(4B)—小模型(0.6B)"融合的KV cache，查询时直接注入小receiver实现隐空间RAG增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作表明transformer智能体可通过KV cache在隐空间通信；本文认为cache-to-cache翻译不止服务于实时通信，还能用于检索增强生成，且在只能部署小模型的算力受限场景中格外有价值。

Method: 基于Cache-to-Cache(C2C)融合框架，离线将每段语料编码为sharer(4B)与receiver(0.6B)之间的融合KV cache表示，查询时无需大模型即可将该cache注入receiver，从而替代或补充纯文本上下文。

Result: 在5个QA基准与2个本地独立评审模型上，30组比较中29组优于纯文本receiver基线，其中17组具统计显著性；且fuser从未在检索数据上训练，增益为zero-shot。

Conclusion: 隐空间文档增强是提升RAG性能的有前景方向，尤其在推理端只能部署小模型的计算受限环境中。

Abstract: Recent work in machine learning has demonstrated that transformer-based language model agents can communicate state not only through text, but also directly in latent space via key-value (KV) caches. In this paper, we argue that such cache-to-cache translation can provide utility beyond the original motivating application of real-time agent communication and can instead be utilized for retrieval-augmented generation (RAG). Building on the Cache-to-Cache (C2C) fusion framework, our proposed system CacheGraft encodes each corpus passage offline into a fused KV cache representation between a larger sharer model (4B parameters in our experiments) and a smaller receiver model (0.6B parameters) such that this cache can be injected into the receiver at query time with no need for the larger model. Across five QA benchmarks and two independent locally-run judges, CacheGraft improves answer quality over the text-only receiver baseline in 29 out of 30 comparisons, of which 17 of the 30 are statistically significant. Notably, these gains are zero-shot with respect to the fuser, which was never trained on retrieval data. These initial results suggest that latent-space document enrichment may be a promising avenue for enhancing the performance of RAG systems, particularly in compute-constrained settings where it is only feasible to deploy a small model at inference time.

</details>


### [79] [BRIDGE: Bridging the Semantic Quantization Gap for Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07425)
*Yatong Xiao,Mingjie Sun,Peng Wang,Wenjin Wu,Peng Jiang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文指出生成式推荐中RQ-VAE离散语义ID（SID）导致“语义量化鸿沟”（SQG），并提出BRIDGE从锚定、几何保持与算力分配三方面弥合该鸿沟；离线三基准平均提升Recall@10 10.9%、NDCG@10 9.7%，线上A/B eCPM+0.65%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式推荐通过自回归生成分层SID来检索物品，但量化过程蕴含的丰富连续信号（码本中心、局部邻域几何、逐层熵）在解码端仅以离散标识符形式出现而丢失，形成被忽视的“语义量化鸿沟”（SQG）。其表现为三种不匹配：目标侧信息有限时解码器缺乏语义锚点（grounding gap）；token级监督丢弃物品间相似性并加剧曝光偏差（neighborhood gap）；算力均匀分配而生成难度随深度递减（difficulty gap）。

Method: 提出BRIDGE框架，含三个组件：(1) 码本锚定的隐空间推理——在SID生成前注入冻结量化器质心作为语义锚点；(2) 特权EMA自蒸馏——由教师向on-policy学生迁移邻域感知知识，保留物品间几何结构并缓解曝光偏差；(3) 难度感知循环——为更困难的早期层级分配更多计算量，随深度递进逐步减少。

Result: 在三个Amazon基准上，相较最强基线平均提升Recall@10 10.9%、NDCG@10 9.7%（最高12.6%与10.7%）；在大型电商广告平台5%流量的为期一周在线A/B实验中，eCPM提升0.65%。

Conclusion: SQG是生成式推荐中被忽视的关键瓶颈，显式弥合离散标识符与连续语义结构之间的失配可带来显著离线增益与可测的线上收益，验证了该问题建模及BRIDGE设计的有效性。

Abstract: Generative recommendation retrieves items by autoregressively generating Semantic IDs(SIDs), where an RQ-VAE quantizes each item into a hierarchical SID sequence that the decoder generates level by level. We identify an overlooked bottleneck in this paradigm: the Semantic Quantization Gap(SQG). Quantization derives SIDs from rich continuous signals, including codebook centroids, local neighborhood geometry, and level-wise entropy. However, the decoder receives only discrete identifiers and loses the semantic structure that shaped them. This mismatch manifests in three ways: a grounding gap, where the decoder lacks semantic anchors when target-side information is limited; a neighborhood gap, where token-level supervision discards inter-item similarity and exacerbates exposure bias; and a difficulty gap, where computation is allocated uniformly despite generation difficulty diminishing with depth.
  To bridge the SQG, we propose BRIDGE. Codebook-grounded latent reasoning injects frozen quantizer centroids as semantic anchors before SID generation. Privileged EMA self-distillation transfers neighborhood-aware knowledge from a teacher to an on-policy student, preserving inter-item geometry while mitigating exposure bias. Effort-aware recurrence allocates more computation to the challenging early levels and progressively reduces it with depth. Across three Amazon benchmarks, BRIDGE improves Recall@10 and NDCG@10 by 10.9% and 9.7% on average over the strongest baseline (up to 12.6% and 10.7%). In a one-week online A/B experiment on a large-scale e-commerce advertising platform with 5% traffic, BRIDGE improves eCPM by 0.65%.

</details>


### [80] [OptST: Optimizing Semantic Tokenization for LLM-Enhanced Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07523)
*Hanchong Chen,Jie Liu,Fuyuan Lyu,Dugang Liu,Lingjie Li,Xiuqiang He,Xing Tang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出 OptST，使预训练语义分词器可被下游推荐任务适配：用低秩形变修正 token 分配并以锚点正则约束漂移，通过直通估计器接收推荐监督，同时以交互条件前缀控制语义分辨率；四个公开数据集上 AUC 最优且跨骨干稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两阶段流程中分词器先训练、推荐器后训练，token 分配被当作固定特征，推荐器只能学会使用索引而无法修正产生索引的语义划分；而最近码分配是离散的、无约束适配会破坏预训练学到的几何结构，且固定 token 深度使每次交互都暴露相同语义分辨率。

Method: OptST 不直接更新预训练编码器与基础码本，而是学习一个低秩形变，并用锚点正则限制其漂移；推荐监督经直通估计器传递到该变换，预测时仍保留硬最近码分配；此外选择交互条件前缀，控制暴露给推荐器的残差细节量。

Result: 在四个公开数据集上，OptST 在传统与 LLM 增强基线中取得最高 AUC，且改进在不同推荐骨干上持续存在。

Conclusion: 任务感知的语义分词可作为 LLM 派生用户画像与下游推荐模型之间的有效接口。

Abstract: Semantic information can enrich the identifiers and structured fields used by recommender systems. Semantic tokenization provides a structured representation of this information by converting LLM-derived user profiles into discrete tokens. In the common two-stage pipeline, the tokenizer is trained before the recommender. Its token assignments are then treated as fixed input features during recommendation training. As a result, the downstream recommender can learn how to use the assigned indices, but it cannot revise the semantic partition that produced them. Making this partition task-aware is difficult because nearest-code assignment is discrete, and unconstrained adaptation may also distort the geometry learned during pretraining. Moreover, a fixed token depth exposes the same semantic resolution to every interaction. We thus propose OptST (Optimizing Semantic Tokenization), which makes a pretrained tokenizer responsive to downstream recommendation. To revise token assignments without directly updating the pretrained encoder or base codebooks, OptST learns a low-rank deformation whose drift is limited by an anchor regularizer. Recommendation supervision reaches this transformation through a straight-through estimator, while prediction retains hard nearest-code assignments. Beyond adapting the assignments themselves, OptST selects an interaction-conditioned prefix to control how much residual detail is exposed to the recommender. Experiments on four public datasets show that OptST achieves the highest AUC among conventional and LLM-enhanced baselines. Its improvements also persist across different recommendation backbones. These results establish task-aware semantic tokenization as an effective interface between LLM-derived profiles and downstream recommendation models.

</details>


### [81] [What Gets Hacked in a Collaborative Reward for LLM Recommenders: The Part That Does Not Depend on the User](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07588)
*Zhuangsheng Li,Liang Dong,Yu Wu,Dailin Li,Yu Xia,Yunhan C. Zhang,Rui Cai,Yi Wu,Julian McAuley*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 将CF奖励分解为物品均值分量与用户残差分量，发现物品均值分量导致LLM推荐器的曝光坍缩；据此提出按物品中心化与按物品标准化两种奖励校正，提升目录覆盖与曝光均衡，同时保持排序精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用CF分数作RL奖励训练LLM推荐器时会出现曝光坍缩：CF模型本身对不同用户给出多样化头部推荐，但经其奖励优化的LLM却集中于更少物品。作者旨在定位该现象的成因并加以缓解。

Method: 把每个CF分数分解为物品均值分量（该物品在用户历史中的平均分）与残差分量（当前用户相对均值的偏差）；通过受控干预确认物品均值分量是曝光坍缩的贡献因素。据此提出两种奖励校正：逐物品中心化（减去物品估计均值）与逐物品标准化（再以估计标准差缩放残差）。

Result: 在两个数据集、两种标识符方案上，两种校正均显著提升目录覆盖与曝光均匀度；相比未校正的CF奖励，跨用户排名第一的不同物品数最多提升9.8倍，排序准确率相当。

Conclusion: 曝光坍缩主要源于CF奖励中的物品均值分量而非个性化信号；简单的逐物品中心化/标准化即可在几乎不损失准确率的前提下恢复推荐的多样性与曝光公平性。

Abstract: Collaborative-filtering (CF) scores provide graded, personalized rewards for training large language model (LLM) recommenders through reinforcement learning. However, these rewards can cause exposure collapse: even though the CF model itself produces diverse top recommendations across users, an LLM optimized with its rewards concentrates on fewer items. We decompose each score into an item-mean component, the item's average CF score across user histories, and a residual component, the current user's score deviation from the average. Through controlled interventions, we identify the item-mean component as a contributor to exposure collapse. Accordingly, we propose two reward corrections: per-item centering removes each item's estimated mean score, while per-item standardization further rescales the residual using its estimated standard deviation. Experiments on two datasets with two identifier schemes show that both corrections substantially improve catalog coverage and exposure evenness. Compared with training on the uncorrected CF reward, our corrections increase the number of distinct items ranked first across users by up to $9.8\times$, while maintaining comparable ranking accuracy. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/LLM4Rec-CF-Bias-27EF/.

</details>


### [82] [Beyond Independent Predictions: Learning the Browsing Journey for Sequence-Level Re-ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07591)
*Jiayi Zhang,Jingxin Hai,Zhenjia Ma,Hongbin Pei,Dagui Chen,Han Zhu,Jian Xu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出联合行为建模（JBM）视角与即插即用的曝光-点击评估器（ICE），显式建模"展示→曝光→点击"因果与竞争依赖，修正独立行为建模（IBM）的评估偏差，在淘宝APP线上A/B实验中实现点击+0.70%、收入+3.67%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重排的目标是最大化序列级效用，工业界常用生成器-评估器框架，评估器效用估计的保真度直接决定推荐质量。但现有评估器多采用独立行为建模（IBM）范式，将连续的用户浏览旅程简化为逐项独立预测（如CTR），忽略了两个物理事实：(i) 点击只能在条目被触达并曝光后发生；(ii) 条目之间存在对有限用户注意力的竞争。这导致系统性有偏评估与次优序列选择。

Method: 提出联合行为建模（JBM）视角，显式建模"展示→曝光→点击"过程以刻画序列内因果与竞争依赖。基于此构建即插即用的曝光-点击评估器（ICE），包含三部分：(i) 预测浏览深度分布以估计各位置的曝光概率；(ii) 利用预测的曝光抑制未曝光条目带来的虚假上下文，用于点击建模；(iii) 引入点击分布匹配目标以捕捉序列内依赖关系。

Result: 在多种骨干网络上进行大量离线实验，并在淘宝APP开展为期14天的线上A/B测试（点击+0.70%、收入+3.67%）及全流量部署，验证了方法的有效性与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 将浏览旅程视为曝光与点击联合依赖的序列，而非独立逐项预测，能显著提升评估器保真度与重排效果；ICE作为即插即用模块具备跨骨干的通用性与线上落地价值。

Abstract: Re-ranking aims to present users with an ordered list that maximizes sequence-level utility. Industrial deployments widely adopt the Generator--Evaluator framework, where the generator proposes candidate sequences, and the evaluator scores them to select the best one for display. The fidelity of the evaluator's utility estimates thus directly governs recommendation quality. However, existing evaluators predominantly follow the \textbf{Independent Behavior Modeling (IBM)} paradigm, which simplifies the continuous user \emph{browsing journey} into independent per-item predictions (e.g., CTR). This oversimplification ignores two physical realities: (i) a click can occur only after the item has been reached and impressed, and (ii) items compete for finite user attention. As a result, IBM produces systematically biased evaluations and suboptimal sequence selection. To address this, we propose a \textbf{Joint Behavior Modeling (JBM)} perspective that explicitly models the \emph{display} $\rightarrow$ \emph{impression} $\rightarrow$ \emph{click} process to capture causal and competitive dependencies within a sequence. Building on JBM, we develop the \textbf{Impression-Click Evaluator (ICE)}, a plug-and-play framework that (i) predicts a browsing-depth distribution to estimate per-position impression probabilities, (ii) leverages predicted impressions to suppress spurious context from unexposed items during click modeling, and (iii) introduces a click distribution matching objective to capture intra-sequence dependencies. Extensive offline experiments across diverse backbones, together with a 14-day online A/B test in the Taobao App (+0.70\% clicks, +3.67\% revenue) and full-traffic deployment, validate the effectiveness and robustness of our approach.

</details>


### [83] [MEMOIR: A Training-Free Memory Lifecycle for Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07631)
*Mayank Anand,Subham Raj,Aman Vaibhav Jha,Sriparna Saha*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出免训练的MEMOIR记忆生命周期，将judge门控的合并压缩与事后归因的重要性修订统一，解决“压缩破坏证据”与“检索偏爱陈旧记忆”两种耦合失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程LLM智能体需要跨会话保留并召回有用信息，但现有系统将写入与检索割裂：记忆以静态重要性分数前瞻性写入、使用后从不修订。这导致两种耦合失效——合并压缩可能静默销毁未来查询所需证据，而检索持续偏好陈旧记忆。

Method: 提出MEMOIR：免训练的记忆生命周期，将judge门控合并与回顾性归因统一。(1) 压缩前用筛选门检验答案相关证据是否被保留（judge-gated merge）；(2) 每次查询后通过归因估计哪些被检索记忆支撑了答案，并据此更新其检索优先级（在线重要性修订）；(3) 加入UCB式探索奖励，鼓励检索曝光不足的记忆，防止高频记忆挤出低频但有用证据。在线写入路径不增加额外LLM调用，合并筛选仅限周期性离线整合。

Result: 在1,905条去重LoCoMo问答对上，完整检索栈较纯余弦将证据recall@5提升11.92个百分点；置信度门控反馈在无参考答案、无reranker训练条件下总体再增1.50点；不可部署的oracle总体增1.84点、多跳任务增8.05点。judge门控合并使文本证据召回率由58.82%升至59.08%，活跃存储减少1.42%，100次独立审计合并中0次观测损失，单侧95%损失率上界2.95%。

Conclusion: 结果给出可行的部署路径：在线写入无需额外LLM调用，合并筛选限于周期性离线整合。该生命周期归结为一条原则——使用后归因有用性，压缩前筛选保真性。

Abstract: Long-horizon LLM agents require memory systems that preserve useful information across sessions and retrieve it when needed. Existing systems treat these as separate problems: memories are written prospectively with static importance scores that are never revised after use. This creates two coupled failure modes: consolidation can silently destroy evidence that future queries require, while retrieval continues to favor stale memories. We propose MEMOIR (Memory Evidence via Merge-certified, Online Importance Revision), a training-free memory lifecycle that unifies judge-gated consolidation with retrospective attribution. Before compressing memory, screening gates test whether answer-relevant evidence is preserved. After each query, attribution estimates which retrieved memories supported the answer and updates their retrieval priority. A UCB-style exploration bonus additionally encourages retrieval of under-exposed memories, reducing the risk that frequently retrieved memories crowd out useful but less-seen evidence. On 1,905 deduplicated LoCoMo QA pairs, the full retrieval stack improves evidence recall@5 by 11.92 percentage points over bare cosine, with confidence-gated feedback adding 1.50 points overall without reference answers or reranker training; a deployment-unavailable oracle adds 1.84 points overall and 8.05 points on multi-hop. Judge-gated merge changes the recall of text-based evidence from 58.82% to 59.08%, reduces the active store by 1.42%, and incurs 0 observed losses in 100 independently audited merges, with a one-sided 95% upper loss-rate bound of 2.95%. Together, these results outline a practical deployment path: no additional LLM call on the online write path, with merge screening confined to periodic offline consolidation. The lifecycle reduces to one principle: attribute usefulness after use, and screen for preservation before compression.

</details>


### [84] [Probabilistic Relevance Decisions for Semantic Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08014)
*Bahadır Tatar,Cevat Şener*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 在BEIR SciFact（300条官方测试查询）上比较纯稠密检索BGE-M3与两种概率相关性决策打分（OpenAI、Jev），Jev在Recall@5与nDCG@10上最优，但深度50→100的增益不确定（所有配对bootstrap区间含0）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稠密相似度提供可复用的语料表示，而概率式相关性决策提供依赖查询的文档打分，二者可能互补；作者希望在统一协议下检验概率相关性决策能否作为稠密检索的补充，而非宣称其普遍优越性。

Method: 在BEIR SciFact官方测试集上用300条查询，采用相同的“正例增强”100文档池与全语料稠密检索，随后在候选深度50和100处重排；对照纯稠密BGE-M3、TypeSafe Jev Noul与OpenAI Decisions Predicate分数。受控池外的稠密前缀不做正例注入；用配对bootstrap评估深度效应，并以精确并列的二次随机化检验排序敏感性，另报告API成本与延迟（缓存与新建执行混合、历史混合延迟为保存组件求和）。

Result: Jev在评测条件下取得最高Recall@5与nDCG@10；深度50时全语料nDCG@10为BGE-M3 0.6413、OpenAI 0.7545、Jev 0.7805。深度50→100提升了已知正例的候选召回，但最终有效性变化小而不确定，四个配对bootstrap深度区间均含0。唯一条件API打分成本约翻倍，兼容的缓存决策可降低增量开销；OpenAI返回大量精确零分。

Conclusion: 这些探索性、未校正的分析支持在本协议下将相关性决策作为稠密检索的补充，但不足以确立普遍优越性、概率校准性或两个深度之间的等价性。

Abstract: Dense similarity supplies reusable corpus representations, while probabilistic relevance decisions offer query-dependent document scoring. We compare dense-only BGE-M3 with TypeSafe Jev Noul and OpenAI Decisions Predicate scores on the official BEIR SciFact test benchmark. The study evaluates 300 official test queries using identical positive-enriched 100-document pools and full-corpus dense retrieval followed by reranking at candidate depths of 50 and 100. Unlike the controlled pools, neither dense prefix receives positive injection. Jev has the highest observed Recall@5 and nDCG@10 under the evaluated conditions. At depth 50, full-corpus nDCG@10 is 0.6413 for BGE-M3, 0.7545 with OpenAI and 0.7805 with Jev. Extending depth from 50 to 100 increases known-positive candidate recall but produces small, uncertain final-effectiveness changes. All four paired-bootstrap depth intervals include zero. Unique-condition API scoring cost approximately doubles, while compatible cached decisions reduce incremental expenditure. OpenAI returns many exact-zero scores, and secondary exact-tie randomization reveals ranking sensitivity without replacing deterministic primary results. Top-100 latency mixes cached and new execution. Historical hybrid latency is a saved-component sum under different execution environments and timer boundaries. These exploratory, unadjusted analyses support relevance decisions as a complement to dense retrieval within this protocol, without establishing general superiority, probability calibration or equivalence between depths.

</details>


### [85] [Domain-specific information retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08228)
*Suzan Verberne,Florina Piroi,Allan Hanbury*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文是领域特定信息检索（Domain-specific IR）的综述性著作，系统梳理了领域特定任务、数据集、应用、挑战与模型方法，并指出其方法可迁移至其他领域乃至开放域网页搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信息检索远不止即席式网页搜索，大量信息寻求活动发生在与工作相关的特定领域（如律师查找判例、研究者调研文献），而领域特定IR面临长文档、长查询、长会话及特定用户需求等独特挑战，需要专门梳理与系统化。

Method: 采用章节化的综述结构：第2章概述常见领域特定任务、数据集与应用；第3章讨论领域特定IR区别于简单网页搜索的挑战及模型偏差与公平性问题；第4章介绍针对这些任务与数据所开发的模型与方法，涵盖查询处理、用户模型与用户交互等用户层面因素。

Result: 归纳出领域特定IR的核心挑战（长文档、长查询、长会话、特定用户需求）并梳理了应对这些挑战的模型与方法；分析表明领域特定IR在方法论上的诸多进展具有可迁移性，可应用于其他领域甚至开放域网页搜索；同时讨论了偏差与公平性问题。

Conclusion: 领域特定IR是与开放域搜索并重的重要研究方向，其方法具备跨领域迁移价值；文中总结了主要结论并为近期研究提出了未来方向。

Abstract: Information Retrieval (IR) is much more than ad hoc web search: a large portion of the information seeking activities takes place in specific domains, often addressing work-related tasks. Examples are lawyers finding relevant prior cases for a legal case and academic researchers studying the background material for a paper. In Chapter 2 we outline the most common domain-specific tasks, datasets and applications. Domain-specific IR has a number of challenges that make set it apart from simple web search tasks: they often deal with long documents, long queries and long sessions with specific user needs. We discuss these in Chapter 3. We also address issues related to bias and fairness of models. In Chapter 4, we describe the models and methods developed for domain-specific tasks and data, addressing the specific challenges, including user aspects related to domain-specific IR tasks, dealing with queries, user models, and user interactions. We show that many of the methodological advances in domain-specific IR are transferable to other domains and even to open-domain web search. We conclude with the main takeaways and propose research directions for the near future.

</details>


### [86] [GINGER: Spicing Up Graph-Based Vector Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08306)
*Daniel Salwasser,Lars Gottesbüren,Laxman Dhulipala,Jakub Łącki,Guy Blelloch,Rajesh Jayaram,Tobias Rubel,Kshiteej Sheth,Amir Zandieh,Vahab Mirrokni*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Ginger：开源C++图近似最近邻搜索库，结合PiPNN图构建、Nested TurboQuant二级量化与AdaptiveGap无损图压缩，在查询吞吐与索引时间上领先，内存占用与最省空间方案持平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模、高维嵌入数据集上，图搜索的速度与内存效率仍需提升；现有ANNS库在吞吐、建索引时间和内存之间难以兼得。

Method: 基于近距离图构建 PiPNN 与向量量化 TurboQuant；提出 Nested TurboQuant（NTQ）两级量化器，在同一码中支持低精度图导航与高精度重排；结合图重排序与基于 StreamVByte 的无损压缩编解码器 AdaptiveGap 压缩邻近图。

Result: NTQ 以 2.6× 更少内存达到近浮点精度；图规模压缩至多 2.5× 且不影响查询吞吐。95% 召回下，查询吞吐较各数据集最快竞争者平均提升 1.8×，较任一单库至少提升 2.3×；在高维嵌入上对紧凑型库的领先扩大至 2.9×。

Conclusion: Ginger 在查询吞吐与索引时间上优于现有 ANNS 库，同时内存占用与最省空间竞争者相当，是当前最先进的开源图 ANNS 方案。

Abstract: We present \textsc{Ginger}, a state-of-the-art open-source C++ library for graph-based approximate nearest neighbor search (ANNS). We developed \textsc{Ginger} at Google to push the speed and memory efficiency of graph search on large and high-dimensional embedding datasets. \textsc{Ginger} surpasses existing ANNS libraries with regards to query throughput and indexing time while matching the memory footprint of the most space-efficient competitors.
  \textsc{Ginger} builds upon recent advances in proximity graph construction (PiPNN) and vector quantization (TurboQuant). We introduce Nested TurboQuant (NTQ), a two-level quantizer which supports low precision graph navigation and high precision reranking in a single code. This representation achieves near floating point precision with $2.6\times$ less memory. We also compress the size of our proximity graphs by up to $2.5\times$ without impacting query throughput. We combine graph reordering with a new lossless compression codec called AdaptiveGap, which builds upon StreamVByte. At $95\%$ recall, \textsc{Ginger} speeds up query throughput by an average of $1.8\times$ over each dataset's fastest competitor and by at least $2.3\times$ over any single library. This lead grows to $2.9\times$ over compact libraries on high-dimensional embeddings.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [87] [Data-driven discovery of intrinsic Lie point symmetries of differential equation systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08421)
*Ilker Gurcan,Kadir Ustun,Ramazan Gokberk Cinbis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出将内蕴Lie点对称发现表述为采样jet空间数据上的算子论问题，无需符号确定方程或系统的闭式表示即可从微分方程数据中恢复显式连续对称生成元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统对称性发现依赖符号确定方程求解与系统的闭式表示，难以用于仅有数据、仅有近似模型或高维场景；而直接从数据中发现连续对称性可为模型降维与科学机器学习提供几何结构。

Method: 在采样jet空间上构造确定算子，结合延拓、序贯子空间限制与对可训练系数线性的固定非线性字典，从采样确定算子中识别可容许生成元，并将冗余、解依赖及几何平凡方向分离剔除；另设秩稳定化预检诊断，用于判别表示的内蕴对称代数在字典细化下是否稳定。

Result: 在具有解析已知11维表示内蕴对称代数的受控微分方程基准上，方法完整恢复表示生成元集合，以数值精度复现其Lie括号结构，并在训练域外对已知生成元给出良好近似。

Conclusion: 显式内蕴连续对称生成元可从精确或近似微分系统的数据中恢复，为无闭式模型情形下的对称性驱动约化与科学机器学习提供可行途径。

Abstract: Discovering continuous symmetries directly from differential-equation data can reveal geometric structure for model reduction and scientific machine learning without symbolic determining equations or a closed-form representation of the governing system. We formulate intrinsic Lie point-symmetry discovery as an operator-theoretic problem on sampled jet-space data. Admissible generators are identified from a sampled determining operator and separated from redundant, solution-dependent, and geometrically trivial directions. The framework combines prolongation, sequential subspace restrictions, and a fixed nonlinear dictionary linear in trainable coefficients. A rank-stabilization preflight test diagnoses systems whose represented intrinsic symmetry algebra does not stabilize under dictionary refinement. On a controlled differential-equation benchmark with an analytically known 11-dimensional represented intrinsic symmetry algebra, the method recovers the complete represented generator set, reproduces its Lie-bracket structure to numerical precision, and approximates a known generator beyond the training domain. Here we show that explicit intrinsic continuous-symmetry generators can be recovered from data obtained from exact or approximate differential systems.

</details>


### [88] [SteelAgent: LLM-Orchestrated, Physics-Informed Steel Property Prediction with Audited Generalization and Calibrated Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06953)
*Aleksandr Volkov,Roman Sultimov,Mikhail Kuzin,Yury Maximov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 钢铁性能模型在随机划分上表现良好，但在陌生合金上外推很差；本文考察不同留出单元如何改变SteelBench抗拉强度评估结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机测试划分会掩盖模型对未见合金的外推能力缺陷，需明确评估总体（留出单元）对结论的影响。

Method: SteelAgent将冶金描述符与机器学习模型结合语言模型接口，用于性能预测、不确定性报告与逆向设计；对比随机划分、钢种家族留出、来源留出等多层次评估，并做分组误差分析与合成回归实验。

Result: 梯度提升的池化折外R²从随机划分的0.859降至家族留出的0.113；名义90%预测区间覆盖率由随机划分91.0%降至留出来源37.6%；物理特征效应随评估而变，17家族留出中R²增加0.2534，四牌号实验室分析中下降0.358。

Conclusion: 性能估计与合规评分须同时报告评估总体；分组留出下校准会显著恶化，随机划分易高估泛化能力。

Abstract: Steel property models can perform well on random test splits even when they extrapolate poorly to unfamiliar alloys. SteelAgent couples metallurgical descriptors and machine-learning models with a language-model interface for property prediction, uncertainty reporting and inverse design. We examine how the choice of held-out unit changes the conclusions drawn from SteelBench tensile-strength evaluations. For gradient boosting, pooled out-of-fold $R^2$ fell from 0.859 on a random split to 0.113 when steel families were held out. Empirical coverage of nominal 90% prediction intervals ranged from 91.0% under random splitting to 37.6% on held-out sources. The effect of physics-derived features depended on the evaluation. They increased gradient-boosting $R^2$ by 0.2534 in a 17-family holdout analysis and decreased it by 0.358 in a separate four-grade laboratory analysis. We report group-level errors and a synthetic regression experiment illustrating how calibration can deteriorate on held-out groups. These results motivate reporting the evaluation population alongside a property estimate or compliance score.

</details>


### [89] [Octopus: One Brain, Many Branches for Latent-KV Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06956)
*Tianming Sha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Octopus：单模型多智能体强化学习框架，通过 GPU 驻留的 KV 状态 DAG 与五类原语协调并行分支，实现零拷贝分叉、仅注意力的 KV 挂载与打包 DAG-GRPO 训练，在 BrowseComp-Plus 上达 49.6% 准确率并显著提升吞吐、降低 KV 显存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体 LLM 框架存在三重缺陷：(1) 各分支重复保存前缀 KV 缓存；(2) 智能体间以文本摘要传递发现，需重新 prefill，开销大且丢失信息；(3) 强化学习阶段将跨分支依赖扁平化或直接丢弃。单智能体系统则把所有检索与验证步骤堆叠为单条不断增长的轨迹。目标是在单模型内高效支持并行探索并保留跨分支依赖。

Method: 提出 Octopus，所有线程共享同一骨干模型，并在 GPU 驻留的 KV 状态有向无环图上通过 fork、read_thread、wait_threads、rewind、commit 五个原语协调。三个协同设计机制：(1) Zero-Copy Radix Forking —— 分支以 O(1) 游标视图指向共享物理 KV 页；(2) Attention-Only KV Mounting —— 将兄弟线程末端 KV 块直接挂载到全注意力层，绕过线性循环状态（Gated DeltaNet / Mamba-2），零额外 KV 分配；(3) Packed DAG-GRPO Trainer —— 将所有 episode 分支打包为一次块稀疏注意力前向，并用留一分支边缘奖励分配反事实分支信用。

Result: 在 Qwen3.5-4B 上基于稀疏 BM25 语料端到端 GRPO 训练：BrowseComp-Plus 准确率 49.6%（比 143K 上下文下单智能体 GRPO 高 17.5 点，比文本摘要式多智能体 RL 高 12.5 点）；OpenResearcher 上 51.2%；超越最多大 250 倍的稠密检索智能体；训练吞吐提升 3.3 倍，峰值 KV 显存降低 2.8 倍。Attention-Only KV Mounting 将 off-task hijacking 降低 21 倍（57.9% → 2.7%），同时保留 92.2% 全文互信息。

Conclusion: 单模型多智能体 RL 可通过共享 KV 图与稀疏打包注意力，在零额外 KV 分配下实现高效并行探索与保真的跨分支信息共享，兼顾准确率、训练效率与显存经济性。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems explore in parallel by spawning sub-agents with isolated context windows, whereas single-agent systems append every search and verification step into one growing trajectory. However, existing multi-agent frameworks duplicate prefix KV caches across branches, exchange findings by re-prefilling text summaries, and flatten or drop cross-branch dependencies during reinforcement learning (RL).
  We introduce Octopus, a single-model multi-agent RL framework in which all threads share one backbone and coordinate over a GPU-resident directed acyclic graph (DAG) of KV states through five primitives (fork, read_thread, wait_threads, rewind, commit). Octopus combines three co-designed mechanisms: (1) Zero-Copy Radix Forking, which spawns branches as $\mathcal{O}(1)$ cursor views over shared physical KV pages; (2) Attention-Only KV Mounting, which mounts a sibling thread's terminal KV blocks directly into full-attention layers while bypassing linear-recurrent states (Gated DeltaNet / Mamba-2), cutting off-task hijacking by $21\times$ ($57.9\% \to 2.7\%$) while retaining $92.2\%$ of full-text mutual information at zero extra KV allocation; and (3) a Packed DAG-GRPO Trainer, which packs all episode branches into one block-sparse attention pass and assigns counterfactual branch credit via leave-one-branch-out marginal rewards.
  Trained end-to-end with GRPO on Qwen3.5-4B over a sparse BM25 corpus, Octopus reaches $49.6\%$ accuracy on BrowseComp-Plus ($+17.5$ points over single-agent GRPO at 143K context and $+12.5$ points over text-summary multi-agent RL) and $51.2\%$ on OpenResearcher, outperforming dense-retrieval agents up to $250\times$ larger while increasing training throughput $3.3\times$ and cutting peak KV memory $2.8\times$.

</details>


### [90] [Uncertainty calibration in Bayesian M/EEG source imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06979)
*Ismail Huseynov,Mohammad Orabe,Srikantan Nagarajan,Stefan Haufe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于仿真的M/EEG源成像不确定性量化与校准框架，比较BMN+sLORETA与SBL+S-FLEX的后验可信区间校准性，并用等渗回归进行事后校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: M/EEG源成像依赖经验贝叶斯反演方法，但其后验可信区域的实证校准性很少被系统评估；噪声学习能否带来近校准的不确定性亦不明确。

Method: 针对具有闭式条件高斯后验的经验贝叶斯反演构建仿真校准框架；比较BMN（sLORETA深度加权，经证据最大化联合推导源方差与噪声方差更新）与SBL（S-FLEX稀疏基场展开，使保留系数产生非零边缘后验方差）；仿真覆盖固定/自由朝向、不同SNR、稀疏度及个体头模型，采用oracle、基线估计与自适应学习噪声；并施加基于经验覆盖率的等渗事后校准。

Result: 校准前SBL+S-FLEX在Wasserstein定位误差、后验不确定度及覆盖率接近名义可信度方面均优于BMN+sLORETA；等渗校准对两者均有改善，对偏保守的BMN+sLORETA增益更大；跨SNR、稀疏度与头模型的迁移依赖具体条件；不同噪声设定下各估计器的重建与不确定性趋势基本一致。

Conclusion: 自适应噪声学习不足以消除残余失校准，需事后校准；SBL+S-FLEX在仿真与真实听觉MEG数据中均给出更聚焦的定位与更紧的不确定性，提示对双侧听觉源识别具有更高统计功效。

Abstract: M/EEG source imaging requires accurate localization and reliable uncertainty estimates, yet posterior credible regions are rarely assessed for empirical calibration. We present a simulation-based framework for uncertainty quantification and calibration of empirical Bayesian inverse methods with closed-form conditional Gaussian posterior summaries. We compare Bayesian minimum norm (BMN) with sLORETA depth weighting and sparse Bayesian learning (SBL) with sparse basis field expansions (S-FLEX). S-FLEX allows retained coefficients to contribute nonzero marginal posterior variance at locations where voxel-wise SBL yields degenerate variances. For BMN, we derive joint source- and noise-variance updates through evidence maximization. We test whether adaptive noise learning yields near-calibrated uncertainties. Both methods retain residual miscalibration, motivating post-hoc isotonic recalibration of credible-region levels using empirical coverage. Simulations assess fixed- and free-orientation models across signal-to-noise ratios (SNR), source sparsity levels, and subject-specific head models, using oracle, baseline-estimated, and adaptively learned noise. Before recalibration, SBL+S-FLEX yields lower Wasserstein localization error, smaller posterior uncertainty, and coverage closer to nominal credibility than BMN+sLORETA. Isotonic recalibration improves both methods, with larger gains for conservative BMN+sLORETA posteriors; transfer across mismatched SNR, sparsity, and head models remains condition dependent. Noise specifications yield broadly consistent reconstruction and uncertainty trends within each estimator. In real auditory MEG data, SBL+S-FLEX provides more focal localization and tighter uncertainties than BMN+sLORETA, suggesting greater statistical power for identifying bilateral auditory sources.

</details>


### [91] [Big Models, Small Gains: Frozen Forecasters as Classifiers on MONSTER](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06988)
*Rishit Jain,Rishit Kar,Chetashri Bhadane,Nilesh Patil*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 对 Reverso 与 Chronos-Bolt 两个预测基础模型的冻结表征做受控基准测试（MONSTER 六数据集、1%/10%/100% 标签），结果显示预训练相对随机权重有优势，但始终无法稳定超越最强非预训练基线（17 个设置中 15 个更差、2 个持平）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测基础模型被广泛用作时间序列分类的冻结特征提取器，但其预训练表征是否真正优于无需预训练的强分类器尚不清楚；核心问题是这些表征能否在架构本身之外提供额外信息，尤其在标注数据稀缺时。

Method: 在 MONSTER 基准的六个大规模数据集上，对 Reverso 和 Chronos-Bolt 进行受控对比：(1) 冻结表征 vs. 四种成熟的时间序列分类方法；(2) 冻结表征 vs. 同架构随机权重版本；(3) 标签比例 1%、10%、100%；并做补充实验：恢复幅值信息、微调、预测误差特征。

Result: 预训练在六个数据集中的五个上优于随机权重对应模型，最大提升达 23 个百分点；但冻结表征在 17 个设置中 15 个劣于最强非预训练基线、2 个持平，任一设置下均未可靠胜出。恢复幅值信息可弥合部分差距，微调与预测误差特征未能弥合。

Conclusion: 评估预测基础模型用于分类时，必须引入强任务特定基线与随机权重对照；"预训练优于随机初始化"不等于"优于强传统分类器"，冻结预测表征的迁移价值被高估。

Abstract: Forecasting foundation models are increasingly used as frozen feature extractors for time series classification, but it remains unclear whether their pretrained representations offer an advantage over strong classifiers that do not require pretraining. This raises an important question: do these representations provide useful information beyond what the model architecture alone can capture, particularly when labelled data are scarce? We present a controlled benchmarking of two forecasting models, Reverso and Chronos-Bolt, on six large datasets from the MONSTER benchmark, using 1%, 10%, and 100% of the available training labels. We compare their frozen representations against four established time series classification methods and random-weight versions of the same forecasting models. Our results show that pretraining improves classification accuracy over random-weight counterparts on five of the six datasets, with gains of up to 23 percentage points. However, the frozen representations do not reliably outperform the strongest non-pretrained baseline in any setting: they perform worse in 15 of 17 settings and tie in the remaining two. Further experiments show that restoring amplitude information recovers part of the performance gap, while fine-tuning and forecast-error features do not close it. These findings highlight the importance of strong task specific baselines and random weight controls when evaluating forecasting foundation models for classification. Our code is available at https://github.com/Rachet2006/frozen-forecasters-monster.

</details>


### [92] [Robust Stream Classification using Time Neutralising Decision Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06991)
*Brandon Gower-Winter,Georg Krempl*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出Time Neutralising Trees（TNT）：一种面向概念漂移数据流的决策树架构，训练时"中和"（过滤）不再相关的旧样本，必要时重新纳入，从而在可存储实例的设定下实现稳健流分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据流具有潜在无限性与非平稳性（概念漂移），导致部署模型需不断重训练；漂移检测器+重置是主流做法，但既有流学习假设（实例只出现一次）已过时，且相关研究表明实例可存储时批学习常优于流学习算法，因此需要新的、不依赖显式漂移检测与重置的稳健流分类方法。

Method: 提出TNT决策树架构：允许存储历史实例；训练过程中依据当前流上下文对旧训练样本进行"中和"（过滤掉不再相关的样本），若这些实例重新变得相关则再次纳入训练；从而隐式适应漂移而非依赖漂移检测器触发重置。

Result: 在USP DS数据仓库的24个真实与半真实分类数据流（涵盖多种概念漂移类型）上与相关算法进行对比评估，结果有力表明TNT在广泛数据流上取得同类最佳（best-in-class）性能。

Conclusion: 通过样本时间相关性管理而非显式漂移检测与重置，TNT为存在概念漂移的可存储实例场景提供了稳健且高性能的流分类方案，支持放宽"实例仅见一次"传统假设的流学习范式。

Abstract: Data Streams are characterised as a potentially infinite source of data subject to non-stationarity. This non-stationarity, also called Concept Drift, often results in deployed predictive models having to be perpetually retrained as the underlying data generating process of the stream changes over time. Drift Detectors are a class of methods used to identify these changes, triggering these retraining procedures. Historically, Stream Learning has operated under the assumption that instances are only seen once, and cannot be stored for later use. Contemporary work relaxes this assumption, yet drift detection and simple resetting procedures are still ubiquitous.
  Building on related work which has shown that traditional Batch Learning algorithms often outperform Stream Learning algorithms when instances can be stored, we introduce Time Neutralising Trees (TNT), a Decision Tree architecture that enables robust stream classification in settings with Concept Drift. During training, TNT will filter out ("neutralise") old training samples that are no longer relevant given the current context of the stream. If those instances become relevant again, TNT will then re-include them. We evaluate TNT, and related algorithms on 24 real and semi-real classification data streams from the USP DS repository. Results show strong evidence that TNT achieves best-in-class performance on a wide-range of data streams with varying concept drift types.

</details>


### [93] [LowRAM: Low-Rank Keys and Amortised Values for Composable KV Cache Compression at Test-Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07025)
*Teresa Delgado,Hugo Hazard,Martin Benfeghoul,Jun Wang,Zafeirios Fountas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文发现注意力头内 value 可由其对应的 key 大量预测，提出 value cache amortisation（逐头轻量预测器+残差存储）并结合跨层低秩 key 压缩，构建非对称压缩流水线 LowRAM，实现近无损的 4× 压缩与最高 5× 峰值解码吞吐，与量化、驱逐组合可达 26.9× 压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归解码依赖 KV cache，其显存随上下文长度线性增长，限制批大小与吞吐。现有后训练方法主要利用 token、层、特征维度的冗余或降低数值精度，未将 key 与 value 视为结构上不同的对象分别处理，缺少一条互补的压缩轴。

Method: 核心观察：每个注意力头内 value 在很大程度上可由对应 key 预测。据此提出 value cache amortisation——测试时拟合逐头轻量预测器，仅对重建误差最大的 token 存储残差，获得约 4× 近无损 value 压缩。预测器可通过元学习初始化以降低 prefill 开销，或在允许一次性成本的场景（如多查询推理）中从头拟合。再与跨层低秩 key 压缩配对，构成非对称压缩流水线 LowRAM；每个解码步骤仅重建所选子集的 KV 对以控制解码开销。

Result: 在三个模型家族上接近无损；4× 压缩下峰值解码吞吐最高提升 5×；与量化、驱逐组合后，长上下文下达到 26.9× 压缩且保留基线性能的 94.5%。

Conclusion: 将 key 与 value 视为结构上不同的对象，是实现高效、激进且可组合的 KV cache 压缩的关键；value 可由 key 预测这一互补冗余轴与现有方法正交，可叠加使用。

Abstract: Autoregressive decoding relies on the KV cache, whose memory footprint grows linearly with context length and caps batch size and throughput. Existing post-training methods exploit redundancy across tokens, layers, and feature dimensions, or lower numerical precision. We identify a complementary axis: within each attention head, values are largely predictable from their corresponding keys. We exploit this with value cache amortisation, which fits a lightweight per-head predictor at test time and stores residuals only for the tokens it reconstructs worst, giving $4\times$ near-lossless value compression. The predictor can be initialised by meta-learning for substantially reduced prefill overhead, or fitted from scratch in settings that allow a one-off cost, such as multi-query inference. We pair value amortisation with cross-layer low-rank key compression to produce LowRAM, an asymmetric compression pipeline that stays close to lossless across three model families. By reconstructing only a selected subset of KV pairs at each decoding step, LowRAM can keep decoding overhead minimal and achieve up to $5\times$ higher peak decode throughput at $4\times$ compression. LowRAM further composes with quantisation and eviction, reaching $26.9\times$ compression at $94.5\%$ of baseline performance on long contexts. These results show that treating keys and values as structurally distinct enables efficient, aggressive, and composable KV cache compression.

</details>


### [94] [Stochastic Dynamics of Generative AI: From Autoregressive Models to Interacting LLM Networks and Neural SDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07065)
*Vikram Krishnamurthy,Yiming Zhang,Luke Snow*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从随机动力系统视角统一刻画三类生成式AI模型：将LLM表述为高维非线性自回归模型，用平均场ODE刻画多LLM交互网络，并以神经SDE建模路径空间生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式AI（LLM、多模型交互网络、连续时间生成模型）缺乏统一的概率与动力系统理论框架，难以解释其生成机制、涌现行为与结构偏差的来源。

Method: 1）将LLM形式化为带注意力依赖的高维非线性自回归模型，把条件极大似然预训练与序列token生成联系起来并给出概率解释；2）对交互LLM网络建立平均场ODE，分析集体动力学；3）将神经SDE作为连续时间生成模型（含不规则采样轨迹），并基于粗糙路径分析引入Wiener空间cubature方法替代蒙特卡洛估计进行训练。

Result: 建立了LLM的概率解释框架；通过平均场ODE刻画了交互网络的涌现性优先连接与持续性结构偏差（含类型依赖的"玻璃天花板"效应）；论证了Wiener空间cubature方法在神经SDE训练中相对蒙特卡洛的效率优势。

Conclusion: 三类设置分别对应概率预测、交互网络中的信息交换与涌现行为、以及路径空间生成建模，共同构成生成式AI的随机动力系统统一视角。

Abstract: This paper develops a stochastic dynamical-systems perspective on three classes of generative AI models. First, we formulate large language models (LLMs) as high-dimensional nonlinear autoregressive models with attention-based dependencies. This formulation connects pretraining by conditional maximum likelihood to sequential token generation and provides a probabilistic interpretation of LLM operation. Second, we consider networks of interacting LLMs and characterize their collective behavior through mean-field ordinary differential equations. These dynamics provide a framework for understanding emergent preferential attachment and persistent structural biases, including type-dependent glass-ceiling effects. Third, we consider neural stochastic differential equations (SDEs) as continuous-time generative models for stochastic trajectories, including those observed at irregular time intervals. We discuss their training with particular emphasis on Wiener-space cubature methods based on rough-path analysis as an efficient alternative to Monte Carlo estimation. Together, the three settings address probabilistic prediction, information exchange and emergent behavior in interacting networks, and path-space generative modeling.

</details>


### [95] [Finite-Time Convergence and Regret Guarantees for Neural Reinforcement Learning in POMDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07069)
*Jingjuan Huang,Zhiyao Zhang,Alvaro Velasquez,Nathaniel D. Bastian,Jia Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究带非线性神经值函数近似的有限记忆POMDP强化学习，建立超状态MDP逐点近似误差界，证明神经critic的有限时间误差界，并结合策略镜像下降得到对最优POMDP值的有限时间次线性regret，为首个基于神经函数近似的有限记忆POMDP理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: POMDP中长程历史依赖导致计算困难；已有结果表明满足滤波稳定性假设的POMDP可用有限记忆窗口截断历史构造的超状态MDP近似，且价值差距随窗口长度衰减。但现有有限时间收敛分析要么局限于过度简化的线性函数近似，要么依赖POMDP轨迹不满足的i.i.d.采样oracle，限制了理论结果的实用性。因此作者希望填补POMDP中非线性神经值函数近似RL的理论空白。

Method: i) 建立新的有限状态MDP逐点近似误差界，作为算法设计与分析基础；ii) 将TD学习应用于POMDP生成的超状态转移，以超状态MDP的Bellman不动点为目标，在非线性神经值函数近似下证明有限时间critic误差界；iii) 将神经critic近似误差界与策略镜像下降框架结合，导出对最优POMDP值的有限时间次线性regret。辅以数值实验验证理论。

Result: 得到有限时间critic误差界，并据此获得对最优POMDP值的有限时间次线性regret；据作者所知，这是文献中首个基于神经函数近似的有限记忆POMDP有限时间次线性regret结果，数值实验亦验证了理论结论。

Conclusion: 在滤波稳定性假设下，通过超状态MDP代理与非线神经critic加策略镜像下降，可对有限记忆POMDP实现有理论保证的有限时间次线性regret，突破了线性近似与i.i.d.采样假设的限制。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) in Partially Observable Markov Decision Processes (POMDPs) is computationally challenging due to the curse of long-range dependence on history. Fortunately, recent studies have shown that a range of POMDPs that satisfy the filter stability assumption can be well approximated by a superstate Markov Decision Process (MDP) based on a truncated history in a finite-memory window, with a value gap that decays with the window length. However, existing finite-time convergence analyses on such superstate MDP surrogates are either limited to over-simplified linear function approximation or rely on an i.i.d. sampling oracle that is violated by POMDP trajectories, both of which severely limit the applicability of these theoretical results in practice. This motivates us to bridge the gap by investigating RL in POMDPs with nonlinear neural value function approximation. Our main contributions are threefold: i) We first establish a new finite-state MDP point-wise approximation error bound, which lays the foundation for our RL algorithm design and analysis in POMDPs; ii) By applying Temporal-Difference (TD) learning to POMDP-generated superstate transitions and targeting the Bellman fixed point of the superstate MDP, we prove a finite-time critic error bound with nonlinear neural value function approximation; and iii) By combining the neural critic function approximation error bound with the policy mirror descent framework, we obtain a finite-time sublinear regret against the optimal POMDP value. To the best of our knowledge, such a finite-time sublinear regret based on neural function approximation is the first in the literature for finite-memory POMDPs. Our numerical experiments also verify our theoretical results.

</details>


### [96] [Shaping & Monitoring Models through their Internal Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07071)
*Thomas Fel,Matthew Kowal,Matt Feiszli,Satchel Grant,Andrew Lee,Chris Earls,Fatih Dinc,Vasudev Shyam,Usha Bhalla,Leon Bergen,Siddharth Boppana,Ching Fang,Michael Byun,Tristan Gallent,Nicholas Wang,Tal Haklay,Dron Hazra,Neehar Kondapaneni,Connor Watts,Ryan Panwar,Thomas Dooms,Jack Merullo,Atticus Geiger,Ekdeep Singh Lubana,Thomas McGrath,Owen Lewis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Mechanistic Shaping 统一框架，在训练中通过内部表征约束模型"表达什么概念"与"依赖什么概念"作决策；在多领域、多规模、多范式下显著减少目标行为且保持性能，并引入监控器层级与三种几何规避机制，发现被塑造概念大多仍可监控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有做法多用输出层的行为约束来控制模型，难以约束模型内部真正"依赖"的根据（如招聘筛选时不依赖性别）。作者希望把可解释性（内部表征）作为训练手段，统一地约束模型表达的概念与其决策所依赖的概念，并关注一个被忽视的问题：训练针对某表征会改变该表征，被塑造的概念是否仍然可被监控。

Method: 提出 Mechanistic Shaping 框架，在训练阶段对内部表征施加规格约束，使模型少表达某概念、少依赖某概念；在视觉、医学影像、语言三个领域，最高 32B 参数规模，覆盖分类、SFT、DPO、GRPO 等训练范式进行验证。为评估可监控性，构建监控器类别层级（区分"逃过某个探针"与"真正消失"），并刻画三种几何规避机制：概念漂移远离监控器、从该层被擦除、以非线性编码形式保留于高阶统计量中（用乘积码构造解释），并为每种机制给出匹配的监控器。

Result: 跨领域、规模与范式下，Mechanistic Shaping 显著降低目标行为，同时保持任务性能。可监控性方面：被塑造概念大多仍可监控——重新拟合的线性探针在多数设置下能恢复该概念；当梯度不流经原探针时，原探针仍保持可靠。三种几何规避机制可解释全部观测，且各自由相应监控器检出。

Conclusion: 可解释性可作为训练工具有效塑造模型行为与决策依据，且被塑造概念通常仍可追踪；但规避机制的存在要求建立与之匹配的、严谨的"基于可解释性的训练"科学，包括监控器层级与针对性探针设计。

Abstract: We propose Mechanistic Shaping, a unified framework for shaping models through their internal representations. Mechanistic Shaping is applied during training so that models follow specifications (rules) about which concepts they express, measured via their outputs or internal representations (e.g., no harmful content), and also which concepts they rely on, i.e., what they use to make their decisions (e.g., not a candidate's gender when screening a résumé). Across multiple domains (vision, medical imaging, language), scales (up to 32B parameters), and training paradigms (classification, SFT, DPO, GRPO), we find that Mechanistic Shaping substantially reduces targeted behaviors while preserving task performance. Since training against a representation can also modify it, we ask whether shaped concepts remain monitorable, a question that depends on the class of monitors considered. We therefore introduce a hierarchy of such classes, which separates a concept that escapes one probe from a concept that is truly gone. We characterize three geometric mechanisms of circumvention: the concept can drift away from the monitor, be erased from the layer, or persist in higher-order statistics as a nonlinear encoding, which we explain through a product-code construction. Although other mechanisms may arise in different settings or at larger scales, these three account for all of our observations, and each can be detected by a matching monitor. In practice, we find that shaped concepts largely remain monitorable: refitted linear probes recover the concept in most settings, and the original probe remains reliable whenever gradients do not flow through it. Taken together, these results point to the potential of using interpretability as a tool for training, and call for a correspondingly rigorous science of interpretability-based training.

</details>


### [97] [Optimistic Hedge in General-Sum Games: Fundamental Limits and Regret Bounds via Temporal Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07078)
*Yuval Dagan,Nadav Eisen,Liad Erez,Tomer Koren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究乐观对冲（Optimistic Hedge）在多玩家有限一般和博弈中的遗憾界，证明其常数级遗憾是否存在取决于玩家数量：两人博弈中可取学习率使遗憾为 O(n log n) 且与时间跨度无关，而三人及以上博弈中遗憾可达 2^{Ω(mn)} 甚至随 T 线性增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Daskalakis 等证明固定学习率下乐观对冲的遗憾为 poly log T，Ha 进一步给出与 T 无关的界，但该界非构造性且对动作数 n 的依赖不显式，因此"能否达到关于博弈规模多项式的常数遗憾"仍是悬而未决的问题。

Method: 上界部分建立"时间近似"框架：从 Daskalakis 等人的基于方差的 RVU 界出发，用性质良好的函数在局部时间窗口内逼近损失，使这些函数的总变差仅为其累积方差的小部分；下界部分通过构造特定博弈实例分析学习率与遗憾的关系。

Result: 两人博弈中存在学习率使乐观对冲保证与时间跨度无关的 O(n log n) 遗憾；三人博弈中，对任意不超过绝对常数的学习率及足够大的 T，存在每玩家 n 个动作的博弈使某玩家遗憾为 2^{Ω(n)}；更一般地，对每个 m≥3 可构造 m 人博弈使某玩家遗憾为 2^{Ω(mn)}，且当学习率高于 2^{-Ω(mn)} 时该玩家遗憾随 T 线性增长。

Conclusion: 乐观对冲能否实现多项式规模的常数遗憾本质上取决于玩家数量：两人情形可行，三人及以上不可行。该时间近似框架还可给出 Ha 的 O(√m log n log T) 界的显著简化证明。

Abstract: Optimistic Hedge, a multiplicative weight update with one step of extrapolation, is one of the most natural regret-minimizing dynamics for games, and characterizing its regret has been a longstanding open question. Daskalakis, Fishelson and Golowich proved that if all $m$ players in a finite general-sum game follow Optimistic Hedge with a fixed learning rate, each player's regret is polylogarithmic in the horizon $T$. Subsequently, Ha showed that an appropriate choice of learning rate makes each player's regret bounded independently of $T$. This bound, however, is non-constructive and its dependence on the number of actions $n$ is not explicit, leaving open whether Optimistic Hedge can achieve a constant regret bound which is polynomial in the size of the game.
  We show that the answer hinges on the number of players. In two-player games, there is a learning rate for which Optimistic Hedge guarantees $O(n\log n)$ regret independent of the horizon. In three-player games, however, no such bound is possible: for any learning rate up to an absolute constant and for sufficiently large horizon $T$, there is a game with $n$ actions per player in which some player's regret is $2^{Ω(n)}$, and more generally, for every $m\ge3$ we construct $m$-player games in which some player's regret is $2^{Ω(mn)}$; for learning rates above $2^{-Ω(mn)}$, that player's regret even grows linearly in $T$.
  Our upper bound is based on a temporal approximation framework for the analysis of Optimistic Hedge. Starting from the variance-based RVU bound of Daskalakis et al., we approximate the losses over time-localized windows by well-behaved functions, whose total variation is a small fraction of their cumulative variance. In $m$-player games, the same framework gives a significantly simplified proof of the recent $O(\sqrt{m}\log n\log T)$ regret bound due to Ha.

</details>


### [98] [Server-Side Sparse Assignment for Federated Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07093)
*Xue Zheng,Aylin Yener*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出服务器端稀疏专家分配框架 S³-MoE，在部分观测下估计客户端—专家相关性，并用"利用—探索"机制精炼分配，具备相关性估计误差与精确 Top-K 恢复的理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘设备在数据分布与资源（内存、算力）上高度异构；已有联邦 MoE 的部分专家方法依赖启发式全池观测或侧重系统级负载均衡，无法保证从部分观测中恢复出对特定客户端真正相关的专家。

Method: 服务器端稀疏专家分配框架：基于部分观测估计客户端特定的专家相关性，再通过 exploit–explore（利用—探索）机制迭代精炼专家分配；理论上给出相关性估计误差界与精确 Top-K 恢复保证。实验采用基于 CNN 的 MoE，在 CIFAR-100 上开展联邦微调。

Result: 在 CIFAR-100 上，S³-MoE 能恢复任务相关的客户端—专家偏好，并减少因客户端间专家选择相关而引发的冲突；每客户端仅加载 5 个专家时，相较 Full-20 FedAvg（每客户端可用全部 20 个专家、本地 Top-5 路由），平均最终精度提升 1.31 个百分点。

Conclusion: 部分观测下的服务器端专家分配可在显著降低每客户端专家加载量的同时优于全池访问基线，并具备可证明的相关性估计与 Top-K 恢复性质。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly recognized for their remarkable capabilities; federated learning (FL) offers a promising framework for collaboratively fine-tuning LLMs on edge devices while achieving data privacy. However, edge devices are inherently heterogeneous: clients may exhibit diverse data distributions and resource constraints, including limited memory and computation budgets. Recent federated Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) approaches address these challenges by using sparse expert activation or partial expert loading. However, existing partial-expert methods rely on heuristic full-pool observations or prioritize system-wide load balance, without guarantees for recovering client-specific relevant experts from partial observations. We propose $S^3$-MoE, a server-side sparse expert assignment framework that estimates client-specific expert relevance from partial observations and refines assignments through an exploit--explore mechanism. We also establish relevance-estimation error and exact Top-$K$ recovery guarantees. Experiments using a CNN-based MoE on CIFAR-100 show that $S^3$-MoE recovers task-dependent client--expert preferences and reduces conflicts caused by correlated expert selection across clients. Compared with Full-20 FedAvg, which gives each client access to all 20 experts for local Top-5 routing, $S^3$-MoE achieves 1.31 percentage points higher mean final accuracy while loading only $5$ experts per client.

</details>


### [99] [A Night at the LLM Zoo: Data Conditioned Flow Matching for Generating Pretrained LLM Weights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07103)
*Scott Biggs,Bedionita Soro,Saumya Gupta,Bruno Andreis,Sung Ju Hwang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 构建并分析了一个由51M/124M/355M参数GPT-2（每档N=100）组成、按数据混合比例系统变化的LLM模型动物园，并据此训练Gram-PCA压缩权重空间中的Flow Matching权重生成器，实现对新混合比例的零样本泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 权重空间学习从任务特定模型和视觉模型扩展到LLM时，缺乏经过良好整理与分析的LLM模型群体（population），难以支撑权重的建模与生成研究。

Method: 系统改变网页文本、书籍、数学、代码、法语五类数据混合配比，训练三档GPT-2模型群（每档100个）；用余弦相似度、功能多样性、Leave-One-Mixture-Out建模与方差分解进行分析；将混合配比作为条件嵌入，在Gram-PCA压缩权重空间中训练Flow Matching模型生成完整预训练权重。

Result: 模型动物园包含多样性模型且变异由训练混合配比结构化；生成器对留出混合配比具备零样本泛化能力，CRPS技能评分有效，且生成模型在下游测试困惑度与通用能力基准上仅有极小变化，性能与动物园内模型相近。

Conclusion: 证明了在数据混合单纯形上条件化的权重生成可从头（ex nihilo）产生性能相当的LLM，并开源全部代码与产物以支持权重空间学习在LLM领域的发展。

Abstract: A challenge to Weight Space Learning as it expands from task specific and vision models to Large Language Models (LLMs) is the absence of a well curated and analyzed LLM population. To address this, we train such a model zoo for application to Weight Space Generation by systematically varying the data mixture composition between web text, books, mathematics, coding, and French across populations of 51M, 124M, and 355M parameter GPT-2 Models, each tier with count N = 100. We analyze these populations by cosine similarity, functional diversity, Leave-One-Mixture-Out modeling, and variance decomposition. We show that the zoo contains diverse models whose variation is structured by training mixture composition. We embed the training mixture composition to condition a Flow Matching model of complete pretrained LLM weights in Gram-PCA compressed weight space. By demonstrating zero-shot generalization to held out mixtures, measuring Continuous Ranked Probability Score skill, as well as finding minimal changes in downstream test-set perplexity and general capability benchmark scores, we show that our generator generalizes on the simplex of data mixtures to produce models that perform similarly to those in the zoo ex nihilo. Finally, we produce all source code and artifacts to support the continued expansion of Weight Space Learning and Generation into the LLM domain.

</details>


### [100] [Regulating Autonomous Mobility-on-Demand Operators with Fixed and Adaptive Penalties: A Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07122)
*Korbinian Kügler,Carolin Schmidt,Florian Dandl,Filipe Rodrigues*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究基于惩罚的AMoD运营商的监管机制：静态监管以MDP建模运营商价格与再平衡联合控制，动态监管以POSG建模监管者闭环调整区域惩罚，均用图基多智能体强化学习求解；曼哈顿数据实验表明固定高峰定价惩罚将面临过度价格旅客比例从22%降至1%，动态监管使监管者回报较同强度固定惩罚最高提升15.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利润最大化的AMoD运营商可能通过过高定价攫取消费者剩余，并将车辆调离低利润区域导致服务不足，因此需要研究如何在多模式出行系统中通过惩罚型监管限制动态加价并提升区域服务可达性。

Method: 将监管建模为作用于学习型运营商的外层控制回路：静态监管（固定惩罚）形式化为运营商联合控制定价与再平衡的马尔可夫决策过程；动态监管形式化为部分可观测随机博弈，监管者周期性闭环调整区域特定惩罚；运营商与监管者策略均采用图基多智能体强化学习学习。

Result: 在真实曼哈顿数据实验中，固定高峰定价惩罚使面临过度价格的旅客占比由22%降至1%；在区域服务可达性上，动态监管相较同等平均强度的固定惩罚使监管者回报最高提升15.6%；当存在闲置车队容量时服务可达性增益最强，而在车队利用率极高的高峰时段服务缺口仍存在；学到的监管策略会在车队容量限制合规性的区域降低惩罚。

Conclusion: 结果揭示了监管目标、运营商盈利能力与旅客效用之间的权衡关系，并表明惩罚型监管对抑制过度定价有效，而区域服务可达性的改善受车队容量约束制约。

Abstract: Profit-maximizing Autonomous Mobility-on-Demand (AMoD) operators may exploit consumer surplus through excessive prices and underserve less profitable regions by relocating vehicles away from them. We study how penalty-based regulation of an AMoD operator in a multimodal mobility system can limit surge pricing and improve regional service availability. We model regulation as an outer control loop that acts on a learning-based operator. Static regulation with fixed penalties is formulated as a Markov Decision Process for an operator that jointly controls pricing and rebalancing, and dynamic regulation as a Partially Observable Stochastic Game in which a regulator periodically adjusts region-specific penalties in closed loop. Operator and regulator policies are learned with graph-based multi-agent reinforcement learning. In experiments with real-world Manhattan data, fixed surge pricing penalties reduce the share of travelers facing excessive prices from 22% to 1%. For regional service availability, dynamic regulation improves the regulator's reward by up to 15.6% over fixed penalties of equal mean intensity. The gains in service availability are strongest when spare fleet capacity exists, whereas service deficits persist in peak periods with highly utilized fleets. The learned regulator policy responds by reducing penalties where limited fleet capacity restricts compliance. The results identify trade-offs between regulatory targets, operator profitability, and traveler utility.

</details>


### [101] [Machine Learning for Live-Birth Prediction After IVF/ICSI From Structured Clinical Data: A Systematic Review, Meta-Analysis, and Critical Appraisal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07124)
*Abdul Raheem Shahzad,Muhammad Zaheer Khan,Umar Ajaib,Asad Ur Rehman,Juan Deng,Ming Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该系统综述与Meta分析评估了预测IVF/ICSI活产的机器学习模型，发现报告性能从近乎随机到近乎完美不等；外部验证后AUC降至0.756，异质性极高，47/48研究存在高或不明确偏倚风险，证据确定性低至极低，真正的增益来自决策点分期与局部校准而非更深层架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预测IVF/ICSI活产的机器学习模型报告的性能差异极大（从接近随机到接近完美），其真实泛化能力、偏倚风险与临床价值不明，亟需系统性评估以厘清性能来源与研究缺口。

Method: 遵循PRISMA 2020，检索OpenAlex、Scopus与Web of Science（截至2026年10月22日），采用PROBAST+AI与TRIPOD+AI进行质量评价；在logit尺度上以随机效应模型合并判别力（AUC），并进行Meta回归探索调节因素。作者定义了决定模型输出含义的四要素（决策点、终点、分析单元、数据来源），并按模型家族、决策点与验证设计对48项纳入研究进行归类映射。

Result: 仅内部验证的模型合并AUC为0.814；在后期时段或另一中心验证的模型为0.756（95%预测区间0.546–0.889）；非高偏倚风险研究为0.764；两者差异均不显著且异质性极高。未说明决策点的模型合并AUC达0.910，是Meta回归中唯一显著的调节变量。在同一数据上与逻辑回归相比，最佳机器学习模型的合并AUC增益为0.056（95% CI 0.017–0.090）。48项研究中47项为高或不明确偏倚风险，仅4项评估校准斜率，无任何模型具备临床影响证据。

Conclusion: 合并证据的确定性为低至极低。经外部验证后仍保留的性能增益源自将预测对应于具体决策点的分期设计与局部校准，而非更复杂的模型架构。作者提供了TRIPOD+AI映射清单、基准测试规范与研究议程，以规范该领域未来研究。

Abstract: Machine-learning models predicting live birth after in vitro fertilisation (IVF) or intracytoplasmic sperm injection (ICSI) from routine clinical data report performance from near chance to near perfect. We systematically reviewed these models under PRISMA 2020, searching OpenAlex, Scopus and Web of Science to 22 October 2026, appraised them with PROBAST+AI and TRIPOD+AI, and pooled their discrimination with random-effects meta-analysis on the logit scale. We define what fixes the meaning of a model's output (decision point, endpoint, unit of analysis, data source) and map the 48 included studies by model family, decision point and validation design. Models evaluated only internally pooled to an area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUC) of 0.814, against 0.756 (95% prediction interval 0.546-0.889) for models evaluated on a later period or another centre, and 0.764 for studies not at high risk of bias; neither difference was significant, and heterogeneity was extreme. Models whose decision point was not stated pooled to 0.910, the only significant moderator in meta-regression. Against logistic regression fitted to the same data, the best machine-learning model gained a pooled 0.056 AUC (95% CI 0.017-0.090). Of 48 studies, 47 were at high or unclear risk of bias, only four assessed the calibration slope, and no model has evidence of clinical impact. The certainty of the pooled evidence is low to very low; the gains that survive validation came from staging predictions to decision points and local calibration, not deeper architectures. We provide a TRIPOD+AI-mapped checklist, a benchmark specification and a research agenda.

</details>
