### [102] [Data-Driven Modeling of Molten Chloride Corrosion Using Static Test Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07125)
*Md Mahmudul Hasan Pathik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文汇编291组文献熔融氯化物静态腐蚀实验数据，提出"氯化物盐制备指数(CSPI)"量化盐处理与气氛条件，构建可解释机器学习框架预测腐蚀速率，交叉验证R²=0.85、MAE=0.41 mg·cm⁻²·day⁻¹，并结合SHAP与共形预测实现特征归因与不确定性量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 熔融氯化物腐蚀研究已持续数十年，但跨合金、跨盐体系、跨测试条件的可靠腐蚀速率预测仍十分困难；核心障碍在于文献实验条件（盐纯化方式、气氛控制、预暴露杂质状态）报道不一致且难以统一表征，使历史数据难以被有效利用。

Method: 1) 从文献中整理291组静态熔融氯化物腐蚀实验，构建结构化数据集；2) 提出氯化物盐制备指数（CSPI），将盐处理方法、气氛控制与预暴露杂质状况映射到统一数值尺度；3) 采用混合树集成模型（blended tree-based ensemble）进行腐蚀速率回归，并做交叉验证；4) 使用全局敏感性分析与SHAP进行特征重要性与可解释性分析；5) 采用共形预测（conformal prediction）构建带校准保证的预测区间以量化不确定性。

Result: 混合树集成模型预测一致性最佳，交叉验证R²=0.85、MAE=0.41 mg·cm⁻²·day⁻¹；分级化CSPI比简单"纯化/未纯化"二分类保留更多有效信息，并成为模型的主导描述符；全局敏感性与SHAP表明暴露时间、温度和合金化学组分为主要贡献因素，其趋势与已知熔融氯化物腐蚀机理相符；共形预测区间校准良好，可在点预测之外给出不确定性估计。

Conclusion: 显式表征实验上下文（尤其盐制备条件）后，历史腐蚀数据可支撑定量、可解释且具备不确定性感知的预测建模；同时揭示现有文献在实验报道规范性与针对性实验设计上的不足，指出需更一致的报告标准与补充实验以提升未来熔盐腐蚀模型的可靠性。

Abstract: Molten chloride corrosion has been studied for decades, yet reliable prediction across different alloys, salts, and test conditions remains difficult. In this work, 291 static molten chloride corrosion experiments reported in the literature were compiled into a structured dataset and used to develop an interpretable machine-learning framework for corrosion-rate predictions. To better represent the differences in salt condition across studies, a Chloride Salt Preparation Index (CSPI) was introduced to bring the reported salt-treatment method, atmosphere control, and pre-exposure impurity condition on a common numerical scale. A blended tree-based ensemble provided the most consistent predictive performance, achieving a cross-validated \(R^2\) of 0.85 and an MAE of 0.41 mg cm\(^{-2}\) day\(^{-1}\). The graded CSPI retained more useful information than a simple purified/unpurified classification and emerged as the dominant model descriptor. Global sensitivity and SHAP analyses further identified exposure time, temperature, and alloy chemistry as important contributors to the model predictions, with several observed trends consistent with the established molten-chloride corrosion behavior. The conformal prediction intervals were well calibrated, providing an estimate of prediction uncertainty in addition to the point prediction. Overall, the framework demonstrates that historical corrosion data can support quantitative, interpretable, and uncertainty-aware prediction when experimental context is represented explicitly, while also highlighting where more consistent reporting and targeted experiments are needed to improve future molten-salt corrosion models.

</details>


### [103] [Scalable Bayesian Gaussian Process Classification for Cancer Histopathology: Calibrated Uncertainty Under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07127)
*Yunru Zheng,Wun Ting Chan,Sampras Dsouza,Zhenyu Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出可扩展的贝叶斯高斯过程分类框架，在冻结的病理基础模型嵌入上结合RPCholesky低秩分解、非中心化参数化的HMC后验采样与"枢轴作诱导点"预测方案，在约30万训练点上实现HMC低秩GPC，并在跨院分布偏移下显著改善校准与预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床部署癌症诊断模型需要可靠的不确定性量化，但神经网络与变分高斯过程常产生过度自信预测，尤其在模型跨医院应用时的分布偏移下更为严重；同时既有MCMC-based GPC难以扩展到大规模数据集。

Method: 在冻结的病理基础模型Phikon嵌入上构建可扩展贝叶斯GPC：用加速的Randomly Pivoted Cholesky（RPCholesky）进行低秩核分解；在低秩潜表示上采用非中心化参数化的Hamiltonian Monte Carlo进行后验采样；以枢轴点（pivots）作为诱导点完成预测。

Result: 在PCam与CAMELYON17-WILDS两个乳腺癌转移检测基准上（后者检验院级分布偏移下的校准），相较随机变分GP与神经网络基线，校准与预测性能提升：期望校准误差最高降低88%，假阴性率最高降低90%，且增益在分布偏移下最大。

Conclusion: HMC-based后验采样可为跨站点病理分类提供更可信的不确定性估计，使MCMC低秩GPC可扩展到远超以往演示的约30万点规模。

Abstract: Reliable uncertainty quantification is essential for clinical deployment of cancer diagnosis models, yet neural networks and variational Gaussian process methods can produce overconfident predictions, particularly under the distribution shifts that arise when models trained at one hospital are applied at another. We present a scalable Bayesian Gaussian Process Classification (GPC) framework that operates on frozen pathology foundation model embeddings (Phikon) and combines accelerated Randomly Pivoted Cholesky (RPCholesky) for low-rank kernel factorization, Hamiltonian Monte Carlo (HMC) with non-centered parameterization for posterior sampling, and a pivots-as-inducing-points scheme for prediction. This makes HMC posterior sampling in the low-rank latent representation tractable at N approximately 300,000 training points, enabling HC-based low-rank GPC on substantially larger datasets than prior MCMC-based demonstrations. We evaluate on two histopathology benchmarks for breast cancer metastasis detection: PCam and CAMELYON17-WILDS, where the latter tests calibration under hospital-level distribution shift. Compared to stochastic variational GP and neural network baselines, our method improves calibration and predictive performance - reducing expected calibration error by up to 88% and false negative rate by up to 90% - with the largest gains appearing under distribution shift, suggesting that HMC-based posterior sampling provides more trustworthy uncertainty estimates for cross-site pathology classification.

</details>


### [104] [Teaching Agents the "Principle", Not the "Reward"](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07130)
*Shan Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出"规定性策略学习"(PPL),通过直接向智能体传授关于任务的更高层知识(即行为原则,如保持平衡、积累能量、平稳着陆),而非依赖奖励信号,使智能体在无奖励情况下学到良好且可泛化的策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在复杂任务中即使有精心设计的奖励和充足样本,仍难以学到良好、可泛化的策略。作者认为根源在于"奖励解码负担":设计者将期望行为的知识编码为奖励与惩罚,智能体必须从长序列的噪声标量中反推这些知识,负担过重。

Method: 提出PPL方法,直接教授高层行为原则。通过合理选取关键量(key quantities)与参考(references),在每一步直接指示智能体是否遵循该原则以及遵循程度,从而绕过奖励设计,无需任何奖励信号即可指导学习。

Result: 实验显示智能体可在完全无奖励条件下学到良好、可泛化的策略;仅教授简单启发式原则的PPL,以远更少的样本超越基于奖励的方法;所学策略可直接迁移到更难设定中并保持良好表现,展现强泛化能力。

Conclusion: PPL提供了新范式:即便系统动力学无法精确建模,只要对期望行为有粗略知识,即可让智能体学到良好且可泛化的策略,减轻奖励解码负担。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has achieved strong performance in many tasks by letting agents learn from nothing more than a reward signal. However, in more complex tasks, agents still struggle to learn good, generalizable policies, even with carefully designed rewards and abundant samples. We argue that this difficulty stems from the reward decoding burden: designers encode their knowledge of the desired behavior into rewards and penalties, and agents must infer this knowledge from long sequences of noisy scalars. To address this burden, we propose prescriptive policy learning (PPL), which directly teaches agents higher-level knowledge about the tasks, termed principles of behavior, such as how to maintain balance, how to accumulate energy, and how to land smoothly. Through appropriately selected key quantities and references, PPL can directly indicate at every step whether the agent follows the principles and to what extent. Our experiments show that agents can learn good, generalizable policies without any reward. Taught only simple heuristic principles, PPL agents outperform reward-based methods with far fewer samples. Their policies also transfer directly to harder settings and keep performing well, demonstrating their strong generalization ability. In summary, PPL provides a new approach in which rough knowledge of the desired behavior is enough for agents to learn good, generalizable policies, even when the system dynamics cannot be accurately modeled.

</details>


### [105] [Depth or Description? When a One-Step Latent Score Replaces Lookahead in Proactive Information Acquisition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07155)
*Biswa Sengupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"主动提问"决策中，一步式分数是否足够取决于潜在因果变量的描述方式；合适的潜在变量分数可替代大部分前瞻深度，并需用小规模潜在变量与精确值校验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动型智能体需在行动前决定问什么。精确前瞻（lookahead）要枚举问题组合、代价高且依赖拟合模型；一步式评分便宜但可能漏掉答案间的交互作用。作者希望厘清"何时需要深层搜索"这一根本问题。

Method: 理论/实证对比分析：比较精确前瞻值与一步式评分在（a）标签层面与（b）合适潜在因果变量层面上的表现；构建小型潜在表示并在其上打分；以精确值作为基准进行校验；并考察跨语言模型的概率可迁移性。

Result: 核心发现是深度需求常隐藏在"隐藏原因如何被描述"之中：对标签的一步评分会对答案间交互视而不见，而对合适潜在原因评分则能识别；当潜在变量编码了决策相关信息时，可取代大部分搜索深度而仅用极小代价。同一问题换一种描述即可击溃该分数，而精确值不变。此外，从一个语言模型答案中学到的概率不能迁移到另一个模型，除非规划同时考虑两者；从token读出的概率仅相当于少量标签的价值。

Conclusion: 应保持潜在变量规模小而精，并用精确值对其进行校验；潜在表示的描述方式决定了深度搜索的必要性。跨模型概率不可直接迁移，需在规划中显式建模；token级概率信息价值有限。

Abstract: A proactive agent must decide what to ask before it acts. Exact lookahead values combinations of questions but is expensive and leans on a fitted model; a one-step score is cheap but can miss them. Our main finding: the need for depth often lies in how the hidden cause is described. A one-step score on the label can be blind to an interaction among answers. A score on a suitable latent cause is not, and where that latent encodes the decision it replaces most of depth at a fraction of the search. Described differently, the same problem can defeat the score while exact values do not move, so keep the latent small and check it against exact values. Probabilities learned from one language model's answers do not carry to another unless planning allows for both; those read from tokens are worth only a few labels.

</details>


### [106] [A Survey of Nonlinear Filtering Methods for Data Assimilation and Recent Advances in Generative AI-Enabled Score Filters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07160)
*Cath Pham,Kadesha Reynolds*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该综述系统梳理了集合评分滤波器(EnSF)的数学基础，论证其是非高斯、高维滤波的最先进方法，并介绍14项扩展工作（其中12项保留无训练蒙特卡洛估计器核心）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的非线性混沌动力系统（如数值天气预报、野火追踪）受多源不确定性影响，需要数据同化融合模型与部分含噪观测以提升预测精度。而最优滤波问题（求解隐状态在观测序列下的条件分布）的传统方法存在三类局限：Kalman类假设高斯后验且非线性观测下产生偏差；粒子类受维度灾难影响在高维中坍缩；核方法难以负担高维下密度近似的维护。EnSF旨在同时规避这三点。

Method: EnSF将滤波密度编码为评分函数(score function)，并用无训练的蒙特卡洛估计器近似该评分，从而绕开高斯假设、离散粒子表示与核密度近似三重限制。

Result: 综述给出EnSF的数学基础，确立其在高维混沌系统中滤波的稳定性与SOTA地位，并归纳14项扩展；其中12项在更新核心保留无训练蒙特卡洛估计器，仅2项为行星尺度部署而偏离该核心。

Conclusion: EnSF为非高斯、高维数据同化提供了统一且可扩展的滤波框架，其扩展谱系展示了从理论到业务化部署的可延展性，并指出了偏离核心估计器以实现超大规模应用的两个方向。

Abstract: Many real-world applications, such as numerical weather prediction or wildfire tracking, involve modeling highly nonlinear dynamical systems that are chaotic and influenced by various sources of uncertainty. To improve model prediction accuracy and reduce such uncertainties, data assimilation (DA) combines the dynamical model with partial, noisy observations to estimate the evolution of the system's state. Doing so depends on solving the optimal filtering problem: computing the conditional distribution of a hidden state given a sequence of observations. Classical approaches fail along three distinct axes: Kalman-based methods assume Gaussian posteriors and produce biased estimates under nonlinear observation operators; particle-based methods represent distributions with weighted samples and collapse in high dimensions due to the curse of dimensionality; and kernel-based methods move beyond discrete particle representations but cannot maintain their density approximation affordably as dimension grows. The Ensemble Score Filter (EnSF) avoids all three constraints by encoding the filtering density in a score function and approximating that score with a training-free Monte Carlo estimator, enabling stable filtering in high-dimensional, chaotic systems. This survey traces EnSF's mathematical foundations, establishes it as the state of the art for non-Gaussian, high-dimensional filtering, and examines fourteen extensions that expand its operational reach. Twelve of the fourteen extensions preserve this training-free Monte Carlo estimator at the core of the update, with only two departing from it to achieve planetary-scale deployment.

</details>


### [107] [Structural Conditional Independence Test via Neural Quantile Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07165)
*Khoa Nguyen,Bao Duong,Thin Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出QRCIT——基于分位数形变的条件独立性检验框架，通过共享残差分位流模型比较P(X|Z)与P(X|Y,Z)，并可解释依赖发生在何处；同时发布结构化依赖基准SDB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有条件独立性检验在依赖较弱、局部化或仅表现为分布变化（而非全局均值平移）时难以检出；且通常只输出标量统计量或p值，无法刻画条件分布在哪里发生变化。

Method: QRCIT先用轻量残差分位流模型学习基线条件分位律P(X|Z)，再学习Y引入的附加形变，构造全局预测增益统计量，配合条件随机化校准，并在context与分位水平上输出可检视的形变场；另构建Structural Dependency Benchmark（SDB），涵盖context-local、tail-only、sign-reversing、moving-ridge、mean-neutral等依赖结构。

Result: 在合成PNL与SDB基准上，QRCIT对结构化备择假设的检出能力提升，并在多种设定下评估了经验Type-I误差；中等样本量下更高分位分辨率提升merged-SDB功效；限定于已知受影响区域的诊断可改善局部依赖检出。在Sachs、METABRIC及CCLE/PLX4720真实数据上表现具竞争力，并给出可解释的形变模式。

Conclusion: 分位数分辨的条件律形变可作为检验与诊断结构化条件依赖的实用框架。

Abstract: Conditional independence testing is difficult when dependence is weak, localized, or expressed through distributional changes rather than a global mean shift. Existing tests usually return a scalar statistic or p-value, which summarizes evidence against independence but does not describe where the conditional law changes. We propose QRCIT, a quantile-resolution conditional independence test that compares P(X|Z) and P(X|Y,Z) through a quantile deformation learned by a shared model. A lightweight residual quantile-flow model first learns the baseline conditional quantile law and then learns the additional deformation induced by Y, yielding a global predictive-gain statistic with conditional-randomization calibration and an inspectable deformation field over context and quantile level. We also introduce the Structural Dependency Benchmark (SDB), which evaluates dependence structures motivated by real applications, such as context-local, tail-only, sign-reversing, moving-ridge, and mean-neutral patterns. Across synthetic post-nonlinear (PNL) and SDB benchmarks, QRCIT improves detection of structured alternatives, with empirical Type-I behavior evaluated across settings. Higher quantile resolution improves merged-SDB power at moderate sample sizes, while diagnostics restricted to known affected regions improve detection of localized dependence. On Sachs, METABRIC breast cancer, and CCLE/PLX4720, QRCIT provides competitive real-data performance and interpretable deformation patterns. These results support quantile-resolved conditional-law deformation as a practical framework for testing and diagnosing structured conditional dependence.

</details>


### [108] [Meta-Frieren: Beyond Single Level Online Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07169)
*Vahan Arsenyan,Arnak Dalalyan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: We study a two-stage online optimization problem: at each round, a meta-learner first selects an agent-environment pair, then the chosen agent interacts with the environment, and receives a reward. We introduce Meta-Frieren, a general framework for maximizing cumulative reward in this setting. Our analysis yields a simple UCB-style criterion for meta-level selection and recovers prior guarantees as special cases while extending them to broader settings. We instantiate Meta-Frieren with GP-UCB under Bayesian and frequentist assumptions. We also derive, to our knowledge, the first high-probability regret bound for Feel-Good Thompson Sampling, specialize it to finite and parametric function classes, and integrate it into Meta-Frieren. Experiments support our theoretical results and demonstrate that Meta-Frieren can be successfully applied to hyperparameter optimization across multiple model classes.

</details>


### [109] [Backbone-Informed Adaptation after EEG Pretraining: A Capacity-Controlled Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07192)
*Mingyang Jiang,Yamin Li,Fan Ma,Daniel Moyer,Hua Xu,Catie Chang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文比较"骨架对齐型(native)适配器"与逐token MLP对照在参数量匹配下的表现，发现native适配器无一致优势，二者相对优劣取决于预训练骨架、任务与下游训练协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG基础模型学到的表征融合了通道与时间信息，理应让下游适配器建模同样的关系；但这类骨架对齐设计是否优于其他候选适配器尚不清楚，且比较时需控制可训练参数量，以避免容量差异混淆结论。

Method: 固定预训练编码器，比较两种下游适配器：native适配器（结构与骨架一致地建模通道/时间关系）与逐token MLP对照（独立变换每个特征向量）；使二者可训练参数量近似匹配；在2个EEG基础模型、3类任务（情绪识别、睡眠分期、EEG事件分类）上评估。

Result: native适配器无一致优势。对一个骨架，两种设计的差异小于多次运行的波动；对另一个骨架，token-wise对照在情绪识别与EEG事件分类上更优；睡眠分期上，当同时训练处理EEG片段序列的组件时，native适配器的优势被削弱。

Conclusion: 骨架对齐型适配器未必更高效地利用给定容量；其与容量匹配的通用替代方案之间的相对优劣依赖骨架、任务与训练协议，因此应在此类对照基线（容量匹配）下评估骨架对齐适配器。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) foundation models learn representations that combine information across channels and time. This structure motivates adapters that model the same relationships during downstream learning. However, it is not clear whether such a design outperforms other candidate adapters. Moreover, in order to arrive at a fair comparison between candidate adapters, we also need to consider their trainable parameters. Here, we ask whether a backbone-informed (native) adapter makes better use of a given capacity by comparing it against a token-wise multilayer perceptron control that transforms each feature vector independently. We approximately match their trainable parameter counts and keep the pretrained encoder fixed. Across two EEG foundation models and tasks covering emotion recognition, sleep staging, and EEG event classification, the native adapter shows no consistent advantage. For one backbone, differences between the designs are small relative to variation across runs. For the other, the token-wise control performs better on emotion recognition and EEG event classification. On sleep staging, the native adapter's advantage over the token-wise control is reduced when we also train the component that processes sequences of EEG segments. The relative performance of the two adapters depends on the backbone, task, and downstream training protocol. These findings motivate evaluating backbone-informed adapters against capacity-matched alternatives.

</details>


### [110] [TrustMix: Graceful Degradation for Pretrained Models under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07206)
*Juliusz Ziomek,Maike Osborne,Yee Whye Teh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TrustMix 是一种轻量级后处理方法，通过将预训练模型的预测分布与用户指定的回退分布 $p_0(y|x)$ 混合，使模型在分布外（OOD）情形下的外推行为显式化且可控，并随 OOD 偏差统计量增大而渐进收敛到回退分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测超出训练域的样本本质上需要对真值外推方式作出假设，但现有神经网络大多仅通过架构隐式地承载这些假设，缺乏显式且可控的外推机制。

Method: 提出 TrustMix：将预训练模型的预测分布与用户给定或基于域无关平稳性假设的回退分布 $p_0(y|x)$ 结合，并以 OOD 偏差统计量为依据进行加权坍塌；无需重训练基模型，适用于回归与分类两类任务。

Result: 在表格、图像（CIFAR-10/100、ImageNet）与自然语言等多类合成及真实分布偏移上评测，涵盖 CNN 与 Transformer 等架构；TrustMix 在分布内保持竞争性，在分布偏移下改善了预测不确定性，回归的预测似然与分类的校准均有提升。

Conclusion: TrustMix 能提供平滑的性能退化，可作为决策应用中有用的预测分布来源（如作为贝叶斯优化的替代模型），是一种简单、通用、无需重训的 OOD 外推控制手段。

Abstract: Predicting beyond the training domain necessarily requires assumptions about how the ground truth extrapolates, yet in most neural networks these assumptions are made only implicit through the architecture. We propose TrustMix, a lightweight post-hoc method that makes a pretrained model's extrapolative behaviour explicit and controllable. TrustMix combines the predictive distribution produced by a pretrained model with a user-specified fallback distribution $p_0(y|x)$, such that as the out-of-distribution (OOD) deviation statistic grows, we collapse towards $p_0(y|x)$. TrustMix provides graceful degradation: the predictive distribution converges toward the fallback distribution as the OOD signal grows. The fallback distribution $p_0(y|x)$ can reflect domain knowledge, if available, or rely on a domain-agnostic stationarity assumption. TrustMix does not require retraining of the base model and can be applied to both regression and classification problems. We evaluated TrustMix on several synthetic and real-world distribution shifts, including tabular, image, and natural language processing tasks. We tested TrustMix on datasets such as CIFAR-10/100 and ImageNet and on numerous architectures including Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) and Transformers. TrustMix remains competitive in-distribution while improving predictive uncertainty under distribution shift, with gains in predictive likelihood for regression and calibration for classification. Finally, we showed that TrustMix can provide useful predictive distributions for decision-making applications by utilizing it as a surrogate for Bayesian Optimisation.

</details>


### [111] [Structured Machine Theory of Mind from Agent Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07208)
*Nigel F. Doering,Rahath Malladi,Arshia Sangwan,M Tanjid Hasan Tonmoy,Jacob Doan,David Danks,Tauhidur Rahman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出结构化机器心智理论框架SMToM及BDIBottleneck模型，在可控行人导航域中显式学习信念与欲望表征，实现可编辑、可反事实干预的轨迹预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模轨迹数据训练的预测模型只优化统计精度，不表征因果驱动行为的心理状态，因而只能预测而无法做有原则的干预（如回答"若信念或偏好改变，行为会如何变化"）。

Method: SMToM框架在BDI因果结构下从观测轨迹中归因出显式、独立监督的信念与欲望表征；核心是一个goal head，仅接收预测的心理状态通道与当前轨迹嵌入，从而使命望的反事实干预成为直接操作。在真值心理状态由构造已知的可控行人导航域中实例化BDIBottleneck，并评估归因准确性、局部通道干预及轨迹级反事实诊断。

Result: BDIBottleneck优于纯轨迹与无监督上下文神经基线；与匹配的辅助监督上下文基线相当，但额外提供后者所缺的可编辑心理状态接口。欲望反事实实验显示，替换推断偏好会使预测目的地一致性地向相关地点偏移；信念反事实实验显示，将某地点标记为不可用会降低其作为目的地的预测概率，但影响方式较复杂。

Conclusion: 在可控导航设定下，显式BDI结构监督是纵向轨迹数据可解释与反事实分析的可行基础。

Abstract: Predictive models of human behavior trained on large-scale trajectory data optimize for statistical accuracy without representing the mental states that causally generate behavior. Such models support prediction but not principled intervention: they cannot answer how an agent's behavior would change if its beliefs or preferences were different. We introduce Structured Machine Theory of Mind (SMToM), a framework that attributes explicit, independently supervised belief and desire representations from observed trajectories within a Belief-Desire-Intention (BDI) causal structure. The central architectural element is a goal head that consumes only the predicted mental-state channels and a current-trajectory embedding; counterfactual intervention on beliefs and desires is a direct operation. We instantiate SMToM on a controlled pedestrian navigation domain where ground-truth mental states are known by construction, enabling evaluation of attribution accuracy, local channel interventions, and trajectory-level counterfactual diagnostics. The resulting model, BDIBottleneck, improves over trajectory-only and unsupervised-context neural baselines. It is also competitive with a matched auxiliary-supervised context baseline while providing an editable mental-state interface that the unbottlenecked baseline lacks. Counterfactual experiments on desires confirm that substituting an agent's inferred preferences with a different activity type coherently shifts predicted destinations toward relevant locations. Counterfactual experiments on beliefs confirm that marking a location as unavailable in the agent's belief state reduces its predicted probability as a destination, though in a complex way. These results demonstrate, in a controlled navigation setting, that explicit BDI-structured supervision is a viable foundation for interpretable and counterfactual analysis of longitudinal trajectory data.

</details>


### [112] [Canonical Correlation Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07212)
*Andrea Cavallo,Sundeep Prabhakar Chepuri,Charilaos Kanatsoulis,Elvin Isufi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CAnonical correlation NEural networks (CANEs)，将视图内协方差与跨视图协方差分别作为关系型归纳偏置，用谱分析与CCA类比，并在下游任务中直接训练；理论上给出有限样本协方差扰动下的输出误差界，实验表明在脑影像与运动传感的七个数据集上匹配或优于单视图、双视图及基于CCA的基线，且参数更少、协方差重估时更稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网格、图等关系型归纳偏置能减少参数并提升泛化，但需预先已知结构；无结构时协方差矩阵可作为有原则的替代（VNN已证明其在PCA理论、有限样本稳定性与参数效率上的优势）。然而VNN仅限单视图，而许多应用通过多视图观测同一现象，每视图各有视图内协方差并有跨视图协方差连接二者，需将上述矩阵以有原则方式纳入归纳偏置。

Method: 提出CANEs架构：以视图内协方差与跨视图协方差作为两种不同的关系型偏置；通过谱分析建立其与典型相关分析（CCA）的类比；不同于CCA以计算共享视图表示为目标，CANEs直接训练以求解下游任务；推导在有限样本协方差扰动下的输出误差上界，该界依赖所学滤波器的可控制Lipschitz常数，而非CCA稳定性所依赖的奇异值间隔。

Result: 在来自脑影像与运动传感的七个数据集上，CANEs匹配或优于单视图、双视图及基于CCA的基线，且通常参数更少；在由更少样本重新估计协方差时仍保持性能。

Conclusion: CANEs将多视图协方差作为可分离的关系型归纳偏置并直接面向下游任务训练，其稳定性由可学习的Lipschitz常数而非奇异值间隔保证，因而比CCA类方法更稳健，兼具参数效率与估计误差鲁棒性。

Abstract: Relational inductive biases such as grids or graphs allow neural networks with fewer trainable parameters and improved generalization, but require a known structure beforehand. When no structure is available, the covariance matrix provides a principled substitute, and coVariance Neural Networks (VNNs) have shown that using it yields models that are theoretically grounded in Principal Component Analysis, stable to finite-sample estimation errors and parameter-efficient. This framework, however, is limited to single-view data, whereas many applications observe the same phenomenon through multiple views, each with its own in-view covariance and a cross-view covariance linking them. To incorporate these matrices as inductive biases in a principled way, we introduce CAnonical correlation NEural networks (CANEs), architectures that use in-view and cross-view covariances as distinct relational biases to solve downstream tasks. Via spectral analysis, CANEs draw analogies with Canonical Correlation Analysis (CCA), a classical technique to project two-view data onto a correlated subspace. However, while CCA's objective is to compute shared view representations, CANEs are trained to solve a downstream task directly. We bound their output error under finite-sample covariance perturbations and show that it depends on controllable Lipschitz constants of the learned filters in place of the singular-value gap that governs the stability of CCA, thus guaranteeing better robustness. Experiments on seven datasets from brain imaging and motion sensing show that CANEs match or outperform single-view, two-view and CCA-based baselines, often with fewer parameters, and retain their performance when covariances are re-estimated from fewer samples.

</details>


### [113] [Fractional physics-informed neural networks to model social media virality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07215)
*Alex Koong,George Em Karniadakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出分数阶物理信息神经网络（fPINN），同时学习社交媒体传播中的时间记忆（分数阶次）与非线性增长动力学，并用符号回归导出可解释方程；针对噪声下点式物理残差的尺度伪影提出全白化残差，并在X平台348条帖子上验证分段建模的优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有传播力模型要么预设参与度增长的函数形式，要么假定过去活动影响未来参与的固定机制，缺乏对时间记忆强度与增长律的联合、数据驱动推断。

Method: 构建分数阶PINN，联合估计分数阶导数的阶次与反应项（增长律）；用符号回归将学到的动力学转化为可解释方程；在24小时逐时观测分辨率上做合成实验检验可辨识性；发现噪声观测下标准点式物理残差会因尺度伪影把分数阶次推向下界，遂采用将观测噪声经分数算子传播的全白化残差；应用于X上348条帖子，并将24小时窗口切分为四段teacher-forcing片段进行分段估计。

Result: 干净数据下分数阶次与反应律可联合恢复；含噪时点式残差导致阶次偏向低界，白化残差可纠正。实际数据中单窗口模型估计出强记忆依赖（阶次低）但外推重建差；四分段的段阶次由0.23递增至0.99，各阶段增长律可解释；分段模型以MSE≤0.01重建64%的留出轨迹，优于九个经典基线中最强者（44%）。

Conclusion: 分数阶物理信息方程发现能够揭示可解释的参与度动力学，但噪声感知估计与时间分段对准确性与稳健性至关重要。

Abstract: Existing models of social media virality either prescribe the functional form of engagement growth or assume a fixed mechanism by which past activity influences future engagement. We present a fractional physics-informed neural network that learns both the temporal memory and nonlinear growth dynamics of social media engagement, using symbolic regression to translate the learned dynamics into interpretable equations. Synthetic experiments at the deployment resolution of 24 hourly observations demonstrate that the fractional order and reaction law can be jointly recovered from clean data. Under observation noise, however, the standard pointwise physics residual drives the fractional order toward its lower bound through a scale artifact. We address this failure using a fully whitened residual that propagates observation noise through the fractional operator. Applying the framework to 348 posts from X (formerly Twitter), we find that a single-window model estimates strongly memory-dependent dynamics but fails to accurately reconstruct held-out trajectories. Partitioning the 24-hour window into four teacher-forced segments substantially improves performance, yielding segment-specific fractional orders that increase from 0.23 to 0.99 and interpretable growth laws for each phase. The segmented model reconstructs 64% of held-out trajectories within a mean squared error of 0.01, compared with 44% for the strongest of nine classical baselines. Together, these results demonstrate the potential of fractional physics-informed equation discovery to uncover interpretable engagement dynamics while highlighting the importance of noise-aware estimation and temporal segmentation.

</details>


### [114] [Compositional Unalignment: Auditing Safety in Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07216)
*Hong kyu Lee,Kai Shu,Li Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出无训练的去对齐方法CMaG，通过拒绝意图梯度隔离跨模态交互项，证明组合式安全对齐在行为层面看似稳健实则脆弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有组合式安全基准依赖LLM裁判评分，只能反映观测行为，无法揭示安全对齐的内在建构稳健性；作者主张用"去对齐"（un-alignment）作为对齐稳健性的互补审计手段。

Method: 1) 先证明最小监督微调即可达到与多阶段RL流程相当的LLM裁判分数，说明同分不能证明稳健性；2) 发现组合式危害的"识别"与"拒绝"相互解耦，九种方向寻找方法可达AUC=0.99但消融方向后拒绝行为不变或模型连贯性崩塌；3) 提出CMaG：以拒绝vs.顺从的对数概率边际度量拒绝意图，对中层激活求梯度，并与部分良性样本对做对比以隔离跨模态交互项，单次反向传播（80个样本）即得可单调将拒绝转为顺从的方向。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-VL-7B与LLaVA-1.5-7B上，CMaG使组合式安全分数最多下降65分，同时>98%输出保持连贯，优于基于梯度的基线；所得方向可迁移至文本显式危害，并能去对齐经防御性训练加固的模型。

Conclusion: 组合式安全可在行为上表现强劲，却对针对性的内部干预高度脆弱；un-alignment可作为评估对齐稳健性的有效审计工具。

Abstract: Large vision-language models (LVLMs) are vulnerable to compositional harm: an image and a prompt that are benign in isolation become harmful through the model's cross-modal reasoning. Recent alignment methods report strong scores on compositional safety benchmarks, but an LLM judge only measures observed behavior and cannot reveal how robustly safety is integrated into the model. We argue that un-alignment provides a complementary audit of alignment robustness. We first show that minimal supervised fine-tuning reaches an LLM-judge score comparable to a multi-stage reinforcement-learning pipeline, so similar benchmark scores cannot establish robustness by themselves. We then show that recognition and refusal of compositional harm are decoupled: nine standard direction-finding methods detect compositional harm with up to AUC=0.99, yet ablating the recovered directions either leaves refusal intact or destroys model coherence. We therefore propose CMaG, a training-free un-alignment method for compositional safety. CMaG measures refusal intention with a refusal-versus-compliance log-probability margin, takes its gradient with respect to mid-layer activations, and contrasts it against partially benign pairs to isolate the cross-modal interaction term. A single backward pass over 80 examples yields a direction along which steering monotonically converts refusal into compliance. On Qwen2.5-VL-7B and LLaVA-1.5-7B, CMaG reduces compositional safety scores by up to 65 points while over 98% of outputs remain coherent, outperforming gradient-based baselines; the direction also transfers to text-explicit harm and un-aligns models hardened by defensive training. Compositional safety can thus appear behaviorally strong while remaining fragile to targeted internal intervention. Warning: this paper contains unsafe model outputs, including references to self-harm.

</details>


### [115] [TileQuant: Tile as the Precision-Allocation Unit for LLM Mixed-Precision Post-Training Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07217)
*Hanzhi Zhang,Qiao Zhang,Qinglei Cao,Heng Fan,Yan Huang,Kewei Sha,Yunhe Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TileQuant：面向硬件对齐二维权重分块的无训练混合精度PTQ框架，按块分配16/8-bit精度并配套高效内核，吞吐达原模型2.66倍且质量保持良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM部署成本高，PTQ可降低成本但需处理权重敏感度不均：统一PTQ全用同一权重量化位宽；重要性感知PTQ仅在该位宽内调scale或重建权重；列级混合精度仅保留整列高精度——而敏感度在列内也存在差异，整列保留会保护非敏感权重；运行时离群点检测又带来解码开销。

Method: 训练-free框架，将精度分配到硬件对齐的2D权重块（tile）。离线校准提供两类打分：基于前向统计的输入激活感知打分（标定成本低）与基于损失敏感度的梯度引导打分（质量保持更强）。在每个投影层的保留预算内，得分最高的块保持16-bit，其余为8-bit。生成的分块精度图引导权重重构进紧凑缓冲区，内核复用该缓冲区以原生整数与16-bit计算及本地累加，降低访存流量。

Result: 生成吞吐达原模型的2.66倍；在语言建模、推理、长上下文检索与问答等任务上保持质量，并在多个任务上超过量化模型乃至原模型。

Conclusion: 结果表明，选择能刻画局部敏感度的精度单元，同时兼顾高效硬件执行，是混合精度PTQ的有效方向。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) reduces large language model (LLM) deployment costs, but preserving quality requires accounting for uneven weight sensitivity. Uniform PTQ uses one weight bit-width, importance-aware PTQ adjusts scales or reconstructs weights within that width, and column-wise mixed precision retains selected columns at higher precision. However, sensitivity varies within columns, so whole-column retention also protects less sensitive weights, while runtime outlier detection adds decoding overhead. We therefore introduce TileQuant, a training-free framework that assigns precision to hardware-aligned 2D weight tiles. Offline calibration provides input-activation-aware scoring from forward statistics for lower calibration cost and gradient-informed scoring from loss sensitivity for stronger quality preservation. Within each projection's retention budget, the highest-scoring tiles remain at 16-bit and the rest use 8-bit. The resulting map guides reconstruction into compact weight buffers, which the kernel reuses for native integer and 16-bit computation with local accumulation to reduce memory traffic. Experiments show that TileQuant reaches 2.66x the original model's generation throughput. Across language modeling, reasoning, long-context retrieval, and question answering, it preserves quality and surpasses quantized and original models on multiple tasks. These results support selecting precision units that capture local sensitivity while enabling efficient hardware execution. The implementation is: https://github.com/HanzhiZhang-Ulrica/TileQuant

</details>


### [116] [Loopback Transformer: Towards a Unified View of Residual Looped Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07226)
*Xuan Ouyang,Zefan Cai,Chenlong Zhang,Cheng Luo,Ke Wan,Junjie Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Looped Transformer参数共享不决定重复计算结果如何复用；论文从深度-记忆视角拆解残差，提出只读"单个更新"并分离输入路径的Loopback Transformer，以几何衰减控制总更新权重、注意力学习分配，在191M–3B规模上以40–75%参数取得更低验证损失与更高下游均值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环Transformer通过重复应用共享块减少参数量，但参数共享本身并不能决定重复计算结果被如何复用。作者希望从残差的"深度-记忆"视角出发，明确每个块应用后究竟存储了哪种表示、后续读取如何对其加权，从而找出一套更可控的复用机制。

Method: 1）分析发现整块输出同时包含其输入与新增更新，对其加权会连带重新加权输入已携带的历史，控制耦合；2）改为读取"单个更新"，并将共享栈的输入保留在独立路径上，从而对输入强度、总更新权重、更新间分配三者分别控制；3）Loopback Transformer实现该设计：用几何衰减设定总更新权重并提供近因先验，用注意力学习权重分配。

Result: 在ClimbMix上，参考规模191M至3B，在训练token与执行深度匹配条件下，以基线标准Transformer 40–75%的参数（不含token embedding和输出头）取得更低验证损失；1.4B与3B下十三项下游任务平均准确率更高；在191M、374M、778M上，验证损失最低且十三任务均值最高，优于标准Transformer与七种循环残差设计。

Conclusion: 残差连接的读写语义是循环Transformer高效复用的关键：将"更新"与"输入路径"分离，并以几何衰减+注意力分配显式控制，可在显著更少参数下稳定优于标准Transformer及多种循环残差变体。

Abstract: Looped Transformers reduce parameter count by repeatedly applying shared blocks, but parameter sharing alone does not determine how the results of repeated computation are reused. We take the depth-memory view of residual connections and analyze which representation is stored after each block application and how later reads weight it. A full block output contains both its input and its new update, so weighting that output also reweights the history already carried by the input. Reading individual updates instead, with the shared stack's input kept on a separate path, gives separate controls over input strength, total update weight, and allocation across updates. The Loopback Transformer implements these controls: geometric decay sets the total update weight and supplies a recency prior, and attention learns how to allocate that weight. On ClimbMix, Loopback reaches lower validation loss than standard Transformer baselines with 40-75% of their Transformer parameters (excluding token embeddings and the output head) at reference scales from 191M to 3B, with training tokens and executed depth matched. It also has higher mean accuracy over thirteen downstream tasks at 1.4B and 3B. At 191M, 374M, and 778M, it has the lowest validation loss and the highest thirteen-task mean among the standard Transformer and seven looped residual designs.

</details>


### [117] [Neural Compression over Equivalence Classes: Exact Gauges and Functional Rate-Distortion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07233)
*Toshiaki Koike-Akino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 利用神经网络表示自由度（精确保函数变换与受控函数扰动），在原压缩器不变的前提下搜索更便宜表示，实现无损/近无损的检查点压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络同一或近似同一函数存在多种参数化，但部署通常只保存训练产出的那一组参数向量，这一表示自由度未被利用。作者希望将这种自由度本身作为一种压缩资源。

Method: 不改动编解码器本身，而是在精确保函数的规范（gauge）变换族与可控函数扰动上做搜索，并用未修改的物理压缩器作为选择准则来挑选代价更低的等价表示；精确gauge族提供零失真参考，允许小幅函数变化则释放更大的率失真收益。

Result: 在SmolLM2与Qwen3上，Zstandard压缩后检查点体积最高减少23.4%，在gzip、Brotli、ZipNN下取得相近的相对缩减。

Conclusion: 训练网络的表示自由度可作为实用的存储资源，且与现有压缩器正交、可叠加使用。

Abstract: Neural networks admit many parameterizations of the same or nearly the same function, but deployment typically stores whichever parameter vector training happens to produce. We show that this representational freedom can itself be used for compression. Rather than redesigning the codec, we search over exact function-preserving transformations and controlled functional perturbations, using the unchanged physical compressor itself to select cheaper representations. Exact gauge families provide a zero-distortion reference under the strict audit; allowing small functional changes exposes much larger rate-distortion gains. Across SmolLM2 and Qwen3, our method reduces Zstandard-compressed checkpoint size by up to 23.4\%, with similar relative reductions under gzip, Brotli, and ZipNN, establishing trained-network representational freedom as a practical storage resource.

</details>


### [118] [$D^2$-OPD: Separating Direction and Distance in Extrapolative On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07241)
*Bowen Fang,Dingmin Wang,Hengzhi Pei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 $D^2$-OPD，将外推式 on-policy 蒸馏（OPD）中的外推方向与外推距离解耦：用逐前缀硬 KL 约束限定目标相对教师分布的距离，用教师后训练偏移与初始学生-教师差距的自适应组合确定外推方向，并以 forward KL 优化学生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有外推式 OPD 方法将从参考策略到教师的 log-probability 偏移整体放大以构造更强的学习目标，但外推方向与距离相互耦合，导致教师偏移的幅度反过来决定目标移动的距离，缺乏可控性。

Method: 1) 逐前缀硬 KL 约束：限制蒸馏目标与教师分布之间的距离，使外推距离可独立控制；2) 自适应外推方向：由教师的后训练偏移与初始学生-教师差距共同决定；3) 以 forward KL 为目标优化学生，使低概率 token 也能获得学习信号。

Result: 在数学推理基准上，于三种设置（标准 OPD 表现良好、基础模型初始化下 OPD 表现欠佳、无教师参考可用）中均取得最高分，最高超过最强基线 9.45 分；消融实验验证了各组件的贡献。

Conclusion: 解耦外推方向与距离是提升外推式 on-policy 蒸馏稳定性与效果的有效设计，$D^2$-OPD 在多种初始化与监督条件（含无教师参考场景）下均优于现有基线。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student model to match a teacher's next-token distributions on student-generated prefixes. Extrapolative OPD further extends the teacher's log-probability shift relative to a reference policy to construct stronger learning targets. However, existing methods couple extrapolation direction and distance, allowing the magnitude of the teacher's shift to dictate how far the target moves. We propose $D^2$-OPD, decoupling these two factors: a per-prefix hard KL constraint bounds the target's distance from the teacher, while an adaptive combination of the teacher's post-training shift and the initial student--teacher gap determines the extrapolation direction. We optimize the student toward this target using forward KL, providing learning signals even for low-probability tokens. We evaluate three settings: where standard OPD performs well, where it struggles with base-model initialization, and where no teacher reference is available. On mathematical reasoning benchmarks, $D^2$-OPD attains the highest score in all three settings, outperforming the strongest baseline by up to 9.45 points. Ablations validate the contributions of each component.

</details>


### [119] [MAC-v2: Long-horizon model-based value expansion from pixels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07245)
*Kwanyoung Park,Sergey Levine*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 MAC-v2，通过无重建隐式世界模型与动作分块，使长时域模型价值展开在像素级离线 RL 中可行并取得强性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 像素级离线 RL 中做长时域模型价值展开比状态环境困难得多：需要同时学习表征与动力学，二者误差会在想象轨迹上递归累积。

Method: MAC-v2 训练无重建的隐式世界模型并结合动作分块（action chunking）；表征由预测与控制目标共同学习，避免编码与控制无关的视觉细节；动作分块减少价值展开所需的递归预测步数。

Result: 在视觉 OGBench、MMBench2、Robomimic、LIBERO-10 等基准上优于无模型与基于模型的离线 RL 基线；消融表明长时域价值展开、动作分块与隐式学习目标均不可或缺。

Conclusion: 长时域模型价值展开可作为像素级离线 RL 的有效框架，前提是采用无重建隐式表征与动作分块以抑制误差累积。

Abstract: We study whether \emph{long-horizon model-based value expansion} can serve as an effective framework in pixel-based offline reinforcement learning (RL). Long-horizon model-based value expansion from pixels is substantially harder than state-based environments, as the agent must learn both the representation and its dynamics, and errors from either can accumulate recursively over imagined trajectories. We introduce MAC-v2, which enables long-horizon model-based expansion from pixels by training a reconstruction-free latent world model with action chunking. By learning representations from prediction and control objectives, MAC-v2 does not need to encode control-irrelevant visual details that can hinder the dynamics prediction. Action chunking further mitigates the prediction error by reducing the number of recursive model predictions required for value expansion. Across diverse visual benchmarks including visual OGBench, MMBench2, Robomimic, and LIBERO-10, MAC-v2 achieves strong performance against model-free and model-based offline RL baselines. Our analyses show that long-horizon model-based value expansion is critical for the performance of MAC-v2, and that both action chunking and latent learning objectives are essential for reliable long-horizon latent imagination.

</details>


### [120] [KG-Neighbor: Knowledge-Guided Neighborhood Construction for Embedding Visualization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07246)
*Chang Won Kim,Jaewook Lee,Haeun Min,JunNyeong Jeong,Minwoo Sim,Barom Kang,Kyohoon Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出知识引导邻域图构建方法KGN，用KG三元组共享度修正嵌入距离以重建top-k邻域图，并引入IDProfile诊断残余错配，在八个数据集-编码器条件下NMI与LT_2D均超过kNN基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 嵌入可视化中邻近实例常被解读为语义相关，但由输入嵌入得到的邻域未必反映内容关系；基于邻域的降维方法会进一步传播这一偏差。因此需要在不使用类标签的前提下，使邻域构建更贴近内容支持的语义关系。

Method: KGN将每个实例的关系知识表示为知识图谱三元组集合，以三元组共享程度作为成对语义证据来调整嵌入距离并重建top-k邻域图，KG抽取与邻域构建均不使用类标签；同时提出IDProfile(IDP)，独立评估源内容是否支持所分配的类标签与邻域多数类，以解释残余错配。实验覆盖八个文本与图像数据集-编码器条件，扫描完整引导强度并比较四种KG相似度度量。

Result: 在全部八个条件下，扫描中的最大NMI与LT_2D均超过对应kNN基线，最大增益分别为0.68与1.35；增益与邻域重构幅度随条件变化，且在kNN基线分数较低的编码器条件下更大。IDP分析显示最大幅度下降集中于"Placement Issue"候选，而"Label Issue"与"Unresolved Case"实例在剩余复核集中占比更大。

Conclusion: KGN能够缩小嵌入邻域与内容支持语义关系之间在所评估范围内的差距；IDP表明残余错配不应被自动解释为投影失败。

Abstract: In embedding visualizations, nearby instances are interpreted as semantically related, although neighborhoods derived from input embeddings may not reflect content relationships. Neighborhood-based dimensionality reduction (DR) methods can propagate this limitation by optimizing layouts from embedding-based neighborhood graphs. We propose Knowledge-Guided Neighborhood Graph Construction (KGN), which represents relational knowledge derived from each instance as a set of knowledge graph (KG) triples. It uses the degree of triple sharing as pairwise semantic evidence to adjust embedding distances and reconstruct the top-k neighborhood graph; neither KG extraction nor neighborhood construction uses class labels. We also introduce IDProfile (IDP), which interprets residual mismatches by independently assessing whether the source content supports the assigned class label and the neighborhood-majority class. Across eight text and image dataset-encoder conditions, we characterize KGN over the full guidance-strength sweep and four KG similarity measures. Across this sweep, the maximum NMI and LT_2D each exceed the corresponding kNN baseline in all eight conditions, with the largest gains reaching 0.68 and 1.35, respectively. The gains and the extent of neighborhood reconfiguration vary across conditions and are larger under encoder conditions with lower kNN baseline scores. The IDP analysis shows that the largest reductions are concentrated in Placement Issue candidates, whereas Label Issue and Unresolved Case instances account for a larger share of the remaining review set. These results indicate that KGN can narrow the evaluated gap between embedding-based neighborhoods and content-supported semantic relations, while IDP shows that a residual mismatch should not automatically be interpreted as a projection failure.

</details>


### [121] [Using Transformers for Teaching and Learning via Symbolic Distillation of Neural A-life Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07254)
*Shuwei Wang,Vadim Bulitko,Matthew R. G. Brown,Michael Youngblood,Ramon Lawrence,Roscoe Smith,Shinichi Nakagawa,William Yeoh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出用预训练于随机感知-动作数据的Transformer替代遗传算法，将A-life智能体的神经网络策略蒸馏为紧凑符号策略，显著加速蒸馏并支撑种群层面的知识共享。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: A-life中演化出的神经网络虽能让智能体存活，但对人类不透明，且权重无法在不同神经架构间迁移。将其蒸馏为符号策略可提升可解释性并支持紧凑的知识共享与种群级学习；但符号策略空间巨大，通常依赖遗传算法等不完备随机搜索，蒸馏计算代价高昂。

Method: 预先在易于获取的随机感知-动作数据上预训练Transformer，用其加速符号策略搜索以逼近神经策略；Transformer架构本身通过神经架构搜索（NAS）获得。实验比较不同蒸馏方法，并考察符号空间对A-life种群动态的影响（通过自我反思、教与学机制）。

Result: 预训练Transformer的蒸馏方法相较其他蒸馏方法显著加快蒸馏速度；符号空间的选择会影响经由自我反思及教与学产生的种群动态。

Conclusion: 在随机感知-动作数据上预训练的Transformer是高效蒸馏神经策略为符号策略的可行途径，兼顾可解释性与跨架构知识共享，并可用于研究符号表示对A-life种群动力学的作用。

Abstract: Neural networks controlling behavior of A-life agents can evolve over multiple generations. While effective in making the agents survive, such networks are generally opaque to a human observer. Furthermore, the neural weights cannot be transferred between agents with different neural architectures. Distilling such neural networks into compact symbolic policies can help humans understand agent behavior in A-life. Additionally, it allows the agents to share their knowledge in a compact fashion, improving population-level learning. Distilling a neural policy into a symbolic policy is typically performed by searching the space of symbolic policies to find one that approximates the neural policy. However, even with a domain-specific language the space of policies can be large, necessitating incomplete stochastic search such as genetic algorithms. Yet the distillation remains computationally expensive. We propose to instead use a transformer pre-trained on readily available random percept-action data. Doing so speeds up distillation substantially. We derive the transformer architecture via a neural architecture search. Empirical results compare different distillation methods and the influence of the symbolic space on A-life population dynamics through self-reflection and teaching and learning.

</details>


### [122] [TriAttention: Unified Sparse Mixed-Precision Attention for Long-Context LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07256)
*Hanzhi Zhang,Qiao Zhang,Qinglei Cao,Heng Fan,Yan Huang,Kewei Sha,Yunhe Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TriAttention 将注意力计算中的 QK tile 动态分配为跳过、4-bit 或 8-bit 三种处理路径，用两条对角距离边界紧凑编码每个 head 的计算区域，并通过离线校准与全模型预算分配器选出边界，配合融合 kernel 与压缩 KV cache，实现最高 7.88x（相对 Torch）和 2.65x（相对 BF16）的端到端加速且质量基本无损。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏化与量化虽能降低注意力成本，但二者被独立处理：不同交互对精度与保留的需求不同——部分交互可容忍低精度分数却对删除敏感。现有方法未能区分这种"低精度可容忍但不可跳过"的交互，导致要么浪费计算、要么损失质量。

Method: 1) 三档策略：把 QK tile 分派到跳过、4-bit、8-bit 三类计算，用 4-bit 以更低代价保留量化容忍型交互；2) 紧凑表示：用两条对角距离边界刻画每个 head 的计算区域，使策略存储与上下文长度无关；3) 离线校准：以归一化投影输出误差与完整 tile 执行成本评估候选边界对；4) 全模型分配器：在共享计算预算下为每个 head 选择最小化校准误差的边界；5) 系统实现：prefill 阶段按精度分组遍历，融合 kernel 跳过省略 tile 并将两种 QK 精度贡献合并为一次归一化注意力输出；prefill 中量化的 K/V 复用于压缩 KV cache 供解码，减少 prompt 数据重复读取。

Result: 端到端生成加速最高达 Torch attention 的 7.88x、BF16 基线的 2.65x；在通用任务与长上下文任务上质量与原模型相当。

Conclusion: 将"保留与否"和"保留精度"统一为一个紧凑的 head 级策略，可把局部敏感度有效转化为注意力计算量与重复 KV-cache 访问的节省，验证了稀疏—量化联合设计的可行性与收益。

Abstract: Sparsity and quantization reduce the cost of attention by skipping interactions and lowering the precision of those retained. However, useful interactions differ in their precision requirements: some tolerate low-precision scores while remaining sensitive to removal. We therefore introduce TriAttention, which assigns query-key (QK) tiles to skipping, 4-bit, or 8-bit computation, using 4-bit to retain quantization-tolerant interactions at lower cost. To represent compactly, we encode each head's computation regions with two diagonal-distance boundaries, keeping policy storage independent of context length. Offline calibration then evaluates candidate boundary pairs using normalized projected-output errors and complete-tile execution costs. Using these measurements, a model-wide allocator selects each head's boundaries to minimize calibration error under a shared computation budget. During prefill, the selected boundaries generate precision-grouped traversals for a fused kernel that skips omitted tiles and combines contributions from both QK precisions into one normalized attention output. The quantized keys and values prepared during prefill are then reused in a compressed key-value (KV) cache for decoding, reducing repeated reads of prompt data. Experiments show that TriAttention achieves end-to-end generation speedups of up to 7.88x over the Torch attention and 2.65x over the BF16 baseline. Meanwhile, TriAttention maintains quality comparable to the original models on general-purpose and long-context tasks. These results show how a compact retention-and-precision policy translates local sensitivities into attention computation and repeated KV-cache access. The implementation is https://github.com/HanzhiZhang-Ulrica/TriAttention.

</details>


### [123] [Evolutionary Adaptive Neighborhood Learning: A Genetic Algorithm for Instance-Dependent k Optimization in Nearest Neighbor Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07257)
*Isaac Touza,Kaladzavi Guidedi,Kolyang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出EANK方法，用低维策略网络将局部邻域描述子映射为自适应近邻数k(x)，并以实数编码精英遗传算法优化，在理论性质与12个公开数据集实验上验证其相对调优全局-k的竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: k-NN的k是核心超参数，通常由交叉验证或进化搜索全局选取；但偏差-方差权衡本质上是局部的：稠密无噪声区域适合小邻域，稀疏或噪声区域需要大邻域。全局-k优化仅一维，用遗传算法替代穷举几乎无计算优势，因此应优化实例相关的邻域策略而非单一k。

Method: EANK使用低维策略将局部邻域描述子（密度、标签熵、投票边际、集中度）映射为自适应k(x)；采用实数编码精英遗传算法，以留一法精度加邻域平滑惩罚为适应度；预计算邻居投票以降低适应度评估开销。

Result: 理论分析给出在训练准则上对全局-k解的支配性、进化收敛性、策略正则性与泛化保证；在具空间异质结构的合成数据和12个公开数据集上评估预测性能、鲁棒性与计算开销。嵌套交叉验证下EANK相对调优全局-k基线具竞争力，并优于默认k=5；同时分析了训练-测试性能差距以评估自适应邻域选择的实用价值。

Conclusion: 以进化算法优化实例级邻域策略是可行且有理论支撑的k-NN改进方向，性能与调优全局-k相当并优于默认k=5，但自适应带来的训练-测试差距提示其实际收益需结合泛化差距综合评判。

Abstract: The number of neighbours k is the central hyperparameter of the k-nearest neighbour (k-NN) classifier and is usually selected globally through cross-validation or evolutionary search. However, the bias-variance trade-off is inherently local: dense and noise-free regions may favour small neighbourhoods, whereas sparse or noisy regions may require larger ones. Since global-k optimization is one-dimensional, replacing exhaustive search with a genetic algorithm offers little computational advantage. We therefore optimize an instance-dependent neighbourhood policy instead. We propose EANK (Evolutionary Adaptive Neighborhood K), which uses a low-dimensional policy to map local neighbourhood descriptors, including density, label entropy, vote margin, and concentration, to an adaptive number of neighbours k(x). A real-coded elitist genetic algorithm optimizes this policy using leave-one-out accuracy with a neighbourhood-smoothness penalty. Precomputed neighbour votes reduce fitness evaluation costs. We investigate theoretical properties of the method, including dominance over global-k solutions on the training criterion, evolutionary convergence, policy regularity, and generalization guarantees. Experiments on synthetic data with spatially heterogeneous structure and twelve public datasets evaluate predictive performance, robustness, and computational cost. Under nested cross-validation, EANK achieves competitive performance relative to tuned global-k baselines and improves upon the default k=5. We also examine the gap between training and test performance to assess the practical value of adaptive neighbourhood selection.

</details>


### [124] [Reweighting is not Harmless: Understanding the Role of Dataset Characteristics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07258)
*Amalia Ionescu,Jose G. Hernandez,Emily F. Wong,Anil K. Saini,Jason H. Moore,Tiffani J. Bright*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 重加权（reweighting）这一常用公平性预处理方法可在某些数据条件下制造“虚假的公平改善”：它通过压低高基率组的阳性判定、抬高其FNR来换取组间差距缩小，且总体准确率几乎不变，因而在聚合指标中不可见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重加权常被认为能缓解对代表性不足群体的系统性低估，但其收益依赖数据条件，可能带来未被察觉的伤害。作者希望系统考察群体规模与基线事件率如何塑造重加权的实际影响，并检验"公平性提升"是否真实。

Method: 采用受控模拟：变化群体规模与基线事件发生率（事件率7%–14%），在3,000个模拟数据集上施加重加权预处理，并跨六种分类器（从逻辑回归到神经网络）比较处理前后的组间阳性分类率差距与FNR差距、各组阳性分类数与FNR、以及总体准确率；进一步将FNR差距的改善分解为"高基率组FNR上升"与"另一组FNR下降"两部分贡献。

Result: 重加权确实改善了阳性分类率与FNR的组间均衡，但部分源于对高基线事件率组的损害：其阳性判定减少、FNR上升。当该组为少数群体时效应最强——FNR升高约6个百分点（约每万名组员多82例漏判），且约四分之三的FNR均衡改善来自该组FNR上升而非另一组获益。该模式在六种分类器下稳健复现，总体准确率基本不变，变化在聚合指标中不可见。

Conclusion: 重加权对公平性的影响高度依赖数据特征，可能以牺牲一组为代价换取组间均衡，且该代价被总体性能掩盖。实践者应结合具体问题域与目标审慎评估；若目标是降低某组被低估程度而不在另一组引入新低估，重加权可能并非合适手段。

Abstract: Reweighting is a widely used pre-processing method for promoting discrimination-free model behavior by assigning different weights to data points during model training. While it is often credited with improving fairness, particularly by mitigating systematic underestimation for underrepresented groups, it can also introduce unintended harms under certain data conditions. Using controlled simulations that vary group size and baseline event prevalence, we show how reweighting can yield misleading improvements in fairness by reducing underestimation for one group while introducing it for another. Across 3,000 simulated datasets with event prevalence of 7-14%, reweighting improved parity in positive classification and false negative rates (FNR) between groups, but did so partly by decreasing positive classifications and increasing FNR for the group with higher baseline prevalence. These effects were most pronounced when that group was a minority: its FNR rose by 6 percentage points, approximately 82 additional missed cases per 10,000 group members, and roughly three-quarters of the improvement in FNR parity came from this increase rather than from gains for the other group. The pattern held across six classifiers, from logistic regression to neural networks, while overall accuracy was essentially unchanged, leaving the shift invisible in aggregate performance metrics. These findings mirror patterns reported in real-world applications and underscore the critical role of data characteristics in shaping the impact of this fairness intervention. While reweighting may create desirable outcomes in some applications, practitioners should critically evaluate it relative to their problem area and goals. Specifically, when the goal is to reduce underestimation in one group without introducing underestimation in another, reweighting may not be the appropriate approach.

</details>


### [125] [Energy Measurement and Optimization of TinyML in Resource-Constrained Embedded Systems: A Systematic Mapping Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07264)
*Clariele Almeida,Renan Beserra,Rafael Moura,Danilo Araújo,Bruno Nogueira,Sérgio Chevtchenko,Ermeson Andrade*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 对能量感知TinyML研究进行系统性映射综述，梳理模型与学习范式、能耗评估方法与指标、影响因素及优化技术分类体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IoT边缘设备数据激增且受内存、算力与能量严重约束，TinyML可将ML任务部署于微控制器等资源受限平台；但现有文献在评估算法、能耗测量方法、报告指标、影响因素与优化策略方面高度异质，缺乏统一认识。

Method: 系统性映射研究（systematic mapping），识别主要模型与学习范式，对能耗评估方法与指标进行分类，分析影响能耗的技术因素，并将优化技术归纳为结构化分类体系。

Result: 卷积与轻量级架构使用频繁；测量手段以直接测量与解析估算为主；能耗影响因素涉及模型架构、硬件、通信、数值精度与输入特性；优化策略归为模型压缩、硬件感知设计、系统与通信优化、数据与处理优化四类。同时发现实验平台、测量流程与报告规范缺乏标准化，制约可复现性与跨研究可比性。

Conclusion: 该映射提供了领域的最新概览，并为开发与评估更节能的TinyML方案提供指导，同时指出标准化建设是提升可复现性与可比性的关键方向。

Abstract: The rapid expansion of the Internet of Things (IoT) has increased the volume of data generated at the network edge, where many devices operate under severe memory, processing, and energy constraints. In this context, Tiny Machine Learning (TinyML) enables machine learning (ML) tasks to be executed on microcontrollers, smart sensors, and other resource-constrained platforms. However, the literature remains heterogeneous regarding the algorithms evaluated, energy-measurement methods, reported metrics, influencing factors, and optimization strategies. This study presents a systematic mapping of energy-aware TinyML research. It identifies the main models and learning approaches, classifies energy-evaluation methodologies and metrics, examines the technical factors affecting energy consumption, and organizes the reported optimization techniques into a structured taxonomy. The findings highlight the frequent use of convolutional and lightweight architectures, direct measurements and analytical estimations, and factors related to model architecture, hardware, communication, numerical precision, and input characteristics. The optimization strategies were grouped into model compression, hardware-aware design, system and communication optimization, and data and processing optimization. The results also reveal limited standardization in experimental platforms, measurement procedures, and reporting practices, limiting reproducibility and direct comparison across studies. This mapping provides an updated overview of the field and guidance for developing and evaluating more energy-efficient TinyML solutions.

</details>


### [126] [How Deep is the Signal? Time-Context up to 30 Years for Colorectal Cancer Prediction in a Primary-Care EHR Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07265)
*Achille Raviez,Lan Wang,Brendan Delaney*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出"时间上下文"(time-context)概念，首次系统评估EHR基础模型的十年尺度历史回看窗口，发现结直肠癌预测性能随回看窗口延长在18年处饱和，约80%增益在10年内获得。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EHR基础模型仅以token数衡量上下文长度，而"模型需回看多久才能捕获最早预测信号"未知；时间尺度上的上下文与token上下文是不同的设计维度，需单独量化。

Method: 在西北伦敦360万初级保健患者14亿编码事件上预训练34.6M参数decoder-only Transformer；以12个月诊断前缓冲期微调预测结直肠癌发病。冻结队列含1008例病例与9582名匹配对照（均注册≥30年），在2–30年共15个嵌套回看窗口下做同一患者内比较截断效果；并加入XGBoost基线及RoPE位置编码仿真。

Result: AUROC从2年0.551升至18年0.590后趋于平台，约80%增益在10年内实现；XGBoost收敛至相近水平（峰值0.589）。RoPE在默认基频下对经过时间的编码在约20年后出现混叠，需重新调整θ。

Conclusion: 时间上下文是可量化、区别于token上下文的临床预测模型设计轴，应在模型报告中明确披露；十年量级的回看窗口已能捕获大部分可利用信号，更长窗口收益有限。

Abstract: EHR foundation models read medical events within a fixed context window, and long context has so far been measured only in tokens. How far back in time a model must look to capture the earliest predictive signal is unknown. We introduce time-context, the calendar lookback window before the prediction point, and provide, to our knowledge, the first systematic evaluation of decade-scale time-context for an EHR foundation model. We pretrained a decoder-only transformer (34.6M parameters) on 1.4B coded events from 3.6M primary-care patients in North West London and fine-tuned it to predict incident colorectal cancer under a 12-month pre-diagnostic buffer. A frozen cohort of 1,008 cases and 9,582 matched controls, all registered for at least 30 years, was evaluated at 15 nested lookback windows from 2 to 30 years, so that every truncation is compared within the same patients. Discrimination rose from an AUROC of 0.551 at 2 years to 0.590 at 18 years, then plateaued, with about 80% of the gain reached within 10 years; an XGBoost baseline converged to the same level (peak AUROC 0.589). A simulation further suggests that rotary position embeddings (RoPE) over elapsed time alias beyond about 20 years at the default base frequency, motivating a retuned $θ$. Time-context is a measurable design axis for clinical prediction models, distinct from token-context, and should be reported.

</details>


### [127] [Satisficing as competitive Bayesian weighting: a minimal model of goal-directed choice under an unattained target](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07270)
*Moto Shinriki,Yu Kono,Tatsuji Takahashi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文将风险敏感满意化（RS）规则改写为类贝叶斯估计器（ACB），论证"未达成的目标"以约束形式与有限注意资源共同塑造选择，产生无数据丢弃的"伪遗忘"与持续的探索行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动物与人类常不最大化，而是相对于尚未达到的抱负水平（目标）行动。作者希望回答"未实现的目标如何组织选择"，并检验Deacon (2012) 的"ententional"（缺席指涉的组织）与García-Valdecasas & Deacon (2024) 把目的理解为约束而非特定状态的观点。

Method: 在最简形式化设定中考察风险敏感满意化（RS）——一种将每个动作估值设为其过去试验份额乘以相对抱负水平的盈余或亏空的赌博机学习规则；并将其重写为类贝叶斯估计器"注意竞争贝叶斯"（ACB），其中抱负水平为所有动作的先验均值，有限试验预算决定证据能多强地覆盖先验；再以伯努利赌博机仿真检验抱负水平依证据调整的效果。

Result: ACB下不再被采样的动作其估计向抱负水平松弛，而非被过去观测覆盖，且不丢弃任何数据（"伪遗忘"），区别于含显式遗忘率的模型；当抱负不可达到时探索不会收敛；仿真中依据证据调整抱负水平降低了后悔（期望损失）。

Conclusion: ACB提供了一个可解析的模型，其中未达成的目标与有限的注意资源共同约束行为，并就生物体如何重估其已放弃的选项给出可检验的预测。

Abstract: Animals and humans often do not maximize; they act relative to goals (aspiration levels) that are frequently not yet met. How can a goal that is not (yet) realized organize choice? Deacon (2012) calls such absence-referenced organization \emph{ententional}, and García-Valdecasas and Deacon (2024) argue that ends are best understood as constraints rather than as particular states. We examine this idea in a minimal formal setting: Risk-sensitive Satisficing (RS), a bandit learning rule that values each action by its share of past trials multiplied by its surplus or shortfall relative to an aspiration level. We show that RS can be rewritten as a Bayesian-like estimator, Attention Competitive Bayes (ACB), in which the aspiration level is the prior mean of every action and the finite budget of trials sets how strongly evidence can override it. Two consequences follow. First, actions that are no longer sampled are partially overridden by the goal rather than by past observations: their estimates relax toward the aspiration level without any data being discarded. This ``pseudo-forgetting'' distinguishes ACB from models with an explicit forgetting rate. Second, when the aspiration is unattainable, exploration does not settle; in Bernoulli bandit simulations, adjusting the aspiration level from evidence reduced regret (expected loss). ACB offers an analyzable model in which an unattained goal and a limited attentional resource jointly constrain behavior, and it yields testable predictions about how organisms revalue options they have abandoned.

</details>


### [128] [Graph Neural Networks for Resource Allocation in Integrated Sensing and Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07277)
*Junyu Liao,Lili Chen,Tian Han,Jamie Evans*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GNN-ISAC：一种基于图神经网络的功率分配算法，用于车载通感一体化网络中在感知SINR约束下最大化通信和速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车载ISAC网络中，每辆服务提供商车辆需在有限功率预算下同时服务多个感知目标与通信接收端；在感知SINR约束下最大化通信和速率是非凸问题，传统迭代优化需针对每种网络布局重新求解，计算代价高。

Method: 构建含两类边的图表示：一类刻画提供商间干扰，另一类刻画提供商内功率预算共享；用感知惩罚的和速率损失进行无标签训练；同时通过引入"感知功率预留"步骤，将WMMSE算法适配到该问题作为对比基准。

Result: 仿真表明GNN-ISAC的通信和速率接近WMMSE，感知链路约束违反率低且计算量显著降低；性能优于现有异构GNN与信道盲基线，并可在不重训练的情况下泛化到更大规模网络。

Conclusion: GNN-ISAC以无监督学习方式高效求解非凸功率分配问题，兼顾通信性能与感知约束，具备良好的计算效率与规模泛化能力，适合动态车载网络布局下的实时部署。

Abstract: In vehicular integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) networks, each service-provider vehicle uses a limited power budget to serve multiple sensing targets and communication receivers. Maximizing the communication sum-rate subject to sensing signal-to-interference-plus-noise ratio (SINR) constraints is nonconvex, and iterative optimization is costly to rerun for every network layout. This paper proposes a graph neural network (GNN)-based power allocation algorithm, termed GNN-ISAC. Concretely, GNN-ISAC addresses the aforementioned problem by considering two distinct edge types in the graph representation, one capturing the inter-provider interference, the other representing the intra-provider power-budget sharing. GNN-ISAC is trained without labels using a sensing-penalized sum-rate loss. We also adapt the weighted mean-square error minimization (WMMSE) algorithm to this problem by introducing a sensing-power reservation step. Simulation results show that GNN-ISAC achieves communication sum-rates close to WMMSE with low sensing-link violation rates and much less computation. It also outperforms an existing heterogeneous GNN and a channel-blind baseline, and generalizes to larger networks without retraining.

</details>


### [129] [Scenario Augmentation for Rare Financial Events via Multimodal Latent Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07282)
*Mahdi Arab Loodaricheh,Anita Raja,Indranil SenGupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多模态隐空间生成增强框架：用三个变分自编码器分别编码市场结构化变量、空间图像变换和组合收益序列，再用类条件扩散模型学习其联合隐分布，并以CVaR定义稀有窗口、引导生成少数类样本，从而提升稀有金融窗口的表征与分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极端市场行情稀缺、异质，历史数据高度不平衡，难以学习；同时不同金融表征各有互补信息。作者希望验证生成式增强能否在保留多表征互补结构的前提下，改善以CVaR标注的稀有金融窗口的表征与分类。

Method: 将每个市场窗口表示为三种对齐模态（结构化市场变量、空间图像变换、组合收益序列）；各模态经独立VAE映射为紧凑隐码；类条件扩散模型学习其联合隐分布；以CVaR作为风险感知的稀有窗口定义，并将生成导向少数类机制。

Result: 在美国科技股四个窗口长度上，合成样本在保真度和支持度上优于表格扩散基线；用真实与生成样本训练的XGBoost分类器AUC达0.953–0.960，超过表格生成器及纯真实数据基线。

Conclusion: 多模态隐空间生成可为数据稀缺的金融风险模型提供有用的合成压力情景，验证了生成式增强在稀有极端事件建模中的价值。

Abstract: Extreme market movements are scarce, heterogeneous, and difficult to learn from imbalanced historical data. We study whether generative augmentation can improve the representation and classification of CVaR-labeled rare financial windows while retaining the complementary structure exposed by different financial representations. Each market window is expressed through three aligned modalities: structured market variables, a spatial image transformation, and a sequence of portfolio returns. Separate variational autoencoders map the modalities to compact latent codes, and a class-conditional diffusion model learns their joint latent distribution. Conditional Value-at-Risk (CVaR) provides a risk-aware definition of rare windows and directs generation toward the minority regime. Across four window lengths on U.S. technology equities, the resulting synthetic samples improve fidelity and support relative to tabular diffusion baselines. An XGBoost classifier trained with real and generated samples attains AUCs of 0.953 - 0.960, exceeding both tabular generators and the corresponding real-only baselines. These results show that multimodal latent generation can provide useful synthetic stress scenarios for data-scarce financial risk models.

</details>


### [130] [How do Transformers Perform Low-Level Control In-Context?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07291)
*Ebonye Smith,Aidan Andrews,Phoenix Wilson,Alexis Frias,Ayush Pandey,Gireeja Ranade*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Transformer模型能够仅凭上下文轨迹实现对未知参数非线性不稳定动力系统的“上下文”控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 借助Transformer的上下文学习能力，探索在仅知系统所属族类与系统演化数据、而不知具体参数信息时，能否对系统进行控制。

Method: 采用多头、GPT-2风格架构；上下文仅含无受控系统动力学；在cartpole与two-link acrobot两个经典非线性系统上评估，系统参数对Transformer未知；训练时可获得专家控制器轨迹但推理时不可用；并识别训练低层控制所必需的关键训练技巧。

Result: 通过检查点分析表明上下文控制可行，学习策略性能通常随上下文长度增加而提升；模型还能泛化以稳定分布外系统。

Conclusion: Transformer可在无显式参数信息下实现不稳定非线性系统的上下文控制，并具备对分布外系统的稳定泛化能力。

Abstract: One of the remarkable abilities of Transformer-based neural networks is in-context learning. This motivates us to think about "in-context" control. Can we control a system knowing only (1) that it belongs to a particular family of systems and (2) some data on system evolution (provided as the context trajectory) but no specific parameter information? Specifically, we investigate the ability of Transformer-models to perform "in-context" control of unstable, nonlinear dynamical systems, where context consists only of the unforced system dynamics. We leverage a multi-head, GPT-2-style architecture, and we evaluate performance for two classical nonlinear systems -- the cartpole system and the two-link acrobot system, where the system parameters are unknown to the Transformer. We consider a setting where trajectories from an expert controller are available at training time but not at inference time. Here, we identify training techniques that are essential to train Transformers to perform low-level control. Through an analysis over checkpoints, we show that in-context control is possible, and the learned policy performance generally improves as context increases. Furthermore, the Transformer models can generalize to stabilize systems that are out-of-distribution.

</details>


### [131] [Provably Efficient Representation Learning for Low-Rank CMDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07295)
*Kaixuan Liu,Guojun Xiong,Shengpu Tang,Wanyun Si,Jian Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出首个面向低秩CMDP的无模型表示学习算法MFRLC，结合LSVI-UCB与原始-对偶方法，获得与状态空间规模无关的Õ(K^{3/4})奖励遗憾与约束违反界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低秩约束马尔可夫决策过程（CMDP）中，价值与奖励函数由未知表示向量近似、转移动力学具低秩分解，需在满足期望累积效用约束下最大化累积奖励；现有方法缺乏对未知特征的表示学习与可证明的次线性保证。

Method: 提出无模型算法MFRLC：采用原始-对偶框架平衡奖励遗憾与约束违反；将LSVI-UCB与原始-对偶技术结合；在价值函数估计中引入函数近似奖励(bonus)；直接学习表示到价值函数的映射，避免显式估计转移核。

Result: 以高概率在K轮部署上取得Õ(K^{3/4})的奖励遗憾与累积约束违反，二者均与状态空间大小无关；这是未知特征CMDP下首个次线性遗憾与违反保证。

Conclusion: MFRLC为低秩CMDP表示学习提供了首个具理论保证的无模型算法，证明利用低秩结构与表示学习可在未知特征下实现可扩展的约束强化学习。

Abstract: We study representation learning in low-rank Constrained Markov Decision Processes (CMDPs), where both value and reward functions are approximated by a set of unknown representation vectors, and the transition dynamics admit a low-rank factorization. The objective is to maximize expected cumulative rewards while complying with constraints on the expected cumulative utility. To achieve this, we propose \texttt{MFRLC} (Model-Free Representation Learning for Low-Rank CMDP), a model-free algorithm that uses a primal-dual scheme to effectively balance reward regret and constraint violations. To the best of our knowledge, \texttt{MFRLC} is the first representation-learning method for low-rank CMDPs that combines the Least-Squares Value Iteration with Upper Confidence Bound (LSVI-UCB) and primal-dual techniques. \texttt{MFRLC} further enhances value function estimation with bonuses for function approximation and learns the mapping from representations to value functions directly, avoiding explicit estimation of the transition kernel. We prove that, with high probability, \texttt{MFRLC} achieves $\widetilde O(K^{3/4})$ reward regret and cumulative constraint violation over $K$ deployment rounds, independent of the size of the state space. To our knowledge, these are the first sublinear regret and violation guarantees for CMDPs with unknown features.

</details>


### [132] [WeightLoop: Weight-Space Programming with Experimental Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07319)
*Mengran Li,Bo Li,Weijing Zhao,Chengyang Zhang,Boyu Zhang,Jinfeng Xu,Zelin Zang,Victor C. M. Leung,Stan Z. Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: WeightLoop提出一种将实验反馈转化为部署模型持久参数更新的框架：大模型编写可复用的参数编辑程序，在预测器演化过程中保持编辑器固定，实现无需重新调用LLM或梯度训练的反复更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 权重空间编程（WSP）表明语言模型能够推理并改写神经网络权重，递归自我改进（RSI）强调反馈驱动的持续提升；而实际导引实验的模型可获得具体反馈（已完成测定揭示预测误差并提示下一步测试内容），但如何将这些实验证据转化为对已部署预测器的稳定改进尚未解决。

Method: 在离线回放中，由GPT-6 Astra检查任务规范与模型实现，编写有界、可复用的参数编辑程序；随新测量数据到达，程序识别预测偏差、利用观测结果验证候选编辑，并将选定修改写回原始模型。编辑器保持固定、预测器持续演化，无需额外LLM调用或梯度重训练，且保留原始推理接口。

Result: 在八种端点预算设置中的七种里，定向编辑提升了平均排序效用（相对随机编辑则在全部八种设置中均有提升）；并在三个顺序任务上提升了平均最终发现量。

Conclusion: WeightLoop将权重空间编程与受RSI启发的改进循环相结合，使实验证据成为模型变化及其后续决策的直接驱动因素。

Abstract: Weight-space programming (WSP) shows that language models can reason about and rewrite neural network weights; recursive self-improvement (RSI) motivates feedback-driven improvement. Models guiding experiments receive concrete feedback: completed assays reveal prediction errors and inform what to test next. We introduce WeightLoop, a framework that turns experimental feedback into persistent updates to deployed predictors. GPT-6 Astra inspects a task specification and the model implementation, then writes a bounded, reusable parameter-editing program. As new measurements arrive, the program identifies prediction discrepancies, validates candidate edits using observed outcomes, and writes the selected changes back to the original model. The editor remains fixed while the predictor evolves, enabling repeated updates without further LLM calls or gradient retraining while preserving the original inference interface. In offline replay, targeted edits improve mean ranking utility in seven of eight endpoint-budget settings (all eight versus random edits) and mean final discovery across three sequential tasks. WeightLoop connects WSP with an RSI-inspired improvement loop, making experimental evidence a direct driver of model change and subsequent decisions.

</details>


### [133] [Rethinking Data Selection: The Importance of Coverage over Difficulty in LLM Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07323)
*Lalchand Pandia,Kanishka Misra,Allyson Ettinger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 基于难度的数据选择在LLM微调中往往不如随机选择，原因在于随机选择的覆盖性更好；本文提出基于聚类的覆盖优先选择方法，在单领域任务上达到或超过随机选择，在多领域Tülu 3混合数据上优于所有对比方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据选择可降低LLM微调的计算成本。已有工作多基于"更难的样本信息量更大"的直觉设计难度分数，但这类方法在小型分类模型上有效，在LLM微调中却常常无法超过随机选择。作者希望解释这一现象并给出更有效的选择准则。

Method: 分析随机选择优于难度选择的原因，指其优势来自对输入空间更充分的覆盖（代表性更好）；据此实现一个简单的基于聚类的选择方法，选取更贴合底层输入分布的数据子集。

Result: 在多个多样化的单领域任务上，该聚类方法性能达到或超过随机选择；在多领域Tülu 3混合数据上（随机采样不再保证子集均衡），该方法优于所有对比方法。

Conclusion: 对LLM微调的数据选择而言，优先考虑覆盖性而非难度能带来更有效的选择效果。

Abstract: Selecting high-quality training data can reduce computation cost for LLM fine-tuning. Prior work on data selection developed a variety of difficulty-based scores, motivated by the intuition that harder examples are more informative. While effective for smaller classification models, we find that such methods often fail to outperform random selection for LLM fine-tuning. Our analysis reveals that random selection performs better because it is more representative of the underlying input space---i.e., has better coverage. To validate the importance of coverage, we implement a simple clustering-based selection method which selects data that is more representative of the underlying input distribution. On a set of diverse single-domain tasks, this method yields performance at or above that of random selection. On the diverse, multi-domain Tülu 3 mixture, where random sampling no longer guarantees a balanced subset, our method outperforms all methods. Overall, our results indicate that prioritizing coverage over difficulty leads to more effective data selection for LLM fine-tuning.

</details>


### [134] [Permanent and Transient Representations for Continual Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07327)
*Nishanth Anand,Doina Precup*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出互补学习系统的系统级分解：长期学习用参数化表示做泛化，瞬态学习用非参数化表示做情境适配，并给出新的非参数化瞬态价值函数近似方法，在图像任务与Craftax-Classic上实现在线学习并超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续强化学习(CRL)存在稳定性-可塑性权衡：智能体难以在保留旧知识的同时适应新情境。虽已有Anand & Precup (2023)用永久与瞬态价值函数实例化互补学习系统，但两系统共享相同特征，未区分表示类型。作者认为长期学习应更多依赖参数化表示以获得广范泛化，瞬态学习更适合非参数化表示以做情境特定适配。

Method: 从理论与实验两方面探究上述假设；针对复杂任务中的瞬态价值函数估计，引入一种新的非参数化近似方法，与慢速长期(参数化)系统构成互补的两系统架构。

Result: 所提方法支持在线学习，并在基于图像的任务和Craftax-Classic上优于竞争性基线。

Conclusion: 结果印证了按表示类型进行系统级分解的有效性：参数化/非参数化互补分工可缓解稳定性-可塑性权衡，提升CRL的在线适应能力。

Abstract: Continual Reinforcement Learning (CRL) agents struggle to adapt to new situations while retaining prior knowledge, leading to a stability-plasticity trade-off. One approach to solving this problem is by using complementary learning systems: one for slow, long-term learning and another for quick, transient adaptation. Anand & Precup (2023) instantiated this idea using permanent and transient value functions, but used the same features in both systems. Intuitively, long-term learning should rely more on parametric representations for broad generalization, while transient learning would be best served by non-parametric representations for situation-specific adaptation. In this paper, we explore this idea both theoretically and empirically. By introducing a novel non-parametric approximation for estimating the transient value function in complex tasks, we demonstrate that our method enables online learning and outperforms competitive baselines on image-based tasks and Craftax-Classic, underscoring the effectiveness of our system-level decomposition.

</details>


### [135] [AtlasULP: Domain-aware Universal Link Prediction via Relation Atlas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07347)
*Yujing Liu,Yixin Liu,Yu Zheng,Lianhua Chi,Alan Wee-Chung Liew,Heng Tao Shen,Shirui Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AtlasULP框架：用关系图谱(relation atlas)从全局与局部刻画节点对结构关联，并配合领域引导提示实现免训练的通用链路预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统链路预测按数据集单独训练，成本高；现有通用链路预测(ULP)依赖子图采样，因高阶邻域指数增长而无法捕获高阶信息，在长程信号关键的图（如生物网络）上表现不佳，且预测模型领域无关、泛化有限。

Method: 引入关系图谱(relation atlas)，从全局和局部两个视角捕捉节点对间的结构关联，形成跨领域可复用的连接性表征；在此基础上构建领域引导提示(domain-guided prompting)的ULP模型，由上下文结构生成领域感知的提示token，并以提示引导的上下文内(in-context)推理完成自适应链路预测。

Result: 在多种图领域的广泛实验中，AtlasULP 一致优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 以关系图谱替代子图采样可更有效表征高阶/长程连接信号，结合领域自适应提示，可显著提升通用链路预测的跨领域泛化能力。

Abstract: Link prediction (LP) is a widely applied task in graph learning. Conventional LP approaches typically follow a dataset-specific paradigm, requiring independent training for each graph and incurring high computational and maintenance costs in large-scale applications. Motivated by these limitations, recent work explores Universal Link Prediction (ULP), aiming to enable training-free inference on arbitrary unseen graphs. However, existing methods primarily rely on subgraph sampling strategies to construct universal representations, which are unable to capture higher-order information due to the exponential growth of higher-order neighborhoods. As a result, they often under-perform on graphs where long-range signals are essential, such as biological networks. Moreover, their prediction models are typically domain-agnostic and lack the ability to adapt to different graph domains, leading to limited generalization. To address these challenges, we propose an Atlas-based Universal Link Prediction framework (AtlasULP), which introduces the relation atlas to capture structural associations between node pairs from both global and local perspectives, enabling a comprehensive characterization of connectivity signals across diverse graph domains. Building upon this representation, we develop a domain-guided prompting ULP model, which generates domain-aware prompt tokens from contextual structures and performs prompt-guided in-context prediction for adaptive link inference. Extensive experiments demonstrate that AtlasULP consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods across diverse graph domains.

</details>


### [136] [Acoustic Anomaly Detection for Early Identification of Drill Jamming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07356)
*Maria Beatriz Silva,Lucas Edson Silva de Araújo,Sergio F. Chevtchenko,Ermeson Andrade,Danilo Araujo,Saeed Afshar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出基于超声波声学传感的钻头卡滞故障预兆检测系统，发布17支钻头的时/频域声学特征数据集，并以留一钻头交叉验证比较半监督自编码器（AE）正常性建模与监督XGBoost分类的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业4.0背景下预测性维护对减少非计划停机与保障运行安全至关重要；在机加工中钻头卡滞是关键失效模式，会同时损害工件质量与刀具完整性，因此需要可靠的早期故障识别手段。

Method: 采用超声波传感器采集CNC加工声学信号，提取时域与频域特征并公开数据集；以半监督自编码器进行正常状态建模（基于重构误差的异常检测），与监督式XGBoost分类器对比，采用留一钻头交叉验证（Leave-One-Drill-Out）评估跨刀具泛化能力，并进行阈值无关分析（AUC）。

Result: AE在安全性上表现突出，Recall达1.00，综合F1为0.81，综合AUC为0.8294；监督XGBoost在跨刀具协变量偏移下综合AUC仅0.7559，平均F1为0.55，且对部分钻头未能检出故障。

Conclusion: 超声波频谱为识别初期机械失稳提供了可靠窗口；AE模型通过在测试钻头上预先捕获全部卡滞事件，提供了稳健的安全包络，在半监督范式下优于受跨刀具协变量偏移影响的监督模型。

Abstract: In the context of Industry 4.0, predictive maintenance is essential to minimize unplanned downtime and ensure operational safety. In machining processes, drill jamming is a critical failure that compromises both workpiece quality and tool integrity. This work proposes an acoustic anomaly detection system using ultrasonic sensors to identify pre-failure patterns in Computer Numerical Control (CNC) machining. We release a public dataset of time- and frequency-domain acoustic features from 17 drills, and benchmark semi-supervised autoencoder-based normality modelling against supervised XGBoost classification using Leave-One-Drill-Out validation. The results demonstrate that the Autoencoder is highly effective for industrial safety, achieving a Recall of 1.00 and a consolidated F1-score of 0.81. Furthermore, the threshold-independent analysis shows that the Autoencoder better separates normal and pre-failure acoustic patterns, achieving a consolidated AUC of 0.8294, compared with 0.7559 for the supervised model under inter-tool covariate shift. In contrast, the supervised XGBoost achieved an average F1-score of 0.55 and failed to detect failures in some of the evaluated drills.
