The findings highlight that the ultrasonic spectrum provides a reliable window for identifying incipient mechanical instabilities, and the AE model offers a robust safety envelope by anticipating all jamming events across the tested drill bits.

</details>


### [137] [Test-Time Self-Play: Scaling without a Verifier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07367)
*Akira Yoshiyama,Akmal Ashirmatov,Jonas Hübotter,Ido Hakimi,Andreas Krause*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Test-Time Self-Play (TTSP)，一种不依赖验证器（verifier）的非对称自博弈测试时扩展方法，在 LiveCodeBench v6 难度校准子集上于各种验证器强度下均表现最优，无验证器时 mean@8 绝对提升 35%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流测试时扩展方法依赖验证器对候选解评分以做选择或精炼，但在许多部署场景中，目标问题缺乏强验证器。作者希望构建一种在无验证器（或验证器很弱）条件下仍能有效利用额外推理算力的测试时扩展方案。

Method: 提出 Test-Time Self-Play (TTSP)：基于非对称自博弈（asymmetric self-play）实现测试时扩展，在部署阶段为困难目标问题投入额外算力，而不依赖验证器对候选解的正确性打分。实验在 LiveCodeBench v6 的难度校准子集上进行，并与依赖验证器的基线方法在不同验证器强度下比较（含无验证器极限情形）。

Result: 在所评估的每一种验证器强度下，TTSP 均为表现最佳的方法；且验证器越弱，相对最强基线的优势越大。在无验证器极限下，TTSP 使 mean@8 绝对提升 35%。增益在较宽的验证器强度范围内成立，但存在一个例外：当基础策略对正确答案的覆盖率（coverage）接近零时，增益不再出现。

Conclusion: 测试时扩展不必依赖强验证器；非对称自博弈可在验证器缺失或很弱时提供更稳健的收益，且优势随验证器退化而扩大。该方法的有效性受限于基础策略的覆盖率：当基础策略几乎无法生成正确解时，自博弈式扩展难以奏效。

Abstract: Test-time scaling methods spend additional compute at deployment to improve a large language model's performance on a hard target problem. Most methods rely on a verifier that scores candidate solutions for correctness, using it to select among or refine the model's outputs. In many deployment regimes, however, a strong verifier on the target problem is unavailable. We introduce Test-Time Self-Play (TTSP), an approach to test-time scaling using asymmetric self-play without reliance on a verifier. On a difficulty-calibrated subset of LiveCodeBench v6, TTSP is the best-performing method at every verifier strength we evaluate, and the gap to the strongest baseline widens as the verifier weakens; in the no-verifier limit, TTSP yields a 35\% absolute improvement in mean@8. The gains hold across a range of verifier strengths, except when the base policy has near-zero coverage of the correct answer.

</details>


### [138] [When Scores Conflict with Preferences: Pre-Alignment Diagnosis and Adaptive Correction of Constraint Drift in DPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07375)
*Ping Liu,Yan Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文揭示并刻画了DPO在成对偏好与逐点约束冲突时的"约束漂移"失效模式，并基于闭式诊断量ρ*_eq提出冲突感知DPO（CoDPO），实现更优的漂移-偏好权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DPO越来越多地用于成对偏好与逐点准则（长度、正确性、安全分数等）并存场景；当二者不一致时，标准DPO对冲突对与匹配对一视同仁，缺乏对潜在漂移的识别与干预手段。

Method: 形式化刻画"约束漂移"：小更新下冲突对倾向于增大约束代价、匹配对倾向于修复，净变化取决于二者平衡；由此导出可由数据集统计计算的闭式预对齐诊断量ρ*_eq，判断数据是否自纠正。据此提出CoDPO，用ρ*_eq确定强度对冲突对降权（免调参），并加入自适应margin偏移以缓解训练中的残余漂移。

Result: 在冲突主导领域，漂移随冲突暴露单调增长；ρ*_eq给出的干预判断在每个领域均与实测漂移一致（含被判定为自纠正的领域）；主对比四个领域上CoDPO降低漂移且基本保持原DPO的偏好学习能力；相比硬过滤及近期约束/多目标基线，取得更优的漂移-偏好权衡。

Conclusion: 冲突对有系统性危害且可通过数据统计量事前预判；以诊断量驱动的降权加自适应margin是缓解约束漂移的有效且低成本的方案。

Abstract: Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) is increasingly used in settings where pairwise preferences coexist with pointwise criteria such as response length, answer correctness, or safety scores. When the two disagree, standard DPO treats pairs whose preferred output scores worse on the pointwise criterion (conflict pairs) in the same way as the remaining (matching) pairs. We characterize a systematic failure mode, constraint drift: for sufficiently small updates, conflict pairs tend to increase constraint cost while matching pairs tend to repair it, and the net change depends on their balance. This balance yields a closed-form pre-alignment diagnostic, $ρ^{*}_{\mathrm{eq}}$, computable from dataset statistics, that indicates whether a dataset is self-correcting or requires intervention. Building on this diagnostic, we propose Conflict-aware DPO (CoDPO), which down-weights conflict pairs with a strength set from $ρ^{*}_{\mathrm{eq}}$ rather than tuned, and adds an adaptive margin shift that addresses residual drift during training. Empirically, drift grows monotonically with conflict exposure on the conflict-dominated domains, and the intervention decision given by $ρ^{*}_{\mathrm{eq}}$ matches the observed drift on every domain, including one that it identifies as self-correcting. On all four domains of the main comparison, CoDPO reduces drift while largely preserving the preference learning of vanilla DPO. Compared with hard filtering and recent constrained and multi-objective baselines, CoDPO also achieves a more favorable drift-preference trade-off.

</details>


### [139] [Adversarial Multiplicity: Cherry-Picked Models and the Limits of Disclosure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07378)
*Cassandra C. Chou,Benjamin Q. Huynh,Allison Koenecke,Elizabeth T. Chin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 预测多重性可被恶意方利用：软操纵（调超参/特征工程）与硬操纵（直接改参数）均能在几乎不损失性能、通过黑箱或白箱审计的情况下反转单条预测乃至聚合效应估计，需以预注册、训练证明、敏感性分析等缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测多重性（性能相近的模型在个体预测上存在分歧）本可用于在不损失预测性能的前提下优化公平性等次要指标，但这也给有动机的当事方（如诉讼、消费纠纷中的自动化决策使用者，或“博弈”目标的编码智能体）提供了把预测导向自身利益的操纵空间。论文旨在刻画这种对抗性多重性场景的风险边界，并评估现有监管/披露政策能否拦住此类操纵。

Method: 提出软操纵与硬操纵的二分框架：软操纵在无害的模型选择空间（超参数调优、特征工程）内优化，硬操纵直接扰动模型参数。分别评估二者在仅黑箱审计与需完整代码与数据的白箱审计下的可检测性，并对聚合效应估计（气候变化对野火的影响）做操纵实验；同时用 Reconcile 算法检验模型分歧的消解情况，并映射各类操纵对应可蒙混过关的监管与披露政策。

Result: 足够灵活的模型可被操纵以反转任一个体预测且性能变化极小，并能通过黑箱审计，说明硬操纵在多数监管环境下可行；软操纵效应较小，但可通过白箱审计。聚合估计同样可被操纵——气候变化对野火影响的方向可被硬编辑与软搜索完全反转。Reconcile 通常能解决多数模型分歧，但当对手模型被对抗性选择而非随机选取时，结果更偏向操纵者。

Conclusion: 对抗性多重性构成实质性的决策操纵风险，现有监管与披露机制不足。建议的缓解措施包括：模型预注册、训练过程证明、敏感性分析、多指标评估，以及承诺使用版本化的公开模型；并给出各类操纵可蒙混过关的政策图谱。

Abstract: Predictive multiplicity -- where similarly accurate models disagree on individual predictions -- can be leveraged to optimize secondary metrics (e.g., fairness) without loss in predictive performance. However, exploiting multiplicity opens the opportunity for motivated parties (or coding agents gaming their objectives) to steer predictions toward their own ends, such as in litigation or consumer disputes with automated decision-making tools. In these adversarial multiplicity settings, we distinguish soft manipulation, where parties optimize over innocuous model choices (e.g., hyperparameter tuning, feature engineering), from hard manipulation, where parties directly perturb model parameters. We find a sufficiently flexible model can be manipulated to reverse any individual prediction with minimal change in predictive performance while passing black-box audits, making hard manipulation viable in most regulatory settings. Soft manipulation has smaller effects but can pass white-box audits requiring full access to code and data. Even aggregate estimates can be manipulated: a model's estimated effect of climate change on wildfires can be fully reversed by hard edits and soft search. We find the Reconcile algorithm typically resolves most disagreements between models, but more often in favor of the manipulator when the opposing model is chosen adversarially compared to at random. We map which manipulations can plausibly pass existing regulations and disclosure policies, and recommend mitigations such as pre-registration, proof of model training, sensitivity analyses, multi-metric evaluations, and committing to versioned public models.

</details>


### [140] [Spectral symmetry fingerprints in equivariant representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07410)
*Jiacheng Cen,Anyi Li,Ning Lin,Zihe Wang,Ji-Rong Wen,Wenbing Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出谱对称指纹（SymFP），利用等变表示的奇异值谱作为结构对称性的统一指纹，实现无参数、方向不变的点群识别与连续对称性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在几何深度学习中，对称性对等变表示施加秩约束，限制了等变图神经网络的表达能力。作者反过来利用这一约束，将其作为刻画对称性的信息来源。

Method: 提取等变表示的奇异值谱构成SymFP：通过秩模板匹配进行离散点群识别，通过量化目标秩约束之外的谱能量分析连续对称性；方法方向不变、与架构无关，可实现无参数GNN，谱可跨多个目标群复用，泄漏分数满足子群单调性。

Result: 在合成结构、分子、配位配合物及大型生物组装体上，SymFP准确识别点群，追踪受控对称破缺，并揭示被全局几何度量掩盖的局部与按度数分辨的对称性变化。

Conclusion: 等变表示的谱结构可作为跨物理系统刻画对称性的通用框架。

Abstract: Symmetry governs the organization of physical structures, while symmetry breaking reveals their transformations. In geometric deep learning, symmetry imposes rank constraints on equivariant representations, limiting the expressive capacity of equivariant graph neural networks (GNNs). Here we show that these constraints can instead be exploited: the singular-value spectra of equivariant representations provide a unified fingerprint of structural symmetry. We introduce spectral symmetry fingerprints (SymFP), which enable discrete point-group identification through rank-template matching and continuous symmetry analysis by quantifying spectral energy beyond target rank constraints. SymFP is orientation invariant and architecture agnostic, enabling a parameter-free GNN implementation. Its spectra can be reused across multiple target groups, and the resulting leakage scores satisfy subgroup monotonicity. Across synthetic structures, molecules, coordination complexes and large biological assemblies, SymFP accurately identifies point groups, tracks controlled symmetry breaking and reveals local and degree-resolved symmetry changes that can be obscured by global geometric measures. These results establish the spectral structure of equivariant representations as a general framework for characterizing symmetry across physical systems.

</details>


### [141] [IMUDINO: Self-distilled Foundation Model for Inertial Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07437)
*Zhoutong Ye,Chengwen Zhang,Zixuan Wang,Xufeng Jian,Zhe He,Qingyang Wan,Xutong Wang,Jiaqi Liu,Mingze Sun,Yu Mei,Chun Yu,Yuanchun Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出IMUDINO——首个跨任务通用的惯性传感基础模型，将DINO自监督范式通过语义保持的运动变换迁移到IMU数据，配合频率感知位置编码的Transformer，在HAR、健康、动作捕捉与里程计上取得竞争力表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有惯性基础模型多局限于单一子领域（如仅HAR），部分性能甚至不及SOTA监督方法；且通常要求固定输入长度与采样率，对未见传感器佩戴位置的泛化能力差，与视觉、语言的通用基础模型差距明显。

Method: 将DINO自监督学习范式适配到惯性数据：设计一组语义保持的运动变换，模拟运动模式变化与传感器配置变化；架构采用纯Transformer编码器加频率感知位置编码，原生支持变长输入与变采样率。

Result: 简单方案即显著有效地学到全局与稠密特征，可跨用户、传感器配置、任务泛化；在机器人里程计任务上还能跨本体（embodiment）泛化，并在HAR等任务上达到与SOTA监督方法相当或更优的竞争力水平。

Conclusion: IMUDINO可作为惯性传感的通用骨干网络，为通用惯性基础模型的发展奠定起点。

Abstract: We present IMUDINO, the first foundation model (FM) for inertial sensing to achieve competitive performance across diverse sensing tasks including human activity recognition (HAR), health, motion capture, and odometry. Prior inertial FMs often focus on one subfield of inertial sensing and sometimes underperform state-of-the-art supervised methods (e.g., in HAR), making their scope considerably narrower than FMs for vision and natural language. Moreover, they often require fixed input length and sampling rate, and generalize poorly to unseen sensor locations. To build a general-purpose foundation model for inertial sensing, we adapt the DINO self-supervised learning (SSL) paradigm to inertial data through a set of semantics-preserving motion transformations that simulate variations in motion pattern and sensor setup. For model architecture, we use a plain transformer encoder with frequency-aware positional encoding that natively supports varying input length and sampling rate. This simple setup proved remarkably effective for learning global and dense features generalizable across users, sensor setups, tasks, and, in the case of robot odometry, embodiments. We hope IMUDINO can serve as a backbone for inertial sensing and kickstart the development of general-purpose inertial FMs.

</details>


### [142] [Two-stage residual learning for calibrated uncertainty in solar irradiance forecasts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07444)
*RR Flanagan,E Kasha,PW Talbot,AS Epiny,MR Deinert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种两阶段方法将预测误差与期望辐照度分开建模：首个LSTM预测期望GHI，第二个网络建模误差分布并与均值相加得到概率预测；在NSRDB数据上用PIT评估，预测标准差与历史值的偏差降低34.6%（单独LSTM）至56.5%（LSTM+GPR）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光伏出力预测不仅需要点预测，还需长时程下可靠的不确定性估计。机器学习模型通常只输出单值，对不确定性刻画不佳；常规做法是直接让模型预测假定分布的参数，但本文希望由合成太阳能信号生成获得启发，将预测误差与期望辐照度解耦建模。

Method: 两阶段框架：第一阶段用LSTM预测期望全局水平辐照度（GHI）；第二阶段用第二个网络建模预测误差的分布，将其加回均值，从而同时给出预测值与不确定性。使用NSRDB历史气象数据，采用概率积分变换（PIT）进行评估，并与dropout类方法及GPR耦合配置对比。

Result: 显式误差建模使预测标准差显著贴近历史值：单独LSTM下偏差降低34.6%，LSTM与高斯过程回归（GPR）耦合（测试中最可靠配置）下降低56.5%。

Conclusion: 由于直接建模总预测误差，该方法捕捉的是决策所需的完整预测不确定性，而非dropout方法仅估计的模型贡献部分；显式误差建模是提升太阳能概率预测可靠性的有效途径。

Abstract: Accurate forecasting of renewable generation requires not only point predictions but also reliable estimates of the uncertainty attached to them, particularly at extended horizons. Machine learning models are increasingly used to predict solar output, but they typically report a single value and capture the associated uncertainty poorly. The standard remedy is to train a model to predict the parameters of an assumed probability distribution directly. Here we develop an alternative two-stage method, motivated by synthetic solar signal generation, in which the forecast error is modeled separately from the expected irradiance. A first long short-term memory (LSTM) network predicts the expected global horizontal irradiance, and a second network models the distribution of the forecast error, which is added back to the mean to give both the forecast and its uncertainty. Using historical weather data from the National Solar Radiation Database and a probability integral transform evaluation, we show that explicitly modeling the error brings the predicted standard deviation markedly closer to the historical value, reducing the deviation between the two by 34.6 percent for a standalone LSTM and by 56.5 percent when the LSTM is coupled to Gaussian process regression, the most reliable of the configurations tested. Because the method models the total forecast error directly, it captures the full predictive uncertainty that matters for decisions, rather than only the model contribution that dropout-based approaches estimate.

</details>


### [143] [Echoes of Error: Residual-Directional Local Rollout Consistency for Timeseries Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07473)
*Zheng Wang,Kaixuan Zhang,Xiaonan Lu,Wanfang Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出“局部 rollout 一致性”概念，用一阶局部替代（惩罚沿真实反馈扰动的雅可比-向量积）来约束自生成误差回灌对未来预测的影响，并从预测流形的法丛几何角度给出稳定性管道理论条件，实验表明该正则提升预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时序预测中，rollout 误差并非仅仅是一步拟合偏差：误差一旦反馈回演化状态，会扰动未来预测（“误差回声”）。作者希望在训练中直接抑制这种自生成误差对未来预测的可见性，但直接跟踪残差诱导的下一状态扰动需要设计残差到状态的对齐算子，在输入状态与输出块维度不同、成分异构或布局依赖架构时难以实现。

Method: 1) 形式化“局部 rollout 一致性”：比较 teacher forcing 下与自由运行下的下一步预测。2) 因残差-状态对齐算子难设计，改用一阶局部替代：惩罚预测器沿实际反馈扰动方向的雅可比-向量积（JVP），精准抑制自生成误差对下一预测可见的方向。3) 给出几何解释：在预测流形附近，通过控制残差诱导的、探测法丛的方向，导出自由运行轨迹保持在流形周围“稳定性管道”内的理论条件。

Result: 在时间序列预测任务上的实验支持该观点，所提正则化方法提升了预测精度；代码开源。

Conclusion: 论文提出一条时序预测器与序列预测器的训练原则：模型不仅要使一步误差小，还要学会不让自身误差回响到未来；所提一阶 JVP 正则与稳定性管道理论为这一原则提供了可操作且可解释的实现。

Abstract: In time-series forecasting, rollout errors are not merely one-step fitting discrepancies: once fed back into the evolving state, they can perturb future predictions. We formalize this effect through local rollout consistency, which compares the next prediction produced under teacher forcing with that produced under free running. In principle, this consistency can be enforced directly by tracking the residual-induced perturbation of the next state. In practice, however, the residual-to-state alignment operator is often difficult to design when the input state and output block have different dimensions, heterogeneous components, or architecture-dependent layouts. We therefore use a first-order local surrogate that penalizes the Jacobian-vector product of the predictor along the actual feedback perturbation, suppressing precisely the directions through which self-generated errors remain visible to future predictions. We further give the same loss a geometric interpretation near a predictive manifold: by controlling residual-induced directions that probe the normal bundle, the objective yields theoretical conditions under which free-running trajectories remain inside a stability tube around the manifold. Experiments on time series forecasting tasks support this view, showing that the proposed regularization improves predictive accuracy. More broadly, our results suggest a training principle for time-series forecasters and sequential predictors: a model should not only make small one-step errors, but also learn not to let its own errors echo into the future. Code is available at https://github.com/Wangzzzzzzzz/Echoes-Of-Error.

</details>


### [144] [Adversarial Robustness of Kernel Machines: A Comprehensive Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07474)
*Rahul Kumar Yadav,Tunir Ghosh,Sudhendu Ahir,Parthe Pandit,Arjun Bhagoji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统实证研究了核机器（含随机特征分类器与递归特征机）在二分类与多分类任务上对抗白盒/黑盒逃逸攻击的鲁棒性，并提出一种核机器近似对抗训练算法；结果表明核机器原生鲁棒性强于神经网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代核机器对对抗样本的鲁棒性此前缺乏系统性研究，作者希望填补这一空白，厘清核方法与神经网络在对抗鲁棒性上的差异。

Method: 在二分类与多分类任务上，通过白盒与黑盒逃逸攻击，评估三类模型的原生鲁棒性：标准核机器、随机特征分类器、递归特征机（Recursive Feature Machines）；并给出一种核机器近似对抗训练算法；实验覆盖图像与表格数据集。

Result: 实验一致表明核机器的原生对抗鲁棒性优于神经网络，即面对对抗扰动时其性能退化更为平缓（下降更 gracefully），且该结论在图像与表格数据上均成立。

Conclusion: 核机器相较神经网络具有更强的内在鲁棒性；近似对抗训练可进一步提升其鲁棒性，提示核方法在鲁棒机器学习中具有研究与应用价值。

Abstract: Kernel machines are an important class of models in machine learning. However, the question of how robust modern kernel machines are to adversarial examples has not been studied comprehensively. In this paper, we provide a thorough empirical investigation of the adversarial robustness of kernel-based models for both binary and multiclass classification tasks, via white-box and black-box evasion attacks. We evaluate the native robustness of kernel machines, random feature classifiers, and the relatively newer Recursive Feature Machines. In addition, we provide an algorithm for approximate adversarial training of kernel machines. Our experiments on classification tasks on image and tabular datasets strongly indicate that kernel machines are natively more robust than neural networks, i.e., their performance degrades more gracefully against adversarial perturbations.

</details>


### [145] [Localization Does Not Imply Steerability: A Mechanistic Study of Sign Errors in LLM Mathematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07478)
*Shradha Agarwal,Tariq J.,Deepak Rajbhar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究定位大模型多步数学中"符号错误"在网络中的写入位置：跨6个开源模型（14B–675B），错误负号由网络最后四分之一的MLP层晚期写入；因果干预（减去符号方向）可在Llama-3.3-70B翻转14个留出错误中的13个，并具表征特异性；但Phi-4虽同样可定位，却因符号冗余分布于约15层而几乎不可被翻转，说明"可定位性≠可操纵性"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型做多步数学时存在典型失败模式：中途写错一个符号token（如余子式符号、分部积分递推的负号），后续算术正确但答案错误。作者试图回答：这个错误符号究竟在网络内部何处被写入，以及该写入是否可被因果干预所纠正。

Method: 逐层readout定位（per-layer readout）识别符号提交的层与模块；跨6个模型、14B–675B参数比较；在Qwen基座模型（指令微调前）验证该模式是否先于对齐出现；机制分析区分"固定减号印章层"、"已选符号放大器"与Qwen的"下游救援层"；因果实验通过在残差流中减去单一符号方向进行干预，并在Llama-3.3-70B上留出14个负号错误做翻转测试，复制到Qwen、Gemma、Mistral-Small；设置表征特异性对照（同剂量对正确负号答案的破坏、对正确正号答案的保留，0/72 on Llama）、随机方向与对照层干预作为null对照；核心结论预注册（git中冻结通过标准），29项检查中23项通过并报告全部未达标项。

Result: 错误符号在晚期（网络后25%的MLP层）被提交，各模型与任务中占70–100%；基座模型已存在该模式（Qwen）。机制因模型而异：固定"减号印章"、符号放大器、以及Qwen的下游救援层。因果性成立：减去单一符号方向在Llama-3.3-70B翻转13/14留出错误，在Qwen、Gemma、Mistral-Small各单元复制率24–70%；特异性明显（Llama上0/72误伤正确正号答案），随机方向与对照层干预无效。但Phi-4在同一晚期深度实现同等良好定位的回路，同样干预几乎不能翻转：符号冗余分布于约15层，裕度数倍更大（中位17.8 logits）。

Conclusion: 符号错误的写入位置可被精确定位且可因果干预，但可定位性不蕴含可操纵性：两个模型可共享同一回路位置，却在可翻转程度上差异巨大。冗余分布与写入裕度是决定转向难易的关键因素。

Abstract: When large language models do multi-step mathematics, a characteristic failure recurs: the model writes one wrong sign token mid-computation -- a cofactor sign, the minus of a recursive integration-by-parts step -- then executes the rest of the arithmetic correctly, and the answer comes out wrong. We ask where inside the network that sign is written. Across six open models spanning 14B to 675B parameters, a per-layer readout shows the wrong sign is committed late -- by MLP layers in the last quarter of the network (70-100% per model and task), a pattern present, where tested (Qwen), in the base model before instruction tuning. The mechanism varies: some models carry fixed "minus-stamp" layers that push a minus regardless of the right answer, others carry amplifiers of whichever sign was already chosen, and Qwen adds a downstream rescue layer that opposes the stamp. The circuit is causal: subtracting a single sign direction from the residual stream flips 13 of 14 held-out minus-written integration-by-parts errors on Llama-3.3-70B, with flips replicating on Qwen, Gemma, and Mistral-Small (24-70% per cell), and with representation-level specificity -- the same dose breaks correctly-minus answers while sparing correct-plus answers where the direction is surgical (0/72 on Llama), and matched random-direction and control-layer interventions are null. Core claims were preregistered with pass criteria frozen in git before held-out data was seen: 23 of 29 checks passed, all misses reported. But causality is not uniform: Phi-4 implements an equally well-localized circuit at the same late depth, yet the identical intervention flips almost nothing -- the sign is written redundantly across ~15 layers with margins several times larger (median 17.8 logits). Localization, we conclude, does not imply steerability: two models can share a circuit location yet differ enormously in how overturnable it is.

</details>


### [146] [Learning Multi-Agent Orchestration:Higher-Order Interactions in Policy Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07495)
*Kaixuan Liu,Guojun Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: IAPO：面向LLM多智能体编排的交互感知策略优化，将策略性能差精确分解为单决策效应与交互效应，并从采样轨迹中联合优化两者，同时保证每次更新均为改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习式编排方法以逐决策独立优势函数进行信用分配，隐含假设回合内其他决策保持不变；但在编排场景中，某一决策对最终结果的贡献依赖其前置决策，联合更新的收益不等于各单决策效应之和，该假设失效。

Method: 提出IAPO框架：将两个策略间的性能差精确分解为各决策的个体效应与决策间交互效应（并证明交互项可达可获得增益的一半），基于采样轨迹同时优化个体项与交互项，并在理论上保证每次更新都带来性能提升。

Result: 在GSM8K、MATH、DROP、MBPP、HumanEval的长输入版本上，与同一7B执行器上的15个已发表多智能体与工作流搜索系统对比，IAPO部署的计划以更少token达到最强系统的准确率；在代码任务上移除交互项导致准确率下降7至17个点。

Conclusion: 编排中的决策交互不可忽略，显式建模并优化交互效应可在保证单调改进的前提下提升多智能体编排的样本与经济效率。

Abstract: Learned orchestration has emerged as a promising paradigm for large language model (LLM)-based multi-agent systems, in which a central orchestrator selects agents and controls the information they receive throughout a task. Despite rapid empirical progress, existing methods train the orchestrator by crediting each decision with its own advantage, implicitly assuming that the other decisions in the episode stay fixed. This assumption fails in orchestration, where the contribution of each decision to the final outcome depends on the decisions preceding it, so the gain of a joint update is not the sum of its per-decision effects. To address this challenge, we introduce \textbf{IAPO}, an interaction-aware policy optimization framework that explicitly accounts for interactions among decisions. Specifically, IAPO exactly decomposes the performance difference between two policies into the effects of individual decisions and their interactions, which we prove can account for half of the attainable gain, and then optimizes both from sampled trajectories while certifying that every update is an improvement. On long-input versions of GSM8K, MATH, DROP, MBPP and HumanEval, against fifteen published multi-agent and workflow-search systems on the same 7B executor, IAPO's deployed plans reach the strongest system's accuracy with fewer tokens, and removing the interaction term costs 7 to 17 points of accuracy on code.

</details>


### [147] [RLGPA: A Reinforcement Learning-Guided Granularity Planning Attack for Decision-Based Sparse Adversarial Examples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07512)
*Chao Zhou,Shenghua He,Jiaxin Mo,Qiangguo Yu,Fengchuang Xing*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RLGPA：一种在线强化学习框架，在硬查询预算下调度基于决策的稀疏攻击的支撑集缩减执行器及其粒度，通过学习状态条件化策略显著提升高稀疏度下的定向攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于决策的稀疏攻击只能利用受害模型的预测标签来降低扰动的支撑集（support）。作者观察到最优的恢复策略随支撑集收缩而变化：粗粒度移除在早期有效，而尾部状态需要更精细、依赖历史的测试。现有方法未对这种阶段性差异进行调度。

Method: 提出 RLGPA。采用带掩码的 Dueling Double DQN，在七个稳定的策略—尺度（policy–scale）动作中选择，每个动作生成相对于支撑集的恢复组，并在每次宏决策后重建。可行性保持执行器只提交对抗性恢复，并将支撑集增益与查询代价反馈给规划器。整个框架在硬查询预算下进行在线调度。

Result: 在匹配 top-1 标签 oracle 下，CIFAR-10 稀疏度 0.01 时定向 ASR 达 61%（SparseEvo 25%，Pointwise 0%）；ResNet50/ViT 在稀疏度 0.005 下达 75.5%/73.0%（对比 29.0%/15.5%）。固定探针测试中，40 张图像后中位 L0 从 165 降至 23 且无需更新。状态盲化与状态打乱对照组显著退化，验证策略确实利用了攻击状态。

Conclusion: 主要增益来自状态条件化的执行器调度与动态重划分。学习到的调度策略有效利用攻击状态，在高稀疏度条件下大幅优于现有基线。

Abstract: Decision-based sparse attacks must reduce perturbation support using only the victim's predicted label. The best restoration strategy varies as support contracts: coarse removal works early, while tail states need finer, history-aware tests. We propose RLGPA, an online reinforcement learning method that schedules support-reduction executors and their granularity under a hard query budget. A masked Dueling Double DQN selects among seven stable policy--scale actions; each action produces support-relative restoration groups that are rebuilt after every macro decision. A feasibility-preserving executor commits only adversarial restorations and feeds support gain and query cost back to the planner. Under the matched top-1-label oracle, RLGPA reaches 61\% targeted ASR at sparsity 0.01 on CIFAR-10 (vs. 25\% SparseEvo, 0\% Pointwise), and 75.5\%/73.0\% on ResNet50/ViT at sparsity 0.005 (vs. 29.0\%/15.5\%). A fixed-probe test reduces median \(L_0\) from 165 to 23 after 40 images without any update. State-blind and state-shuffled controls degrade significantly, confirming that the learned policy uses attack state; the main gains come from state-conditioned executor scheduling and dynamic repartition. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/Acui001/RLGPA.git.

</details>


### [148] [Understanding Data Influence in Transportation Networks: A Physics-Constrained Influence-Function Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07526)
*Hamidreza Attari,Yueyue Fan,Johannes Royset*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出面向约束学习的硬物理约束影响函数框架，在交通网络物理可行空间内刻画训练样本对模型预测的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有影响函数方法主要针对无约束学习，未显式考虑耦合物流各分量的物理约束；在约束下扰动只能沿物理可行方向传播，样本影响的大小、方向与空间分布可能被改变。

Method: 构建硬物理约束影响函数框架，将数据影响定义于网络约束所确定的可行空间；给出解析结果刻画约束对影响度量、方向与空间分布的作用；以流量守恒约束下的交通预测为案例，并用正交分解量化扰动中经由流量守恒在网络上传播的部分与被约束投影剔除的部分。

Result: 所提框架识别出的高影响样本与传统影响函数识别结果存在系统性差异；正交分解定量揭示扰动在可行方向传播与被投影消除的比例。

Conclusion: 该框架为约束交通学习问题提供物理一致的数据影响评估方法，可应用于数据诊断、传感器评估与定向数据采集。

Abstract: Understanding how individual training observations influence model predictions is important for developing reliable and interpretable data-driven transportation systems. Existing influence-function methods, however, are primarily formulated for unconstrained learning and do not explicitly account for physical constraints that couple different components of a transportation network. Under such constraints, perturbing a training observation can affect model parameters and predictions only through physically feasible directions, potentially changing how its influence propagates across the network. This paper develops a hard physics-informed influence-function framework for constrained learning, in which training-data influence is characterized within the physically feasible space defined by network constraints. The analytical results reveal how physical constraints alter the magnitude, direction, and spatial distribution of training-sample influence relative to conventional influence measures. Using traffic prediction under flow-conservation constraints as a case study, we show that influential observations identified by the proposed framework differ systematically from those identified by classical influence functions. Through orthogonal decomposition, we quantitatively characterize how much of a perturbation in the training data is propagated through the network via the flow-conservation constraints and how much is removed by the constraint projection. The proposed influence framework provides a physically consistent approach for evaluating training-data influence in constrained transportation learning problems, with potential applications to data diagnostics, sensor evaluation, and targeted data collection.

</details>


### [149] [Provable Zeroth-Order Safe Exploration for Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07561)
*Weiqin Chen,Xinmeng Huang,Dongsheng Ding,Santiago Paternain*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出零阶对数障碍策略梯度（ZO-LBPG），实现RLHF中的安全探索，保证训练期间所有策略（含探索策略）保持可行，并给出全局收敛与样本复杂度界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有原始-对偶方法虽能收敛到可行策略，但训练过程中仍会发生约束违反；论文研究更强的"安全探索"要求——训练中执行的每个策略（包括辅助探索策略）都需保持可行。

Method: 无奖励模型方法：用直接由人类偏好导出的零阶估计来优化对数障碍目标；为应对噪声反馈，采用保守约束裕度估计、耦合rollout（coupled rollouts）以及自适应步长，防止更新越过约束边界。

Result: 理论证明以高概率所有迭代与探索策略严格可行，ZO-LBPG全局收敛到近似最优约束策略；样本复杂度为约O(ε^{-6}+d^3ε^{-5})条轨迹与O(ε^{-8}+d^5ε^{-7})次人类查询；在合成CMDP与语言模型对齐任务上实验验证了训练期安全性保证。

Conclusion: ZO-LBPG在无需奖励建模的前提下将安全探索引入RLHF，兼顾训练全程可行性保证与全局收敛性，实验支持其理论安全性。

Abstract: Safe reinforcement learning from human feedback (Safe-RLHF) seeks to improve helpfulness while satisfying harmlessness constraints. Existing primal-dual approaches can converge to feasible policies but do not prevent constraint violations during training. We study a stronger requirement, safe exploration for RLHF, under which every policy executed during training, including auxiliary exploration policies, remains feasible. We propose Zeroth-Order Log-Barrier Policy Gradient (ZO-LBPG), a reward-model-free method that optimizes a log-barrier objective using zeroth-order estimates derived directly from human preferences. To ensure safety under noisy feedback, we combine conservative constraint margin estimates with coupled rollouts and an adaptive step size that prevents updates from crossing constraint boundaries. We prove that, with high probability, all iterates and exploration policies remain strictly feasible, and that ZO-LBPG globally converges to an approximately optimal constrained policy. We establish a sample complexity of $\widetilde{\mathcal O}(\varepsilon^{-6}+d^3\varepsilon^{-5})$ trajectories and $\widetilde{\mathcal O}(\varepsilon^{-8}+d^5\varepsilon^{-7})$ human queries. Experiments on a synthetic CMDP and a language-model alignment task corroborate the training-time safety guarantees.

</details>


### [150] [MambaTG: Candidate-Aware State Space Modeling for Future Link Prediction on Temporal Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07569)
*Nguyen Hong Quan,Sitao Luan,Yilun Zheng,Lihui Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出MambaTG，一个候选感知的Mamba框架，通过联合建模"历史序列演化"和"候选-历史兼容性"两个互补信号，来改进时序图上首次交互（未见过边）的链接预测，并在13个数据集上取得一致且高效的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时序图链接预测包含重复边预测和首次交互（未见过边）预测。重复边可依赖直接的成对历史证据，而未见边必须超越历史重现进行泛化。现有模型多依赖与重复交互或历史重叠相关的信号，这些信号对重复边信息量高，但对未见边有限甚至不可用，导致未见边预测性能显著下降。

Method: 基于上述观察，作者将超越历史重现的序列动态建模分解为两个互补信号：(1) 历史序列演化，刻画源节点按时间排序的交互之间的依赖；(2) 候选-历史兼容性，刻画候选节点与源节点演化历史的匹配程度。据此提出MambaTG——候选感知的Mamba框架，同时建模这两类信号。Mamba的选择性递归用于捕捉有序交互历史的演化，其依赖输入的状态转移使历史状态能适应不同候选输入。

Result: 在13个时序图数据集上与13个代表性基线模型进行大量实验，MambaTG在重复边和未见边预测上均取得强而一致的性能，同时保持计算高效。

Conclusion: 超越历史重现的两信号分解（历史序列演化与候选-历史兼容性）能有效提升未见边预测，Mamba的选择性递归机制与该设计自然契合；MambaTG在重复与未见过边上表现稳健且计算高效。

Abstract: Future link prediction on temporal graphs involves both repeated interactions between previously connected node pairs and first-time interactions between pairs with no prior interaction. While repeated-edge prediction can exploit direct pairwise evidence, unseen-edge prediction must generalize beyond historical recurrence. Existing temporal graph models often exploit signals tied to recurring interactions or historical overlap, which are highly informative for repeated edges but become limited or unavailable for unseen edges, contributing to substantial performance degradation on unseen-edge prediction. Based on this observation, we identify two complementary signals for modeling sequential dynamics beyond historical recurrence: (1) historical-sequence evolution, which captures dependencies among the source node's chronologically ordered interactions, and (2) candidate--history compatibility, which captures how well a candidate fits the source node's evolving history. Based on the decomposition, we propose MambaTG, a candidate-aware Mamba-based framework that jointly models these two signals. Mamba provides a natural mechanism for this design: its selective recurrence captures the evolution of ordered interaction histories, while its input-dependent state transitions allow the history state to adapt to different candidate inputs. Extensive experiments on 13 temporal graph datasets against 13 representative baselines show that MambaTG achieves strong and consistent performance across both repeated and unseen edges while remaining computationally efficient.

</details>


### [151] [ACSeL: Adaptive Configuration Search for Hardware-Constrained LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07570)
*Bokun Wang,Hung-Yueh Chiang,Diana Marculescu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ACSeL框架，在内存与延迟SLO约束下联合搜索模型尺寸、批大小、量化算法与逐层混合精度配方，用RMQD作为质量代理，结合质量代理模型、FMC延迟校准与BandPareto结构化搜索，在A6000与Jetson Thor上取得最高可行率与最低质量差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 资源受限（含边缘设备）部署LLM需在严格内存与延迟SLO下同时选择模型尺寸、批大小、量化算法和混合精度配方，搜索空间组合爆炸难以穷举；且此类场景以内存/延迟为约束、最大化生成质量为目标，与数据中心以吞吐/生成速度为优先的范式不同。

Method: (1) 以RMQD（候选模型与参考模型在小型校准语料上下一词分布散度）作为质量代理，廉价且与下游benchmark强相关；(2) 轻量质量代理模型，直接从配置预测RMQD；(3) Few-shot Median Calibration (FMC)，用少量端到端profile校准基于算子profile的延迟估计；(4) BandPareto结构化搜索算法探索逐层混合精度配置。

Result: RMQD在各模型尺寸与精度下评估成本低且与下游benchmark性能强相关；在NVIDIA RTX A6000与Jetson Thor上，FMC显著提升部署可行性，BandPareto+FMC取得最高可行率及相对最佳实测配置的最低RMQD差距。

Conclusion: 以约束可行性优先、质量最大化为目标的代理式组合搜索可有效替代穷举，FMC校准与BandPareto搜索的组合是资源受限LLM部署配置选择的有效方案。

Abstract: Serving large language models (LLMs) under tight memory and latency service-level objectives (SLOs), including on edge devices, requires jointly selecting the model size, batch size, quantization algorithm, and mixed-precision quantization recipes. Exhaustive search over this combinatorially large space is infeasible. Unlike data-center serving, which often prioritizes throughput or generation speed, resource-constrained deployments typically set memory and latency budgets as constraints and then seek the configuration with the highest generation quality among feasible ones. To this end, we propose ACSeL, a framework that identifies configurations predicted to satisfy deployment constraints while achieving high predicted generation quality. To enable efficient quality estimation, we use Relative Model Quality Degradation (RMQD), a proxy based on the divergence between the candidate and reference models' next-token distributions over a small calibration corpus. Across the evaluated model sizes and precisions, RMQD is inexpensive to evaluate and strongly correlated with downstream benchmark performance. ACSeL combines (i) a lightweight quality surrogate that predicts RMQD directly from a configuration; (ii) Few-shot Median Calibration (FMC), which calibrates operator-profile-based latency estimates using a small number of end-to-end profiles; and (iii) a structured search algorithm BandPareto for exploring layerwise mixed-precision configurations. Experiments on an NVIDIA RTX A6000 workstation and an NVIDIA Jetson Thor edge platform show that FMC substantially improves deployment feasibility, while BandPareto + FMC achieves the highest feasibility and the lowest RMQD gaps to the best measured configurations in the evaluation set.

</details>


### [152] [Binding Paraphrased Instructions to Encountered Objects in sLLM Agents under Partial Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07599)
*Chanyoung Kim,Harim Song,Byoungchan Ko,Keonyoung Lee,Myungwon Lee,Dahuin Jung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出ALFWorld-Para基准揭示RL微调的sLLM智能体在同义改写指令下成功率下降21-52%，并将其归因于"指称知识—物体绑定"鸿沟；提出免训练框架BINDIntent，通过语义邻近与持久性估计实现指称表达式与观测标签的概率绑定，同义改写场景SR提升17.5-49.1pp，GiGPO-7B整体SR由61.8%升至77.4%，超过提示的24B LLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL微调的sLLM智能体在具身任务上可与更大的提示型LLM抗衡，但其对指令改写的鲁棒性尚不明确；智能体虽有同义词指称知识，却常在观察到目标后仍不去拿取，存在指称知识与物体绑定之间的鸿沟，部分可观测性加剧该问题。

Method: 构建ALFWorld-Para，在受控同义改写条件下评测鲁棒性并做归因分析；提出BINDIntent免训练框架，基于语义邻近度与对未见候选的持久性估计，将指称表达式概率性地绑定到已观测标签，无需额外训练或LLM调用。

Result: ALFWorld-Para上成功率下降21-52%，主要源于物体同义词；BINDIntent在无训练与无LLM调用下，使同义词改写成功率提升17.5-49.1pp，每步仅0.4ms CPU开销；GiGPO-7B整体SR从61.8%提升至77.4%，超过提示的24B LLM。

Conclusion: 指称绑定而非指称知识是sLLM具身智能体 paraphrase 鲁棒性的关键瓶颈；轻量、免训练的测试时绑定机制可显著提升鲁棒性，并具备实际部署效率优势。

Abstract: Reliable deployment of LLM agents requires grounding diverse user expressions in their environments. Although RL-finetuned smaller large language model (sLLM) agents rival larger prompted LLMs on embodied tasks, their robustness to instruction paraphrases remains unclear. We introduce ALFWorld-Para to evaluate this robustness under controlled paraphrases, revealing 21-52% declines in success rates (SRs), predominantly driven by object synonyms. Despite retaining knowledge of synonym referents, agents often fail to take the corresponding objects even after observing them, exposing a gap between referential knowledge and object binding. Partial observability further complicates binding, as referents may remain unseen or have already been encountered under different labels. To address this challenge, we propose BINDIntent, a training-free framework that probabilistically binds referring expressions to observed labels using semantic proximity and estimated persistence against unseen candidates. Without additional training or LLM calls, BINDIntent improves synonym-paraphrase success of RL-finetuned sLLMs by 17.5-49.1 percentage points (pp) with only 0.4 ms of CPU overhead per step. It also raises GiGPO-7B's overall SR from 61.8% to 77.4%, surpassing a prompted 24B LLM. Code and benchmark are available at https://github.com/cau-hai-lab/ALFWorld-Para.

</details>


### [153] [Monotone Viability Learning for Offline Safe Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07616)
*Ziqian Wang,Zhen Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MoViL 通过单调有限时域集合递归从离线数据学习 HJ 可行集，保持语义并可收敛到无限时域可行核，实现安全约束下的离线强化学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线安全强化学习需要从固定数据中获得可靠的长时域安全估计，而学习 HJ 可行性面临语义保持、收敛性与数值稳定性三大挑战；同时现实系统常缺少可靠动力学模型且在线探索不安全。

Method: 提出 Monotone Viability Learning（MoViL）：以单调有限时域集合递归从离线转移样本学习可行集；采用二值监督使目标尺度在各时域保持固定，避免折扣导致的数值衰减；建立恢复行为支持可行子集的充分条件；部署时用学到的可行性估计对动作进行认证。

Result: Dubins car 实验验证所学可行集的数值精度；在安全 RL 基准上取得 SOTA 的 reward-cost 表现，在所有任务上满足平均代价约束，且平均代价为对比方法中最低。

Conclusion: MoViL 可将长时域 HJ 可行性分析推广至缺乏可靠动力学模型、在线探索不安全的安全关键系统。

Abstract: Offline safe reinforcement learning requires reliable long-horizon safety estimates from fixed data. Learning Hamilton-Jacobi (HJ) viability in this setting calls for semantic preservation, convergence, and numerical stability. To address these challenges, we propose Monotone Viability Learning (MoViL), which learns viable sets from offline transitions through monotone finite-horizon set recursion. The recursion preserves finite-horizon safety semantics and converges to the infinite-horizon viability kernel. Binary supervision maintains a fixed target scale across horizons, avoiding discount-induced numerical attenuation. We further establish sufficient conditions for recovering behavior-supported viable subsets. The learned viability estimates are used to certify actions at deployment. Dubins car experiments validate the numerical accuracy of the learned viable sets. Evaluations on safe RL benchmarks demonstrate state-of-the-art reward-cost performance. MoViL satisfies the average cost constraint on every task and achieves the lowest average cost among the compared methods. These results highlight MoViL's potential to extend long-horizon HJ viability analysis to safety-critical systems where reliable dynamics models are unavailable and online exploration is unsafe.

</details>


### [154] [Online Learning with Ordered Experts (via Two-point Feedback)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07629)
*Shiliang Zuo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在完全有序动作空间、专家建议可任意重排的对抗在线学习中，研究两点反馈（每轮查询两个当前建议并支付其平均损失）。对分离单调问题取得 O(log(|F|T)√(T log|F|)) 遗憾，对 BV 问题取得 Õ(√T+|F|) 遗憾，核心技术是边际保持的端点流估计器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典全信息在线学习已有接近最优的遗憾界，但实际场景（如动态定价的菜单设计）中每轮只能查询/播放少量动作，反馈极为有限；同时专家建议会随时间变化并任意重排，破坏了有序结构上的常规假设。作者希望在仅两点反馈的严苛条件下，仍逼近全信息速率。

Method: 提出边际保持的端点流估计器（marginal-preserving endpoint flow estimator）：两次观测揭示沿序的损失增量，利用随机路径的 telescoping 恒等式把局部增量转化为对所有当前建议的无偏损失估计；可逆成对采样器（reversible pair sampler）保证在两个被播放动作处保持学习者的目标边际分布；并给出该估计器的二阶矩与稳定性界，以支撑两种反馈模型下的在线更新。

Result: 分离单调问题中，两次付费观测即达到 O(log(|F|T)√(T log|F|)) 遗憾，与经典全信息界仅差对数因子；BV（有界变差）问题达到 Õ(√T+|F|) 遗憾。在带专家建议的动态定价应用中，两选项菜单即可在 log 因子内匹配全信息速率。

Conclusion: 两点反馈在有序动作空间上足以接近全信息最优速率，序结构加随机路径技巧可弥补信息缺失；该框架可推广到动态定价等只需少量选项的实际决策问题。

Abstract: We study adversarial online learning with expert recommendations in a totally ordered action space, where the experts' recommendations may change and be reordered arbitrarily. Our focus is two-point feedback: on each round the learner plays two current recommendations, observes both, and pays their average. We study two problems. In the separated monotone problem, the loss is the sum of a hidden nondecreasing component and a hidden nonincreasing component, and a query reveals the two components separately. In the BV problem, the losses are bounded and have bounded variation (BV) along the order, and a query reveals only the loss.
  In the separated monotone problem, two paid observations achieve \[ O\bigl(\log(|\cF|T)\sqrt{T\log|\cF|}\bigr) \] regret against the best of $|\cF|$ experts, within a logarithmic factor of the classical full-information bound. For the BV problem, we obtain $\wtO(\sqrt T+|\cF|)$ regret. We give an application in dynamic pricing with expert advice, showing that two-option menus matches the full information rate up to $\log$ factors.
  The main technical ingredient is a marginal-preserving endpoint flow estimator. Two observations reveal a loss increment along the order; random paths supply telescoping identities that turn this local information into unbiased loss estimates for all current recommendations. A reversible pair sampler preserves the learner's intended marginal at both played actions. The estimator's second-moment and stability bounds support online updates for both feedback models.

</details>


### [155] [Measuring the Marginal Value of Verifier-Based RL Beyond Supervised Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07639)
*Amit Sagtani,Saloni Garg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在确定性合成的GPU/LLM训练故障诊断基准gpu-train-debug（1,877任务/30类）上直接度量verifier-based RL的增量贡献：SFT（1200条金标示范）将困难划分从0.31提升到0.785，而预注册规模（60步GRPO，lr=1e-6）的RL在分布内无显著提升（+0.22，CI[-0.06,+0.51]），仅在分布偏移下有小幅可测增益（+0.33，CI[+0.02,+0.68]）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR通常被当作SFT的替代或后续阶段，隐含假设RL阶段本身带来增益，但该增量贡献未被受控地直接测量。本文在可控、可跑到底并能精确对比的设定下检验"verifier-based RL相对于示范（demonstrations）究竟额外贡献了什么"。

Method: 构建确定性基准gpu-train-debug（1,877个合成GPU与LLM训练故障诊断任务、30个原型，程序化验证器部分给分，校准至中档模型得分0.60–0.75区间）；先做试点（1200条金标示范SFT vs 从基座15步GRPO），随后在书面预注册的正式规模（60步GRPO、学习率1e-6）下运行，并设置全量60步冷启动GRPO对照组；同时分析SFT残余误差的样本间一致性以解释GRPO组内学习信号不足。

Result: 试点中1200条金标示范近乎解决基准（SFT在hard split上0.785），而15步GRPO无提升（0.314 vs 基座0.311）。预注册规模下：分布内无可检测提升（+0.22点，95% CI [-0.06, +0.51]）；分布外held-out划分有虽小但可测的提升（+0.33点，CI [+0.02, +0.68]）。60步冷启动GRPO在全时程上复现试点结论。SFT残余误差大多为系统性、样本间一致，可能削弱GRPO可用的组内学习信号。

Conclusion: 可用提升主要由SFT获得，verifier-based RL仅带来分布偏移下的小幅额外增益，分布内几乎无贡献。全研究计算成本仅$8.69，且可完全复现（归档生成、冻结配置、代码打标签），为"RL优于示范"的隐含假设提供了受控的反证。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR) is usually evaluated as an alternative to supervised fine-tuning (SFT), or as a stage that follows it, with the implicit assumption that the RL stage contributes gains of its own. We measure that contribution directly, in a controlled setting where both stages can be run to completion and compared exactly. We built gpu-train-debug, a deterministic benchmark of 1,877 synthetic GPU and LLM training failure diagnosis tasks across 30 archetypes, graded by a programmatic verifier with partial credit, and calibrated so that a mid-tier model scores in a 0.60 to 0.75 band. A pilot produced a surprise: 1,200 gold demonstrations nearly solved the benchmark (SFT 0.785 on the hard split), while 15 steps of GRPO from the base model did not move it (0.314 vs 0.311 base). We therefore amended the study, in writing and before the full run, to ask the sharper question: what does verifier-based RL add beyond demonstrations? At the pre-registered scale (60 GRPO steps, learning rate 1e-6), the answer is: no detectable improvement in-distribution (+0.22 points, 95% CI [-0.06, +0.51]), and a modest but measurable improvement under distribution shift (+0.33 points on a held-out OOD split, CI [+0.02, +0.68]). SFT accounted for most of the available improvement, while verifier-based RL produced only modest additional gains under distribution shift. The residual errors SFT leaves are mostly systematic, identical across samples, which likely reduces the within-group learning signal available to GRPO. A full 60-step cold-start GRPO arm confirms the pilot at the full horizon. The whole study cost $8.69 of compute and is fully reproducible from archived generations, frozen configs, and tagged code.

</details>


### [156] [WaSP: Wasserstein Data Synthesis Pipeline for Solver-Free Operator Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07646)
*Ryan Liu,Chuwei Wang,Ricardo Baptista,Andrew Stuart,Anima Anandkumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: WaSP：通过沿中间分布路径分阶段合成制造输入-解对，逐步缓解分布偏移，实现无需求解器的神经算子训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经算子求解PDE需要昂贵的输入-解对；无求解器的两条替代路线各有缺陷：物理信息学习受残差范数权衡（强范数控制误差但增大mini-batch方差、阻碍优化），制造数据训练分布可能与目标分布互奇异，导致目标分布上精度差。

Method: 提出Wasserstein数据合成流水线（WaSP）：构造收敛到目标输入分布的中间分布路径，分阶段训练。每阶段由上一代理模型生成解，再用精确PDE算子反推对应输入，形成下一代理模型的训练对，从而随训练推进逐步缩小分布偏移。

Result: 理论上证明合成过程中分布偏移的逐步误差传播界；实验上在Allen-Cahn、Kuramoto-Siminzaki（Kuramoto-Sivashinsky）、Ginzburg-Landau等PDE上，相比现有无求解器方法将解误差降低3.9×–9.5×，并更好复现两个混沌系统的统计可观测量。

Conclusion: 以分布路径分阶段自举合成数据，可在完全无需求解器的前提下兼顾优化稳定性与目标分布精度，是训练PDE神经算子的有效范式。

Abstract: Neural operators offer fast surrogates for solving PDEs, but training them typically requires expensive input-solution pairs. Two solver-free alternatives include physics-informed learning and training on manufactured data. Physics-informed learning approximates solutions by minimizing PDE residuals. While the residual norm must be strong enough to control solution error, stronger norms increase mini-batch variance and hinder optimization. Manufactured data constructs input-solution pairs for learning by applying the PDE operator to artificial solutions. However, the resulting training distribution can be mutually singular with the target distribution, yielding poor accuracy on the target distribution. We introduce the Wasserstein Data Synthesis Pipeline (WaSP), which trains on manufactured input-solution pairs while gradually mitigating the distribution shift. WaSP synthesizes new pairs by applying the learned operator to a path of intermediate distributions converging to the target input distribution. Training proceeds in stages along this path. At each stage, the previous surrogate generates solutions, and the exact PDE operator derives corresponding inputs, yielding training pairs for the next surrogate. Theoretically, we prove a stepwise error-propagation bound for the distribution shift introduced during synthesis. Empirically, WaSP reduces solution error by $3.9\times$ to $9.5\times$ compared to existing solver-free methods on PDEs such as Allen-Cahn, Kuramoto-Sivashinsky, and Ginzburg-Landau, and better reproduces the two chaotic systems' statistical observables.

</details>


### [157] [Simple Meta-Learning for HPO and CASH Using Performance Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07659)
*Emiel Witting,Cheng Yan,Tom Julian Viering*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于"历史性能"的元学习表示：将每个配置描述为其在先前任务上的历史表现，直接与标准代理模型（如线性Bayesian ridge回归）配对，并用于AutoML迭代选择；在TabArena上多模型CASH精度相当但试验次数约减半，单模型HPO排名第二，且无需预训练/调参。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维搜索空间中，以显式超参数编码作为代理模型输入的贝叶斯优化难以学到有效的性能模型；且完整AutoML框架（运行时间估计、成本预算、集成）复杂度高。

Method: 将配置表示为其在先前任务上的历史性能向量（性能基表示），直接接入标准代理模型；范围限定为AutoML迭代选择环节（从固定候选集中选下一个待评估配置）；并该表示用于MMR（最大边际相关性）多样性重排序，作为无后验的采集规则。

Result: 在TabArena多模型选择与优化中，性能基表示+线性Bayesian ridge回归优于专用元学习基线，达到同等调优性能约需一半试验数；单模型HPO中位列第二；两基准上遗憾接近或更优，复杂度与成本大幅降低，无需预训练或调参，且可扩展性与搜索空间维度无关。

Conclusion: 性能基元学习表示是替代显式超参数编码的简洁有效方案，配合简单代理模型即可实现高效AutoML迭代选择；但论文仅覆盖选择组件，未涉及运行时间估计、成本感知预算与集成等完整框架功能。

Abstract: Bayesian optimization for Hyperparameter Optimization (HPO) and Combined Algorithm Selection and Hyperparameter Optimization (CASH) often relies on explicit hyperparameter encodings as surrogate inputs. In high-dimensional search spaces, however, such representations can make it difficult for standard surrogates to learn useful performance models. We instead study an alternative meta-learning representation that describes each configuration directly as its historical performance across prior tasks. This representation can be paired directly with standard surrogate models. Our scope is the iterative selection component of AutoML, deciding which configuration to evaluate next from a fixed candidate set, rather than a complete framework with features such as runtime estimation, cost-aware budgeting and ensembling. We further apply this representation to maximal marginal relevance (MMR), a diversity-based re-ranking criterion from recommender systems, as a posterior-free acquisition rule. When selecting and optimising across multiple models on the TabArena benchmark, the performance-based representation paired with a linear Bayesian ridge regression model outperforms dedicated meta-learning baselines, achieving the same tuned performance in roughly half the number of trials. For single-model HPO, it remains a competitive second. Across both benchmarks this method closely matches or improves on regret at a fraction of the complexity and cost, requiring no pre-training or tuning and scaling independently of search space dimensionality. Code for reproducing our results is available at https://github.com/EWitting/simple-metalearning.

</details>


### [158] [An Information Bottleneck for Post-hoc Memorization Mitigation in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07669)
*Dina EL Zein,Shashi Kumar,James Henderson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过在冻结LLM骨干与语言建模头之间插入非参数变分信息瓶颈(NVIB)，实现不改动、不重训的后处理式记忆缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型可记忆并复现受版权保护的训练数据，带来隐私与知识产权风险；作者希望在不修改或不重新训练原模型的前提下缓解记忆化。

Method: 提出模块化的即插即用框架：在冻结的LLM骨干与其语言建模头之间插入非参数变分信息瓶颈(NVIB)，在token预测前压缩最终隐表示，以保留通用语言知识、过滤高度具体的记忆序列。

Result: 在书籍段落记忆提取任务与标准语言建模基准上评估记忆缩减与下游效用的权衡；实验显示NVIB层显著降低可提取性，同时在基准任务上保持有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 添加信息瓶颈为预训练LLM的事后记忆缓解提供了一条实用且与架构无关的方向。

Abstract: Recent studies have shown that large language models (LLMs) can memorize and reproduce copyrighted training data, raising serious privacy and intellectual property concerns. In this work, we investigate whether memorization can be mitigated without modifying or retraining the underlying model through an information bottleneck. We propose a modular plug-and-play framework that inserts a nonparametric variational information bottleneck (NVIB) between a frozen LLM backbone and its language modeling head. By compressing the final hidden representations before token prediction, the proposed bottleneck aims to preserve general linguistic knowledge while filtering out highly specific memorized sequences. We evaluate our approach on memorization extraction tasks involving book passages and on standard language modeling benchmarks to measure the trade-off between memorization reduction and downstream utility. Our experiments show that the NVIB layer substantially reduces extractability while maintaining competitive performance on benchmark tasks. These results suggest that adding an information bottleneck offers a practical and architecture agnostic direction for post-hoc memorization mitigation in pretrained LLMs.

</details>


### [159] [Calibrated Target Noise Recovers Curvature from the Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07671)
*Arash Jamshidi,Katsiaryna Haitsiukevich,Aristides Gionis,Kai Puolamäki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种目标扰动（target-perturbation）方案，仅利用聚合小批量梯度即可恢复曲率信息；该方法向目标注入方差随局部输出曲率标定的零均值噪声，使噪声梯度的协方差对应实用曲率矩阵，对非线性模型可恢复GGN矩阵，对前馈ReLU网络还可恢复总体Hessian的对角块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从梯度中估计曲率是机器学习的根本问题，但在仅有聚合小批量梯度、无逐样本梯度与二阶导数的场景下，预条件优化、采样与早停等应用仍需要曲率信息，现有方法难以低成本获取。

Method: 向训练目标注入零均值噪声，噪声方差按局部输出曲率标定；由此得到的含噪批量梯度的协方差即可恢复实际可用的曲率矩阵，无需逐样本梯度、二阶导数或其他网络内部量。

Result: 对一类广泛的非线性模型可精确恢复广义高斯-牛顿（GGN）矩阵；对前馈ReLU网络还可恢复总体Hessian的对角块。实验表明曲率恢复准确，用于预条件时优化更快，估计的GGN对早停有效。

Conclusion: 该曲率估计器简单、轻量、可证明最优，且易于集成到现有训练流程中，为仅凭梯度获取曲率提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Estimating curvature information from gradients alone is a fundamental problem in machine learning. The challenge becomes particularly acute in settings where only aggregate mini-batch gradients are available, yet curvature information is still required for applications such as preconditioning in optimisation, sampling, and early stopping. Perhaps surprisingly, we show that a simple target-perturbation scheme suffices to recover such curvature information from gradients alone. Our method injects zero-mean noise into the targets, with variance calibrated to the local output curvature. The covariance of the resulting noisy batch gradients then recovers practical curvature matrices, without requiring per-sample gradients, second-order derivatives, or other internal network quantities. For a broad class of non-linear models, our scheme recovers the Generalised Gauss--Newton (GGN) matrix; for feed-forward ReLU networks, it additionally recovers the diagonal blocks of the population Hessian. We demonstrate that the resulting estimator is simple, lightweight, provably optimal, and easy to integrate into existing training pipelines. Experiments demonstrate accurate curvature recovery, faster optimisation when the estimator is used for preconditioning, and the utility of the estimated GGN for early stopping.

</details>


### [160] [Instrumental Variables in Continuous-Time Stochastic Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07674)
*Lars Pennig,Søren Wengel Mogensen,Niki Kilbertus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出面向由SDE刻画的平稳连续时间随机过程的工具变量(IV)框架，将工具变量本身视为随机过程，在漂移项的无穷小层面编码结构稀疏性与因果效应，并导出基于路径型工具变量的矩条件，实现混杂连续时间系统中的一致因果效应估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续时间演化系统中普遍存在因果问题，而现有IV方法仅适用于静态或离散时间设定，无法处理连续时间动态；同时最大似然估计与离散化IV方法在存在未观测混杂时会产生系统性偏差。

Method: 以SDE参数化的平稳连续时间随机过程为对象，工具变量取为随机过程；直接在漂移项的无穷小层面编码结构稀疏性与因果效应假设，并与同一过程离散化后的IV假设进行对比；基于路径型工具变量推导矩限制，针对Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程给出矩条件的简化形式、可识别性条件及一致估计方法；进一步借助signature核表达路径矩限制，将框架推广至非线性因果效应。

Result: 在存在混杂的连续时间系统中，该方法能够恢复真实因果效应，而基于最大似然的估计与离散时间IV方法则表现出系统性偏差；给出了OU过程下的可识别性条件与一致估计量，并实现了非线性效应下的估计。

Conclusion: 将IV思想直接建立在连续时间的无穷小结构上，可避免离散化带来的假设错配与偏差，为连续时间动态系统中的因果推断提供了统一的识别与估计框架。

Abstract: Causal questions often arise in systems that evolve in continuous time. While instrumental variable (IV) methods provide a principled approach to identify causal effects in static or discrete-time settings, even in the presence of unobserved confounding, they do not yet extend to continuous-time dynamics. In this work, we develop an IV framework for stationary continuous-time stochastic processes parameterized by stochastic differential equations (SDE), where the instrument itself takes the form of a stochastic process. We encode the structural sparsity and causal effects directly at the infinitesimal level of the drift, and contrast these assumptions with the IV assumptions on the discretized version of the same process. Based on these structural assumptions, we derive moment restrictions using path-valued instruments. We show how these restrictions simplify for Ornstein-Uhlenbeck processes and present an identification condition together with a corresponding consistent estimation method. Finally, we extend our estimation framework to nonlinear causal effects by expressing the path-based moment restrictions leveraging the signature kernel. Empirically, our method recovers causal effects in confounded continuous-time systems where maximum-likelihood-based estimation and discrete-time IV methods are systematically biased.

</details>


### [161] [$O(1/n)$ Convergence Rate for RVI $Q$-Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07676)
*Avik Kar,Siddharth Chandak,Rahul Singh,Shalabh Bhatnagar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为有限弱连通MDP上的同步相对值迭代(RVI) Q学习建立了有限时间收敛保证，证明使用多项式步长时可获得 $O(n^{-1})$ 的均方Bellman跨度残差界与策略次优性界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RVI Q学习常用于平均奖励MDP，但缺乏有限时间收敛速率的理论保证，尤其在弱连通（非遍历）MDP情形下。作者希望为同步RVI Q学习填补这一空白，给出实例相关的显式收敛界。

Method: 先在一般框架下分析由非扩张、线性正则映射驱动的随机Krasnosel'skiĭ–Mann迭代，在条件二阶矩至多随残差平方线性增长的鞅差噪声下，建立最后迭代的均方残差界；采用 $n^{-a}$ 阶多项式步长得到 $O(n^{-a})$ 速率（$0<a\leq1$）。随后验证中心化Bellman算子对有限弱连通MDP满足线性正则性与噪声条件，并将一般结果应用到RVI Q学习。

Result: 对合适步长序列，RVI Q学习迭代的均方Bellman跨度残差达到 $O(n^{-1})$ 界；借助最优与次优平稳确定性策略之间的正间隙，得到贪心策略期望次优性为 $O(n^{-1})$，即 $O(\varepsilon^{-1})$ 次同步迭代即可获得 $\varepsilon$-最优策略（期望意义）。常数依赖于命中时间参数与次优策略的次优性间隙。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了同步RVI Q学习的首个（实例相关）有限时间 $O(1/n)$ 收敛速率，表明弱连通MDP下该算法具备实用收敛效率，且界由命中时间与策略间隙等可解释量刻画。

Abstract: We establish finite-time convergence guarantee for synchronous relative value iteration (RVI) $Q$-learning for finite weakly communicating Markov decision processes (MDPs). For stochastic Krasnosel'skiĭ--Mann iterations driven by a non-expansive, linearly regular map on a finite-dimensional normed space, we establish last-iterate mean-squared residual bounds under martingale-difference noise whose conditional second moment grows at most linearly with the squared residual. Suitable polynomial stepsizes of order $n^{-a}$ yield an $O(n^{-a})$ rate for $0<a\leq1$. We verify linear regularity and the noise condition for the centered Bellman operator for finite weakly communicating MDPs. Applying the general result gives an $O(n^{-1})$ bound on the mean-squared Bellman span residual of RVI $Q$-learning iterates when a suitable stepsize sequence is used. The positive gap between optimal and suboptimal stationary deterministic policies then yields an $O(n^{-1})$ bound on the expected suboptimality of the greedy policy. Consequently, $O(ε^{-1})$ synchronous iterations suffice for an $ε$-optimal policy in expectation. These guarantees are instance-dependent, with constants that depend on the hitting-time parameters and the suboptimality gap of the second-best policy.

</details>


### [162] [Non-Parametric Mutual Information Estimation with Vine Copulas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07693)
*Sigurd Holmsen,Berit Omli Øksnes,Ingrid Hobæk Haff,Sylvia Richardson,Ali Ramezani-Kebrya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出VINE——基于简化D-vine Copula的非参数互信息估计器，通过将联合密度近似为简化D-vine使密度比简化为成对Copula之积，从而可非参数估计，在低至中维数据上以更低计算成本取得有竞争力的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SotA互信息估计多依赖神经方法，计算开销大；作者希望获得兼顾灵活性与计算效率的MI估计器。

Method: 以简化D-vine Copula近似联合密度，使密度比简化为成对Copula的乘积，并对各成对Copula进行非参数估计，得到VINE估计器。

Result: 在多种数据集上与SotA神经MI估计器对比，涵盖简化D-vine假设成立与违背两种情形，在低至中维数据上以更低计算成本实现有竞争力的精度。

Conclusion: VINE是一种灵活、计算高效的非参数MI估计器，适用于低至中维场景；其在假设违背时仍表现稳健，但对高维情形未作评估。

Abstract: We introduce VINE: a non-parametric mutual information (MI) estimator based on simplified D-vine copulas. Approximating the joint density with a simplified D-vine allows the density ratio to simplify to a product of pair copulas, which we can estimate non-parametrically. This structural simplification yields VINE, a flexible and computationally efficient MI estimator. We compare VINE against SotA neural MI estimators on a range of datasets, both under settings where the simplified D-vine assumption holds and where it is violated, demonstrating competitive accuracy at a lower computational cost for low- to medium-dimensional datasets.

</details>


### [163] [Learning Noise Optimization for Reward Alignment in Few-Step Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06255)
*Hongeun Kim,Jeongsol Kim,Jong Chul Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LeNO 将奖励引导的初始噪声优化摊销为一次学习的残差传输，在冻结预训练生成器的前提下用 Local GRPO 训练锚点条件的噪声修正分布，实现推理时单次修正即可提升奖励。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散/流模型的奖励对齐通常建立在随机轨迹上，不适配少步确定性生成器；而无梯度的初始噪声优化虽无需轨迹，却需在推理时逐样本昂贵搜索。

Method: 学习锚点条件的噪声修正分布（残差传输），生成器冻结；采用 Local GRPO 与标量奖励训练，消除测试时迭代优化；针对高斯初始噪声配合保范投影，推导修正噪声分布与原先验间 KL 散度的可处理上界，作为有原则的正则目标。

Result: 在少步文本到图像与蛋白质结构生成（含昂贵的黑盒可设计性奖励）上，单次噪声修正即提升目标奖励，且较好保持生成质量与多样性。

Conclusion: 将初始噪声优化摊销为学习式残差传输，可在无需测试时搜索的条件下有效实现少步确定性生成器的奖励对齐，兼具质量与多样性保持。

Abstract: Reward alignment for diffusion- and flow-based generative models is often formulated over stochastic trajectories, making it less suitable for few-step deterministic generators. Initial noise optimization provides a trajectory-free alternative, but existing gradient-free methods require costly per-sample search at inference. We introduce LeNO (Learning Noise Optimization), which amortizes reward-guided initial noise optimization into a learned residual transport while keeping the pretrained generator frozen. LeNO learns an anchor-conditioned distribution over noise corrections using Local GRPO and scalar rewards, eliminating iterative test-time optimization. For Gaussian initial noise with norm-preserving projection, we derive a tractable upper bound on the KL divergence between the corrected noise distribution and the original prior, providing a principled regularization objective. We evaluate LeNO on few-step text-to-image and protein structure generation, including expensive black-box designability rewards. Across both domains, LeNO improves target rewards with a single noise correction at inference while largely preserving generation quality and diversity.

</details>


### [164] [Equivariant Deep Learning in Direct-Product Space for Electronic-Structure Hamiltonian Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07695)
*Shi Yin,Zujian Dai,Xinyang Pan,Lixin He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DP-Learner框架，直接在直积空间中对类哈密顿量算符块进行等变消息传递，避免Clebsch–Gordan分解，并构建MP-HAM-110K数据集，在精度与推理效率上显著超越SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有哈密顿量预测的等变架构在不可约球张量的直和空间中传播特征，而目标哈密顿量天然是局域轨道与自旋基上直积表示中的算符块。这种表示错配会割裂连续的哈密顿量块、难以直接操纵轨道耦合，并将特征组织为需大量变换、与硬件执行和内存管理不匹配的不规则角动量分量。

Method: 提出DP-Learner，在直积空间的类哈密顿量算符块上直接进行消息传递。理论上引入基于李生成元的方向嵌入，以及不变加权的左右乘积与缩并，在无需显式Clebsch–Gordan分解与逆重构的情况下保持对SO(3)、SU(2)及空间反演的等变性。方法上实例化为Transformer架构：类哈密顿量特征矩阵/张量通过直积空间等变算符演化，非线性不变标量路由控制矩阵单元门控、方向传播、注意力与环境嵌入上下文交互。

Result: 构建MP-HAM-110K大规模哈密顿量数据集，包含逾11万材料结构、覆盖68种元素，含哈密顿量与波函数数据。在MP-HAM-110K与Materials-HAM-SOC上，DP-Learner在哈密顿量预测精度与推理效率上均大幅优于SOTA。

Conclusion: 通过在直积表示空间直接进行等变建模可同时缓解表示错配与计算瓶颈，兼顾等变正确性、预测精度与推理效率，并配套提供大规模含波函数的数据集。

Abstract: Most existing equivariant architectures for Hamiltonian prediction propagate features in direct-sum spaces of irreducible spherical tensors, whereas the target Hamiltonian is naturally an operator block in a direct-product representation over local orbital and spin bases. This representation mismatch fragments contiguous Hamiltonian blocks, obscures direct manipulation of orbital couplings, and organizes features into irregular angular-momentum components that require numerous transformations and are poorly aligned with efficient hardware execution and memory management. To address both the representation mismatch and computational bottleneck, we propose DP-Learner, an equivariant learning framework that performs message passing directly on Hamiltonian-like operator blocks in the direct-product space. Theoretically, we propose Lie-generator-based directional embedding and invariantly weighted left--right products and contractions that preserve equivariance to SO(3), SU(2), and spatial inversions, without explicit Clebsch--Gordan decomposition or inverse reconstruction. Methodologically, we instantiate this principle as a Transformer architecture, in which Hamiltonian-like feature matrices or tensors evolve through equivariant operators in the direct-product space, while non-linear invariant scalar routing controls structured gating over matrix units, directional propagation, attention, and environment-embedded context interactions. We further construct MP-HAM-110K, a large-scale Hamiltonian dataset comprising more than 110,000 material structures covering 68 elements with both Hamiltonian and wavefunction data. Experiments on MP-HAM-110K and Materials-HAM-SOC demonstrate that DP-Learner dramatically improves Hamiltonian prediction accuracy and inference efficiency over the state-of-the-art method.

</details>


### [165] [Evaluating Language Model Features for Zero-Shot Time-Series Anomaly Detection Under Isolation Assumption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07720)
*Romain Hermary,Nesryne Mejri,Djamila Aouada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将LLM纯粹作为特征提取器，采用逐时间步（step-wise）简单分词保留细粒度局部信息，直接分析LLM诱导的多变量时间序列特征空间，并用无监督Isolation Forest做零样本MTSAD评估，同时检验性能是否源于语言预训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MTSAD本身无监督、需高局部精度且标签稀缺；现有将LLM跨模态迁移到时间序列的方法不适合多变量场景：分词常遮蔽细粒度的时间与变量结构，且多优化预测/重构等辅助目标。作者认为随着模型容量增大，这类代理目标不可靠——高表达力模型可能把异常也重构/预测出来，从而瓦解两类策略的基础。

Method: 不做预测或重构训练，将LLM仅用作特征提取器并直接分析其特征空间；采用每token一个时间步的简单step-wise分词以保留局部信息；通过特征可分性（异常特征应远离正常特征）分析其时序与上下文判别能力；以无监督Isolation Forest在零样本设定下直接评价表示质量；并做对照实验考察性能是否可归因于语言相关预训练。

Result: 提供了关于LLM表示多变量时间序列以用于异常检测能力的经验性洞见，验证了所提零样本分类评估框架下特征的可分性与判别性，并区分了语言预训练对性能的贡献与模型自身独立产生的作用。

Conclusion: LLM作为纯特征提取器结合细粒度step-wise分词，可为多变量时间序列异常检测提供有效表示，替代不可靠的预测/重构代理目标；该工作凸显了跨模态基础模型更广泛的应用潜力。

Abstract: Time-Series Anomaly Detection (TSAD) is a fundamentally challenging task: it is unsupervised, requires high local precision capabilities, and suffers from limited labelled data. Recent work has explored adapting Large Language Models (LLMs) to time-series via cross-modal transfer. However, existing approaches are poorly suited to Multivariate TSAD (MTSAD), as tokenisation often obscures fine-grained temporal and variables structures, and most methods optimise auxiliary objectives such as forecasting or reconstruction. We posit that these surrogate objectives become unreliable as model capacity increases. Highly expressive models risk reconstructing or forecasting anomalies, undermining the foundations of both strategies. Instead, we propose to use LLMs purely as feature extractors and directly analyse their induced feature spaces. In this work, investigate the ability of LLMs to provide useful representations for MTSADs in a zero-shot setting through classification. To this end, we study the separability of the extracted features, under the assumption that anomalous features should be isolated from normal ones. We analyse their temporal and contextual discriminative ability using simple step-wise tokenisation schemes, with one time-step per token, which preserve fine-grained local information. Their usability for MTSAD is assessed using an unsupervised Isolation Forest, enabling a direct evaluation of the resulting representation quality. We further investigate whether the observed performance can be attributed to the language-related pre-training of the LLMs or arises independently of it. Our results provide empirical insight into the ability of LLMs to represent multivariate time-series for anomaly detection and highlight broader applications of cross-modal foundation models.

</details>


### [166] [Reinforcing What the Student Has Learned: Teacher-Guided On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07722)
*Bozhen Fang,Linhao Zhong,Xuehao Wang,Huanyi Zheng,Hao Chen,Chunhua Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SD-OPD，用中心化的学生-基座差异作为更新系数并按符号一致性筛选，缓解OPD中风格位置振幅过大问题，数学六任务平均pass@1较OPD提升7.76–10.37个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线策略蒸馏（OPD）通过教师对学生生成回复的稠密反馈提升推理能力，但反馈存在振幅失衡：在数学推理回复中，风格相关位置的平均绝对教师-学生对数概率差是数学内容位置的3.5倍。按该差异缩放更新会使大部分更新强度用于模仿教师的表达风格而非推理能力。

Method: 以学生自身预训练基座为参照：学生-基座差异记录后训练阶段获得的token偏好，且不依赖学生与教师的距离。据此提出Student-Delta On-Policy Distillation（SD-OPD）：使用中心化的学生-基座差异作为更新系数；仅当该系数符号与中心化教师-学生反馈符号一致时保留；每轮训练重新计算学生信号。

Result: 在5种教师-学生配置下（Qwen3学生模型1.7B至8B参数），SD-OPD在六项数学任务的平均pass@1（avg@16）上较OPD提升7.76至10.37个百分点。

Conclusion: 以学生自身基座作为参照的更新系数能避免OPD中风格位置主导更新强度的问题，符号一致性筛选进一步保证监督方向可靠，SD-OPD在不同规模学生模型上稳定优于OPD。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) improves language-model reasoning through dense teacher feedback on student-generated responses.We first observe an amplitude disparity in this feedback: on math reasoning responses, style-related positions exhibit 3.5 times the mean absolute teacher--student log-probability difference of mathematical positions.Scaling OPD updates by these discrepancies thus directs much of the update strength toward matching the teacher's expression. We instead take the student's own pretrained base as a reference: the student--base difference records the token preferences acquired during post-training and provides supervision that does not depend on the student's distance from the teacher. Building on this reference, we propose Student-Delta On-Policy Distillation (SD-OPD), which uses centered student--base differences as update coefficients, keeps each coefficient only when its sign agrees with centered teacher--student feedback, and recomputes the student signal every training round. Across 5 teacher--student configurations with Qwen3 students of 1.7B to 8B parameters, SD-OPD improves six-task mathematics average pass@1 (avg@16) over OPD by 7.76 to 10.37 percentage points. Code is available at https://github.com/aim-uofa/sd-opd.

</details>


### [167] [Static Recovery Is Not Dynamic Stability: Dynamics-Aware Benchmarking of Protein Motif Scaffolding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07723)
*Emile de Bruyn,Moritz Schäffler,Jannik Schneider,Sebastian R. Schmidt,Chanwoong Hwang,Aleena Siji,Utkarsh Upadhyay,Stefan Bauer,Alexander Schug,Stefan Kesselheim,Artur Yakimovich,Alina Bazarova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BILBY-PRO（220个来自174个PROSITE条目、系统构建的结构保守功能motif基准）与BILBY-MD（基于参考模拟的全原子MD评估框架），从靶点覆盖、独特解产率与MD支持的动力学保真度三轴评估motif脚手架生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有motif脚手架基准多用小型人工挑选的motif集合和静态refolding判据，而蛋白功能常依赖构象系综与构象转变，故需同时扩展基准覆盖范围并改进成功判据。

Method: 构建BILBY-PRO：从PROSITE关联的实验结构中系统提取结构保守功能motif，共220例、涵盖174个PROSITE motif模式条目；用其评估6个近期脚手架生成模型。另提出BILBY-MD：基于参考模拟的全原子MD评估，比较motif漂移、scaffold漂移、motif-接触保持以及motif二面角自由能景观，并应用于原MotifBench中静态成功的55个模型-问题对，设定MD支持判据与全套检查。

Result: 六模型在靶点覆盖与独特解产率上呈现不同模式：RFDiffusion与Proteina解出更多靶点，ESM3在所解靶点上产生更多独特成功scaffold。55个静态成功的模型-问题对中，31个满足MD支持判据，仅8个通过全部检查；紧凑、自包含motif常获MD支持，界面型或上下文依赖motif更易失败。

Conclusion: 静态refolding成功只能作为局部motif恢复的证据，不能确立完整scaffold的动力学保真度；motif脚手架评估应综合靶点覆盖、独特解产率与MD支持的动力学保真度三个互补维度。

Abstract: Protein motif scaffolding benchmarks assess whether generative models can build scaffolds around functionally important residues. Their conclusions depend on which motifs are tested and which criteria define success. Existing benchmarks typically use small hand-curated motif sets and static refolding criteria, but protein function often depends on conformational ensembles and transitions. Here, we address both benchmark scope and success criteria. We introduce BILBY-PRO, a systematically derived benchmark of structurally conserved functional motifs from PROSITE-linked experimental structures, comprising 220 cases from 174 PROSITE motif-pattern entries. Evaluating six recent scaffold-generation models reveals distinct patterns in target coverage and unique-solution yield: RFDiffusion and Proteina solve more targets, whereas ESM3 produces more unique successful scaffolds on the targets it solves. We further introduce BILBY-MD, a reference-based all-atom molecular dynamics evaluation framework. Applied to statically solved designs from the original MotifBench benchmark, it compares motif drift, scaffold drift, motif-contact preservation, and motif-dihedral free-energy landscapes against reference simulations. Of 55 statically solved model-problem pairs, 31 satisfy our MD-supported criterion and only 8 pass all checks. Compact, self-contained motifs are often MD-supported, whereas interface-like or context-dependent motifs fail more frequently. Static refolding success therefore provides useful evidence of local motif recovery but does not establish dynamical fidelity of the full scaffold. BILBY highlights three complementary axes of motif-scaffolding evaluation: target coverage, unique-solution yield, and MD-supported dynamical fidelity.

</details>


### [168] [Comparing Classification and Feature Learning by Analyzing Their Loss Functions in Normalized Spaces with Different Scale Factors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07741)
*Qiufu Li,Huibin Xiao,Linlin Shen,Weijia Fan,Xi Jia,Yongdong Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出分类精度只反映样本级局部性质，而特征学习追求数据集级全局性质，据此从特征学习第一性原理重新设计BCE损失，并在归一化特征空间分析BCE与CE的极小值与收敛行为，揭示尺度因子γ对分类与特征质量的差异化影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监督特征学习通常以训练分类模型实现，默认"分类精度越高特征越好"。但分类精度刻画的是模型在数据集上的样本级局部性质，特征学习关注的却是类内紧凑、类间可分等数据集级全局性质，二者存在错位，需要重新审视损失函数设计。

Method: 从特征学习第一性原理出发重新设计二分类交叉熵（BCE）损失，作为分类中CE损失的对应形式；在归一化特征空间中分析BCE与CE的极小值，论证二者均可导致神经坍缩（Neural Collapse）；进一步研究不同尺度因子γ下两种损失的收敛行为。

Result: BCE与CE的极小值均可实现不同类特征的最大紧凑与最大分离；固定训练策略下，极大γ可获得完美分类精度但收敛到理想特征几何更慢，极小γ使分类对微小扰动高度敏感，适中γ才能兼顾可靠分类与良好特征结构。大量实验验证了上述结论，并显示BCE在长尾与开集识别上具优势。

Conclusion: 分类精度与特征质量并非同向一致，需从全局特征性质出发设计损失；BCE更契合特征学习的全局需求，在长尾与开集识别等场景更具优势，且尺度因子γ需取适中值以平衡分类可靠性与特征结构质量。

Abstract: Classification and feature learning are two closely interrelated fundamental tasks. Feature learning in supervised settings is typically achieved by training classification models, under the common assumption that higher classification accuracy implies better feature quality. In this paper, we first point out that classification accuracy reflects the sample-wise local properties of a model on a dataset, whereas feature learning aims to learn features with good dataset-level global properties, such as intra-class compactness and inter-class separability. From the first principle of feature learning, we redesign the binary cross-entropy (BCE) loss, a counterpart of the cross-entropy (CE) loss used in classification. Then, in normalized feature space, we analyze the minima of the BCE and CE losses and show that both can lead to neural collapse, where sample features from different classes become maximally compact and maximally separated. More importantly, by investigating the convergence behaviors of the two losses as the scale factor $γ$ varies in the normalized space, we reveal how classification accuracy and feature quality change differently across spaces of different sizes. Under a fixed training strategy, a very large $γ$ achieves perfect classification accuracy but slows convergence toward the ideal feature geometry, whereas a very small $γ$ makes classification highly sensitive to small perturbations. A moderate $γ$ is therefore necessary for simultaneously achieving reliable classification and well-structured features. Extensive experiments validate these findings and demonstrate that BCE loss exhibits advantages in long-tail and open-set recognitions, as it better aligns with the global requirements of feature learning.

</details>


### [169] [Ordering Multivariate Time Series by Volatility Memory: A Temporal Convolutional Approach to Volatility Heredity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07746)
*Kaoutar El Azaar,Mohamed Hamlich,Moncef Garouani,Franck Ravat,Asmae Chakir,Mohamed Tabaa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出QS-TCN方法，将波动率记忆的统计估计与时间卷积学习相结合，用于对多变量时间序列进行排序（提取时间先后关系）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 波动率动态蕴含条件均值中无法显现的时间关系信息，但各变量自身的持续性会掩盖其对其他变量的依赖，使这类信息难以提取。

Method: QS-TCN将准极大似然估计的持续性（波动率记忆）与Matrix Pencil方法提取的时间模态作为结构化滤波嵌入网络，并与可学习滤波共同表征目标历史；据此对变量间的方向性证据进行比较，并汇总为全局优先顺序（并不识别因果图）。

Result: 在具有线性与非线性波动率交互的合成系统及参考关系已知的Finance、fMRI基准上评估；四种合成波动率机制下，QS-TCN的平均排序质量高于外部lead–lag排序及其平方输入变体，但在两种机制下相对该变体的增益不确定；另对货币、黄金与加密货币市场给出描述性应用结果。

Conclusion: 结合统计波动率记忆与时间卷积学习可有效提升多变量时间序列的排序质量；输出为排序而非因果图，在部分机制下优势尚不稳健，金融市场的应用仅为描述性。

Abstract: Volatility dynamics can carry information about temporal relationships that is not apparent in the conditional mean. Extracting this information is difficult because the persistence of each variable can obscure its dependence on other variables. This paper presents QS-TCN, a method for ordering multivariate time series that combines statistical estimates of volatility memory with temporal convolutional learning. Persistence estimates obtained by quasi-maximum likelihood and temporal modes extracted by the Matrix Pencil method define structured filters within the network. Together with learned filters, they represent the history of the target and support comparisons of directional evidence between variables. These comparisons are combined into a global order of precedence, which does not identify a causal graph. The method is evaluated on synthetic systems with linear and nonlinear volatility interactions and on the Finance and fMRI benchmarks, whose reference relations are known. Across the four synthetic volatility regimes studied, QS-TCN reaches a higher mean ordering quality than an external lead--lag ordering and its squared-input adaptation, although the gain over the adaptation is uncertain in two regimes. Applications to currency, gold and cryptocurrency markets are reported descriptively.

</details>


### [170] [ConvPE: Convolutional Position Embedding Improves Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07749)
*Zehao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ConvPE 通过对隐藏状态的低维投影做轻量因果卷积，向注意力分数注入窗口化、内容相关的位置偏置，作为即插即用模块在语言建模、长上下文扩展与视觉任务上同时提升性能与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有位置编码（如 RoPE 等 1D/2D 方案）主要依赖与内容无关的固定位置信号，难以刻画局部窗口内与内容相关的位置关系；同时希望在不牺牲效率、不破坏现有注意力机制的前提下提升 Transformer 表达力。

Method: 对隐藏状态做低维投影后进行轻量因果卷积，将所得偏置注入注意力分数，形成窗口化、内容相关的位置嵌入；该模块可作为插件与 RoPE、注意力掩码、KV 拼接兼容，并可通过高维卷积推广到高维位置嵌入。

Result: 在复现实验中，ConvPE 优于 5 个一维基线方法与 8 个二维基线方法；在语言建模、长上下文扩展与视觉任务上均提升了性能与效率。

Conclusion: ConvPE 是一种轻量、通用、即插即用的位置编码增强方案，兼具性能与效率优势，且对现有注意力机制改动小、扩展性强；代码已开源。

Abstract: ConvPE (Convolutional Position Embedding) improves both the performance and efficiency of Transformer models in language modeling, long-context extension, and vision. It injects a windowed, content-dependent bias into attention scores through a lightweight causal convolution over a low-dimensional projection of the hidden states. As a lightweight plug-in, ConvPE is compatible with RoPE, attention masks, and KV concatenation. It also supports high-dimensional position embeddings via high-dimensional convolutions. In a reproduction, ConvPE outperforms five 1D baselines and eight 2D baselines. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/wzhagi/convpe.

</details>


### [171] [Foresee to Trade: Forecast-Augmented Policy Optimization for Portfolio Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07750)
*Zeyu Liu,Xuanzhi Feng,Haoran Xu,Lai Sing Kwong,Yang Li,Hualei Zhang,Hao Chen,Jie Zhang,Xiaoyi Pang,Song Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出Forecast-Augmented Relative Policy Optimization（FRPO），通过资产机会层面的预测监督与账户层面的相对结果监督联合强化交易决策，在五个投资设定上取得最高平均累计与年化收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线投资服务需将资产机会转化为账户投资结果，但同一市场机会对不同账户应触发不同动作，单一监督信号难以同时刻画资产机会与账户决策。

Method: FRPO包含三部分：Financial State Head（FSH）学习未来相对收益目标，提供资产级机会监督；Same-Path Group-Relative Optimization在同一市场路径与相同初始账户下比较交易轨迹，提供账户级相对结果监督；Adaptive Gate基于预测方差与梯度对齐协调辅助监督。FSH仅用于训练，推理时仅用编码器与策略。

Result: 在DJIA30、TOPIX Core30及三种投资风格组合共五个设定上，十种子评估显示FRPO的平均累计收益与年化收益均优于对比方法。

Conclusion: 资产机会监督与交易结果监督的联合信号可有效增强投资智能体的交易决策能力。

Abstract: Online investment services must translate asset opportunities into investment outcomes for users' accounts, yet the same market opportunity can warrant different account actions. We study how supervision from future asset opportunities can strengthen account-level trading decisions and propose Forecast-Augmented Relative Policy Optimization (FRPO). FRPO separately supervises asset opportunities and account decisions through a jointly learned representation: a Financial State Head (FSH) learns future relative-return targets to provide asset-level opportunity supervision, while Same-Path Group-Relative Optimization compares trading trajectories under the same market path and initial account to provide account-level relative-outcome supervision. An Adaptive Gate coordinates auxiliary supervision using predictive variance and gradient alignment. FSH is used only during training; inference uses the encoder and policy for trading.
