Across DJIA30, TOPIX Core30, and three investment-style portfolios, ten-seed evaluations show that FRPO achieves the highest mean cumulative and annualized returns among the compared methods in all five settings. These results support strengthening investment agents' trading decisions through joint supervision from asset opportunities and trading outcomes.

</details>


### [172] [Neural Collapse on Imbalanced Datasets and Open-Set Scenario](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07753)
*Qiufu Li,Huibing Xiao,Linlin Shen,Yongdong Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将神经坍缩(NC)理论从平衡/闭集推广到类别不平衡与开集场景，证明在类平均损失最小时标准NC结构仍然成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准NC建立在全局平均损失的平衡数据集假设上，而现实中的类别不平衡（及无训练样本的未知类）一般会破坏该几何结构，因此需要重新理论刻画不平衡与开集下的NC。

Method: 以"类内平均损失"（而非全局平均损失）为优化对象重新建立NC分析框架，分别对交叉熵(CE)与二元交叉熵(BCE)推导极小点条件；进一步构造含无训练样本类的开集式场景，并分析按样本频率倒数重平衡的Softmax/Sigmoid对比损失。

Result: 证明在类平均损失达到最小时：(1) 平衡或不平衡数据集上均呈现标准NC（CE与BCE均成立）；(2) 开集场景下已知类仍呈现标准NC，未知类的分类器向量彼此重合且与已知类正交；(3) 重平衡对比损失在极小点处不同类特征同样构成标准NC。

Conclusion: NC是更一般条件下的内在性质：只要以类平均损失为优化目标，不平衡与开集（未知类）设置下依然保持标准NC结构，为不平衡/开集学习中的特征几何提供了统一的理论解释。

Abstract: Neural collapse (NC) describes the elegant geometric structure that sample features and classifier vectors exhibit when a classification model's loss reaches its minimum. Class imbalance, however, generally distorts standard NC. In this paper, we theoretically reformulate NC on imbalanced datasets and in open-set settings. Our analysis is based on an optimization problem over class-wise averaged losses, rather than the global averaged losses used in prior NC studies. We prove that when class-wise averaged losses attain their minima, standard NC is achieved on balanced or imbalanced datasets, for both cross-entropy (CE) and binary cross-entropy (BCE) losses. We further extend the analysis to an open-set-inspired scenario, where some classes contain no training samples yet are still assigned classifier vectors in the losses. We find that in this scenario, when class-wise averaged losses reach their minima, standard NC still emerges on the known classes with training samples, while the classifier vectors of unknown classes without training samples overlap with each other and become orthogonal to those of the known classes. Finally, we analyze Softmax- and Sigmoid-based contrastive losses re-balanced by inverse sample frequency, proving that at their minima on balanced or imbalanced datasets, sample features of different classes likewise form standard NC.

</details>


### [173] [Scaling Laws for SAE Training Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07756)
*Nikita Koriagin,Nikita Balagansky,Daniil Gavrilov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 稀疏自编码器训练中激活存储是瓶颈。本文研究固定训练预算下所需唯一激活数量的取舍：质量随缓冲区增大迅速提升后进入平台期，据此提出"有限数据的缩放律"，并给出实用训练规则——选择达到质量平台的最小缓冲区并在剩余预算中重放，可降低激活存储8–64倍且质量损失可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAE训练中激活存储常成为瓶颈；需要明确在训练总预算固定时，究竟需要多少唯一激活才够用，避免缓冲区随训练预算无谓增长。

Method: 通过控制缓冲区大小（唯一激活数量）与重复次数的实验，刻画多样性与重复之间的权衡，并提出一个面向有限数据的缩放律（scaling law for limited data）来描述该权衡；在多种字典大小、token预算、Llama-3.1早/中/后期层、不同Qwen3模型规模、以及BatchTopK、TopK、JumpReLU三类SAE上验证，并用数学文本与Python代码做激活来源变化的扫描实验。

Result: 质量随缓冲区增长迅速提升后进入平台期，此时重放缓冲区几乎与全程使用新鲜激活等效；超过该点后新增唯一激活对重建与可解释性收益甚微。评估涵盖解释方差（EV）、重建MSE、特征吸收、自动可解释性与稀疏探针。实验中将激活存储降低8–64倍，质量损失可忽略。

Conclusion: 缓冲区无需随训练预算增长；实用规则是选取达到质量平台的最小缓冲区，并在剩余预算中重放该缓冲区，从而在几乎不损失质量的前提下大幅降低激活存储开销。

Abstract: Activation storage is often a bottleneck in sparse autoencoder (SAE) training. We study how many unique activations an SAE needs when its total training budget is fixed. Quality improves rapidly as the buffer grows, then reaches a plateau where replaying the buffer is nearly as effective as using fresh activations throughout training. Beyond this point, additional unique activations bring little benefit to reconstruction or interpretability, so the buffer need not grow with the training budget. We describe this tradeoff between diversity and repetition with a scaling law for limited data. We validate it across dictionary sizes and token budgets, early, middle, and late layers of Llama-3.1, Qwen3 model sizes, and BatchTopK, TopK, and JumpReLU SAEs. Sweeps using mathematical text and Python code test the effect of changing the activation source. Evaluation covers explained variance (EV), reconstruction MSE, feature absorption, automated interpretability, and sparse probing. These results give a practical training rule: choose the smallest buffer that reaches the quality plateau and replay it for the remaining budget. In our experiments, this reduces activation storage by $8\text{--}64\times$ with negligible quality loss.

</details>


### [174] [Optimal Learning in Games with Arbitrary Delays: Best-of-Both-Worlds for Self-Play and Adversarial Opponents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07773)
*Emmanuel Esposito,Andrew Jacobsen,Hao Qiu,Alexander Ryabchenko,Mengxiao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 MAO-OMD 单一算法，在带延迟反馈的零和博弈中同时实现自博弈快速率与对抗任意对手的极小极大最优保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带延迟反馈的零和博弈学习：已有方法难以在自博弈中取得平滑博弈的最优快速率，同时又要对任意对手保持极小极大最优保证；标准黑盒归约到无延迟设定无法兼得两种最优率。

Method: Multi-Anchor Optimistic Online Mirror Descent（MAO-OMD）：采用多锚点（multi-anchor）结构结合乐观在线镜像下降，直接处理任意且未知的反馈延迟，而非通过黑盒归约到无延迟问题。

Result: 双方均使用 MAO-OMD 时，在平滑零和博弈中获得 O(σ_max+1) 的社会遗憾与个体遗憾（σ_max 为最大未完成梯度数）；对任意对手保证极小极大最优的 O(√(T+d_tot)) 个体遗憾（d_tot 为总延迟）。并证明标准黑盒归约无法同时达到这两种最优率；结果可推广至多人博弈及玩家相关延迟序列。

Conclusion: MAO-OMD 首次以单一算法在延迟反馈下统一自博弈快速收敛与鲁棒极小极大最优性，且优于黑盒归约范式，适用于多人及异构延迟场景。

Abstract: We study learning in zero-sum games with delayed feedback. We introduce Multi-Anchor Optimistic Online Mirror Descent (MAO-OMD), a single algorithm which obtains optimal fast rates in self-play on smooth games, while simultaneously maintaining minimax optimal guarantees against arbitrary opponents. In particular, in smooth zero-sum games with arbitrary and unknown feedback delays, MAO-OMD obtains $O(σ_{\max}+1)$ social and individual regrets when used by both players, where $σ_{\max}$ denotes the maximum number of outstanding gradients, while also guaranteeing minimax optimal $O(\sqrt{T+d_{\mathrm{tot}}})$ individual regret against arbitrary opponents, where $d_{\mathrm{tot}}$ is the total delay. Moreover, we show that these optimal rates cannot be obtained simultaneously using the standard black-box reduction to the undelayed setting. We also extend our results to multi-player games, and to games with player-dependent delay sequences.

</details>


### [175] [Adapters as Data: The Latent Space of LoRAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07775)
*Moritz Grävinghoff,Konstantin Wehmeyer,Aron Asefaw,Boris Knyazev,Damian Borth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将LoRA适配器视为一种数据模态，在身份适配器总体上训练transformer自编码器映射到非线性潜空间，与直接在原始权重上做相同操作对比；潜空间保留采样与属性编辑能力，且对位移更鲁棒，并扩展为可容纳混合秩适配器的共享潜空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作把LoRA适配器当作成品工件而非数据模态；PCA线性基已能较好组织其低维流形并支持采样、编辑、反演等操作，因此需要回答：学到的非线性潜表示在此之上还能额外提供什么。

Method: 在LoRA身份适配器总体上训练transformer自编码器，把每个适配器映射到潜空间；将每个操作与直接施加于原始适配器权重的相同操作逐一对比。针对定维权重表示无法原生容纳多秩适配器的问题，将序列自编码器扩展为在单一共享潜空间中承载混合秩适配器。

Result: 潜空间保持了权重空间中已有的能力（采样多样身份、编辑单个属性）；此外潜空间对位移的鲁棒性显著强于权重空间，退化表现为失真但仍有效的人脸而非纹理。扩展后的模型可在同一共享潜空间中表示混合秩适配器。

Conclusion: 研究为把不同架构的适配器权重当作单一通用模态（而非绑定固定秩）提供了路径，并指出学习到的非线性潜表示是进一步泛化到异构适配器总体的潜在机制。

Abstract: LoRA adapters are usually treated as finished artifacts rather than as a data modality. Prior work has shown that LoRA adapters populate a low-dimensional manifold that a linear PCA basis already organizes well enough to support different operations such as sampling, editing, and inversion. We ask what a learned, nonlinear latent adds beyond this. We train a transformer autoencoder on the same population of LoRA identity adapters, mapping each into a latent space and compare every operation against the identical operation applied directly to the raw adapter weights. The latent preserves the capabilities already available in weight space, sampling diverse identities and editing individual attributes. Beyond preservation, we show that the latent is far more robust to displacement than weight space, degrading into distorted but still valid faces rather than texture. Fixed-dimensional weight-space representations cannot natively host adapters of more than one rank, so we extend our sequential autoencoder to hold mixed-rank adapters within one shared latent. This gives a path toward treating adapter weights of varying architecture as a single, general modality rather than one tied to a fixed rank, and points to a learned, nonlinear latent as the mechanism that could carry this generalization further, toward heterogeneous populations of adapters.

</details>


### [176] [PBPO: Prefix-Block Policy Optimization for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07783)
*Katherine Tieu,Dongqi Fu,Si Zhang,Hanqing Zeng,Tianxin Wei,Chenglin Wei,Yinglong Xia,Hong Li,Jingrui He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PBPO：为扩散大语言模型提出块级策略优化，用前缀块状态与单次前向构造块似然的可分解one-step代理，对每个token分别施加重要性加权与裁剪，并通过重采样扰动视图增强更新多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: dLLM按块自回归解码、块内迭代去噪，块级策略优化是自然选择，但完整块的精确似然因需对中间状态与reveal顺序边缘化而不可解，需构造可行替代。

Method: 构造prefix-block状态，用单次模型评估给出块似然的因子化一步代理；块代理比率为各token代理比率之积，使单点变化传播至整块后再裁剪；块级条件化但对每个token分别做代理重要性加权与裁剪；并借鉴masked-dLLM预训练，重采样prompt与已揭示前缀的加噪视图以增加重复更新的多样性。

Result: 理论分析表明，在给定假设下块乘积加权相比token局部加权会放大梯度贡献的方差；在两种LLaDA系列初始化、六个基准、三种生成长度上，PBPO性能与现有dLLM强化学习方法相比具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 块级策略优化可在dLLM上以可计算的代理形式落地，PBPO在保持块级条件化与token级稳定性之间取得平衡，具备与现有方法相当的性能。

Abstract: Diffusion Large Language Models (dLLMs) commonly decode responses by proceeding autoregressively across blocks while iteratively denoising multiple tokens within each block. This structure makes block-level policy optimization a natural adaptation of autoregressive policy optimization. However, the exact likelihood of a completed block is intractable because it marginalizes over the intermediate states and reveal orders of the denoising process. To realize the block-level formulation, we construct a prefix-block state and use a single model evaluation to define a factorized one-step surrogate for the block likelihood. The resulting block surrogate ratio is a product of its token surrogate ratios, allowing variation at individual positions to propagate across the entire block before clipping. We therefore introduce Prefix-Block Policy Optimization (PBPO), which preserves block-level conditioning but applies surrogate importance weighting and clipping separately to each token. PBPO further diversifies repeated updates by resampling corrupted views of the prompt and revealed prefix, a design motivated by masked-dLLM pre-training. Under our analysis assumptions, block-product weighting amplifies the variance of gradient contributions relative to token-local weighting. Across two LLaDA-family initializations, six benchmarks, and three generation lengths, PBPO achieves competitive performance against existing dLLM RL methods.

</details>


### [177] [EnergyBench: a live, gate-aligned benchmark of day-ahead forecasts for European power markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07784)
*Evgenii Genov,Ander Martinez Alonso,Christoph Bergmeir*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EnergyBench 提出一个"实盘"能源预测基准：每日 11:30 CET/CEST 日在日前市场闸门关闭前，由基准统一运行模型预测次日电价、风/光发电与负荷（15 分钟粒度、六个竞价区），按 Bradley-Terry/Elo 评级对模型排名。首 32 天结果显示：统一冻结的气象协变量使 10 个基础模型误差降低 37–51%，零样本基础模型可媲美同协变量训练的 LightGBM，模型规模影响较小，协变量模型的中位数集成排名最高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有能源预测评估依赖回测与固定公开数据集：回测可能使用预测时点尚不存在的信息；固定测试集被反复复用、且可能已进入基础模型预训练数据，导致基准过拟合。因此需要一种在真实市场截止时刻、用统一且已冻结的信息集进行"实盘"评估的基准。

Method: 构建 EnergyBench 在线基准：每日 11:30 CET/CEST（SDAC 日前闸门关闭前 30 分钟），由基准自身运行各模型，在冻结并公开的同一信息集（含三个数值天气预报模型）上预测次日电价、风电、光伏与负荷，覆盖 BE、NL、FR、DE-LU、DK1、DK2 六个区的 15 分钟网格。误差按序列对季节朴素基线做池化，模型以 Elo 刻度的 Bradley-Terry 评级排名，并给出按日自助法区间。参赛对象涵盖基线、基于冻结气象的线性与梯度提升模型、11 个家族共 18 个时间序列基础模型、集成模型以及电网运营商自身预测。

Result: 首 32 个实盘日中，在匹配赛道上气象协变量使 10 个分别"有/无协变量"运行的基础模型误差降低 37–51%（风电 64–73%，光伏 48–61%）；零样本使用时，这些基础模型达到或超过同协变量训练的 LightGBM；模型规模（3M–331M 参数）影响明显更小；协变量模型的中位数集成排名最高。电网运营商的闸门关闭前预测仍在风电与光伏上领先，最佳单一基础模型误差高出 16–22%；负荷上差距为 4–6%，落在区间之内。

Conclusion: 实盘、信息集冻结且由基准统一运行的评估方式可避免回测信息泄漏与固定测试集过拟合问题。结论表明：统一气象协变量是当前性能的主要驱动因素，零样本基础模型已具竞争力，模型规模并非关键；与电网运营商业务预测的差距主要在风电与光伏，负荷上已基本可比。

Abstract: Energy forecasting models are usually compared on backtests and fixed public datasets. Backtests can use data that did not yet exist when the forecast would have been made, and a fixed test set, reused across studies and possibly contained in the pretraining data of foundation models, invites overfitting to the benchmark. EnergyBench evaluates every entry live instead. Each day at 11:30 CET/CEST, thirty minutes before the day-ahead (SDAC) gate closes, every model forecasts the next day's price, wind and solar generation and load on the 15-minute grid for six bidding zones (BE, NL, FR, DE-LU, DK1, DK2). Unlike existing live benchmarks, EnergyBench runs the models itself, on one information set that it freezes at the cut-off and publishes, including three numerical weather prediction models, so that foundation models can be compared with and without the same weather covariates under the actual market deadline. Errors are pooled per series against a seasonal-naive baseline, and models are ranked by a Bradley-Terry rating on the Elo scale with a day-bootstrap interval. The field spans baselines, linear and gradient-boosted models on the frozen weather, eighteen time-series foundation models from eleven families, ensembles, and the grid operators' own forecasts. Over the first thirty-two live days, the covariates reduced the error of each of the ten foundation models run with and without them by 37-51% on matched races (64-73% on wind, 48-61% on solar). Used zero-shot, these models match or outperform a LightGBM model trained on the same covariates. Model size, from 3M to 331M parameters, has a much smaller effect, and median ensembles of the covariate models rank highest. The grid operators' pre-gate forecasts remain ahead on wind and solar, where the best single foundation models have 16-22% more error; on load the gap is 4-6%, within the intervals.

</details>


### [178] [Computation, not weight access: large language models as auditors of clinical prediction networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07847)
*Zhiyu Liu,Xinyu Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过分离"直接读权重"与"运行计算"两种条件，在模拟队列训练的Tanh网络上检验GPT-6等模型的可审计性：无工具时仅能可靠读取约10^5以下规模的小网络，借助仅计算工具时可在高达38,401权重网络上全部成功，且黑盒与白盒同样有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿大模型被报道可读取、编辑、写入小型神经网络权重，理论上可仅凭文件审计临床预测模型；但已有公开演示混淆了"读取原始权重"与"运行代码"两种条件，无法判断审计能力究竟来自权重访问还是计算能力与提示引导。作者据此在内部预先设定的模拟实验中把两类条件彻底分离。

Method: 在模拟的蛛网膜下腔出血患者队列上训练权重数从105到38,401的Tanh网络，部分植入真实关系、部分为空网络，另有一个网络使用在某一亚组中低报死亡的标签训练。GPT-6 Astra及另外四个GPT模型在默认或显式系统指令下回答单一提示，条件为：(a) 权重直接写入提示且无工具；(b) 提供仅计算工具，可暴露权重（白盒）或仅暴露网络预测（黑盒）。

Result: 无工具条件下，Astra在353权重网络中能复原植入关系，但在3,457权重时变得不可靠；默认指令下12项审计与修复任务中有11项被其以"不可识别"为由拒绝，显式指令消除了这些拒答。使用工具后，在高达38,401权重的网络上29次运行全部复原植入关系，15次空网络中未出现误报，6次运行中均识别出存在偏倚的亚组。黑盒访问与白盒访问表现相当，其他GPT-6模型读取精度相近；一个设计良好的脚本在审计任务上与对照模型相当，并达到最佳LLM修复水平。

Conclusion: 对此类网络的审计而言，决定成败的是计算能力与提示引导（elicitation），而非是否直接访问权重；权重本身并非瓶颈，黑盒接口即可提供足够信息。

Abstract: Frontier large language models (LLMs) have been reported to read, edit and write the weights of small neural networks, raising the prospect of auditing clinical prediction models from their files alone. Public demonstrations, however, mixed reading raw weights with running code. We separated these conditions in internally pre-specified simulation experiments. Tanh networks of 105 to 38,401 weights were trained on simulated cohorts of patients with subarachnoid haemorrhage, each with a planted relationship or none, and one network was trained on labels that under-recorded deaths in a subgroup. GPT-6 Astra and four other GPT models answered single prompts under a default or an explicit system instruction, either with the weights in the prompt and no tools, or with a calculation-only tool that exposed the weights (white box) or only the network's predictions (black box). Without tools, Astra recovered planted relationships in 353-weight networks but became unreliable at 3,457 weights, and under the default instruction it declined 11 of 12 audit and repair tasks as unidentifiable; the explicit instruction removed these refusals. With the tool, it recovered the planted relationship in all 29 runs on networks of up to 38,401 weights, raised no false alarm in 15 runs on null networks, and found the biased subgroup in 6 of 6 runs. Black-box access performed as well as white-box access, and other GPT-6 models read as accurately. A well-designed script matched the comparator models on the audit and the best LLM repair. For auditing these networks, what mattered was computation and elicitation, not access to the weights.

</details>


### [179] [When, Where, How: Active In-Context Learning for Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07886)
*Christian M. M. Frey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究表格基础模型（TFM）在主动上下文学习中，多样性、预测不确定性与目标感知信息增益三类采集准则的价值随标签增加如何变化，并提出TANN与EPIC，在20个基准上验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TFM仅通过上下文中的有标签行适配新任务，而真实场景获取标签成本高（需高度专业领域知识）。主动上下文学习中，指导采集的模型每增加一个标签就会变化，因此需重新审视经典采集准则（多样性、不确定性、信息增益）在这一动态设定下的价值。

Method: 提出目标感知最近邻（TANN）采集规则与上下文期望预测信息增益（EPIC），并与经典采集规则对比；在20个基准、两种成熟TFM以及经典GBDT上进行实验评估。

Result: 三点发现：(i) 何时——基于多样性的采集在最初几轮采集迭代中最佳；(ii) 何处——通过简单的目标感知最近邻规则标注那些服务于目标输入的行，效果显著；(iii) 如何——EPIC在标签预算下得到最佳模型，在池子最大的8个基准上仅用5-10%的池标签即保留平均80-87%的经机会校正的全数据性能，且所选标签也能提升GBDT的预测性能。

Conclusion: 主动上下文学习中，采集准则的价值随上下文增长而演化：早期宜用多样性，中期宜用目标感知最近邻规则，整体上EPIC在有限预算下最具优势，且所选标签具有跨模型（GBDT）的可迁移价值。

Abstract: Tabular Foundation Models (TFMs) adapt to a new task only through the labeled rows in their context. However, labels are expensive to acquire in real-world scenarios due to the need for highly specific domain knowledge to ensure high-quality data. Active in-context learning transfers classical acquisition rules to this setting, in which the model that guides acquisition changes with every label added. We ask how the value of diversity, predictive uncertainty and target-aware information gain changes as a frozen TFM acquires more context. For that, we study Target-Aware Nearest Neighbor (TANN) acquisition and the Expected Predictive Information Gain in Context (EPIC) and evaluate them against classical rules. Our study across twenty benchmarks with two well-established TFMs and classical GBDTs highlights three findings: (i) When: diversity-based acquisition is best during the first acquisition iterations; (ii) Where: labeling pool rows that serve the target inputs via a simple target-aware nearest-neighbor rule matters substantially; (iii) How: EPIC yields the best models at the label budget, retains on average 80--87% of the chance-corrected full-data performance with only 5-10% of the pool labeled on the eight benchmarks with the largest pools, and selects labels that also improve the predictive performance of gradient-boosted decision trees.

</details>


### [180] [DISCERN: Distributional Synthetic Replay for Retain-Free Incremental Machine Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07892)
*Nicolás Segura-Kunsagi,Juan Luis Suárez-Díaz,Iván Sevillano-García,Francisco Herrera*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DISCERN 用「按类拟合参数分布生成合成嵌入」替代 retain set，支撑无需保留数据的增量类遗忘，在 Transformer 骨干的序列删除中显著更稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实部署中删除请求随时间到达，需增量类遗忘（ICU）：类被顺序移除、已遗忘知识不得复现、其余类性能需保持。同时隐私/存储约束下往往无法访问 retain set；而基于原型回放的方法默认各类在质心周围呈各向同性高斯，与 Transformer 嵌入近似位于超球面、沿类方向集中的几何特征不符。

Method: 提出基于干预的类遗忘方法 DISCERN：以各类别的参数化分布采样合成嵌入替代真实 retain set。对每个类拟合欧氏族（高斯、Laplace、Student's t）与方向族（von Mises–Fisher、Fisher–Bingham），通过统计检验选择分布族，若全部被拒绝则以交叉验证对数似然择优。在 MNIST、CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100 上，用 2 个卷积与 2 个 Transformer 骨干，对比 7 个 retain-free 基线，覆盖单类、多类与增量遗忘设置；并做配对消融验证分布选择的作用。

Result: 一次性删除多类时 DISCERN 具竞争力；在 Transformer 上的 ICU 最稳定：ViT-B/16 上 20 次顺序删除后调和平均保持 0.797，而同设置下最强基线从 0.918 跌至 0.048。配对消融显示按类分布选择能显著抑制已遗忘类的「复现（resurfacing）」，其作用主要是稳定累积遗忘而非带来均匀一致的提升。

Conclusion: 结果表明潜在几何与骨干架构相关，按类自适应选择分布族的合成回放是 retain-free 增量类遗忘的有效手段，其核心价值在于抑制顺序删除中的累积遗忘与旧类复现，而非全面提升单次遗忘性能。

Abstract: Machine unlearning aims to remove the influence of specific training data from a trained model without retraining from scratch. In real deployments, however, deletion requests arrive over time, calling for incremental class unlearning (ICU): classes are removed sequentially, previously forgotten knowledge must remain forgotten, and the remaining classes must be preserved. We present DIstributional SynthetiC rEplay for Retain-free iNcremental unlearning (DISCERN), an intervention-based class unlearning method that replaces access to the retain set with synthetic embeddings sampled from per-class parametric distributions. Unlike prototype-based replay, which assumes an isotropic Gaussian around each class centroid, DISCERN fits Euclidean (Gaussian, Laplace, Student's $t$) and directional (von Mises--Fisher, Fisher--Bingham) families to each class and selects among them through statistical tests and, when none of them accepts, cross-validated log-likelihood. This design is motivated by the architecture-dependent latent geometry: transformer embeddings lie approximately on a hypersphere and concentrate around class directions, unlike those of convolutional networks. We evaluate DISCERN against seven retain-free baselines on MNIST, CIFAR-10, and CIFAR-100 with two convolutional and two transformer backbones, under single-class, multi-class, and incremental unlearning. DISCERN is competitive when classes are removed at once and is the most stable method in ICU on transformers: on ViT-B/16, after 20 sequential deletions it retains a harmonic mean of $0.797$, whereas the strongest method in the simultaneous setting drops from $0.918$ to $0.048$. A paired ablation shows that per-class distribution selection substantially reduces the resurfacing of previously forgotten classes, acting as a stabilizer of accumulated forgetting rather than a uniform improvement.

</details>


### [181] [Same Answer, Different Future: Matched Predictions Do Not Imply Matched Belief Updating in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07913)
*Weihan Li,Junhao Wu,Xinlei Chen,Tianshi Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 语言模型在观测结果、阅读摘要、接收指令或激活干预后可产生相似的概率预测，但这些途径是否形成等价信念状态尚不明确。该文在Bernoulli推断任务（以解析贝叶斯为参照）中比较四种文本途径与两种激活干预，用"有效证据权重"（相当于多少条新观测）度量初始信念的持续性，并追加相同新证据检验更新。发现证据格式决定哪种读出表达其内容，且各途径有效权重差异巨大；一次性steering虽复制即时预测，但在后续证据下效果大部分消失。结论：当前读出的一致性不足以确立信念更新等价性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前预测一致（agreement in current predictions/readouts）常被用作不同提示或干预"诱导相同信念"的证据，但预测一致并不能说明模型将如何回应后续证据。作者要检验：通过观测结果序列、摘要、显式指令、激活干预等不同途径获得的信念状态是否真正等价，即其在证据更新动力学上是否一致。核心问题在于把"信念获取方式"与"信念读出方式"对后续更新的联合影响分离出来。

Method: 在具有解析贝叶斯参照（analytic Bayesian reference）的Bernoulli推断任务上，对三个开源权重（open-weight）语言模型：1) 通过四种文本途径（结果序列、摘要、陈述比率、指令等）与两种激活层面干预（activation-level interventions，含一次性steering）构建信念状态；2) 同时测量下一结果续写（next-outcome continuations）与显式概率估计（explicit probabilistic estimates）两类读出；3) 从每个预备状态追加相同的新证据，观察更新；4) 用"有效证据权重"（effective evidence weight，即以等价新观测条数表示的初始信念持续性）量化各途径信念强度；并检验摘要权重与其声称观测数的缩放关系、提问方式对推断权重的影响（数量级差异）。

Result: 1) 证据格式决定读出通道：结果序列主要影响续写，摘要与陈述比率主要影响显式估计。2) 有效权重在不同途径间差异显著：摘要所得权重随其声称观测数仅弱缩放（scaling weakly）；提问/诱发方式（elicitation questions）可使推断权重相差数个数量级。3) 一次性steering干预虽能复现自然记录（natural record）的即时预测，但在追加后续证据后其效应大部分丧失。说明即时预测一致与更新行为一致相分离。

Conclusion: 当前读出上的一致性不足以确立信念更新的等价性；信念如何被获取与如何被读出，共同决定其后续动力学。评估模型"知道什么"需采用多读出、带后续证据的动态范式，而非仅比较单一预测的一致性；激活干预与文本途径产生的状态在可更新性上并不等同。

Abstract: Language models can produce similar probabilistic predictions after observing outcomes, reading summaries, receiving instructions, or undergoing activation interventions. Whether these routes induce equivalent belief states remains unclear: agreement in current predictions does not establish how a model will respond to subsequent evidence. We investigate this distinction in a Bernoulli inference task with an analytic Bayesian reference. Across three open-weight language models, we prepare states through four textual routes and two activation-level interventions, measure both next-outcome continuations and explicit probabilistic estimates, and append identical new evidence from each prepared state. We quantify the persistence of the initial belief through its effective evidence weight, the number of new observations it is worth. We find that the format of evidence decides which readouts express its content: outcome sequences primarily affect continuations, whereas summaries and stated rates primarily affect explicit estimates. Moreover, effective weights differ substantially across routes, with summaries receiving weights that scale weakly with their stated observation counts and elicitation questions changing inferred weights by orders of magnitude. In a one-shot steering setting, an intervention that reproduces a natural record's immediate prediction loses most of its effect under subsequent evidence. These results show that agreement in current readouts is insufficient to establish equivalence in belief updating: how a belief is acquired and how it is read jointly determine its subsequent dynamics.

</details>


### [182] [Flux as a Codec: Zero-Shot Lossy Compression with Flux](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07929)
*Fuma Kimishima,Jinjia Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 FluxC，以预训练扩散模型 Flux 作为压缩先验的零样本编解码器，并从编码代价/比特分配、重建方式、低码率失真三方面分析率-失真-感知行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DiffC 已表明预训练扩散模型可作零样本编解码器，但编码路径选择与重建方法如何影响率-失真-感知性能尚不清楚。

Method: 以 Flux 为压缩先验构建零样本编解码器；沿三轴分析：编码代价与比特分配、重建方法（ancestral sampling、一步 ODE、多步 ODE）、低码率失真，并在高斯源与自然图像上验证。

Result: 匹配 SNR 下路径选择不改变理想信息率，但改变比特在各时间步上的分配；ODE 重建失真低于 ancestral sampling，低码率下尤为明显；实验与理论预测一致，性能与近期神经图像压缩方法相当甚至更优。

Conclusion: Flux 可作为有理论依据的零样本编解码先验，ODE 重建更适合低码率场景。

Abstract: We propose FluxC, a zero-shot codec that uses Flux as a compression prior. Although DiffC has shown that pretrained diffusion models can serve as zero-shot codecs, we ask how the choice of coding path and reconstruction method affects rate--distortion--perception behavior. We analyze FluxC along three axes: coding cost and bit allocation, reconstruction method, and low rate distortion. At matched signal-to-noise ratio (SNR), we show that the path choice does not affect the ideal information rate, but changes how bits are allocated across time steps. For reconstruction, we compare ancestral sampling, one-step ODE reconstruction, and many-step ODE reconstruction. Our analysis shows that ODE-based reconstruction can achieve lower distortion than ancestral sampling, especially in the low rate regime. Experiments on Gaussian sources and natural images support this prediction. Moreover, FluxC performs competitively with, and in some cases outperforms, recent neural image compression methods, suggesting that Flux can serve as a principled zero-shot codec.

</details>


### [183] [AttnSR: Attention Step Reward for Long-Horizon Agentic RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07932)
*Mengsong Wu,Tianchen Zhou,Zhaochen Su,Zhenhua Liu,Zhe Cui,Aimin Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AttnSR，一种基于策略注意力回溯的步级奖励增强方法，无需额外奖励模型或rollout，即可为长程LLM智能体的多步决策提供直接监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程LLM智能体需进行多步决策，但仅使用结果奖励的强化学习对中间单步缺乏直接监督信号，导致信用分配困难。

Method: AttnSR从训练replay中提取策略模型的注意力，沿注意力图反向追踪以估计早期步骤与最终响应的相关性，据此分配步级奖励；终局步相对运行基线的表现决定这些奖励是强化还是惩罚所选步骤。方法无需单独训练的奖励模型，也无需额外rollout。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct的受控实验中，AttnSR在ALFWorld所有评估checkpoint上均优于PPO基线，最终held-out成功率由91.2%提升至97.1%；消融实验表明收益取决于中间奖励的分配方式而非步奖励的简单累积；在ScienceWorld各checkpoint均有提升，并将环境交互减少29.9%。

Conclusion: 策略注意力可作为长程智能体强化学习中步级奖励分配的实用先验，AttnSR为细粒度信用分配提供了一种高效且有效的方案。

Abstract: Long-horizon LLM agents make multi-step decisions, yet outcome-reward-only reinforcement learning provides little direct supervision for individual steps. We introduce AttnSR, a step-level reward augmentation method that derives reward allocations from policy attention extracted during training replay, without a separately trained reward model or additional rollouts. AttnSR traces backward through the policy model attention graph to estimate the relevance of earlier steps to the final response. The terminal step, measured relative to a running baseline, determines whether these rewards reinforce or penalize the selected steps. In a controlled Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct study, AttnSR consistently outperforms the PPO baseline across all evaluated checkpoints on ALFWorld, raising final held-out success from 91.2% to 97.1%. The ablation experiment shows that the benefit depends on how intermediate rewards are assigned, rather than simple accumulation of step rewards over time. AttnSR also improves ScienceWorld at every checkpoint while cutting environment interactions by 29.9%. These results support policy attention as a practical prior for step-level reward allocation in long-horizon agentic RL. Code is available at https://github.com/fairyshine/AttnSR .

</details>


### [184] [The Blindfolded Controller: Decision Model-Guided Training for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07934)
*Muhammad Ibrahim Khan,Aniket Dixit,Faizan Ahmed,James Brusey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DMGT用决策模型初始化强化学习策略，结合DM无需训练即可行动与RL从奖励中改进的能力，在稀疏奖励任务上提升效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL在稀疏奖励与探索困难任务中从零学习慢且样本效率低；决策模型(DM)虽能凭环境与任务描述在稀疏奖励任务中表现出色，但不从奖励学习，无法逼近最优解。二者互补。

Method: 提出决策模型引导训练(DMGT)：给定环境与任务描述，用DM初始化RL策略，使智能体从最初回合即能到达目标并获得奖励，避免从零探索，随后由RL基于奖励继续改进策略。并通过消融实验总结向DM描述环境与任务的准则。

Result: 在七项任务上，DMGT均优于纯RL，并在五项任务上优于DM。

Conclusion: DM初始化与RL奖励驱动改进的结合有效克服了稀疏奖励下的探索瓶颈，并给出环境与任务描述的系统性指导。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) provides a framework for continuous improvement through interaction with an environment. However, RL agents struggle against tasks that offer exploration challenges and sparse rewards, because learning efficient policies from scratch is slow and sample-inefficient. In contrast, decision models (DMs), given a description of the environment and task, can perform well in sparse-reward tasks, but, as they do not learn from reward, they are not able to improve towards the optimal solution. In this paper, we present Decision Model-Guided Training (DMGT), a technique that uses a DM with a description of the environment and task to initialise an RL policy, combining the DM's ability to act without training with RL's ability to improve from reward. Instead of exploring from scratch, the agent reaches the goal and receives reward from its first episodes, and RL then improves its policy beyond what the DM achieves. We demonstrate through experiments on seven tasks that our approach outperforms RL in all seven and DM in five. In addition, we provide comprehensive guidelines, derived from ablation studies, for describing an environment and task to a DM.

</details>


### [185] [Leakage- and Label-Aware Benchmarking of Class-Imbalance Strategies for Multimodal Chest X-Ray and EHR Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07950)
*Dang-Khoa Bui,Quang-Huy Tran,Minh-Khoi Nguyen-Bui,Duc-Tuan Ngo,Thanh-Trong Tran,Tuan-Khoi Nguyen,Hoang-Anh Ngo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文审计了一个多模态胸片(EHR+CXR)类别不平衡基准，发现记录级划分存在患者泄漏与标签来源缺陷；在患者分离、入院对齐与规则精化标签下，原结论不成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CXR+EHR多模态类别不平衡策略的基准多在记录级划分上评估，而同一患者贡献多张影像，可能导致患者跨集泄漏；同时标签与EHR特征的构造来源未被核查，基准结论的可靠性存疑。

Method: 在MIMIC-CXR的29,083张影像、10,479名患者、8个诊断目标上复现并审计基准：1) 用"同EHR向量查表复制标签"的基线检验记录级划分的泄漏程度；2) 追溯标签至MIMIC-IV诊断编码，核对住院对齐情况；3) 改用患者分离划分、入院对齐与规则精化标签重新评估不平衡策略、树集成与逻辑回归、EHR变量与影像嵌入（含CheXpert预训练DenseNet与RAD-DINO）的增益，并做AUROC探索性检验。

Result: 记录级基准具有误导性：查表基线几乎追平最佳模型，未见患者上各模型退化至全阳性水平。约三分之一影像的标签与自身入院编码不一致。患者分离评估下，无任何不平衡策略使宏观F2提升超过0.01，调优树集成相对逻辑回归无稳定优势，发布版EHR变量仅达全阳性基线水平；而影像时点可得的诊断历史与影像嵌入显著优于基线，规则精化标签下未见过这些影像的CheXpert编码器亦然（RAD-DINO自监督预训练已见过96.6%影像）。将编码器加入诊断历史后F2无显著增益，AUROC增益仅在RAD-DINO下显著。

Conclusion: 记录级划分与标签构造缺陷会系统性高估多模态CXR预测与不平衡策略的效果；应使用患者分离、入院对齐且规则精化的评估协议。作者提供可复用诊断工具、防泄漏协议与检查清单。

Abstract: Benchmarks of class-imbalance strategies for multimodal chest X-ray (CXR) and electronic health record (EHR) prediction are often evaluated on record-level splits of cohorts in which one patient contributes several radiographs. We audit such a benchmark on 29,083 MIMIC-CXR radiographs of 10,479 patients with eight diagnosis targets. First, the record-split benchmark was misleading: a lookup table that copies the label of a development-set radiograph with the same EHR vector nearly matched the best model, and on unseen patients every model fell to the level of predicting every case positive. Second, tracing the labels back to MIMIC-IV explained why: each patient's labels are keyword matches on the diagnosis codes of a single admission, copied, with one EHR vector per patient, to all of the patient's radiographs, so about a third of the radiographs carry labels that differ from the codes of their own admission. Third, under patient-separated evaluation with admission-aligned and rule-refined labels, the initial conclusions do not hold: in the evaluated configurations no imbalance strategy raised the observed mean macro F2 over no augmentation by more than 0.01, tuned tree ensembles showed no consistent advantage over tuned logistic regression, and the released EHR variables stayed at the level of the all-positive baseline, whereas the diagnostic history available at imaging time and image embeddings exceeded it, including, with rule-refined labels, a CheXpert-trained encoder that never saw these radiographs; RAD-DINO had seen 96.6% of them during self-supervised pretraining. Adding either encoder to the diagnostic history gave no statistically significant F2 gain; exploratory AUROC gains were significant with RAD-DINO but not with DenseNet. We provide reusable diagnostics, a leakage-aware protocol and a checklist.

</details>


### [186] [Invariance before structure: two modes of emergent compression in representation learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07952)
*Hardik Rajpal,Dan Goodman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出EARL框架，将学习表征中的涌现分为“状态空间压缩”和“参数空间压缩”两种模式，以Fisher敏感性形式化并给出集合级信息论度量；在带训练瓶颈的循环储备池中发现参数空间压缩先于状态空间压缩约30个epoch出现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习表征在生物与人工神经网络间表现出显著不变性，且能抵抗漂移、异质性与初始化差异的影响；作者希望理解这种不变性从何而来、以何种机制涌现。

Method: 提出Emergent Adaptive Representation Learning（EARL）框架，区分两种涌现模式：状态空间压缩（表征对自身未来获得超越单个单元预测能力的预测力）与参数空间压缩（不同微观参数的网络收敛到同一表征）；二者均形式化为Fisher敏感性，并引入集合级信息论度量；实验平台为带训练瓶颈的循环储备池，跨训练epoch进行测量。

Result: 参数空间压缩在训练最初几个epoch内即上升，且部分独立于任务；状态空间压缩约晚30个epoch才上升，且仅在网络开始学习任务时间结构时出现。

Conclusion: 对“基质”的不变性与任务特定的涌现结构是在学习的不同阶段分别获得的，二者在时间上可分离。

Abstract: Learned representations show striking invariance across biological and artificial neural networks, with different substrates and variations driven by drift, heterogeneity and initialisation. To understand this invariance, we propose Emergent Adaptive Representation Learning (EARL), a framework that distinguishes two modes of emergence in learned representations: state-space compression, where a learned representation gains predictive power over its own future beyond what the network's units can predict individually, and parameter-space compression, where networks with different micro-level parameters converge to the same representation. We formalise both as Fisher sensitivities and introduce ensemble-level information-theoretic measures for each. In a recurrent reservoir with a trained bottleneck, parameter-space compression rises within the first few epochs of training and partly independently of the task, whereas state-space compression rises about 30 epochs later and only when the network begins to learn the temporal structure of the task. These results suggest that invariance to the substrate and task-specific emergent structure are acquired in distinct phases of learning.

</details>


### [187] [CoRD: Coupled Rollouts Fill the Blind Spot of On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07959)
*Guhan Chen,Bohan Li,Jun Rao,Hejin Wang,Songtao Tian,Yexin Xie,Xiaojun Meng,Peng Yuan,Jiansheng Wei,Xuebo Liu,Zixiong Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CoRD（耦合式 rollout 蒸馏）在保留 on-policy 损失的同时，用 speculative decoding 的极大耦合对学生与教师分布做几何插值采样训练状态，使教师可达而学生罕见的轨迹获得监督，从而显著提升数学推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: On-policy 蒸馏仅用学生自身 rollout 训练，导致教师能到达而学生很少访问的状态得不到监督，训练状态分布与学生实际分布之间存在偏差。

Method: 提出 CoRD：保持 on-policy 损失不变，仅将训练状态的分布向教师偏移。通过 speculative decoding 的极大耦合对师生分布做几何插值来采样 rollout：两分布一致时保留学生 token，否则从学生低估的目标分布部分替换采样，从而使干预频率达到任何精确采样器所能允许的最低水平，并随学生能力提升而减少。

Result: 在两个模型家族（Qwen3、Qwen3.5，0.6B–4B）共六个基座学生上，等步数与等数据条件下，CoRD 在五个公开数学基准上相较 on-policy 蒸馏将平均 pass@1 提升 0.9–2.0 分、pass@8 提升 1.2–3.4 分。在冻结的教师可达状态上，4B 学生用 CoRD 训练 16 步即达到 on-policy 蒸馏 80 步仍未达到的差距闭合程度；在其他方向以至少同等频率干预的替换规则均不及 CoRD。

Conclusion: 将训练状态分布而非损失函数作为干预对象是有效的蒸馏改进方向；基于极大耦合的低频替换机制在效率与效果上均优于基线，验证了状态分布对齐设计的关键作用。

Abstract: On-policy distillation trains a student on its own rollouts, so states the teacher would reach but the student does not are seldom supervised. We introduce CoRD (coupled-rollout distillation), which keeps the on-policy loss and moves only the distribution of training states toward the teacher, drawing rollouts from a geometric interpolation of student and teacher through the maximal coupling of speculative decoding. The coupling keeps the student's token wherever the two distributions agree and otherwise substitutes a token from the part of the target the student underweights, so interventions are as rare as any exact sampler allows and recede as the student learns. Across six base students from two model families (Qwen3 and Qwen3.5, 0.6B to 4B), CoRD raises average pass@1 over on-policy distillation by 0.9 to 2.0 points and pass@8 by 1.2 to 3.4 points on five public math benchmarks at equal steps and data. On frozen teacher-reached states, a 4B student trained with CoRD reaches in 16 steps a gap closure that on-policy distillation does not reach in 80, and substitution rules intervening at least as often in other directions fall short of CoRD.

</details>


### [188] [Tight Asymptotic Bounds on the Maximum Number of Linear Regions of Piecewise-Linear Kolmogorov--Arnold networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07973)
*Ye Su,Yong Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文给出了CPWL KAN线性区域数的紧致上下界：随k的增长阶为Θ_n(k^{Σρ_{ℓ-1}})，当所有隐藏层宽度≥d时退化为Θ_n(k^{dT})；并证明在参数预算Λ与固定深度T下，KAN相对ReLU网络的最大区域数之比为Θ_{d,T}(Λ^{d⌊T/2⌋})，T≥2时有多项式优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Schoots et al.(AISTATS 2025)仅给出CPWL KAN线性区域数的宽松上界k^{Σ n_{ℓ-1}n_ℓ}，缺乏下界，无法刻画其可达几何复杂度，也难以公平比较KAN与ReLU网络的表达能力。

Method: 对T层、宽度向量n、每边至多k≥2个仿射段的CPWL KAN，构造匹配的上界与下界；引入ρ_{ℓ-1}=min_{0≤s≤ℓ-1}n_s刻画每层有效维度；再在等参数预算Λ、固定非线性深度T、稠密参数化下统计可训练结点位置，并优化宽度与k，进行KAN与ReLU的对比。

Result: 得到紧阶Θ_n(k^{Σ_{ℓ=1}^T ρ_{ℓ-1}})，隐藏层宽度均≥d时为Θ_n(k^{dT})；相对Schoots上界的改进因子为Θ_n(k^{Σ n_{ℓ-1}n_ℓ − Σ ρ_{ℓ-1}})，任一隐藏层宽度大于1即严格渐近改进；KAN/ReLU最大区域数之比为Θ_{d,T}(Λ^{d⌊T/2⌋})，Λ→∞。

Conclusion: 这些紧界精确刻画了CPWL KAN可达到的几何复杂度，为理解其表达能力提供理论基础并指导架构设计；当T≥2时KAN在区域计数上具有多项式优势。

Abstract: Schoots et al.~\citep{schoots2025relating} (AISTATS 2025) established a generally loose upper bound on the number of linear regions of continuous piecewise-linear (CPWL) Kolmogorov--Arnold networks (KANs), without providing a lower bound. We consider a $T$-layer network with width vector $\mathbf n=(n_0,\ldots,n_T)$, input dimension $n_0=d$, output width $n_T=1$, and at most $k\geq2$ affine segments per edge. Our matching upper and lower bounds determine the tight growth order $Θ_{\mathbf n}(k^{\sum_{\ell=1}^{T}ρ_{\ell-1}})$ in $k$, where $ρ_{\ell-1}:=\min_{0\leq s\leq\ell-1}n_s$. When every hidden width is at least $d$, the tight order simplifies to $Θ_{\mathbf n}(k^{dT})$. The upper bound of \citep{schoots2025relating} is $k^{\sum_{\ell=1}^{T}n_{\ell-1}n_\ell}$. Our upper bound improves it by a factor of $Θ_{\mathbf n}\!\left(k^{\sum_{\ell=1}^{T}n_{\ell-1}n_\ell-\sum_{\ell=1}^{T}ρ_{\ell-1}} \right)$, with strict asymptotic improvement whenever a hidden layer has width greater than one. We further compare CPWL KANs with rectified linear unit (ReLU) networks using dense parameterizations with at most $Λ$ parameters per model and the same fixed nonlinear depth $T$. Counting trainable knot locations and optimizing the widths and $k$, we prove that the maximum region count of CPWL KANs divided by that of ReLU networks is $Θ_{d,T}(Λ^{d\lfloor T/2\rfloor})$ as $Λ\to\infty$, yielding a polynomial advantage for KANs when $T\geq2$. These tight bounds characterize the attainable geometric complexity of CPWL KANs, providing a foundation for understanding their expressive power and guiding architectural design.

</details>


### [189] [QRAE: Query-Relevant Attention Entropy for Context Compression in DeepSearch Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07976)
*Yingjie Tan,Haiquan Qiu,Quanming Yao,Manyi Zhong,Xianzhi Yu,Yaqing Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Query-Relevant Attention Entropy (QRAE)，一种在线上下文压缩方法，通过稀疏证据评估注意力头判断哪些完整检索块应在推理步骤间保留，在多个 DeepSearch 基准上提升成功率并显著降低延迟与 token 消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DeepSearch LLM 智能体在推理中反复检索并将来源带入后续步骤，上下文不断膨胀导致 token 消耗与延迟上升，而许多检索结果对后续推理并无贡献，因此需要在线压缩上下文。

Method: QRAE 使用稀疏证据评估头（evidence-assessment heads），通过成对 prefill 对有界候选批次打分：无查询的对照 prefill 抑制共享的注意力响应，再用归一化熵衡量每个块内正向响应的广度。高分块进入持久化的证据白名单并纳入推理上下文；不确定块留在候选缓存中以便后续重评估。方法无需额外生成式压缩过程，也不修改注意力算子。

Result: 在多个 DeepSearch 基准与多种推理模型上，任务成功率平均提升 2.35 个百分点，端到端延迟与上报 token 用量分别平均降低 30% 和 45%，且效果可推广到近 100B 参数模型。

Conclusion: QRAE 提供了一种无需修改注意力机制、无需额外生成式压缩的高效在线上下文压缩方案，在提升 DeepSearch 智能体任务成功率的同时大幅降低延迟与 token 开销，并具备良好的规模泛化性。

Abstract: DeepSearch LLM agents repeatedly search and carry retrieved sources into subsequent reasoning steps. This expanding context increases token consumption and latency, although many results do not contribute to later reasoning. We propose Query-Relevant Attention Entropy (QRAE), an online context compression method that uses sparse evidence-assessment heads to decide which intact retrieved chunks should persist across reasoning steps. It scores bounded candidate batches through paired prefills: a query-free control suppresses shared attention responses, while normalized entropy measures the breadth of the positive response within each chunk. High-scoring chunks enter a persistent evidence whitelist and are included in the reasoning context. Uncertain chunks remain outside the reasoning context but are retained in a candidate cache, where they can be retrieved for later reevaluation. QRAE requires neither an additional generative compression pass nor modification of the attention operator. Across multiple DeepSearch benchmarks and reasoning models, QRAE improves task success by an average of 2.35 percentage points and reduces end-to-end latency and reported token usage by an average of 30% and 45%, respectively. These benefits generalize to a model with nearly 100B parameters.

</details>


### [190] [Towards Multiscale Molecular Representation Learning through Geometry-Based Fragmentation for Polymer Property Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07998)
*Makoto Takamoto,Chaitanya Sampara,Viktor Zaverkin,Daniel Oñoro-Rubio,Mathias Niepert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出将分子碎片化形式化为受半径约束的k-center优化问题，实现不依赖预设化学规则的几何碎片化多尺度表示，并构建几何碎片注意力结合p-SMILES与3D构象的多模态模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于碎片化的机器学习方法多依赖预定义化学规则，对未见或化学陌生结构可能产生次优碎片，且忽视多尺度分子表示的重要性。

Method: 受k-center思想启发，将分子碎片化表述为半径约束优化问题（簇数非先验固定，而由半径决定）；提出主要利用几何信息分割任意分子/材料结构的几何碎片化方法，可灵活控制碎片尺寸以构建多尺度碎片表示；据此设计几何碎片注意力机制整合多尺度结构信息，并嵌入融合p-SMILES与3D构象信息的多模态模型。

Result: 在聚合物基准数据集上，引入3D构象信息相较仅用p-SMILES的模型提升了多个性质的预测精度；可解释性AI分析揭示了所学表示的物理相关性。

Conclusion: 几何碎片化可在无化学规则先验下提供可控多尺度碎片表示，几何碎片注意力与3D构象融合有效提升预测性能与可解释性。

Abstract: It is well established that molecular substructures can contribute unequally to chemical and physical properties, with specific motifs or side chains often playing dominant roles. Fragment-based representations have therefore emerged as an effective strategy for incorporating chemically meaningful inductive biases, as exemplified by molecular fingerprints and fragment-based attention mechanisms. However, many existing fragmentation-based machine learning methods rely on predefined chemical rules, which may yield suboptimal fragmentation for previously unseen or chemically unfamiliar structures and often overlook the importance of multiscale molecular representations. To address these limitations, we formulate molecular fragmentation as a radius-constrained optimization problem inspired by the k-center formulation, where the number of clusters is not fixed a priori but emerges from the specified radius, and propose a geometry-based fragmentation method that partitions arbitrary molecular or material structures using largely geometric information, without relying on predefined chemical fragmentation rules. The method provides flexible control over fragment size, enabling the construction of multiscale fragment representations for scale-adaptive modeling. Building on this framework, we develop a geometric fragment-based attention mechanism that integrates multiscale structural information and incorporate it into a multimodal machine learning model combining p-SMILES representations with three-dimensional (3D) conformational information. Experiments on a benchmark polymer dataset show that incorporating 3D conformational information improves prediction accuracy for several properties over models based exclusively on p-SMILES representations. Explainable artificial intelligence analyses further provide insights into the physical relevance of the learned representations.

</details>


### [191] [Learning Dynamics of On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08016)
*Guan Zhe Hong,Sumeet Ramesh Motwani,Liang Chen,Yangchen Pan,Junchi Yu,Jialin Yu,Philip Torr,Yi Ren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对on-policy distillation（OPD）进行机制性分析，将反馈循环拆分为"蒸馏信号→参数更新"与"更新经广播重塑rollout分布"两个阶段，解释了学生响应不足、局部一致性提升却导致退化、以及离线蒸馏作为OPD前置准备的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPD结合了稠密教师监督与学生在自身轨迹上的训练，形成"更新改变学生→改变训练分布→影响后续更新"的反馈闭环，但该反馈如何影响学习与泛化缺乏理解，需要机制层面的解释。

Method: 将OPD反馈循环解耦为两个阶段：一是蒸馏信号转化为参数更新（conversion），二是更新通过广播改变不同前缀上的预测并重塑rollout分布（broadcast）；据此分析学生几何、教师监督与演化rollout分布的相互作用，并对比离线蒸馏与OPD的组合效果。

Result: 发现两点：即便师生分歧显著，学生响应仍然有限；提升局部教师一致性的更新会同时提高失败续写的概率，使学生在后续rollout中暴露于不可靠监督，引发退化。离线蒸馏可提升OPD效果但未必立即提升held-out准确率；单独使用OPD可能损害生成，而在充分离线准备后可带来显著准确率增益。

Conclusion: 教师监督、学生几何与动态rollout分布共同决定了OPD的学习动态及下游表现；OPD宜与离线蒸馏配合使用。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) is emerging as an important paradigm for language-model post-training, combining dense teacher supervision with training on student-generated trajectories. This creates a feedback loop where each update changes the student and thereby the training distribution that generates subsequent updates. Yet, how this feedback shapes learning and generalization remains poorly understood. We conduct a mechanistic analysis of OPD's learning dynamics, distinguishing the two feedback loop stages: conversion of the distillation signal into parameter updates, and broadcast through which these updates change predictions across prefixes and reshape the rollout distribution. This framework helps us understand both limited student responsiveness despite substantial teacher-student disagreement, and degeneration when updates that improve local teacher agreement also increase the probability of unsuccessful continuations, exposing the student to unreliable supervision in subsequent rollouts. Finally, we show that offline distillation can prepare a student for OPD without significantly improving immediate held-out accuracy. While OPD alone may degrade generation, it can yield substantial accuracy gains after sufficient offline preparation. Together, our results connect teacher supervision, student geometry, and the evolving rollout distribution in determining the learning dynamics of OPD and its effects on downstream performance.

</details>


### [192] [GTAFL: Addressing Test-Agnostic Long-Tailed Federated Learning in Medical Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08021)
*Guangyu Chen,Jingyun Zeng,Hui Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出“测试无关长尾联邦学习”（Test-Agnostic Long-Tailed FL）新任务，并提出框架GTAFL，通过自适应重采样、专家分类器重训练与自监督学习纠正长尾偏置，并在推理阶段用集成机制应对未知测试分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗数据高度敏感且需隐私保护，联邦学习适配该场景；但真实医疗数据在本地与全局视角均呈长尾分布。现有长尾联邦学习方法假设测试集为均匀分布，而实际医疗测试数据分布未知且不可预测，易导致模型失效。

Method: GTAFL框架：训练阶段采用(1)自适应重采样、(2)专家分类器重训练、(3)自监督学习，以纠正长尾训练分布导致的分类器偏置与特征空间扭曲；推理阶段采用集成机制，融合多个重训练专家分类器以应对未知分布的测试数据。

Result: 在CIFAR10及两个医疗数据集上的大量实验表明，GTAFL优于其他先进方法。代码开源：https://github.com/cgy-lavine/GTAFL。

Conclusion: 将长尾联邦学习拓展至测试分布不可知的现实设定具有必要性，所提GTAFL在训练端校正偏置、在推理端集成专家，可有效提升医疗等场景下模型对未知测试分布的鲁棒性。

Abstract: In healthcare, protecting patient privacy is crucial due to the sensitivity of medical data and its extensive accessibility. Federated Learning (FL) offers a decentralized and privacy-preserving training paradigm, making it an ideal solution for healthcare applications. A critical challenge in healthcare is that real-world medical data often exhibits long-tailed distributions in local and global views. Existing methods addressing long-tailed FL problem typically assume that the model will be evaluated on uniform test data distribution. However, practical test data in healthcare systems is often agnostic and unpredictable, leading to potential model failures in real-world scenarios. In this paper, we introduce a novel task termed Test-Agnostic Long-Tailed Federated Learning and propose GTAFL, a comprehensive framework to address this challenge. During the training stage, GTAFL employs adaptive re-sampling, expert classifier retraining, and self-supervised learning to correct biased classifiers and distorted feature spaces caused by long-tailed training distributions. During the inference stage, an ensemble mechanism combines retrained expert classifiers to handle test data with unknown distributions. Extensive experiments on CIFAR10 and two medical datasets manifest that our framework outperforms other state-of-the-art methods. The code is available at https://github.com/cgy-lavine/GTAFL

</details>


### [193] [Reproducing Kernel (Quasi-)Banach Spaces with the $\ell^p$ Coefficient Norms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08032)
*Tianfang Wang,Guohui Song,Haizhang Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在RKBS理论框架下研究一般ℓ^p正则化，重点构建0<p<1时的RKQBS并给出插值与正则化学习的存在性、稀疏性及（松弛）表示定理，同时分析p>1时基于固定可数字典的RKBS构造及最优松弛表示因子，揭示三种情形p<1、p=1、p>1的本质差异，并用数值实验比较精度与稀疏性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前期工作仅建立了ℓ^1范数下的RKBS基础，而追求更强稀疏性（更接近ℓ^0伪范数）自然促使研究ℓ^p拟范数；同时需厘清0<p<1、p=1与p>1三种正则化机制在表示与稀疏性上的根本区别。

Method: 对0<p<1：构造具ℓ^p拟范数的再生核拟Banach空间（RKQBS），在适当假设下建立插值与正则化学习解的存在性及稀疏性；借助广义Lebesgue常数刻画线性与松弛表示定理，并在核衰减、样本间距与稳定性假设下导出界。对p>1：以固定可数字典构造RKBS，证明极小元存在性，并通过离样本系数算子确定最优松弛表示因子。

Result: 在0<p<1时得到RKQBS框架下插值与正则化学习的存在性和稀疏性结论，以及由广义Lebesgue常数表述的表示定理与相应界；在p>1时得到极小元存在性及最优松弛表示因子的刻画；数值实验对比了三种情形下解的精度与稀疏性。

Conclusion: ℓ^p正则化在RKBS/RKQBS理论中可统一处理，非凸情形0<p<1带来更强稀疏性但需拟Banach结构，而p>1可通过字典构造与离样本系数算子刻画表示；三类机制在表示定理与稀疏性上存在本质差异。

Abstract: This paper advances the theory of reproducing kernel Banach spaces (RKBSs) by investigating the general $\ell^p$ regularization framework, with a particular focus on the non-convex regime $0<p<1$.
  While our preceding work established the foundation for RKBSs with the $\ell^1$ norm, the pursuit of stronger sparsity---closer to the $\ell^0$ pseudo-norm---naturally motivates this exploration into $\ell^p$ quasi-norms.
  For $0<p<1$, we construct reproducing kernel quasi-Banach spaces (RKQBSs) with $\ell^p$ quasi-norms and establish existence and sparsity results for interpolation and regularized learning under appropriate assumptions.
  We characterize the linear and relaxed representer theorems in terms of a generalized Lebesgue constant and derive bounds under kernel decay, sample spacing, and stability assumptions.
  For $p>1$, we construct RKBSs using a fixed countable dictionary, establish minimizer existence, and identify the optimal relaxed representer factor through an off-sample coefficient operator.
  These results reveal fundamental differences between the regimes $0<p<1$, $p=1$, and $p>1$.
  Numerical experiments compare the accuracy and sparsity of solutions for these three cases.

</details>


### [194] [Should We Pay This Much for Robustness? Efficient Proxy Certificates with Marginal Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08035)
*Soroush H. Zargarbashi,Mohammad Sadegh Akhondzadeh,Aleksandar Bojchevski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出将鲁棒认证委托给廉价代理函数，并用鲁棒保形风险控制来统计控制代理偏离原认证的幅度，从而以单次前向的低成本获得边际意义下的鲁棒认证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代分类器对输入扰动敏感，而随机平滑等强模型无关鲁棒认证需每输入约2000次前向，成本过高难以部署，尤其对ViT、语言模型等大模型不可行。

Method: 用一个廉价代理函数替代昂贵的认证器，并在可交换的无标签校准集上通过鲁棒保形风险控制调整代理，以约束其相对原始可靠证书的偏差；保证的是边际（而非逐点）误接受率上界，即未来输入中被代理接受但预测因扰动翻转的概率有界。代理证书可作为独立边际证书，也可作为fallback：仅当代理拒绝时才调用昂贵认证器；并提供单样本与样本重加权两种校准方案。

Result: 作为独立证书时，测试时认证成本降至单次前向，在小半径下保留随机平滑的大部分认证性能；作为fallback时，以较小的加速幅度恢复随机平滑的认证比率。

Conclusion: 该框架为更大架构（如视觉Transformer和语言模型）提供了廉价而有效的鲁棒性保证。

Abstract: Modern classifiers remain sensitive to small input perturbations, while strong model-agnostic robustness certificates such as randomized smoothing are often too expensive for deployment, requiring an extensive number (e.g. 2000) of model forward passes per input. We propose a framework that delegates certification to a cheap proxy function while statistically controlling its deviation from the original sound certificate. Given an exchangeable unlabeled calibration set, we tune the proxy via robust conformal risk control to guarantee a bounded marginal (rather than pointwise) false-approval rate: the probability, over future inputs, that the proxy accepts an input whose prediction is flipped due to perturbation. The resulting proxy certificate can be used either as a standalone marginal certificate or as a fallback mechanism that invokes the expensive certificate only when the proxy rejects. We provide both single-sample and sample-heavy calibration recipes. Empirically, as a standalone certificate our method reduces the test-time certification cost to a single model forward while retaining much of the certified performance of randomized smoothing at small radii, and as a fallback it recovers the certified ratio of randomized smoothing at a more modest speedup. This enables cheap yet effective robustness guarantees for larger architectures such as vision transformers and language models.

</details>


### [195] [Performance metrics in machine learning: a scenario-based guide from the confusion matrix to calibration, regression and uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08048)
*Mehmet Baygin,Sengul Dogan,Turker Tuncer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是一篇方法学教程，通过固定输入的受控场景系统演示类别患病率、决策阈值、聚合规则与软件约定如何影响分类性能指标，并提供目标导向的指标表与报告清单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同一组预测在不同评估设置下会得出不同结论，评估者常误将指标差异归因于模型优劣，而非患病率、阈值、聚合方式或软件默认约定等评估条件。作者希望以可复现的方式阐明这些机制。

Method: 将22种混淆矩阵指标组织为按患病率编码的图谱；设计15个场景，覆盖二分类与多分类、分数区分度、概率校准、回归、不确定性与一致性检验；输入固定于公开文件，每个场景仅改变一个因素；共执行205项验证检查（202项独立、3项内部）。

Result: 全部205项检查通过。在患病率0.010时，"恒预测负类"规则准确率达0.990而平衡准确率仅0.500；灵敏度与特异度固定时，患病率由0.5降至0.001使精确率从0.889跌至0.008；三种概率变体ROC-AUC同为0.825但校准斜率分别为2.08、1.04与0.52；10名参与者时，参与者多数投票准确率0.700的95%区间为0.348–0.933；在不当的合并测试集模型下，折平均得分被错误标记为不一致。

Conclusion: 这些场景用于说明机制而非估计其在已发表研究中的出现频率；作者给出基于目标的指标选择表与报告清单。文献审计与真实数据演示仅作为未来研究方案提出，尚未执行。

Abstract: Performance metrics can yield different conclusions from the same predictions. This methodological tutorial explains the effects of class prevalence, decision thresholds, aggregation rules and software conventions through controlled scenarios. Twenty-two confusion-matrix metrics are organized in a prevalence-coded map. Fifteen scenarios cover binary and multi-class classification, score discrimination, probability calibration, regression, uncertainty and consistency checks. Inputs are fixed in released files, and one factor is varied in each scenario. All 205 verification checks passed; 202 were independent and 3 were internal. An always-negative rule achieved an accuracy of 0.990 at a prevalence of 0.010, with balanced accuracy of 0.500. At fixed sensitivity and specificity, precision fell from 0.889 to 0.008 as prevalence fell from 0.5 to 0.001. Three probability variants shared a ROC-AUC of 0.825 but had calibration slopes of 2.08, 1.04 and 0.52. With 10 participants, participant-majority accuracy of 0.700 had a 95% interval of 0.348-0.933. Fold-averaged scores were falsely flagged as inconsistent under an inappropriate pooled-test-set model. A goal-based metric table and a reporting checklist are provided. The scenarios illustrate mechanisms and do not estimate their frequency in published studies. A literature audit and a real-data demonstration are specified as future protocols and were not executed.

</details>


### [196] [Can an LLM Actually Reason and Program about Neural Network's Weights or is it just memorization on steroids? Exploring "Weight-Space Programming"](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08070)
*Nazmus Sakib Ahmed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文探究大模型"权重空间编程"中的读取能力究竟是权重流推理，还是边缘局部算术加公开数据集的记忆先验，并给出审计协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期有报道称LLM可直接读写神经网络原始权重（权重空间编程）。作者质疑其"读"权重的能力：这究竟源自对权重流的整体推理，还是仅在网络边缘做局部算术、再叠加对公开数据集的记忆先验，因此设计实验加以区分。

Method: 在5个图像数据集上训练约12.5万参数的小型卷积网络，将全部权重以纯文本形式输入Claude Opus 5.5，不提供工具、前向传播、数据集或类别名称。考察其对BN统计量、输出层符号模式、首末层与中间层关注度、语义判断（数据集识别、特征语义解释）的表现；并做标签置换实验检验是否依赖记忆的标签顺序。以单次运行为主的探索性实验。

Result: 局部计算准确：BN统计量使其推断出仅训练3步的网络约3个优化器步数；输出层符号模式可还原CIFAR-10的车/动物二分。模型自述重点读首末层、对中间层采样，若干基于部分追踪的判断未通过验证（但未做直接前向传播检验）。语义判断依赖熟悉数据集：将三个快餐数据集网络均识别为CIFAR-10，把KMNIST误标为MNIST，将准确率99.57%的SAT-6网络读作CIFAR-10动物照片，把"最常在树上触发"的特征所偏好输出称为"frog"。标签置换后输出二分的索引保持但所有类别对解释均错误，说明依赖记忆的标签顺序。

Conclusion: 该模型的零样本权重读取更宜描述为"局部算术+数据集先验"，而非权重流追踪；研究属探索性、多为单次运行，并提供了一套用于审计未来权重空间主张的协议。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have recently been reported to read and write the raw weights of neural networks directly, a capability termed weight-space programming. We ask whether the reading side of this capability reflects reasoning about weight flows or a combination of local arithmetic at the network's edges and memorized priors over public datasets. We test Claude Opus 5.5 on small convolutional networks of about 125,000 parameters trained on five image datasets, providing every weight as plain text with no tools, no forward passes, and no dataset or class names. The model performs accurate local calculations: batch-normalization statistics lead it to infer about three optimizer steps on networks stopped after three updates, and output-layer sign patterns recover the vehicle/animal split of CIFAR-10. The sessions report reading the first and last layers closely and sampling the middle, and several claims built on partial tracing fail verification, although we did not run a direct forward-pass test. Semantic judgments lean on familiar datasets: the model names CIFAR-10 for CIFAR-10 networks but also for all three fast-food networks, relabels KMNIST as MNIST, reads a 99.57%-accurate SAT-6 network as CIFAR-10 animal photos, and calls "frog" the output favored by a feature that fires most on trees. Permuting CIFAR-10's labels preserves the output split at the new indices, but every class pair the model interprets is then wrong, indicating reliance on remembered label order. These are exploratory results from mostly single runs. We conclude that zero-shot weight reading by this model is better described as local arithmetic plus dataset priors than as weight-flow tracing, and we provide a protocol for auditing future weight-space claims.

</details>


### [197] [Value or Attention: Investigating Layer-wise Relevance Propagation for Graph Attention Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08088)
*Ken Schreiber,René P. Klausen,Sebastian Lapuschkin,Wojciech Samek,Jonas Naujoks*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将GAT注意力层中节点所获相关性拆分为value路径（内容相关性）与attention路径（路由相关性），提出带自由权重的乘积型LRP规则，证明现有方法为特例，并说明两路应分开解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GAT在医学、材料科学等需要可解释性的领域广泛应用，但其学到的消息路由对多数解释方法不透明；逐层相关性传播（LRP）虽遵循模型计算，但在注意力层中到达节点的相关性实为两类机制不同的量之和，现有规则要么丢弃其一，要么合并为单一数值，损失了解释力。

Method: 提出单一乘积型规则，对两个因子引入自由权重，使现有方法成为其特例。value路径以零基线衡量节点提供了何种内容；attention路径以邻域为参照衡量节点如何塑造被传递的信息，其符号反映节点特征是否按预测所需的方向移动了门控。

Result: 在有真值标签的分类任务上，同一节点在该规则的三种设置下分别获得负、正与近零的相关性。分离后的相关性图给出一项关于模型的假设：模型通过不读取该节点而获益。

Conclusion: 给出两条路径各自的解读规则，并主张二者应分别解释，而非合并为单一相关性数值。

Abstract: Graph Attention Networks (GATs) are increasingly used in domains where scientists need to read a model's reasoning, from medicine to materials science, yet the routing they learn remains opaque to most explainers. Layer-wise Relevance Propagation (LRP) follows the model's computation, but in an attention layer the relevance reaching a node is the sum of two mechanically different quantities: its content relevance through the value path and its routing relevance through the attention path. Existing rules either drop one or add them into a single number. We write a single product rule with free weights on the two factors that recovers current approaches as special cases. The value path measures what content a node supplied against a zero baseline; the attention path measures how the node shaped what was passed relative to its neighborhood, and its sign records whether the node's features moved the gating the way the prediction demanded. On a ground-truth classification task, we show that the same node receives negative, positive and near-zero relevance under three settings of the rule. The separated relevance maps yield a hypothesis about the model: it gains by not reading that node. We give reading rules for both paths and argue that they should be interpreted separately.

</details>


### [198] [Optimal Competitive Ratios for Chasing Convex Functions with Quadratic Growth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08078)
*Linzhe He,Sifan Yang,Wei Jiang,Lijun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为两类移动代价下的凸函数追逐问题建立了最优的维度无关竞争比：欧氏移动下提出Clipped Proximal Descent (CPD)，达到O(κ^{1/3})并匹配下界；平方欧氏移动下给出精确最优竞争比，并附随机算法匹配下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 凸函数追逐中，欧氏移动下κ-良态函数的已有上下界存在多项式间隙；平方欧氏移动下虽有维度无关保证，但最优竞争比仍不明确。作者旨在闭合这些间隙，确定两类移动代价下的最优竞争比。

Method: 针对欧氏移动，提出Clipped Proximal Descent (CPD)，将平衡的近端步与朝当前最小点的裁剪修正相结合；针对平方欧氏移动，对Regularized Online Balanced Descent (R-OBD)进行新的紧竞争分析，并构造对抗随机算法的匹配下界。

Result: CPD在满足二次增长的平滑凸函数上达到O(κ^{1/3})竞争比，改进此前O(√κ)上界并匹配已有的Ω(κ^{1/3})下界；平方欧氏移动下确立了满足二次增长的凸函数的精确最优维度无关竞争比，且下界对随机算法同样成立。

Conclusion: 两类移动代价下凸函数追逐的最优维度无关竞争比均被确定：欧氏移动下CPD实现上下界匹配的O(κ^{1/3})，平方欧氏移动下R-OBD为最优且不可被随机化改进。

Abstract: Chasing convex function has attracted much attention in recent years, which can be formulated as a game between a learner and an adversary. In each round, the adversary reveals a convex function, and the learner selects a decision and incurs a hitting cost and a movement cost for changing its decision. The performance of the learner is measured by the \emph{competitive ratio}, which compares its cumulative cost with that of an optimal offline decision sequence. For Euclidean movement, existing upper and lower bounds for $κ$-well-conditioned functions exhibit a polynomial gap. For squared Euclidean movement, dimension-free competitive guarantees have been established under quadratic growth, but the optimal competitive ratio remains unclear.
  In this paper, we establish optimal dimension-free competitive ratios for convex function chasing under two types of movement costs. For Euclidean movement, we propose Clipped Proximal Descent (CPD), which combines a balanced proximal step with a clipped correction toward the current minimizer. CPD achieves an $O(κ^{1/3})$ competitive ratio for smooth convex functions satisfying quadratic growth, improving the previous $O(\sqrtκ)$ upper bound and matching the existing $Ω(κ^{1/3})$ lower bound. For squared Euclidean movement, we establish the exact optimal dimension-free competitive ratio for convex functions satisfying quadratic growth. Our result is based on a new tight competitive analysis of Regularized Online Balanced Descent (R-OBD) and a matching lower bound against randomized algorithms.

</details>


### [199] [Gradient Boosting for Efficient Rank Discovery in Tensor Network Kernel Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08093)
*Albert Saiapin,Kim Batselier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GB-CPKM：以CP张量网络为参数化、用梯度提升顺序叠加固定秩学习器，用集成规模替代显式秩搜索，训练快至6倍、峰值内存减半且性能可比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TNKM将非线性特征与低秩张量表示结合以高效建模复杂关系，但张量秩的选择困难：传统秩搜索需训练多个候选模型，计算开销大。

Method: 采用CP（canonical polyadic）张量网络参数化，构建梯度提升框架GB-CPKM：顺序添加固定秩的学习器以得到更高容量的预测器，通过增量提升模型容量来替代显式秩搜索，集成规模由验证集性能确定。

Result: 在真实回归基准上，相比基于交叉验证的传统秩选择方法，GB-CPKM训练速度最高快6倍、峰值内存最高降低2倍，预测性能相当。

Conclusion: 梯度提升可提升张量网络学习的计算效率与可扩展性，为秩选择提供了新视角。

Abstract: Tensor network kernel machines (TNKMs) combine nonlinear features with low-rank tensor representations to model complex relationships efficiently. However, selecting an appropriate tensor rank remains challenging, as conventional rank-search methods require training multiple candidate models, increasing computational costs. In this work, we focus on a canonical polyadic (CP) tensor network parameterization and propose GB-CPKM, a gradient boosting framework that builds a higher-capacity predictor by sequentially adding fixed-rank learners. This approach provides a new perspective on rank selection by incrementally increasing model capacity, with ensemble size selected based on validation performance rather than through explicit rank search. Experiments on real-world regression benchmarks show that GB-CPKM trains up to 6x faster and uses up to 2x less peak memory than a conventional rank-selection method based on cross-validation, while achieving comparable predictive performance. These results highlight the potential of gradient boosting to improve the computational efficiency and scalability of tensor-network learning.

</details>


### [200] [SAE Interprets MoE: Sparse AutoEncoders Reveal That MoE Experts Exhibit Human-Interpretable Fine-Grained Specialization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08089)
*Anyuan Zhuo,Shaohua Zhang,Yuan Lin,Pinyan Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过在MoE层输出上训练稀疏自编码器（SAE），将每个潜变量的预激活精确分解到各专家，从"贡献"而非"门控频次"角度刻画专家专业化，发现贡献远比门控集中、潜变量多由一两个专家主导，并揭示粗粒度领域之下更细的分工。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对MoE专家职责的分析多依赖在领域标注语料上统计专家被选中的频率，这种手段预先固定了分类体系、粒度粗，且只记录"被选中"而非"实际贡献"，难以回答单个专家究竟负责什么。作者希望直接在特征空间中研究专家专业化。

Method: 在MoE层输出上训练SAE。由于该输出是各专家输出的路由加权和，而SAE编码器是仿射的，因此每个潜变量的预激活可以精确地按专家分解。在语料上聚合后，为每个专家得到一组由其主导驱动的潜变量（dominant set）；再通过自动解释（auto-interpretation）分析这些集合的语义，并在Qwen3-30B-A3B的第4、30、46层上验证。

Result: 贡献远比门控集中；大多数潜变量只有一到两个主导专家；主导集合之间的重叠集中在少数专家对上。自动解释显示存在低于粗粒度领域的分工：例如Java与前端Web代码由不同专家负责，几何与算术/多项式也各有专家，而各粗粒度领域本身占用的专家数量极不均衡。

Conclusion: 基于SAE的贡献分解为MoE专家专业化提供了精确、可解释的分析工具，表明专家在特征层面呈现细粒度分工，且实际贡献结构比门控统计所反映的更集中；SAE与代码已开源。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models route each token to a few of many experts, yet what an individual expert is responsible for remains unsettled. Existing analyses count how often an expert is selected on domain-labeled corpora, an instrument that fixes the taxonomy in advance, stays coarse, and records selection rather than contribution. We instead study specialization in feature space by training a sparse autoencoder (SAE) on the output of an MoE layer. Since this output is a router-weighted sum of expert outputs and the SAE encoder is affine, the pre-activation of every latent decomposes exactly over the experts. Aggregated over a corpus, this gives each expert a dominant set of latents whose activation it principally drives. On layers 4, 30 and 46 of Qwen3-30B-A3B, contribution is far more concentrated than gating, most latents have only one or two dominators, and the overlap between dominant sets concentrates on a few expert pairs. Auto-interpreting these sets reveals a division of labor below coarse domains, with distinct experts for Java and front-end web code, or for geometry and for arithmetic and polynomials, while the coarse domains themselves take very unequal numbers of experts. Our SAEs and code are available at https://github.com/Bytedance-Seed/SAE-Interprets-MoE.

</details>


### [201] [Interpretable Multimodal MRI-Clinical Fusion for Functional Outcome Prediction After Acute Ischemic Stroke](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08154)
*Sina Raeisadigh,Henning Müller,Abderrahmane Hedjoudje*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究评估急性缺血性卒中（AIS）患者发病第1天多模态MRI对三个月功能预后的增量预测价值，发现FLAIR-临床融合模型表现最佳（AUC=0.948±0.061），ADC-临床融合次之（AUC=0.941±0.094），全模态融合并未稳定优于单模态融合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究第1天多模态MRI（ADC、DWI、FLAIR）能否在临床变量之外提供增量预后信息，并检验多模态叠加是否必然优于单模态与临床的融合。

Method: 纳入66例具备ADC、DWI、FLAIR及临床数据的AIS患者；从各MRI模态提取三维预训练ResNet嵌入，经按折隔离的主成分分析降维，与结构化临床变量及ADC衍生病灶生物标志物结合；采用线性SVM，通过八折分层分组交叉验证比较各模态组合。

Result: 仅临床模型AUC=0.899±0.151；仅MRI模型判别力有限（AUC约0.67–0.73）；FLAIR-临床融合均值最高（AUC=0.948±0.061），ADC-临床次之（0.941±0.094）；全ADC-DWI-FLAIR融合未稳定超越单模态-临床融合。特征重要性显示年龄、NIHSS分项评分、ADC病灶负荷及学习到的FLAIR表征为关键贡献者，FLAIR激活图在病灶样区域呈响应。

Conclusion: 紧凑的第1天FLAIR与ADC表征可在临床变量之外提供互补预后信息，支持AIS中可解释、数据高效的结果预测。

Abstract: This study evaluates the added prognostic value of day-1 multimodal MRI for predicting three-month functional outcome after acute ischemic stroke (AIS). Sixty-six AIS patients with ADC, DWI, FLAIR, and clinical data were analyzed. Three-dimensional pretrained ResNet embeddings were extracted from each MRI modality, reduced using fold-isolated principal component analysis, and combined with structured clinical variables and ADC-derived lesion biomarkers. Linear support vector machines were evaluated using eight-fold stratified group cross-validation across MRI modality combinations. Clinical-only modeling achieved strong performance (AUC = $0.899 \pm 0.151$), while MRI-only models showed limited discrimination (AUC $\approx$ $0.67$-$0.73$). Among multimodal configurations, FLAIR-clinical fusion achieved the highest mean cross-validated performance (AUC = $0.948 \pm 0.061$), followed by ADC-clinical fusion (AUC = $0.941 \pm 0.094$). Full ADC-DWI-FLAIR fusion did not consistently outperform single-modality clinical fusion. Feature-importance analysis identified age, NIHSS subscores, ADC lesion burden, and learned FLAIR representations as key contributors, while qualitative FLAIR activation maps showed responses over lesion-like regions. These findings suggest that compact day-1 FLAIR and ADC representations provide complementary prognostic information beyond clinical variables and support interpretable, data-efficient outcome prediction in AIS.

</details>


### [202] [Gabor Gabor: E-Gaborformer, a Strictly Causal Speech Recognizer with Gabor-Dictionary Convolutions and Delta-Rule Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08092)
*Nir Krakowski,Tamir Cohen,Ohad Zohar,Chen Zarfati*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: E-Gaborformer 是一种严格因果的流式语音编码器，将 Gated DeltaNet-2 循环层与滑动窗口注意力按 3:1 混合，并用固定因果 Gabor 原子字典构建时间卷积，实现常数级流式状态（约 19 MB/路）且在精度与速度上优于同规模基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时语音代理要求识别器不等待未来音频，且单路调用的显存/内存不随通话时长增长。现有同规模因果注意力编码器在十分钟后状态累积超过 1 GB，无法满足长时流式部署需求。

Method: 提出 E-Gaborformer 编码器：严格因果结构，Gated DeltaNet-2 循环层与滑动窗口注意力以 3:1 比例组合，所有时间卷积均由固定因果 Gabor 原子字典构造，从而保持常数流式状态。另外采用 RNN-T lattice 的两类公式化训练，实现精确损失与梯度下 2.6 倍加速。

Result: 在 LibriSpeech 受控因果研究中更早脱离初始平台期，达到给定精度最快提前 18%，并匹配或超过相同前端与配方训练的 E-Branchformer。生产规模下 0.6B 参数的希伯来语/英语流式 transducer，英语语音代理流量的 WER 比同规模 NVIDIA Nemotron 3.5 ASR Streaming 低 28%–31%，希伯来语 WER 较原生产系统低至多 20%。同一权重可服务严格因果与 480 ms 前瞻解码且无精度损失。

Conclusion: E-Gaborformer 以常数内存和严格因果性满足长时流式语音代理需求，在精度、收敛速度与部署灵活性上均具优势，且两类 RNN-T 公式化可显著加速训练。

Abstract: Real-time voice agents need a speech recognizer that never waits for future audio and whose memory per call does not grow with the length of the call. We present E-Gaborformer, a strictly causal encoder that combines Gated DeltaNet-2 recurrent layers with sliding-window attention in a 3:1 ratio, and builds every temporal convolution from a fixed dictionary of causal Gabor atoms. Its streaming state is constant, about 19 MB per call at production scale including the prediction network, where a causal-attention encoder of the same size accumulates over 1 GB after ten minutes. In a controlled causal study on LibriSpeech, E-Gaborformer leaves the initial plateau earlier, reaches a given accuracy up to 18% sooner, and matches or beats an E-Branchformer trained with the identical front end and recipe. At production scale, a 0.6B-parameter Hebrew and English streaming transducer built on E-Gaborformer has 28 to 31% lower word error rate (WER) than NVIDIA's Nemotron 3.5 ASR Streaming, a model of the same size, on English voice-agent traffic, and up to 20% lower Hebrew WER than our previous production system. The same weights serve strictly causal and 480 ms-lookahead decoding with no loss in accuracy, and a two-class formulation of the RNN-T lattice trains the transducer 2.6x faster with exact loss and gradients.

</details>


### [203] [Scaling Neural Motor Decoding via Decoupled Behavioral Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08263)
*Divyansha Lachi,Vinam Arora,Shivashriganesh P. Mahato,Alexandre Andre,Eva L. Dyer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BEEMO是一个多会话框架，通过解耦行为与神经表征学习，允许以任意比例混合配对神经-行为记录与无配对行为数据训练解码器，仅用行为预训练即可达到大规模神经数据预训练的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经解码依赖大规模配对神经-行为记录，成本高、侵入性强、难以扩展；而行为数据丰富易得，因此提出能否在神经解码中解耦行为与神经表征学习。

Method: BEEMO框架中，行为解码器直接从行为轨迹学习运动动力学，神经活动仅在可用时通过交叉注意力层注入，从而使行为监督与神经监督可独立扩展；支持任意混合配对与无配对行为数据的多会话训练。

Result: 在多个非人灵长类皮层内运动数据集与任务上，引入无配对行为数据提升了解码性能，尤其在向新任务迁移时；加入少量目标任务行为数据可进一步提升迁移效果；纯行为预训练（无神经预训练）性能可与在更大神经数据集上预训练的模型相当。

Conclusion: 扩展行为数据为跨被试、跨任务的神经解码模型提供了实用且低成本的路径，解耦神经与行为数据开辟了新的扩展维度。

Abstract: Recent neural decoding approaches achieve strong performance by training on large collections of paired neural-behavioral recordings, but such datasets are expensive, invasive, and difficult to scale. In contrast, behavioral data is abundant and easy to collect, raising the question of whether neural decoding can benefit from decoupling behavioral and neural representation learning. We introduce BEEMO, a flexible multi-session framework that enables training with arbitrary mixtures of paired neural-behavioral recordings and unpaired behavioral data. A behavior decoder learns movement dynamics directly from behavioral trajectories, while neural activity is incorporated through cross-attention layers when available, allowing behavioral and neural supervision to scale independently. Across multiple intracortical motor datasets and tasks in nonhuman primates, we show that incorporating unpaired behavioral data improves decoding performance, particularly when transferring to new tasks, and that adding even a small amount of behavioral data from the target task further improves transfer. By decoupling neural and behavioral data, we unlock a new dimension for scaling: unpaired behavioral data, which is substantially cheaper and easier to collect than paired neural recordings, can be used directly to improve decoding performance. We further show that behavior-only pretraining, without any neural pretraining, achieves performance comparable to models pretrained on substantially larger neural datasets. Together, our results suggest that scaling behavioral data offers a practical and cost-effective path toward neural decoding models that generalize across subjects and tasks. Code is available at https://github.com/nerdslab/beemo.

</details>


### [204] [Accelerating Safe Reinforcement Learning with Massive Parallelism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08273)
*Joonyoung Lim,Younghwan Yoo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究将安全强化学习扩展到大规模并行训练（短 rollout、多环境）时的核心矛盾：CMDP 的安全约束定义在整回合成本回报上，而并行短片段只覆盖局部阶段。作者将错峰环境重置建模为短轨迹片段上的相位混合分布，刻画相位覆盖导致的偏差，并提出相位聚合成本估计器从未完整回合的短片段批次重建回合级成本；进一步将有限样本不确定性与分布不匹配纳入约束收紧框架，得到可扩展的 on-policy 安全 RL 方法。基于 MJX 版 Safety Gym 的实验表明，方法在墙钟时间大幅缩短的同时达到与非大规模并行实现相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation:  massively parallel 训练提升数据吞吐与墙钟效率，但短 rollout 视线与 CMDP 的回合级成本约束定义不匹配，直接使用片段成本会带来安全评估偏差。

Method: 将错峰重置下的采样视为相位混合分布，分析相位覆盖偏差；提出相位聚合成本估计器，从短 rollout 批次重建回合级成本；结合有限样本不确定性与分布不匹配进行约束收紧，兼容 on-policy 优化。

Result: 在 MJX 实现的 Safety Gym 上，性能与非大规模并行实现相当，同时显著降低墙钟训练时间。

Conclusion: 通过相位混合视角与回合级成本重建，可在保持安全约束有效性的前提下实现可扩展的并行安全 RL 训练。

Abstract: We investigate the problem of scaling safe reinforcement learning (safe RL) to massively parallel training regimes, where a large number of environments are exe- cuted with short rollout horizons. Although this setting improves data throughput and wall-clock efficiency, it creates a mismatch with constrained Markov decision processes (CMDPs), whose safety constraints are defined over full-episode cost returns. We address this mismatch by analyzing staggered environment resets as a phase-mixture distribution over short trajectory segments. This analysis charac- terizes the bias induced by phase coverage and motivates a phase-aggregated cost estimator that reconstructs episode-level cost estimates from short rollout batches. We further incorporate finite-sample uncertainty and distribution mismatch into a constraint-tightening framework, yielding a scalable Safe RL method compatible with on-policy optimization. Using an MJX-based implementation of Safety Gym, our experiments show that the proposed method achieves performance comparable to non-massively parallel implementations while substantially reducing wall-clock training time.

</details>


### [205] [PersonaAgent: Retrieval-Augmented Transaction Anomaly Scoring with Refreshable Multi-Entity Persona Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08094)
*Zhengyu Fang,Liang Wang,Yingtong Dou,Yuzhong Chen,Shubham Jain,Junpeng Wang,Xiran Fan,Menghai Pan,Jiarui Sun,Yan Zheng,Yiwei Cai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PersonaAgent 将实体最新行为信号表示为可刷新记忆中的 "persona"，推理时用轻量适配器检索并增强基础异常分类器，解决大额支付网络中实体交互模式变化快与嵌入表高基数、稀疏的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模支付网络中的实体（卡、商户等）及其交互对交易异常检测至关重要，但实体间交易模式的变化频率通常快于生产模型的刷新周期；同时实体嵌入表存在高基数与交互稀疏问题。

Method: 提出 PersonaAgent 智能体式异常检测框架：将最新实体行为信号表示为 persona 并存入可刷新记忆。离线以每周调度的批处理任务提取最新行为信号并更新记忆；推理时由轻量适配器从记忆中检索每笔交易对应实体的行为信号，用于增强基础异常分类器的打分。

Result: 在 Visa 多种异常检测模型上的基准测试中，PersonaAgent 能高效打分（1,000 笔交易的 CPU 批处理耗时 1–2 ms），PR-AUC 相对提升 0.65–6.58%，部署中生产业务指标相对提升 8.93–14.24%。

Conclusion: 通过解耦行为信号刷新与模型更新、并以轻量适配器在线增强，PersonaAgent 在不重训基础模型的前提下实现了高效且显著的异常检测性能与业务指标提升。

Abstract: In large-scale payment networks, entities (card, merchant, etc.) and their interactions play a key role in transaction anomaly detection. However, transaction patterns between different entities usually change more frequently than the production model refresh schedule. Meanwhile, entity embedding tables suffer from high-cardinality and sparse interaction issues. To resolve the above challenges, we present PersonaAgent, an agentic transaction anomaly detection framework which represents the latest entity behavior signals as personas and saves them in a refreshable memory. Offline, a weekly scheduled batch job is used to extract the latest behavior signals and update them in the memory. At inference time, a lightweight adapter retrieves the corresponding entity behavior signals of each transaction from memory and uses them to augment the base anomaly classifier's scoring. Through comprehensive benchmarking with different anomaly detection models at Visa, PersonaAgent is able to score transactions efficiently (a 1,000-transaction CPU batch in 1-2 ms), lifting the PR-AUC by 0.65-6.58% and the production business metric by 8.93-14.24% relatively in deployment.

</details>


### [206] [AutoCompress: Automated Hardware-Guided Model Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08290)
*Sheldon Liang,Ali Taghibakhshi,Saurav Muralidharan,Sharath Turuvekere Sreenivas,Ashwath Aithal,Pavlo Molchanov,Nima Tajbakhsh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AutoCompress：将硬件/负载约束嵌入可微架构搜索的LLM剪枝框架，直接优化异构结构以满足延迟、吞吐与显存限制，精度优于Minitron。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM剪枝流程以参数量为目标，需人工挑选满足延迟、吞吐与GPU显存限制的模型尺寸；但参数量无法预测上述指标，因其取决于架构、负载、硬件与推理引擎。

Method: 提出硬件引导的压缩框架AutoCompress，将目标约束直接纳入可微架构搜索；联合优化全局与逐层结构以生成异构架构，并以经端到端延迟、吞吐、显存实测校验的服务引擎代价模型作为引导。

Result: 在长上下文本地编码、批处理数据中心服务、延迟敏感的游戏内交互三类场景评估；相比SOTA Minitron，在RTX 5070、RTX 5080、H100上八项基准平均精度分别提升6.57、2.35、0.57个百分点；并将Nemotron 3.5 Super从120B压缩至74B，在单B200 BF16、128K上下文服务下保留95%平均基准得分。

Conclusion: 以硬件实测校验的代价模型驱动可微结构搜索，可生成面向部署约束的异构剪枝架构，在多种硬件与负载下稳定超越参数量驱动的剪枝方法。

Abstract: LLM pruning must preserve quality while meeting hardware- and workload-specific constraints on latency, throughput, and memory.
