Existing pruning workflows often target parameter counts, requiring practitioners to manually select model sizes that meet latency, throughput, and GPU memory limits. However, parameter count alone does not predict these metrics, which depend on the model architecture, workload, hardware, and serving engine. We introduce AutoCompress, a hardware-guided compression framework that directly incorporates such constraints into differentiable architecture search. AutoCompress jointly optimizes global and per-layer structure to produce heterogeneous architectures, guided by serving-engine cost models validated against end-to-end latency, throughput, and memory measurements. We evaluate AutoCompress on long-context local coding, batched datacenter serving, and latency-sensitive in-game interaction. Compared to the state-of-the-art Minitron pruning framework, AutoCompress improves average accuracy across eight benchmarks by 6.57, 2.35, and 0.57 percentage points on RTX 5070, RTX 5080, and H100, respectively. AutoCompress also compresses Nemotron 3.5 Super from 120B to 74B parameters for single-B200 BF16 serving at 128K context, retaining 95% of its mean benchmark score.

</details>


### [207] [How Far Can Untrained Neural Networks Go in Anomaly Detection? From Tables to Pixels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08136)
*Hyojun Yi,Geunyoung Kim,Hogeon Seo,Seunghyoung Ryu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PRISM，将固定随机非线性特征与基于正常参考的Mahalanobis打分结合，在无需神经网络权重优化或预训练表征的情况下，于表格与视觉异常检测任务上取得与学习方法相当甚至更优的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异常检测常被视为表征学习问题，被认为需依赖学习特征或AD专用优化；但这一视角忽视了异常相对正常参考本身可能具有的内在可分性。作者因此探究未经训练的神经网络在异常检测中能达到何种程度。

Method: 提出PRISM框架：使用固定的随机非线性特征，配合以正常参考样本估计的Mahalanobis打分。PRISM-T用于表格异常检测，PRISM-V用于稠密视觉定位。另给出理论分析，刻画非线性变换如何影响Mahalanobis得分差距中的均值项与协方差项贡献，并进行实证的可分性分析。

Result: 在ADBench的47个表格数据集上，PRISM-T取得最高平均AUROC（86.4%）与第二高平均AUPRC（65.9%）。在覆盖27个视觉类别的MVTec AD与VisA上，PRISM-V的图像级与像素级平均AUROC分别为90.4%与96.4%。

Conclusion: PRISM既是有竞争力的异常检测器，也可作为“先于学习”的基线，用于评估学习表征在异常检测中的附加价值；结果表明强性能并非必须依赖学习特征或AD专用优化。

Abstract: Anomaly detection (AD) is often framed as a representation-learning problem, where strong performance is expected to require learned features or AD-specific optimization. However, this view can overlook the intrinsic separability of anomalies from normal references, even in the absence of learned representations. We therefore investigate how far untrained neural networks can go in anomaly detection. To this end, we propose PRISM, a framework combining fixed random nonlinear features with normal-reference Mahalanobis scoring for tabular detection and dense visual localization. Across 47 tabular datasets in ADBench, PRISM-T achieves the highest mean AUROC (86.4%) and the second-highest mean AUPRC (65.9%) among the compared methods. On MVTec AD and VisA, covering 27 visual categories, PRISM-V achieves mean image-level and pixel-level AUROCs of 90.4% and 96.4%, respectively. Notably, both models achieve these results without neural weight optimization or pretrained representations. A theoretical analysis further characterizes how nonlinear transformations affect the mean and covariance contributions to the Mahalanobis score gap, while empirical analyses examine the resulting score separability. These findings establish PRISM as both a competitive anomaly detector and a prior-to-learning baseline for assessing the added value of learned representations in anomaly detection.

</details>


### [208] [Hybrid Machine Learning for Imbalanced Landing-Phase Safety Event Prediction from Flight Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08386)
*Taha Asghar,Bilal Mufti,Syed Qasim Zaheer,Luqman Ahmed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出可解释的LSTM自编码器+统计特征融合+类别加权XGBoost框架，用于多类进近着陆阶段安全事件预测，在NASA DASHlink三机场数据上实现宏观召回率>0.90。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 着陆阶段安全事件预测需在强不平衡多变量飞行数据中检测稀有异常进近；现有方法难以在刻画耦合时序行为的同时区分不同机场运行情境下的事件特异性特征。

Method: 用正常飞行窗口训练的LSTM自编码器输出连续重构误差描述子，与参数级统计飞行历史特征融合，输入类别加权XGBoost分类器，区分Normal、Speed High、Path High、Flaps Late四类窗口；在53,750个NASA DASHlink三机场进近窗口上比较池化与分机场学习策略，并进行组件级基准、重复管线重训与特征归因分析。

Result: 重复划分与重训下各机场宏观召回率均值>0.90；组件对比证实类别加权的重要性，且连续特征融合优于仅重构误差与硬门控方案；数据量较大的两机场受益于局部专门化，最小数据集机场受益于池化训练；特征归因指向垂直航径、构型、速度与进近情境等运行相关变量。

Conclusion: 该框架为稀有事件检测提供可解释方案，并为依据机场数据可用性调整飞行数据监控模型提供实践指导。

Abstract: Landing-phase safety-event prediction requires the detection of rare abnormal approaches within strongly imbalanced multivariate flight data. Capturing coupled temporal behavior while distinguishing event-specific signatures across airport operating contexts remains a central modeling challenge. This study proposes an interpretable hybrid feature-fusion framework for multiclass landing-phase safety-event prediction. A long short-term memory (LSTM) autoencoder trained on nominal flight windows characterizes deviations from routine approach behavior. Its continuous reconstruction-error descriptor is fused with parameter-specific statistical flight-history features and supplied to a class-weighted XGBoost classifier to distinguish Normal, Speed High, Path High, and Flaps Late windows. The framework is evaluated using 53,750 NASA DASHlink approach windows from three arrival airports. Pooled and airport-specific learning strategies are compared alongside component-level benchmarks, repeated pipeline retraining, and feature-attribution analysis. The results demonstrate strong class-balanced detection, with mean macro recall above 0.90 across all airports under repeated partitioning and retraining. Component comparisons establish the importance of class weighting and show that continuous feature fusion outperforms reconstruction-error-only and hard-gate alternatives. Airport-level comparisons reveal benefits from local specialization at the two airports with larger datasets and from pooled training at the airport with the smallest dataset. Feature attributions associate predictions with operationally relevant vertical-path, configuration, speed, and approach-context variables. The framework provides an interpretable approach to rare-event detection and practical guidance for adapting flight-data monitoring models to airport-level data availability.

</details>


### [209] [Detecting Weather Fronts from Satellite Observations: What Does Reanalysis Add?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08148)
*Ioannis Dravilas,Miruna Stoicescu,Roope Tervo,Carlos Horn,Oriol Hinojo Comellas,Sina Montazeri,Andrea Meraner,Ana Lucic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究比较仅用静止卫星红外观测与叠加28个ERA5再分析场的两种机器学习配置，用于北大西洋和欧洲的逐像素锋面检测，量化再分析数据的性能贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 天气锋面对中纬度降水和强天气至关重要，需评估机器学习检测锋面时性能在多大程度上依赖再分析场，以及卫星观测本身的可检测能力边界。

Method: 采用同一ML模型的两种配置做逐像素锋面分类：(1)仅输入静止卫星红外观测；(2)额外加入28个ERA5再分析场；以150 km容差的邻域临界成功指数(CSI@150)为指标，并通过变量可预测性与消融分析诊断各物理量的贡献。

Result: 仅卫星模型CSI@150为0.402，处于不同气象机构分析间一致范围之内；卫星+ERA5模型达0.575，提升0.173。低层风从卫星表征中最难预测(R²=0.23，温度达0.88)，移除风或湿度造成检测性能最大幅下降。

Conclusion: 再分析场显著提升锋面检测精度，其增益主要来自风与湿度信息；仅卫星方案已接近业务分析间的一致水平，具备一定独立检测潜力。

Abstract: Weather fronts are transition regions between air masses with contrasting thermodynamic properties and are associated with mid-latitude precipitation and severe weather. We investigate machine learning (ML) methods for detecting weather fronts and quantify the contribution of reanalysis data to performance. We compare two configurations of the same ML model for pixel-wise frontal classification over the North Atlantic and Europe: (1) using only geostationary infrared satellite observations, and (2) additionally using 28 ERA5 reanalysis fields. The satellite-only model achieves a neighbourhood critical success index at a 150 km tolerance (CSI@150) of $0.402$, which lies within the range of agreement between analyses from different meteorological services. The satellite$+$ERA5 model achieves $0.575$, an improvement of $0.173$. Our analysis shows that low-level winds are the least predictable from the satellite representation ($R^2 = 0.23$, compared with $0.88$ for temperature), while removing winds or moisture causes the largest decreases in detection performance.

</details>


### [210] [Towards interpreting any residual stream direction by scaling activation inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08465)
*Celeste De Schamphelaere,Juan J. Vazquez,Ari Spiesberger,Tomáš Gavenčiak,Gavin Leech,Euan Ong,Jiahai Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出最大化激活示例元模型（MAEM）：用无标签RL训练语言模型，将主体模型的残差流方向映射为能强烈激活该方向的文本示例，实现对方向的"可前向验证"的解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过语料检索获得激活某方向的文本受限于语料本身覆盖范围；而自然语言自编码器（NLA）等元模型的解释无法直接验证。作者希望构建一种可扩展、且解释结果能被主体模型前向传播核验的方向解释方法。

Method: 训练MAEM：输入主体模型（subject model）的残差流方向，输出强激活该方向的文本示例。目标为最大化给定残差流方向与所生成文本激活之间的余弦相似度，构成无标签强化学习目标，从而可利用多样来源的残差流方向大规模训练。以Qwen3.6-27B为主体模型实例化MAEM。

Result: 该方法在留出激活的反演上优于语料检索和NLA；在多数留出语言/领域上优于1000万token规模的语料检索；可激活89%的留出SAE特征；识别steering vector背后概念的能力与NLA相当；比语料检索更常恢复LoRA后门载荷；并能部分口述模型工作区的内部独白。

Conclusion: MAEM示例可通过主体模型的一次前向传播进行验证，相比NLA等元模型提供有依据（grounded）的解释，其无标签RL目标为规模化训练元模型提供了可行路径。

Abstract: To interpret a direction in a language model's residual stream, we can look for text that strongly activates it. But when such text is found by scanning a corpus, it is limited to what that corpus contains. We develop Maximally-Activating Example Metamodels (MAEMs), language models which map residual stream directions from a subject model to text exemplars that strongly activate those directions. Our objective is to maximise the cosine similarity of a supplied residual stream direction with the activations of the text generated by the MAEM. This label-free RL objective helps to scale MAEMs on diverse sources of residual stream directions. Empirically, we find the resultant metamodel produces informative exemplars: a Qwen3.6-27B MAEM inverts held-out activations better than corpus search or a natural-language autoencoder (NLA), beats corpus search (at 10M tokens) on a majority of held-out languages/domains, and activates 89% of held-out SAE features. It also identifies concepts behind steering vectors about as well as NLAs, recovers the payloads of LoRA backdoors more often than corpus search, and can even verbalise some model workspace inner monologue. Unlike explanations produced by other metamodels (e.g. NLAs), MAEM exemplars can be verified with a forward pass on the subject model, making the MAEM objective a grounded alternative for scaling the training of metamodels.

</details>


### [211] [Same Behavior, Different Mechanism: What Finite Experiments Can Certify About Integrated Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08469)
*Linqian Wu,Diaoxuan He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文问"机制层面的量能否被可识别地、连续地、可认证地从有限干预实验中测出"，并以整合信息论的φ作为严苛案例，证明可测性是可计算的设计问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可解释性、AI审计与机器意识评估日益依赖"机制层面"的量（系统因果转移律的泛函，描述"如何计算"而非"输出什么"），但实验只能通过有限、部分、含噪的干预看到该规律。因此需要回答该量何时可从实验访问中识别、在机制上连续、并能从有限数据中得到认证。

Method: 在四、五单元随机网络中（有已知真值）精确计算整合信息论φ。通过比较前馈与循环网络的输入-输出律与内部记录；用单步记录中遗漏单元量化精度下限；在线性奇偶网络中检验仅由保真度乘积决定的对称性及单次干预的破缺；在随机网络中扫描机制空间的"锐边"（分区选择准则与报告准则不一致导致的跳变）；检验拟合良好的代理模型是否误报；并给出单侧下界认证与高维区域φ>1.004 bit的可证明下界。

Result: 行为可能"沉默"：在响应延迟上输入-输出律相同的循环与前馈网络分别含0.28与0 bit，内部记录4-53次试验即可区分。部分访问同样盲：一步记录中遗漏单元造成0.30-1.02 bit的精度下限，任何样本量都无法降低；全但自身奇偶网络中任意长度记录只能通过各隐藏单元保真度的乘积看到它们，精确检验中一次干预即可打破该对称。φ有锐边：在所发现随机网络边缘的99%处，跨边界的无穷小机制变化使φ改变25-100%；约1%几乎完美拟合干预数据的代理模型误报超过20%。单侧结论成立：同一数据可认证边界两侧的下界，并证明64维机制区域内φ>1.004 bit，可由约10^7次受控转移认证，错误认证概率至多α。

Conclusion: 机制层面量的可测性不是一个抽象的哲学问题，而是一个具有可计算答案的设计问题；双侧精确识别常不可行，但可识别、可认证的单侧下界是可行的替代目标。

Abstract: Interpretability, AI auditing and assessments of machine consciousness increasingly rest on mechanism-level quantities: functionals of a system's causal transition law that describe how it computes, not what it outputs. Experiments see that law only through finite, partial and noisy interventions. We ask when such a quantity is identifiable from an experiment's access, continuous in the mechanism and certifiable from finite data, and answer exactly for a deliberately demanding case - the integrated information $\varphi$ of integrated information theory - in stochastic networks of four and five units with known ground truth. Behavior can be silent: a recurrent and a feed-forward network with identical input-output laws at their response delay carry 0.28 and 0 bits, yet internal recordings separate them within 4-53 trials. Partial access can be just as blind: in our examples, units left out of a one-step recording impose precision floors of 0.30-1.02 bits that no sample size lowers, and in all-but-self parity networks, recordings of any length see several hidden units only through the product of their fidelities - a symmetry that one intervention broke in exact checks. The measure has sharp edges: because $\varphi$ selects a partition, and the cause and effect it specifies, by one criterion and reports another, infinitesimal changes of mechanism that cross an edge change it by 25-100% at 99% of the edges we found in random networks, and about 1% of surrogate models that fit interventional data almost perfectly misreport it by more than 20%. One-sided claims survive: the same data certify a lower bound on both sides of an edge, and we prove $\varphi>1.004$ bits throughout a 64-dimensional region of mechanisms and certify it from about $10^7$ controlled transitions with false-certificate probability at most $α$. Measurability is thus a design question with computable answers.

</details>


### [212] [BUMP: Brief Unexpected Mid-Interpolation Peak](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08182)
*Melinda Flóra Kiss,Márton Muntag,Adrián Csiszárik,Dániel Varga*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文发现线性插值经过置换对齐的神经网络时，在插值端点附近测试精度会短暂提升，称为 BUMP，并据此提出权重预测混合集成方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究在插值曲线上虽可见类似的端点附近增益，但未被显式记录与系统分析；作者希望澄清该现象的普遍性、稳健性及其来源，并将其转化为可用的集成手段。

Method: 在置换对齐网络之间进行线性插值，跨数据集、架构、训练配置及早期检查点、极端剪枝等扰动观察 BUMP，并通过受控消融排除平凡正则化效应的解释；进而提出权重预测混合集成（weight-prediction mixed ensembling），将权重空间混合与预测空间混合相结合。

Result: BUMP 在多数据集、多架构、多训练范式中稳定出现，且在强扰动下依然稳健；受控消融表明其不能归因于平凡正则化效应。所提集成方法在实验中一致优于标准 logit 集成，并获得更优的校准权衡。

Conclusion: BUMP 是一种普遍且稳健的插值端点现象，权重空间与预测空间混合具有互补收益，为集成与校准设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: We identify an intriguing phenomenon when linearly interpolating between permutation-aligned neural networks: test accuracy briefly improves near the interpolation endpoints before dropping toward the midpoint, which we term BUMP (Brief Unexpected Mid-interpolation Peak). We observe that BUMP appears consistently across datasets, architectures, training regimes, and remains robust under substantial perturbations such as early checkpoints and extreme pruning. Moreover, controlled ablations suggest that it cannot be attributed to trivial regularization effects. While similar near-endpoint gains are visible in prior interpolation plots, they have not been explicitly documented or systematically and thoroughly analyzed. Leveraging this effect, we propose a simple, novel ensembling scheme: weight-prediction mixed ensembling that combines weight mixing with prediction-space mixing. Across our experiments, the resulting ensembling method consistently outperforms standard logit ensembling in test accuracy and yields improved calibration trade-off, highlighting complementary benefits of weight- and prediction-space mixing.

</details>


### [213] [XStack: A Benchmark for ML Repo Migration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08472)
*Linrong Cai,Taiming Lu,Haozhe Chen,Zhuang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: XStack 是一个评估智能体将机器学习仓库从 PyTorch/NVIDIA GPU 生态迁移到 JAX/Google TPU 生态的基准测试；当前迁移仍不可靠，最佳智能体 Claude Opus 5 仅通过 75%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ML 代码目前主要分属两个生态（PyTorch+NVIDIA GPU 与 JAX+Google TPU），跨框架、跨硬件迁移需要重建并反复测试。迁移既是释放不同硬件算力的现实需求，也是一个天然的高难度长周期基准：它涉及不同框架与硬件、覆盖整个代码仓库。

Method: 提出 XStack 基准：为每个智能体提供一份 PyTorch 仓库和一台干净的 TPU 机器，要求其交付一份 JAX 仓库；输出需通过基于源仓库已发表结果的评分标准，且该标准经过作者认证与校准。

Result: 智能体迁移仍不可靠：Claude Opus 5 通过率 75%，GPT-5.6-sol 通过率 45%，其余所有智能体均不超过 40%。

Conclusion: XStack 是让 ML 代码摆脱生态束缚的一步，其发布旨在使跨框架、跨硬件的可靠迁移变得可度量并推动后续进展。

Abstract: ML code should run where researchers choose, not where it was first written. Today, machine learning code mainly lives in two ecosystems, PyTorch on NVIDIA GPUs and JAX on Google TPUs. Migrating repositories between them means building and testing across frameworks and hardware. This migration is (1) a practical need, since it unlocks different hardware for researchers, and (2) a natural benchmark: it involves different frameworks and hardware, covers whole repositories, and is long-horizon and challenging. We introduce XStack, a benchmark that evaluates agents on migrating ML repositories between these two ecosystems. Each agent receives a PyTorch repository and a clean TPU machine and must deliver a JAX repository. Its output passes if it meets our grading criteria, based on the source repository's published result, authenticated and calibrated by us. Migration by agents remains unreliable: Claude Opus 5 passes 75%, GPT-5.6-sol 45%, and every other agent at most 40%. XStack is a step toward freeing ML code from its ecosystem. We release XStack to make progress toward reliable migration across frameworks and hardware measurable.

</details>


### [214] [Do sEMG Foundation Models Improve Cross-Subject Generalisation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08199)
*William Raftery,Dario Farina*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 系统性评估表明，sEMG基础模型的预训练收益主要体现在同一记录会话内（intra-session），在跨被试（inter-subject）评估下显著消失；在受试者数量较少的数据集上，经典机器学习基线可持平或超过基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: sEMG基础模型宣称可学习跨被试、跨记录条件的通用表征以提升下游任务性能，但现有评估设置无法判断预训练本身是否足以缓解sEMG机器学习中突出的跨被试过拟合问题，因此需要隔离预训练贡献的严格评估。

Method: 在三个手势分类数据集上评估三个sEMG基础模型，采用intra-session与inter-subject两种数据划分，并使用三种微调策略以分离预训练的独立贡献，同时与经典ML基线对比。

Result: 当训练与测试样本来自同一记录会话时，预训练可提升性能；但在跨被试评估下，该收益基本消失。在受试者数量较少的数据集中，预训练几乎无收益，经典ML基线的性能与基础模型相当甚至更优。

Conclusion: 预训练对sEMG的跨被试泛化帮助有限，现有基础模型的优势可能部分源于会话内数据泄漏式的评估设置；低受试者数据场景下基础模型并非必要，亟需更严谨的跨被试协议与真正鲁棒的预训练方法。

Abstract: sEMG Foundation Models aim to learn general purpose representations over diverse subjects and recording conditions that boost performance on a variety of downstream tasks. However, existing evaluations make it difficult to discern whether pretraining alone is sufficient to combat the cross-subject overfitting which makes machine learning on sEMG so challenging. We conduct a systematic evaluation of three sEMG foundation models across three gesture classification datasets under both intra-session and inter-subject splits, using three fine-tuning strategies designed to isolate the contribution of pretraining. We find that pretraining boosts performance when train and test samples are drawn from the same recording session, but this benefit largely disappears under inter-subject evaluation. In low subject count datasets, pretraining provides little to no benefit and classical ML baselines match or exceed foundation model performance.

</details>


### [215] [Beyond Quadratic Attention: Separable Gaussian Operators for ODE Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08236)
*Carlos Boned Riera,David Romero Sanchez,Oriol Ramos Terrades*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SSoG用轴可分离的高斯混合核替代稠密自注意力，将每层代价从O(N²)降至次二次，并在离散、权重共享与连续深度（Neural ODE）三种Transformer中同时提升精度、参数效率与推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自注意力对token数的二次复杂度限制了ViT在高分辨率下的扩展；同时点积注意力的Lipschitz常数在紧致输入域外无界，导致连续深度变体缺少全局解的存在唯一性保证。

Method: 提出SSoG：可分离的和式高斯空间算子，用学习得到的轴因子化高斯核混合替代稠密注意力，使每层复杂度次二次；固定核下算子全局Lipschitz连续，借助Picard–Lindelöf定理证明连续深度版本在整个积分区间上解存在且唯一。

Result: CIFAR-100@224×224、匹配离散/连续设置下，三种宽度Top-1提升2.2–4.1点且参数少8.8–16.6%；循环（weight-shared）设置提升5.4–8.1点。在最高1024×1024、匹配batch下，连续深度架构吞吐最高+154%、峰值显存降低超60%，离散与循环设置吞吐亦提升30%以上，差距随分辨率增大而扩大。

Conclusion: SSoG兼具渐近效率、参数效率与理论可证性（全局Lipschitz与ODE解唯一性），是可跨离散、权重共享与连续深度Transformer通用部署的稠密注意力实用替代方案。

Abstract: Self-attention carries a quadratic cost in token count, which limits how far Vision Transformers can scale with input resolution. We introduce SSoG, a separable sum-of-Gaussians spatial operator that replaces dense self-attention with a learned mixture of axis-factorized Gaussian kernels, reducing the per-layer cost from $\mathcal{O}(N^2)$ to subquadratic in $N$. Unlike dot-product self-attention, whose Lipschitz constant is unbounded outside a compact input domain, SSoG with fixed kernels is globally Lipschitz continuous, which, via the Picard--Lindelöf theorem, guarantees that the continuous-depth variant has a unique solution on the whole integration interval, a global guarantee that dense-attention counterparts lack in general. On CIFAR-100 at $224\times224$, in a matched discrete-Transformer setting and in the continuous setting, SSoG improves Top-1 accuracy by 2.2--4.1 points across three model widths while using 8.8--16.6\% fewer parameters, with depth and training recipe held fixed. In a looped-Transformer setting, SSoG improves Top-1 accuracy by 5.4--8.1 points. We further probe inference-time scaling by evaluating on inputs up to $1024\times1024$ at matched batch size: in the continuous-depth (Neural ODE) architecture, SSoG reaches up to 154\% higher throughput and over 60\% lower peak memory than dense attention, with the gap widening as resolution grows, consistent with the difference in asymptotic cost. In the discrete and looped settings, SSoG also achieves over 30\% higher throughput than dense attention at this resolution. Together, these results establish SSoG as a practical, parameter-efficient, and theoretically grounded alternative to dense attention across discrete, weight-shared, and continuous-depth Transformer variants.

</details>


### [216] [FewStepRL: Fine-tuning Few-step Diffusion Models with Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08240)
*Haitao Lin,Minsi Ren,Yang Li,Xingtong Ge,Zheyuan Hu,Peiyan Hu,Tailin Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 作者提出"变换—分解—适配"三阶段框架，将主流扩散模型RL目标系统性地迁移到少步模型上，从而用少步采样大幅降低RL后训练成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL已成为扩散模型标准后训练手段，但多数目标每次迭代都需用当前策略多步采样，采样开销主导训练成本；少步模型虽能降本，却建模区间平均速度，与面向瞬时速度、多步采样的现有RL目标不匹配。

Method: 三阶段改造：1）变换到x空间，以统一的策略预测（默认在干净端，迁移/流映射目标在显式标记层级）表达各目标，使基于密度、速度或奖励的目标可比；2）将各目标梯度分解为"向参考的自回归项+奖励增强项"，使方法在梯度层面归结为锚点与权重的选择；3）由少步模型在传输末端或沿切线计算锚点，瞬时速度可从网络自身或辅助网络恢复。据此在统一模块化代码库实现FlowGRPO、AWM、DiffusionNFT、DiffusionOPSD、ReFL的少步版本。

Result: 奖励可微且梯度可负担时，基于奖励梯度的方法最有效；否则DiffusionNFT类方法虽单次迭代成本较高，但能在较少迭代内达到高奖励。额外推理步数在总体上无系统性收益，但部分输出在采样步数为训练时的2–4倍时可见改善。

Conclusion: 通过变换、分解与适配，可将多步RL目标统一落地到少步扩散模型，并在单一代码库中公平比较；方法选择应依据奖励可微性与梯度成本，而单纯增加推理步数并非普遍有效。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a standard post-training method for diffusion models, but most RL objectives roll out the current policy at every iteration, and with a many-step sampler these rollouts typically dominate the cost of training. Few-step models cut this cost, yet they model an average velocity over an interval, whereas existing RL objectives are built for an instantaneous velocity with a many-step sampler. We adapt RL objectives to few-step models systematically in three phases, namely a transformation, a decomposition and an adaptation. The transformation to $x$-space expresses each objective we consider through a common policy prediction, at the clean end by default and at an explicitly marked level for transition or flow-map objectives, so that objectives stated over densities, velocities or rewards become comparable. The decomposition writes the gradient of each of these objectives as the sum of a self-regression toward a reference and a reward augmentation, so that a method reduces, at the gradient level, to a choice of anchors and weights. The adaptation computes each anchor from a few-step model at the end of its transport or along its tangent, with the instantaneous velocity recovered from the network itself or from an auxiliary network. This yields few-step versions of FlowGRPO, AWM, DiffusionNFT, DiffusionOPSD and ReFL in one modular codebase. In our experiments, reward-gradient-based methods are the most effective when the reward is differentiable and its gradient is affordable; otherwise DiffusionNFT-style methods generally reach high rewards in relatively few iterations despite a sometimes higher per-iteration cost. Extra inference steps show no systematic aggregate benefit in our experiments, although selected outputs visibly improve with two to four times as many sampling steps as in training.

</details>


### [217] [Geometric Relevance Is Not Sufficient: Evaluating Focused Post-Training Supervision in Diffusion Docking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08249)
*Michael Brown,Maryam Rahimimovassagh,Mehdi Yazdani-Jahromi,Sanjeda Sara Jennifer,Ivan Garibay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在DiffDock固定预算下比较"广泛继续训练"与"聚焦监督"（基于笛卡尔后果或条件目标一致性）、及二者混合策略，结果显示广泛继续训练在Top-1成功率上最优，聚焦/混合监督无一致优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散式分子对接需在较宽的corruption分布上学习校正分数场，但不同位姿误差在结构后果上差异很大，形成后训练阶段的冲突：既要保持分数场的广泛覆盖，又要将监督集中于与对接几何更相关的校正上。

Method: 基于DiffDock进行固定预算对照实验，比较四类训练策略：广泛继续训练、聚焦笛卡尔后果的扭转监督、聚焦条件目标一致性的监督、以及广泛/聚焦混合监督；评测维度分离为固定分数保真度、生成位姿质量、best-of-10采样能力与置信度选择的Top-1对接性能。

Result: 在<5 Å阈值下，广泛继续训练在已训练策略中取得最高平均Top-1成功率；聚焦或混合监督在测试预算下无一致优势。起始模型固定分数保真度更好，而广泛继续训练模型在相同总体best-of-10恢复率下具有更高平均Top-1成功率。

Conclusion: 将监督集中于结构上更关键或指定更一致的校正，并不能稳定提升返回位姿成功率；相较于广覆盖的继续训练，聚焦监督在本设置的预算下未体现收益。

Abstract: Diffusion-based molecular docking requires learning a corrective score field across a broad corruption distribution, yet individual pose errors can differ substantially in their structural consequences. This creates a post-training conflict between preserving broad score-field coverage and concentrating supervision on corrections that appear more relevant to docking geometry. Using DiffDock, a fixed-budget study compares broad continuation with torsional supervision focused on Cartesian consequence or conditional target consistency, and with a broad/focused mixture. Evaluation separates fixed score fidelity, generated-pose quality, best-of-10 sampling capacity, and confidence-selected Top-1 docking performance. At <5 Angstrom, broad continuation had the highest mean Top-1 success among trained policies, and focused or mixed supervision showed no consistent advantage under the tested budget. The starting model had better fixed score fidelity, whereas the evaluated broad-continuation model had higher mean Top-1 success with the same aggregate best-of-10 recovery. Focusing supervision on structurally consequential or more consistently specified corrections did not consistently improve returned-pose success over broad continuation.

</details>


### [218] [Nowcasting of Tropical Cyclone Wind Fields and Intensity from Geostationary Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08255)
*Simon Donike,Pritthijit Nath,Cristina Radin,Arthur Avenas,Ruben Cartuyvels,Emiliano Diaz,William K. Jones,Anna Jungbluth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出用简单神经网络从静止卫星云图预测二维海面风场与风暴尺度强度指标，并通过消融实验证明在隐空间联合预测二维场与标量指标可改善最大风速及其半径的反演，尤其有利于快速增强事件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAR反演的台风高分辨率海面风场虽可支撑风暴结构与强度建模，但时空稀疏且无法近实时获取；静止卫星能连续观测云顶却无法直接解析海面风。故需在二者之间建立映射。

Method: 构建简洁的神经网络，以静止卫星影像为输入，预测二维风场及风暴尺度强度指标（最大风速及其半径）；并开展一系列消融实验，比较从模型隐空间联合预测二维场与标量指标的效果。

Result: 联合预测二维场与标量指标可提升最大风速及其半径的反演精度，且在快速增强事件上改善尤为明显。

Conclusion: 隐空间联合学习二维风场与标量强度指标是一种有效策略，可缓解SAR风场稀疏与静止卫星无法直接测风的局限，为近实时的风暴结构与强度监测提供可行途径。

Abstract: High-resolution tropical cyclone surface wind speeds derived from synthetic aperture radar (SAR) observations can support storm structure and intensity modelling, but remain spatially and temporally sparse and unavailable near-real-time. Geostationary satellites capture cloud tops continuously in space and time, but do not directly resolve surface winds. Here, we present simple neural networks to predict 2D wind fields and storm-scale intensity metrics from geostationary imagery. Through a series of ablation studies, we find that joint prediction of 2D fields and scalar metrics from the model latent space improves the retrieval of the (radius of) maximum wind, especially for rapid intensification events.

</details>


### [219] [Dynamic Optimistic Constrained OCO with Memory via Delay Equivalence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08302)
*Mohammed Abdullah,George Iosifidis,Salah Eddine Elayoubi,Tijani Chahed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出乐观算法，将记忆型在线凸优化（带时变约束）通过"记忆-延迟归约"处理，首次得到同时适应环境非平稳性和预测精度的动态遗憾与累积约束违反界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: COCO-M框架刻画有状态在线学习与非随机控制的预算约束动态，表达力强但研究不足；已有工作依赖函数可分离等限制性假设，且缺乏同时适应路径长度P_T与预测精度的界。

Method: 构造乐观算法利用不可信预测；核心是用从记忆到延迟的归约，将状态相关梯度视为延迟反馈处理；并独立分析延迟型带时变约束OCO。

Result: 首次给出同时适应环境非平稳性（路径长度P_T）和预测精度的界，并放松函数可分离假设；完美预测下遗憾达O(1)，CCV为O(√T)（未知P_T）与O(log T)（已知或在线可观测P_T）；记忆消失时恢复最优COCO速率。

Conclusion: 最坏情况下保持次线性保证，且随预测精度提升而改善；记忆-延迟归约与延迟OCO结论具独立价值。

Abstract: Online Convex Optimization with memory and time-varying constraints (COCO-M) is a recently-introduced framework that captures the dynamics of stateful online learning and non-stochastic control with budget constraints. Despite its expressive power, COCO-M remains largely understudied. In this work, we tackle this problem in its full scale, providing an optimistic algorithm that incorporates untrusted predictions to tame universal dynamic regret and cumulative constraint violation. Our analysis leverages a potent reduction from memory to delay and treats state-dependent gradients as delayed feedback. We establish the first bounds that adapt to both environment non-stationarity (path length $P_T$) and prediction accuracy, while lifting previous restrictive assumptions such as functional separability. En route to these results, we derive findings of independent interest for the memory-to-delay reduction, and for delayed OCO with time-varying constraints. The proposed framework ensures sublinear worst-case guarantees that improve with prediction accuracy, reaching $\mathcal O(1)$ regret under perfect predictions, with cumulative constraint violation (CCV) $\mathcal O(\sqrt{T})$ for unknown $P_T$ and $\mathcal O(\log T)$ for known or online observable $P_T$ , while recovering the optimal COCO rates when memory disappears.

</details>


### [220] [Interpretable many-body interaction laws for collective motion from few trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08314)
*Timon S. Gutleb*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出dACE（directional atomic cluster expansion），将原子团簇展开适配到自驱动个体，构造可系统改进、严格等变的加速度展开，并按体阶唯一分解为1/2/3体及更高阶项，外加一个不吸收相互作用的自身历史项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 群体运动建模通常把社会互动简化为成对力，但实证证据表明存在多体效应；现有神经模型（如SocialNet）需要更多数据、在多体或手性规则上误差较大且限于二维，缺乏可解释、可逐阶分解的互动律学习方法。

Method: 将用于原子间势的原子团簇展开（ACE）迁移到自驱动个体，构建方向性dACE：对个体加速度做严格等变的展开，唯一分解为1、2、3体及更高阶贡献，并单独拟合"自身过去运动"的自项使其不吸收相互作用；在已知规则驱动的二维/三维模拟群体以及斑马鱼（zebrafish）和金体闪光鱼（golden shiners）的追踪数据上验证，并与SocialNet对比，同时分析评价指标。

Result: 在已知规则的模拟群体中，dACE用少量数据即可逐体阶恢复互动律；SocialNet需要更多数据，在多体/手性规则上误差显著更大且仅限2D。在真实鱼群数据中，dACE学到的规律在数据两个不相交半集之间的一致性优于SocialNet。所学规律可解释：金体闪光鱼中，随邻域变密，3体项与成对响应方向相反。此外，下一步预测精度主要反映自驱动与噪声而非相互作用。

Conclusion: dACE提供了可系统改进、等变且可逐体阶解释的多体互动律学习框架，优于纯神经黑箱模型；研究还表明单纯依赖下一步预测精度会误判互动学习质量，应改用更稳健的组合误差度量。

Abstract: Models of collective motion usually reduce social interactions to pairwise forces, despite empirical evidence for many-body effects. We introduce the directional atomic cluster expansion (dACE), which adapts the atomic cluster expansion (ACE) of interatomic potentials to self-propelled agents. dACE provides a systematically improvable, exactly equivariant expansion of an agent's acceleration that splits uniquely into 1-, 2-, 3-body and higher contributions, along with a self term for the agent's own past motion that is fitted so that it does not absorb the interactions. On simulated collectives in two and three dimensions with known rules, dACE recovers the interaction law body order by body order from little data, while the neural model SocialNet, the current closest competitor, needs more data, has much larger errors on many-body or chiral rules and is restricted to 2D. On real data sets of tracked zebrafish and golden shiners, the laws learned by dACE agree better between disjoint halves of the data than SocialNet's. The learned laws are interpretable, and in the shiners the 3-body term opposes the pairwise response as neighbourhoods get dense. Finally, we show that next-step prediction accuracy mostly measures self-propulsion and noise rather than the interactions, and suggest more robust combinations of error measures for interaction learning.

</details>


### [221] [Discovering Learning Rules that Shape Others](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08335)
*Hong Jun Lee,Ziyan Wang,Jun Wang,Stefanos Leonardos,Yali Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DUOS，将元学习发现更新规则的框架扩展到一般和博弈，首个无需访问对手参数/策略/奖励/学习规则即可在一般和博弈中习得更新规则的方法，展现出对手塑形行为并在锦标赛中归一化均分与自博弈均排名第一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对手塑形方法（LOLA、M-FOS）虽能实现互惠合作与利用，但部署时需访问对手的参数、策略、奖励或学习规则，实用性受限；而单智能体强化学习中元学习发现的更新规则（如DiscoRL）仅读取自身经验却达到SOTA，故希望将该框架推广至一般和博弈。

Method: 提出DUOS（Discovered Update Rule for Opponent Shaping）：元训练一个更新规则，以最大化智能体在整个生命周期内的期望回报为目标；该规则仅使用智能体自身经验来更新内层网络，不读取对手的策略或参数。

Result: 所发现的规则在未读取对手策略或参数的情况下表现出与对手塑形一致的行为；在锦标赛参赛者中，归一化平均得分排名第1，自博弈排名第1。

Conclusion: 据作者所知，这是首个在一般和博弈中为智能体发现更新规则的方法，表明元学习到的更新规则可在信息受限条件下实现对手塑形，并为一般和博弈中的学习规则发现提供了新范式。

Abstract: In general-sum games, self-interested learners can reach outcomes that are poor for everyone, which has motivated methods that shape the opponent's learning. Methods like Learning with Opponent-Learning Awareness (LOLA), which differentiates through the opponent's learning process, and Model-Free Opponent Shaping (M-FOS), which uses model-free optimisation methods for long-horizon opponent shaping, have enabled mutual cooperation and exploitation of learners. However, these methods require access to the opponent's parameters, policy, rewards or learning rule during deployment. In single-agent reinforcement learning, update rules discovered by meta-learning have achieved state-of-the-art results (e.g. DiscoRL). These rules are separate from the policy they train and read only the agent's own experience. We hereby extend this framework to general-sum settings by introducing Discovered Update Rule for Opponent Shaping (DUOS). To the best of our knowledge, this is the first method to discover an update rule for an agent in general-sum games. DUOS meta-trains the rule to maximise the agent's expected return over its lifetime, and updates an inner network using the agent's own experience. We show that our discovered rule exhibits behaviour consistent with opponent shaping without reading opponent policies or parameters, and ranks 1st in normalised mean score and 1st in self-play among tournament entrants.

</details>


### [222] [StrataFlux: Memory-Hierarchy-Native Low-Bit LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08363)
*Jake Hyun,Ziyu Zhao,Jae-sun Seo,Mohamed S. Abdelfattah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 StrataFlux 框架，通过对精细化表示、选择与传输的协同设计，利用主机内存与固态硬盘作为额外精度的来源，在保留量化显存与速度优势的前提下，恢复低比特权重与 KV 缓存量化所损失的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 推理中量化可降低显存与带宽需求但会损失精度；而将高精度数据卸载又会导致生成停顿。论文试图打破"精度—显存—延迟"这一权衡。

Method: 协同设计三项要素：(1) 精细化表示（refinement representation）；(2) 精度流的选取（selection）；(3) 数据投递（delivery）。核心为选择性精度流：压缩传输数据量、将数据传输与计算重叠（overlap）、并在解码步间复用已取回的精度数据，使生成无需等待每次传输完成。

Result: 在独立 GPU 与统一内存平台上评测 2–4-bit 权重与 2–3-bit KV 缓存。2-bit Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct 在 RTX 5090 上 WikiText-2 困惑度由 9.46 降至 8.40，保持量化基线 95.4% 的吞吐；2-bit 权重+KV 使 Qwen3-8B 在 AIME 2024/2025 上准确率由 11.0% 提升至 47.1%，GPU 显存占用不变；128K 上下文下，3-bit 权重+2-bit KV 的 Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct 达到与 vLLM 4-bit AWQ 权重+8-bit KV 相当的困惑度与速度，GPU 显存约为后者一半。

Conclusion: GPU 常驻精度不必决定精度上限，卸载层延迟也不必左右解码速度。低比特量化配合选择性精度流可在几乎不牺牲速度与显存的前提下显著恢复精度。

Abstract: Quantization reduces the memory and bandwidth demands of large language model (LLM) inference but can degrade accuracy; offloading higher-precision data can instead stall generation. We introduce StrataFlux, a framework that challenges this tradeoff by recovering accuracy lost to low-bit weight and key-value (KV) cache quantization while retaining quantization's memory and speed advantages. StrataFlux co-designs refinement representation, selection, and delivery to make host RAM and solid-state drives useful sources of additional precision. Its selective precision streaming reduces transfer volume, overlaps delivery with computation, and reuses fetched precision across decoding steps so generation need not wait for every transfer. We evaluate 2-4-bit weights and 2-3-bit KV-cache on dedicated GPUs and unified-memory platforms. For 2-bit Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct weights, StrataFlux reduces WikiText-2 perplexity from 9.46 to 8.40 while maintaining 95.4% of the quantized base's throughput on RTX 5090. With 2-bit weights and KV, StrataFlux raises Qwen3-8B's AIME 2024/2025 accuracy from 11.0% to 47.1% at unchanged GPU memory use. At 128K context, Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct with 3-bit weights and 2-bit KV achieves comparable perplexity and speed to vLLM's 4-bit AWQ weights and 8-bit KV on RTX 5090, using approximately half the GPU memory. These results show that GPU-resident precision need not set the accuracy ceiling, nor offloading-tier latency dictate decoding speed.

</details>


### [223] [Muir: Linear Attention with Sensitivity-Allocated Gating and Matched-Filter Readout](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08376)
*Tushaar Gangavarapu,Gil Tabak,Clemens JS Schaefer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Muir，一种基于敏感度分析的线性注意力架构：以敏感度分配低秩投影融合全部门控、用匹配滤波读出重加权状态行，并采用并行更新保证大分块训练稳定，在166M–1.34B规模上优于 GDN-2 与 Transformer。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性注意力/门控 delta-rule 模型的 state 更新中，擦除与写入的敏感度并不对称：key 激活与单位归一化把 key 能量集中到稀疏通道子集，而 value 写入作用于全部通道，因此 erase gate 可用更低秩参数化；同时慢衰减状态行范数远大于快衰减行，主导未加权读出并迫使写入时更强擦除以抑制干扰。作者据此重新分配参数与计算。

Method: 1) 将各门控融合为单一“敏感度分配”的低秩投影；2) 引入匹配滤波读出门对状态行加权，抵消行范数不均衡；3) 将节省的参数投入更宽的 FFN；4) 针对标准 chunkwise 算法在大 chunk 下数值不稳定，设计并行更新以支持稳定高效训练。基线为 Gated DeltaNet-2 (GDN-2) 与 Transformer。

Result: 166M–1.34B 参数范围内：困惑度较 GDN-2 降低 1.8–2.0%，较 Transformer 降低 2.5–3.6%；常识推理准确率最高提升 1.9 分；抽取式召回优于 GDN-2。1.34B 模型在多跳与多针检索上均超两基线，从 4K 训练长度外推 2,560 倍至 10M token 流而无困惑度漂移，静态读出下 1M token passkey 检索近 100%。吞吐与 GDN-2 持平，100B token 验证损失达成快 1.2 倍，32K 下训练比 Transformer 快 2.7 倍。

Conclusion: 状态更新的参数预算应按擦除/写入的敏感度非对称分配，且读出端需对行范数不均衡做补偿；配合面向大分块稳定性的并行更新，可在提升质量与长上下文能力的同时保持线性注意力的效率优势。

Abstract: Linear attention compresses context into a fixed-size key-value state for linear-time training and constant-memory inference. Gated delta-rule models decay this state and erase its prediction for an incoming key before writing a new value; Gated DeltaNet-2 (GDN-2) generalizes this with channel-wise decay and decoupled, full-rank erase and write gates. However, we show that state updates are far less sensitive to erasure than to writes: key activation and unit normalization concentrate key energy onto a sparse subset of channels, whereas value writes scale all channels, so the erase gate can be parameterized at lower rank. Furthermore, slow-decaying state rows accumulate much larger norms than fast rows, dominating unweighted readout and forcing stronger write-time erasure to suppress interference. Guided by this analysis, we introduce Muir, which fuses all gates into a sensitivity-allocated low-rank projection, re-weights state rows with a matched-filter readout gate, and reinvests the saved parameters in a wider feed-forward network. Because standard chunkwise algorithms become numerically unstable at the large chunk sizes needed to saturate wide matrix units, we design a parallel update for stable, efficient training. Across 166M-1.34B parameters, Muir lowers perplexity by 1.8-2.0% over GDN-2 and 2.5-3.6% over Transformer, raises commonsense accuracy by up to 1.9 points, and improves extractive recall over GDN-2. At 1.34B, Muir outperforms both baselines on multi-hop and multi-needle recall, extrapolates 2,560x from its 4K training length to 10M-token streams without perplexity drift, and maintains near-100% passkey retrieval to 1M tokens with static readout. Muir achieves these gains while matching GDN-2 throughput, reaching its 100B-token validation loss 1.2x faster, and training 2.7x faster than Transformer at 32K.

</details>


### [224] [Module-Aware Optimization for Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08379)
*Guy Hadad,Haggai Roitman,Moshe Eliasof,Bracha Shapira*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 GraphMuon，一种面向图神经网络（GNN）的图感知优化器，将 Muon 的矩阵正交化更新适配到图传播表示的协方差结构上，理论上缓解图诱导的更新放大问题，实验在 OGB 等基准上取得显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同优化器对参数更新施加不同的几何结构：Adam/AdamW 采用逐坐标更新，Muon 通过正交化利用权重矩阵结构。但对 GNN 而言，权重矩阵的有效几何不仅由学习到的通道混合权重决定，还受其作用的图传播表示影响。因此即使更新在矩阵意义上被良好归一化，作用于图传播特征时其对层输出的影响仍可能被放大。现有优化器忽略了这一图诱导的几何差异。

Method: 提出 GraphMuon：在图传播层输入的协方差结构所定义的度量下进行正交化，并按更新对层表示的诱导效应归一化步长，从而得到 Muon 极坐标更新步的图度量类比版本。论文从理论上分析并证明原始 Muon 会遭受图诱导的更新放大，而 GraphMuon 可缓解该效应。

Result: 在三个大规模 OGB 基准上，GraphMuon 相比 AdamW 取得 1.32×–2.42× 的验证损失到达目标时间（time-to-target）加速；在涵盖节点分类、归纳式图分类与图回归的 15 种数据集-架构配置中，13 种情形下达到更低的 time-to-target，同时保持相当或更优的预测性能。

Conclusion: 将优化器的几何设计与 GNN 的图传播特性相结合是有效且必要的：考虑图传播表示协方差结构的 GraphMuon 能在保证预测性能的前提下显著提升收敛速度，说明矩阵感知优化器需针对图结构进行几何适配。

Abstract: Different optimizers impose different geometries on parameter updates. Adaptive methods such as Adam and AdamW use coordinate-wise updates, while matrix-aware optimizers such as Muon utilize weight-matrix structure through orthogonalized updates. For Graph Neural Networks (GNNs), however, the effective geometry of its weight matrices is shaped not only by its learned channel-mixing weights, but also by the graph-propagated representations it acts on. Thus, even if an update is well normalized as a matrix, its effect on the layer output can be amplified when applied to graph-propagated features. To address this gap, we introduce GraphMuon, a graph-aware optimizer that adapts Muon's matrix updates to the covariance structure of graph-propagated layer inputs. GraphMuon performs orthogonalization in a metric defined by these propagated representations and normalizes step sizes according to the update's induced effect on the layer representation. We show that theoretically, vanilla Muon can suffer from graph-induced update amplification, while our GraphMuon can mitigate this effect. As such, our GraphMuon yields a graph-metric analogue of Muon's polar update step. Empirically, GraphMuon achieves $1.32\times$-$2.42\times$ validation-loss time-to-target speedups over AdamW on three large-scale OGB benchmarks. Across 15 dataset-architecture configurations spanning node classification, inductive graph classification, and graph regression, it achieves lower time-to-target in 13 cases, while maintaining competitive or superior predictive performance. Code is available at https://github.com/guy-hadad/GraphMuon.

</details>


### [225] [Synthesis of Ovarian Cancer Multi-Omics Datasets: A Biology-Informed Evaluation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08394)
*Déborah Boyenval,Alina Malyutina,Jaana Oikkonen,Sammeli Liikkanen,Sampsa Hautaniemi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个以生物学为依据、患者不重叠的多组学合成数据评估框架，用于评价配对RNA表达与CNV谱生成，并在DECIDER高级别浆液性卵巢癌纵向队列上做了案例研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 配对多组学癌症队列构建成本高、共享困难，使合成队列成为有价值的补充资源；而生成模型快速发展，研究重点应从"再提一个架构"转向定义"合成数据是否保留生物学相关结构"的评估准则。

Method: 构建患者不重叠（patient-disjoint）的评估协议：以真实训练数据与留出数据之间的一致性作为参照基准，评估各模态自身的保真度以及RNA–CNV配对的模态间一致性，并辅以训练数据邻近性（training-data proximity）诊断；同时基于变分自编码器改造三种多组学整合架构以生成配对谱。

Result: 在多项保真度与一致性指标上表现最好的模型，其训练数据邻近性也最强，表明各项评估维度存在一致性；该协议与案例研究为后续多组学生成器的比较提供了参考。

Conclusion: 评估应聚焦于生物学相关结构的保留而非架构新颖性，所提出的患者不重叠、以真实数据一致性为参照的框架可作为比较多组学合成数据生成方法的标准化基准。

Abstract: Paired multi-omic cancer cohorts are costly to assemble and difficult to share, motivating synthetic cohorts as a complementary resource. As generative model development accelerates, we shift the focus from proposing yet another architecture to defining evaluation criteria that determine whether synthetic data preserve biologically relevant structure. We introduce a biology-informed and patient-disjoint framework for evaluating the generation of paired RNA expression and copy-number variation (CNV) profiles, and apply it to the longitudinal DECIDER high-grade serous ovarian cancer cohort. The framework assesses modality-specific fidelity and coherence between RNA and CNV pairs using agreement between real training and held-out data as a reference, alongside complementary training-data proximity diagnostics. We adapt three multi-omic integration architectures based on variational autoencoders to generate paired profiles. The model leading several fidelity and coherence measures also shows the strongest training-data proximity. The protocol and case study offer the community a reference for comparing future multi-omic generators.

</details>


### [226] [Conformal Prediction for Time-Dependent PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08409)
*Joshua Stiller,Annika Schneider,Eyke Hüllermeier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究PDE代理模型在时间相关问题中的保形预测覆盖失效问题，证明函数空间中任意接近时刻的分布可互奇异导致精确覆盖不可能，离散化下总变差距离随网格分辨率指数饱和，并提出加权保形预测在增长时间域上获得有限样本覆盖保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学机器学习模型常用于洪水预报、金融风险管理、机器热控制等安全关键任务，需要可靠的不确定性量化。保形预测能提供无分布假设的覆盖保证，但在物理与工程中常见的时间相关设定下，这一保证可能失效并导致系统性欠覆盖（undercoverage）。

Method: 以偏微分方程描述的时间相关物理系统的代理模型为研究对象：(1) 在函数空间设定下分析相邻时刻分布的奇异性；(2) 在离散化设定下分析分布间总变差距离随网格分辨率的行为；(3) 针对特定设定采用有原则的加权保形预测（weighted conformal prediction）构造时间域上的覆盖保证。

Result: 证明了函数空间中任意接近时刻的分布可能互奇异，从而精确覆盖保证不可能实现；进一步表明离散设定下分布间的总变差距离通常随网格分辨率指数快速饱和，因此必须对空间离散施加限制才能确保覆盖；在特定设定下，加权保形预测可为增长的时间域提供有限样本覆盖保证。

Conclusion: 时间相关PDE代理模型中的标准保形预测存在根本性局限：连续函数空间下精确覆盖不可得，离散化下需限制空间分辨率。加权保形预测为特定情形提供了可行的有限样本覆盖方案，凸显了针对时间相关物理系统重新设计不确定性量化方法的必要性。

Abstract: Uncertainty quantification is crucial in scientific machine learning, where models inform safety-critical tasks such as flood forecasting, financial risk management, and thermal control in machines. Conformal prediction provides distribution-free coverage guarantees, but in time-dependent settings common to physics and engineering, these guarantees can break down, leading to systematic undercoverage. We study this problem in the context of surrogate models for time-dependent physical systems described by partial differential equations. We prove that in a function space setting, distributions at arbitrarily close times can be mutually singular, making exact coverage guarantees impossible. We further show that in discretized settings, the total variation distance between distributions often saturates exponentially fast with the grid resolution, mandating restrictions on the spatial discretization to ensure coverage. Finally, for certain settings, we show how to use principled weighted conformal prediction to obtain finite-sample coverage guarantees over growing time horizons.

</details>


### [227] [Spectral and Subspace Dynamics of LoRA in Supervised Fine-Tuning and Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08414)
*Chenruo Liu,Zihan Wang,Yijun Dong,Qi Lei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文用奇异谱与子空间分析揭示LoRA在SFT与RL两阶段的动力学差异，定位到组均值基线减除是根源机制，并据此分别设计谱铺平的LoRA更新策略，在数学推理任务上多数设置取得最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA在SFT与RL中常沿用相似优化器设置，但两阶段不同动力学如何体现在LoRA低秩增量上并不清楚，缺乏可解释的机制认识与针对性设计。

Method: 在数学推理任务上追踪LoRA更新的奇异谱与奇异子空间；逐项插值SFT与RL的要素以定位差异来源；用经LoRA更新的二次多指标模型（quadratic multi-index model）给出理论机制；基于结论定制策略：SFT中压平全量更新的谱以分散适配方向，RL中压平当前LoRA增量span之外的部分以促进子空间旋转。

Result: RL在各学习率下均使谱集中，SFT在中等学习率下使谱扩张；RL在小学习率下子空间旋转远快于SFT；组均值基线减除是两项对比的共同来源；玩具模型显示其将连贯拟合与梯度噪声的平衡推向谱集中与更快子空间旋转，并迫使风险有利学习率偏低；多种模型实验表明两阶段各自配置在多数评测设置中优于对比方法。

Conclusion: SFT与RL的LoRA动力学差异由组均值基线减除驱动，可用谱与子空间的几何语言统一刻画；按阶段定制谱铺平策略可有效提升LoRA微调性能。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) is commonly used in both supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning (RL), often under similar optimizer settings. Yet, how the different dynamics of the two stages manifest in the low-rank increment that LoRA optimizes remains unclear. Tracking the singular spectrum and subspaces of LoRA updates on mathematical reasoning tasks, we find that RL concentrates the spectrum at every learning rate, while SFT expands it at moderate learning rates; moreover, RL rotates the singular subspaces much faster than SFT at small learning rates. Interpolating between SFT and RL one ingredient at a time, we pinpoint group-mean baseline subtraction as the source of both contrasts. A quadratic multi-index model updated via LoRA unveils the underlying mechanism: group-mean baseline subtraction tilts the balance between two competing forces, coherent fitting and gradient noise, toward spectral concentration and faster subspace rotation, while forcing the risk-favorable learning rate to be low. Guided by these dynamics, we tailor LoRA updates to each stage: in SFT, we flatten the spectrum of the full update to spread adaptation across singular directions, whereas in RL, we flatten the part of the update outside the current LoRA increment span to promote subspace rotation. Experiments on mathematical reasoning tasks across multiple models show that the respective configurations for SFT and RL yield the best performance among the compared methods in most evaluated settings.

</details>


### [228] [On the limitations of molecular representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08420)
*Acer Blake,Fergus Imrie,Charlotte M. Deane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 系统评估分子表示质量，发现SOTA分子嵌入模型在捕捉图结构、3D形状和ADMET相似性上均不如指纹或理化描述符，而经典指纹也存在表示非唯一性等实质局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子表示（指纹、学习嵌入）是化学信息学与小分子机器学习中聚类、数据集划分、虚拟筛选等任务的核心输入，但其评价通常仅局限于狭窄基准任务上的下游模型性能，缺乏对表示本身质量的系统考察。

Method: 对分子表示质量开展系统性研究，在多个维度（图结构相似性、3D形状相似性、ADMET终点相似性）上比较学习式分子嵌入模型与指纹、理化描述符；并分析表示的非唯一性，统计不同表示在10.8B规模Enamine REAL数据库中的碰撞情况。

Result: 在所考察的各维度上，没有任何SOTA分子嵌入模型能优于指纹或理化描述符；同时经典指纹亦暴露实质局限：不同表示下，Enamine REAL中约225M至4B个分子具有非唯一表示，对虚拟筛选构成影响。

Conclusion: 当前分子表示存在明显局限，亟需发展更稳健的小分子编码方法，为未来研究指明方向。

Abstract: Molecular representations such as fingerprints and learned embeddings play a central role in cheminformatics and small-molecule machine learning. They serve as the primary input for a diverse array of tasks, including clustering, dataset splitting, and virtual screening, yet their evaluation is often limited to downstream model performance on narrow benchmark tasks. We carried out a systematic investigation of the quality of molecular representations, finding that none of the state-of-the-art molecular embedding models capture graph structure, 3D shape, or ADMET endpoint similarity better than fingerprints or physicochemical descriptors. However, classical fingerprints also revealed their own material limitations. For example, we show that depending on the representation, between 225M and $\sim$4B molecules have non-unique representations in the 10.8B Enamine REAL database, with implications for virtual screening. Our findings provide an insight into the current limitations of molecular representations, outlining future research directions for the development of more robust encodings of small molecules.

</details>


### [229] [A Hidden Markov Preference Model for Online Prediction of Adversary Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08445)
*Angel Daruna,Michael Cogswell,Tixiao Shan,Yi Yao,Han-Pang Chiu,Ajay Divakaran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出隐马尔可夫模型刻画攻击者行为：隐状态为活跃行为体制，体制条件动作分布定义在可行性掩码的可执行动作树上，同一似然既给出下一动作分布又更新体制信念，估计分解为凸逻辑回归并由Baum-Welch复用；在CAGE 2基准上滤波隐状态每步值0.8 nats，控制切换后损失三步内开始下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 防御方需在攻击者行动前预测其下一动作分布，但存在两个难点：攻击者行为体制会持续并在一个回合内切换；每个动作由操作与状态相关参数组成，其可行取值随时间变化。现有方法难以同时刻画体制切换与可行性子空间变化。

Method: 构建隐马尔可夫模型：潜变量为当前活跃体制，体制条件动作律定义在经可行性掩码的可执行动作树上；同一似然同时用作下一动作分布与观测到动作后的体制信念更新，部分观测前缀贡献其已解析的局部因子；估计分解为若干凸逻辑回归拟合，体制无标签时由Baum-Welch迭代复用。理论上推导了体制信息的可达加权逐节点KL表达式、切换体制路径的序列级对数损失界与均匀回退保证。

Result: 在CAGE 2网络防御基准上，滤波后的隐状态带来每步约0.8 nats的增益；控制器切换后，本方法损失在3步内开始下降，而循环神经网络基线需10步。

Conclusion: 将体制切换与可行性约束统一到掩码动作树上的HMM，可实现可解释、可凸优化且支持在线信念更新的下一动作预测，并在体制切换后具备更快适应能力。

Abstract: A defender anticipating an attacker needs a probability distribution over the attacker's next action from information available before that action occurs. Two structures complicate this: the attacker's behavioral regime persists and can switch within an episode, and each action combines an operation with state-dependent arguments whose feasible values change over time. We capture both with a hidden Markov model whose latent state is the active regime and whose regime-conditioned action law is defined on a feasibility-masked tree of executable actions. The same likelihood forms the next-action distribution and updates the regime belief once the action is seen, and a partially observed prefix contributes its resolved local factors. Estimation separates into convex logistic fits that Baum-Welch reuses when regimes are unlabeled. We derive a reach-weighted nodewise KL expression for regime information, a sequence-wise log-loss bound for switching regime paths, and a uniform fallback guarantee. On the CAGE 2 cyber defense benchmark the filtered latent state is worth 0.8 nats per step, and after a controller switch the loss begins to fall in three steps against ten for a recurrent baseline.

</details>


### [230] [Unpacking the Compute Efficiency of Strong-to-Weak OPD against GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08453)
*Zeyu Leo Liu,Yang Li,Bo Pang,Emre Can Acikgoz,Xuan-Phi Nguyen,Anurag Koul,Srijan Bansal,Erik Nijkamp,Shafiq Joty,Semih Yavuz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 系统比较on-policy distillation（OPD）与GRPO的“精度/训练FLOPs”效率：外部post-trained教师时OPD总体不如GRPO；但当教师为学生自身的GRPO checkpoint时，OPD在数学上以50×更少FLOPs复现该精度，并可推广到更大模型、代码域与另一模型家族；多教师OPD可将各域专家整合为单一学生，联合训练+蒸馏在两域混合上比joint GRPO少42.0%总FLOPs。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心问题是：在冻结教师逐token稠密监督下、用学生自身rollout训练的OPD，其稠密信号能否比RL（GRPO）以更少训练FLOPs达到目标精度。作者希望以FLOPs为统一度量，厘清OPD相对RL在何种教师设置下才具备计算效率优势，而非笼统声称蒸馏更省算力。

Method: 将vanilla reverse-KL OPD与GRPO在匹配设置下对照，遍历任务难度、学生与教师规模、领域及优化超参；以held-out accuracy对training FLOPs作图评估。分别考察两类教师：(1)外部post-trained教师（含进一步用GRPO训练的教师）；(2)学生自身的GRPO checkpoint。进一步采用Multi-Teacher OPD，将各领域GRPO专家蒸馏进单一学生。

Result: 外部教师下OPD在同等设置下accuracy per training FLOP普遍劣于GRPO，且常出现response-length inflation；以学生自身GRPO checkpoint为教师时，OPD在数学上以50×更少蒸馏FLOPs达到该checkpoint精度，并在更大模型学生、代码域及第二模型家族中一致复现。多教师OPD蒸馏阶段仅占专家训练FLOPs的5.2%（2域）与8.0%（4域），匹配各专家精度；计入专家训练后，2域混合上总FLOPs较joint GRPO减少42.0%。

Conclusion: OPD的计算优势高度依赖教师来源：外部教师下并不优于RL；以自身GRPO checkpoint为教师时，OPD是高效率的精度复现/能力整合手段。单域场景下GRPO checkpoint本身即目标模型，蒸馏只增加总计算，且不同域需不同专家checkpoint，故多教师OPD定位为“专家整合”而非“替代RL训练”，端到端仅在多域混合时节省总FLOPs。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student on its own rollouts under a frozen teacher's per-token supervision. Does this dense signal reduce the training FLOPs needed to reach a target accuracy compared with reinforcement learning? We systematically compare vanilla reverse-KL OPD with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) across task difficulty, student and teacher size, domain, and optimization choices, measuring held-out accuracy against training FLOPs. With an external post-trained teacher, OPD generally loses to GRPO in accuracy per training FLOP at matched settings, even when the teacher is further trained with GRPO, and often suffers response-length inflation. Yet a different teacher changes the picture: when the teacher is the student's own GRPO checkpoint, OPD recovers that checkpoint's accuracy on math with $50\times$ fewer distillation FLOPs than GRPO needs to reach the same accuracy. Following the same teacher setting, we consistently observe this high efficiency with a larger student, on code, and in a second model family. On a single domain, however, the GRPO checkpoint is already the desired model, so distilling it adds to total compute. Across domains, separate GRPO checkpoints hold different capabilities, and no single checkpoint serves every domain. Multi-teacher OPD (DeepSeek-AI, 2026) consolidates per-domain GRPO experts into a single student that matches each expert's accuracy, with the distillation stage costing only 5.2% (on 2 domains) and 8.0% (on 4 domains) of the specialist training FLOPs. Counting specialist training and distillation together, this pipeline reaches joint GRPO's performance on the 2-domain mix with 42.0% fewer total FLOPs.

</details>


### [231] [Adversarial Training with Large Step Sizes: Implicit Bias, Optimization, and Sharpness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08457)
*Yi Feng,Andrea Paudice*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究对抗训练(AT)中大常数步长确定性梯度下降(GD)的动力学，以对抗线性逻辑回归为对象分析隐式偏置与收敛速度，并进一步研究神经网络训练中损失尖锐度的演化，揭示与标准训练(ST)的三点关键差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AT动力学研究多局限于小步长或消失步长情形（此时GD近似连续时间流），但此类步长训练效率低、实际很少使用，因此需要理解大常数步长下AT的真实动力学行为。

Method: 分析大常数步长GD在对抗线性逻辑回归中的动力学，分别刻画模型参数的收敛点（隐式偏置）与损失下降速度（优化）；随后转向神经网络训练，分析AT过程中损失尖锐度的演化规律。

Result: 得到三点发现：(i) AT与ST之间的隐式偏置差距此前仅在小步长下成立，现证明其在任意大常数步长下依然存在；(ii) 与ST不同，AT的优化误差不存在一致收敛速率；(iii) 尖锐度可呈现两阶段动力学，不同于ST中的Edge-of-Stability行为。

Conclusion: 本文推进了对AT中大步长GD行为的理解，系统揭示了其与标准训练在小步长之外的三点本质差异，为实际大步长对抗训练的优化与泛化分析提供理论依据。

Abstract: Adversarial Training (AT) is an important method for improving model robustness. We study the dynamics of deterministic Gradient Descent (GD) in AT. Most existing works in this direction focus on the small or vanishing step sizes regime, where GD resembles a continuous-time flow. However, such step sizes can make training inefficient and are rarely used in practice. In this paper, we study the AT dynamics of GD with large constant step sizes. We first study GD in adversarial linear logistic regression, focusing on two aspects: where the model parameters converge (implicit bias) and how quickly the losses decrease (optimization). We then turn to neural networks training and study how the sharpness of losses evolve during AT. Our results advance the understanding of the behaviors of GD with large step sizes in AT and highlight three key differences from its behavior in Standard Training (ST): (i) the implicit bias gap between AT and ST, previously established only for small step sizes, persists for arbitrarily large constant step sizes, (ii) different from ST, there is no uniform rate of convergence for the optimization error in AT, and (iii) sharpness can exhibit two-stage dynamics that differ from the Edge-of-Stability behavior observed in ST.

</details>


### [232] [User Embeddings are Superpositions of Interpretable Behavioral Modes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08487)
*Jonathan Kozaczuk,Zheng Dong,Noyan Evirgen,Keivan Majidi,Eddie K. H. Ng,Chris Porter,Qi Zhao,Jia Geng,Changmao Li,Brandon R. Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将用户嵌入视为可解释行为模式的稀疏叠加，用稀疏自编码器（SAE）恢复模式并自动命名，实现模型无关的事后可解释性与零样本受众检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户嵌入虽能紧凑编码丰富行为信息，但本质上是稠密且不透明的，可解释性差；本文旨在为这类嵌入提供可解释的结构化理解框架。

Method: 提出“用户嵌入=可解释行为模式的稀疏叠加”这一观点（线性嵌入下由生成模型直接预测，深度学习表示下由叠加假设近似成立）；采用稀疏字典学习（如稀疏自编码器SAE）恢复叠加模式，并对恢复的模式自动进行自然语言命名与查询；框架为事后、模型无关，无需改动上游嵌入系统。

Result: 在具有已知真值的合成数据上验证模式恢复效果，SAE优于经典稀疏基线（含对transformer编码嵌入）；在两个真实数据集的经典与transformer用户嵌入上应用该方法；自然语言标签支持对从未用文本训练的嵌入进行零样本受众检索与刻画。

Conclusion: SAE式稀疏字典学习可将稠密用户嵌入分解为可解释、可命名的行为模式，为推荐与行为建模系统提供通用、即插即用的可解释性方案。

Abstract: User embeddings are central components of modern recommendation, personalization, and behavioral modeling systems. Though these embeddings can compactly encode rich behavioral information, they are inherently dense and opaque, limiting interpretability. To address this gap, we argue that user embeddings can be understood as sparse superpositions of interpretable behavioral modes. This structure is directly predicted by generative models of user behavior for linear embeddings and extends approximately to deep-learning representations by the superposition hypothesis. Superimposed modes can naturally be recovered by sparse dictionary learning methods, such as sparse autoencoders (SAEs), and recovered modes can be automatically named and queried in natural language. We validate mode recovery against known ground truth on synthetic data, where SAEs recover modes better than classical sparse baselines, including on transformer-encoded embeddings. We then apply the method to classical and transformer-based user embeddings from two real-world datasets. We also show how the corresponding natural-language labels enable zero-shot audience retrieval and characterization over embeddings never trained on text. The framework is post-hoc and model-agnostic, requiring no modification to the upstream embedding system.

</details>


<div id='cs.CY'></div>

# cs.CY [[Back]](#toc)

### [233] [Code, Commits, and Complexity in the GenAI Era: A Longitudinal Study of Team-Based Capstone Projects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07297)
*Asma Shakil,Claus Brabrand,Paul Denny*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 对260个毕业设计团队仓库跨十个学期的分析显示，自2024年起学生代码库规模约增至三倍（3.4→10 KLOC），圈复杂度与错误处理密度上升而注释密度不变，提交更频繁但单次提交规模不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI工具在学生中已普及，但团队项目代码库的实际变化缺乏实证；而项目课程常以仓库活动作为进展与学习证据，因此需要了解真实项目情境下代码库如何演变。既有研究多依赖自我报告、受控任务或短期个人作业，难以回答该问题。

Method: 对2021—2025年十个学期的N=260个capstone团队仓库进行纵向分析，覆盖AI前后队列；从三个维度度量：代码规模（KLOC）、代码特征（注释密度、错误处理密度、圈复杂度）、开发行为（提交量、编辑规模、提交频率）。研究为观察性分析，未直接观测AI使用。

Result: 学期末代码库规模由2021—2023年约3.4 KLOC增至2024—2025年约10 KLOC；圈复杂度与错误处理密度上升，注释密度未变，呈现明显解耦；后续队列提交频率与总量显著增加，但单次提交规模基本不变，表明开发节奏改变而非编辑粒度改变；多数变化自2024年起出现且并非单调。

Conclusion: 这些变化与GenAI可用性提升同期出现，但不能归因为AI使用的因果效应；研究提示需重新审视项目课程中学生产出物的使用与评价方式（如以仓库活动作为学习证据的有效性）。

Abstract: Generative AI tools are now widely available to students, but little is known about how team-based project codebases have changed since they appeared. This matters when project courses use repository activity as evidence of progress and learning. Existing studies have largely examined self-reports, controlled tasks, or short individual assignments, leaving open questions around how student codebases evolve in authentic project settings. We present a ten-semester analysis of N=260 capstone team repositories from 2021 to 2025, spanning pre- and post-AI cohorts. We examine three dimensions: code size (lines of code), code characteristics (comment density, error-handling density, and cyclomatic complexity), and development behaviour (commit volume, edit size, and commit frequency). We find that end-of-semester codebases roughly triple, from about 3.4 KLOC in 2021--2023 to about 10 KLOC in 2024--2025. Code characteristics show a clear decoupling: cyclomatic complexity and error-handling density increase, while comment density does not. Later cohorts commit far more often and in greater volume, but individual commit sizes are largely unchanged, suggesting a shift in development rhythm rather than edit granularity. Most changes appear from 2024 onwards and are not uniformly monotonic. We did not directly observe AI use, so we report these as changes that coincide with increased GenAI availability rather than as effects of AI use. Our findings highlight the need to reconsider how student-produced artefacts are used and assessed in project courses.

</details>


### [234] [Digital Security Under Compound Threats: Surveillance, Threat Reasoning, and Habituation Among North Korean Defectors in China](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07576)
*Yibo Meng,Bingyi Liu,Lionel Z. Wang,Hongkun Yang*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 通过对19名在华脱北者的半结构化访谈，揭示其在跨国监控压力、身份绑定型数字基础设施与既往监控认知框架下形成不均衡的数字安全实践，并归纳出"适应性习惯化"与"侵蚀性习惯化"两条轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在华脱北者长期处于数字安全与隐私的不确定状态，但其威胁认知与防护行为如何形成尚缺乏实证刻画，尤其缺乏对无合法身份用户群体的安全推理机制研究。

Method: 对19名脱北者开展半结构化访谈，质性分析其威胁理解与安全行为调整，归纳出三类相互关联的条件：来自朝鲜的跨国监控压力、中国境内与身份绑定的数字基础设施及中介依赖、由既往监控经验塑造的威胁推理模式。

Result: 受访者的防护实践参差不齐，分化出两条轨迹：由知识获取驱动的适应性习惯化，以及因长期未遭遇负面事件而形成的侵蚀性习惯化。

Conclusion: 复合威胁、归因不确定性与受限能动性共同塑造了无合法身份用户的安全推理；研究为脆弱群体的数字安全设计与支持干预提供了经验依据。

Abstract: North Korean defectors residing in China face persistent uncertainty regarding digital security and privacy. Through semi-structured interviews with 19 defectors, we examine how individuals understand digital threats and adapt their security behaviors. We identify three interrelated conditions: transnational surveillance pressure from North Korea, identity-linked digital infrastructure and intermediary dependency in China, and threat reasoning shaped by prior surveillance schemas. Participants developed uneven protective practices and followed two trajectories: adaptive habituation driven by knowledge acquisition and erosive habituation driven by the prolonged absence of adverse events. Our findings show how compound threats, attribution uncertainty, and constrained agency shape security reasoning among undocumented users.

</details>


### [235] [The Popular Get More Popular: Auditing Brand Bias in Generative Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07614)
*Giorgio Monaco,Jacopo Lenti,Francesco Pierri*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文系统审计生成式搜索引擎的两类品牌偏差：品牌流行度偏差（头部品牌被引用频率为平均品牌的1.4–2.8倍）与品牌熟悉度偏差（替换为不熟悉品牌签名会显著降低推荐排名，而保持事实的内容改写几乎无增益），揭示生成式搜索强化"富者愈富"格局并形成参数化进入壁垒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对话式推荐将众多选项综合为少数直接答案，使得对特定供应商的细微系统性偏好产生巨大商业影响；因此需要量化生成式搜索中品牌层面的偏差及其来源与可缓解性。

Method: 从两个维度开展审计：(i) 跨主流引擎对商业查询进行评估，在1,176个头部市场参与者样本中统计品牌被引用频率，考察流行度偏差及外部网页检索的缓解作用；(ii) 在保持产品属性不变的受控上下文实验中，将真实品牌签名替换为不熟悉签名，并对比保持事实的内容改写对排名的影响。

Result: 观测到持续存在的品牌流行度偏差：处于前五分位的优势品牌被引用频率是样本平均品牌的1.4至2.8倍，且该偏差已固化于参数化记忆中，外部检索无法缓解；品牌签名替换为不熟悉形式后推荐排名显著下降，而保持事实的内容改写带来的排名提升可忽略不计。

Conclusion: 生成式搜索引擎通过偏好既有市场领导者强化"富者愈富"动态，并形成标准内容优化难以逾越的参数化进入壁垒。

Abstract: The shift from traditional to generative search has fundamentally transformed online product discovery. Because conversational recommendations synthesize choices into a few direct answers, subtle systemic preferences toward specific providers can exert profound commercial impacts. In this work, we audit this phenomenon through two distinct dimensions: (i) brand popularity bias, the systematic preference for market-dominant entities over long-tail alternatives, and (ii) brand familiarity bias, the ranking advantage an entity encoded in an LLM's parametric memory holds over an unfamiliar competitor. First, evaluating commercial queries across major engines, we observe persistent brand popularity bias: dominant brands in the top quintile are cited 1.4x to 2.8x more frequently than the average brand within a sample of 1,176 top market players, already present in parametric memory that external web retrieval fails to mitigate. Second, through controlled in-context experiments holding product attributes constant, we find that substituting authentic brand signatures with unfamiliar ones significantly degrades recommendation rank. Conversely, fact-preserving content rewrites yield negligible ranking gains. Taken together, our findings demonstrate that generative search engines reinforce a ``rich-get-richer'' dynamic by favoring established market leaders and imposing a parametric barrier to entry that standard content optimization cannot overcome.

</details>


### [236] [Operationalizing NCII and CSAM Law in the Age of AI: Open Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07831)
*Kimberly Mai,Cate Heine,Vinith M. Suriyakumar,Ashia C. Wilson*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文以英格兰与威尔士及美国法律为背景，分析生成式AI引发的合成非自愿私密影像（NCII）与儿童性虐待材料（CSAM）相关法律改革，指出现有法律定义（可识别性、模型能力/意图、图像归因）在技术层面难以操作化，并提出跨技术—法律界面的研究议程，涵盖法律解释、部署前危害预防与犯罪执法三方面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI导致合成NCII与CSAM激增，各国随之进行法律改革；但这些改革依赖的法律定义在技术上缺乏明确的操作化路径，若无技术支撑，法律保护难以落地，因此需要明确技术—法律交界处的开放问题。

Method: 采用法律—技术交叉分析方法：以英格兰与威尔士及美国的相关法律为对象，将法律条文中的关键概念（人物是否可识别、模型是否具备滥用生成能力或有此意图、滥用图像能否溯源至生成模型）与技术研究现状对照，识别可操作化缺口，并据此构建研究议程。

Result: 识别出三类技术—法律界面问题：现有法律的解释与适用、AI工具部署前的危害预防、以及犯罪发生后的执法与溯源；这些问题均缺乏成熟的技术方案。

Conclusion: 技术界具备解决这些问题的能力，推进该领域研究对现实世界中的受害者保护至关重要；作者呼吁技术社区围绕法律需求开展针对性研究。

Abstract: Advances in generative AI have driven a surge in synthetic non-consensual intimate imagery (NCII) and child sexual abuse material (CSAM), prompting a series of legal reforms. These reforms rely on legal definitions where technical operationalization is ambiguous, such as whether a depicted person is identifiable, whether a model is capable of or intended for abuse generation, and whether an abusive image can be traced to the model that produced it. Drawing on the law of England and Wales and the United States, we examine the relevant law alongside the technical research needed to operationalize it. From this, we develop a research agenda of open problems at this technical-legal interface, spanning the interpretation of existing law, the prevention of harm before an AI tool is deployed, and enforcement of offenses. These are problems the technical community is well placed to solve, and where progress is vital to real-world protection.

</details>


### [237] [How Generative AI Redefines Personalization and Diversity in the Newsroom](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07931)
*Cheng Yu,Jim Chi,Aaron J. Snoswell,Orestis Papakyriakopoulos*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 生成式AI改变了新闻个性化与信息多样性张力的作用机制：多样性相关决策正前移至生产环节并变得不可见，记者在形式上负责却难以观测与控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，新闻个性化与信息多样性的张力被理解为"不同内容触达不同人群"，多样性以受众接触的来源、议题与视角广度来衡量。生成式AI使这一理解过时：它一方面改变记者如何为受众改编新闻，另一方面记者所用的AI工具也在按其自身偏好个性化内容生产。作者因此要考察这些实践如何重塑编辑判断，以及在何种条件下新闻多样性得以被生产。

Method: 对公共服务、商业与国有附属新闻机构中的16位媒体从业者进行半结构化访谈，分析其AI整合实践及其对编辑判断与多样性生产条件的影响。

Result: 受访者通过"保留对意义的权威、外包内容呈现环节"来协商AI整合；但其自用工具同时中介了哪些来源与视角可被获得，引入了对组织监督基本不可见的多样性相关决策。受访者能预期面向受众的个性化带来的多样性风险，却主要将自身工具中"多样性受限"视为技术问题。因此，塑造新闻多样性的决策越来越多地发生在发表之前、并分布于故事的多个版本中，既有的发表内容评估与人工审批都难以观察。

Conclusion: 记者仍在形式上对结果负责，但这些结果由他们无法充分看见或控制的决策所塑造。守护新闻多样性需超越已发表内容，转向塑造内容的专业实践、基础设施与控制权分配。

Abstract: The tension between news personalization and information diversity has traditionally been understood as a matter of different content reaching different people, with diversity evaluated by the range of sources, topics, and perspectives to which audiences are exposed. Generative AI outdates this understanding, since it transforms personalization in two ways: how journalists adapt news to audiences and how the AI tools journalists use personalize content production to their own preferences. Drawing on 16 semi-structured interviews with media professionals across public service, commercial, and state-affiliated news settings, we examine how these practices reshape editorial judgment and the conditions under which news diversity is produced. We find that participants negotiate AI integration by maintaining authority over meaning while delegating aspects of content presentation. Yet the tools they adapt for their own work also mediate which sources and perspectives become available, introducing diversity-relevant decisions that remain largely invisible to organizational oversight. While participants anticipate diversity risks in audience-facing personalization, they predominantly interpret diversity-imposed limitations in their own tools as technical problems. As a result, decisions that shape news diversity increasingly occur before publication and across multiple versions of a story, where neither evaluation of published items nor human approval can readily observe them. Journalists thus remain formally responsible for outcomes shaped by decisions they cannot fully see or control. Our findings highlight that safeguarding news diversity requires looking beyond published content to the professional practices, infrastructures, and distributions of control that shape it.

</details>


### [238] [tok: a textual concrete syntax of BPMN and DMN for humans and AI agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07945)
*Marek Deja*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: tok 是一种面向 BPMN 2.0.2 流程模型与 DMN 1.5 决策的纯文本记法，可作为 BPMN/DMN 元模型的替代具体语法；单一引擎可校验、编译为带图的 XML，并通过 MCP 服务语言模型应用。其 token 数为最小 BPMN XML 的 1/2.2、原始导出文件的 1/7.7，故障检出率 98.2%，且大模型首次接触即可写出高比例有效 tok。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BPMN/DMN 现有 XML 具体语法冗长、可读性差、难以被语言模型高效生成与处理，因此需要一种更紧凑、可自动校验与编译的纯文本替代记法，并系统评估其在 token 效率、错误检出与语言模型可用性上的表现。

Method: 提出 tok 纯文本记法，理论上将其定位为 BPMN 与 DMN 元模型的替代具体语法；实现统一引擎：同时按两项标准校验、编译为带自动生成图的 XML、并通过 MCP 向语言模型应用提供服务。实验包括：与 37 个工具导出的 893 个文件比较 token 数量；注入 1,411 个故障比较检出率（对比 XML Schema + bpmnlint）；4 个未见 tok 的语言模型仅凭一页参考生成 tok 与 BPMN XML 的对照；以带精简技能与单一校验工具的智能体模式测试；以及从公开模型模拟 47 个事件日志并做导入一致性检验。

Result: tok 模型 token 数比 893 个文件中最小的 BPMN XML 少 2.2 倍，比原始导出文件少 7.7 倍。校验器检出 1,411 个注入故障中的 98.2%，XML Schema 配合 bpmnlint 为 85.8%。4 个模型仅凭一页参考，对两个数据集的 87 个流程描述生成有效 tok 的比例为 85.7%–100%，且对每个描述均生成有效 BPMN XML；最强模型的 tok token 数比 BPMN XML 少 25% 和 19%。在智能体模式下（精简技能 + 一个校验工具），有效 tok 率达 97.6%–100%，最强模型 tok 比 BPMN XML 节省 21% 和 14%；而通过工具读取参考文档的技能消耗 token 为精简技能的 1.7–2.8 倍。47 个模拟日志中 44 个与其源模型拟合，tok 可导入日志中发现的所有模型。

Conclusion: tok 在语法层面可无损承载 BPMN/DMN 语义，同时显著降低 token 开销、提升故障检出率，并对语言模型友好（含智能体场景）；技能设计应保持精简以避免 token 浪费。日志模拟结果同时提示仿真与现实存在差距，可作为后续改进方向。

Abstract: tok is a plain-text notation for Business Process Model and Notation (BPMN) 2.0.2 process models and Decision Model and Notation (DMN) 1.5 decisions. One engine checks a tok document against both standards, compiles the document to XML with an automatic diagram, and serves language-model applications through the Model Context Protocol. The paper classifies the notation as an alternative concrete syntax of the BPMN and DMN metamodels and reports one study. A tok model takes 2.2 times fewer tokens than the smallest BPMN XML of 893 files exported by 37 tools and 7.7 times fewer than the files as exported. The checker signals 98.2% of 1,411 injected faults, against 85.8% for the XML Schema with bpmnlint. Four language models new to tok write valid tok from a one-page reference for 85.7 to 100% of 87 process descriptions from two datasets and valid BPMN XML for every description, and the strongest model writes tok with 25% and 19% fewer tokens than BPMN XML. As agents with a lean skill and one checker tool, the models write valid tok for 97.6 to 100% of the descriptions, the strongest model spends 21% and 14% fewer tokens on tok than on BPMN XML, and a skill which sends the model to read the reference through a tool costs 1.7 to 2.8 times the tokens of the lean skill. Of 47 event logs simulated from public models, 44 fit their source models, and tok imports every model discovered from the logs.

</details>


### [239] [Technohybridism: Recursive Semantic Normalization in AI-Mediated Societies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07984)
*Charalampos M. Liapis,Epameinondas Panagopoulos*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出"递归语义规范化"概念与"技术混合主义"框架，主张以"混合语义系统"为分析单元，考察机器生成表述经人类采纳、机构化保留后如何参与语义常规的再生产与变革，并给出四环节分析模型、采纳条件、社会认识论后果及实证研究的证据阶梯。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言生成式AI的输出正日益回流到其训练材料所来自的交际与制度实践之中，但既有讨论缺乏对"再进入"过程的社会理论/社会认识论层面的系统刻画，且与模型崩溃、循环效应等相关概念界限不清。

Method: 概念分析与理论建模：整合社会理论、社会认识论与语言哲学；提出"递归语义规范化"与"技术混合主义"；将分析模型分解为生成、选择、采纳、存续四环节，并区分"可归因于机器的变化"与"本就会发生的变化"；将采纳分析为常规化、制度扩散与社会分层化的实践，界定机器衍生规律转为规范的条件；辅以实证研究的证据阶梯。

Result: （1）与模型崩溃、循环效应、机器惯习、机器文化、AI证言认识论等邻近解释作出区分；（2）建立四环节分析模型及机器归因判据；（3）识别三类社会认识论后果：来源洗白削弱认识警觉、认知多样性侵蚀危及集体探究、共享解释学资源收窄；（4）提出证据阶梯。

Conclusion: 该解释不赋予机器输出任何规范权威；真正发生变化的是人类与机构采纳行为所处的作用场域，因而需以混合语义系统而非单一模型或单一人类实践为分析单位。

Abstract: Language-generating AI systems are no longer only objects of linguistic interaction: their outputs increasingly re-enter the communicative and institutional practices from which their training material was drawn. Drawing on social theory, social epistemology, and the philosophy of language, this article develops a sociotechnical account of that re-entry. We introduce recursive semantic normalization: the process through which machine-generated formulations, once recurrently taken up, evaluated, reused, and institutionally retained, participate in the reproduction and transformation of semantic regularities. Under the heading of technohybridism, we argue that the relevant unit of analysis is the hybrid semantic system, a recursively coupled configuration of human practices, computational generation, and institutional uptake. First, we distinguish the concept from neighbouring accounts, including model collapse, looping effects, machine habitus, machine culture, and the epistemology of AI testimony. Second, we develop an analytical model that separates generation, selection, uptake, and persistence and distinguishes machine-attributable change from change that would have occurred anyway. Third, we analyse uptake as routinized, institutionally diffused, and socially stratified practice and state conditions under which machine-derived regularities become norms. Fourth, we identify social-epistemic consequences: the laundering of provenance that weakens epistemic vigilance, the erosion of the cognitive diversity on which collective inquiry depends, and the narrowing of shared hermeneutical resources. An evidential ladder for empirical research follows. The account attributes no normative authority to machine outputs; what changes is the field within which human and institutional uptake operates.

</details>


### [240] [The Moving Frontier: How the National Justice Project Is Using Digital Technology to Expand Access to Justice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08052)
*George Newhouse AM,Trisha Ramesh,Tiara de Silva*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文聚焦被司法科技研究忽视的一类技术——面向消费者免费的数字举报平台：用户记录自身受害经历，平台同步聚合这些陈述，形成可用于倡导、机构问责与法律改革的证据体系，并探讨其对边缘群体的赋权潜力与问责功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商业法律科技集中于律所层面的生产力提升，社区组织关注AI赋能边缘群体，但免费、直接面向消费者的法律技术因难以盈利而少有研究；同时，歧视与行政滥权经历常被监督机构视为个体申诉而遭忽视，导致数字举报平台这一独立技术类别缺乏学理关注。

Method: 以概念分析与理论建构为主的法学/司法科技研究路径：界定数字举报平台这一被忽略的技术类别，围绕"知识—权力鸿沟"与"证据聚合—机构问责"两条分析线索，构建其变革潜力的分析框架。

Result: 提出数字举报平台可作为弥合边缘社区与法律系统间知识与权力差距的机制，并将分散的个体受害叙事聚合为支撑倡导、机构问责和法律改革的证据资源，从而突破"个体申诉"的定性框架。

Conclusion: 数字举报平台构成一类具有独特公共价值却未被充分研究的司法技术，其证据聚合与赋权功能可推动机构问责与社会公平，应纳入司法科技研究的核心议程。

Abstract: Over the past three decades, technological advances have assisted individuals to gain access to legal information, enhance the efficiency of legal services, and streamline dispute resolution. More recently, the proliferation of generative artificial intelligence has reinforced this emphasis on firm-level productivity, with commercial legal practice increasingly leveraging artificial intelligence tools to automate document creation and optimise case management. While commercial technology focuses on productivity, community organisations are exploring how artificial intelligence can level the playing field for marginalised communities. Far less attention has been given to free, direct-to-consumer legal technologies, as these models are often unprofitable. Consequently, justice technology scholarship has overlooked a distinct class of technology: digital reporting platforms that enable people to record their experiences of harm, while simultaneously aggregating those accounts into a body of evidence capable of supporting advocacy, institutional accountability, and law reform. Crucially, experiences of discrimination and administrative abuse are often dismissed by oversight bodies as individual grievances. To understand the transformative potential of these platforms, this article asks two questions. First, how can digital reporting close the knowledge and power gap for marginalised communities interacting with the legal system? Second, how can these tools collect evidence to hold institutions accountable, drive change and contribute to a more just society?

</details>


### [241] [Who Can Evaluate AI Independently? A Framework for Analysing Evaluation Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08134)
*Sumaya Nur Adan,Joanna Wiaterek,Nourhan Abdelrhim,Sydney Reis,Robert F. Trager,Benjamin Rosman,Marina Jirotka*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 论文提出PAAI（权力、准入、充分性、独立性）框架，分析评估基础设施安排如何分配评估权威，并对12个非洲AI评估项目与AI安全研究所网络的联合测试实践进行检验，发现参与评估并不等于拥有评估权威。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI评估日益成为AI安全与治理的核心，但"参与评估"本身并不赋予对评估设计、执行及所依赖资源的控制权。既有讨论多关注评估方法与指标，较少追问谁掌控评估基础设施、以何种条件开放，从而忽视了评估独立性与治理可信度的结构性前提。

Method: 融合科学技术研究（STS）、政治经济学与应用评估标准，构建PAAI框架，从四个维度考察：谁控制评估基础设施、基础设施以何种条件可用、是否与评估目的相称（充分性）、支撑何种程度的自主性。实证上应用于12个非洲AI评估项目（2022–2025），以及AI安全研究所国际网络中三家已公布联合测试中全部十个成员机构的公开角色记录。

Result: 识别出三种反复出现的配置：攫取式合作、依附式创新、受限的独立。非洲行为体多提供数据、语言专业能力与任务设计，而外部机构掌握基础设施与评估框架；仅有设计权而无基础设施权从未带来超过部分的独立性。联合测试亦呈类似分工：主导各次测试的机构同时负责运行模型，肯尼亚作为该网络唯一非洲成员从标注升级到任务设计，但无公开记录的模型执行角色。

Conclusion: 参与评估并不自动产生评估权威。评估若要成为可信的治理机制，准入必须与对优先事项、方法、发表与持续性的控制权相结合；否则评估易沦为外部机构主导下的数据与人力供给环节。

Abstract: AI evaluation is increasingly central to AI safety and governance, yet taking part in an evaluation does not by itself confer authority over its design, its execution or the resources it depends on. This paper examines how infrastructure arrangements distribute that authority, and so shape who can evaluate AI systems independently.
