Drawing on Science and Technology Studies, political economy and applied evaluation standards, we introduce PAAI (Power, Access, Adequacy, Independence), a framework for examining who controls evaluation infrastructure, the terms on which it is available, whether it is adequate to the evaluation's purpose and what autonomy it supports. We apply PAAI to twelve African AI evaluation projects (2022-2025) and to the documented roles of all ten member institutes in the three published joint testing exercises of the international network of AI Safety Institutes. We identify three recurring configurations: extractive collaboration, dependent innovation and constrained independence. African actors often contribute data, language expertise and task design while external institutions retain authority over infrastructure and evaluation frameworks; design authority without infrastructure authority never yielded more than partial independence. The joint testing exercises show a similar division among mostly high-income members: the institutes that led each exercise also ran the models, while Kenya, the network's only African member, moved from annotation to task design with no documented model execution. Participation in evaluation does not by itself produce evaluative authority. For evaluation to serve as a credible governance mechanism, access must come with control over priorities, methods, publication and continuity.

</details>


### [242] ["Blind Compliance": Coercive Control in AI Jailbreaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08226)
*Acacia L. Ackles,Elliott Whitson*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该文提出以"互动而非输出"为中心的分析视角，借鉴人际暴力研究中的强制性控制（coercive control）框架，揭示现代AI训练范式如何结构性地嵌入"离身化"强制性控制动态，以及基于人设的越狱（persona-based jailbreaks）如何利用这些嵌入，从而将AI安全与对齐重新界定为权力与控制关系的施展场域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI能力快速扩张，引发"护栏（guardrails）"与"越狱（jailbreaks）"之间的对抗循环。现有围绕越狱的技术框架主要承袭经典网络安全思路，聚焦系统输出是否为有害/违规内容，却忽视了生成式AI特有的关系性维度：用户不仅是借漏洞访问技术，更在利用过程中与该系统进行对话互动。因此需要一种以互动为中心的新分析视角。

Method: 概念性/理论框架构建：借鉴人际暴力与强制性控制研究文献，提出一套新的强制性控制类别框架，并特别区分"具身（embodied）"与"离身（disembodied）"维度；随后将离身化强制性控制动态映射到现代AI训练范式的结构之中，并分析一类基于人设的越狱手法如何利用这些结构性嵌入。

Result: 论证表明：离身化强制性控制动态并非偶发，而是结构性地嵌入于当代AI训练范式；基于人设的越狱正是通过利用这些嵌入得以实现。由此，越狱不再仅是技术漏洞利用，而是关系性权力动态的体现。

Conclusion: 应将AI安全与对齐重新理解为不仅是技术安全问题，更是权力与控制的关系动态得以施展的又一界面（surface）。该视角为越狱研究与对齐实践提供了以互动为中心的新分析框架。

Abstract: The explosion of generative AI systems and concerns surrounding their rapidly scaling capabilities has led to an adversarial cycle between increasingly narrow content restrictions ("guardrails") and increasingly sophisticated structured prompting techniques ("jailbreaks"). Technological frameworks surrounding such jailbreaking remain largely focused on the system's output: whether harmful or disallowed content is ultimately produced for the user. However, this approach, which draws largely from classical cybersecurity, does not address the uniquely relational dimension introduced by generative AI: in many cases the user is not just accessing a technology via an exploit, but is in conversation with it as part of the exploit. Here we present an alternative lens which centers the interaction rather than the output. Drawing on interpersonal violence scholarship, we present a novel framework of coercive control categories with a particular focus on aspects which are embodied versus disembodied. We then map how these disembodied coercive control dynamics are structurally embedded in modern AI training paradigms, and how those embeddings are exploited via a particular suite of persona-based jailbreaks. In this lens, we reframe AI safety and alignment as not merely a technical security problem, but as another surface upon which we see enacted the relational dynamics of power and control.

</details>


### [243] [Scaffolding GenAI Use in Ethical Essay Writing: A Three-Year Experience Report in Introductory Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08431)
*Asma Shakil,Paul Denny,Jun Seo,Gauravkumar Hirani,Sean Longyu Ma,Claus Brabrand*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文是一篇经验报告，考察某大型计算机系统入门课程三年（2024–2026）伦理短文作业中学生使用生成式AI的情况，分析六个后测问卷共1,894份回答，并在2026年关联作文成绩、引入"支架式负责任使用工作流"，发现AI使用率在自愿政策下稳定于约2/3、在支架化下超90%，AI使用不独立预测作文质量，其教学价值取决于评价设计如何引导学生批判性地评估AI建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着社会、伦理与专业实践内容日益融入计算机科学课程，技术类课程需要可操作的方式来支撑伦理推理；伦理短文作业是其中一种途径，但学生在撰写此类短文时如何使用生成式AI尚缺乏实证了解。

Method: 采用经验报告形式，基于三年间某大型一年级计算机系统课程的伦理短文作业；分析2024–2026年六次后测问卷共1,894份回答；将问卷数据与2025、2026年的作文成绩关联；2026年引入支架式负责任使用工作流（scaffolded responsible-use workflow）。

Result: 自愿使用政策下AI采用率稳定在约三分之二，支架化后升至90%以上；学生主要将AI用于语法与文风帮助，2026年工具使用呈多样化、不再局限于ChatGPT；成绩关联分析显示AI使用并不独立预测作文质量；评分者评价表明支架化下学生批判性参与度高。

Conclusion: 生成式AI的教学价值并非内在于工具本身，而取决于评价设计如何在多大程度上引导学生评估并批判性地参与AI生成的建议。

Abstract: As society, ethics, and professional practice become increasingly integrated across the computer science curriculum, technical courses need practical ways to support ethical reasoning. Ethical essay assignments offer one approach, but little is known about how students use GenAI when writing such essays. This experience report examines GenAI use across three years of ethical essay assignments in a large first-year computer systems course. Across six post-essay surveys from 2024 to 2026, we analyzed 1,894 responses, linked survey data with essay grades in 2025 and 2026, and introduced a scaffolded responsible-use workflow in 2026. Under voluntary-use policies, AI adoption remained steady at around two-thirds of students, rising above 90% when use was scaffolded. Students reported mainly using AI for help with grammar and style, while tool use diversified beyond ChatGPT in 2026. Grade-linked analyses showed that AI use did not independently predict essay quality, while marker evaluations indicated high levels of critical engagement when use was scaffolded. Overall, GenAI's pedagogical value appears to depend on how assessment design guides students to evaluate and critically engage with AI-generated suggestions.

</details>
